| [Theory](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/manuals/pdfs/EFDC_Theory_Document_Ver_12.pdf) PDF 156쪽 / 인쇄 143쪽, 식 **8.7**<br>`\displaystyle P_x=PM_xf_1(N)f_2(I)f_3(T)f_4(S)`<br>[판독 기록:24](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/_staging/recovery/read/EFDC/manuals/pdfs/EFDC_Theory_Document_Ver_12.pdf/p145-162.md:24) | [Eutrophication/mod_wq.f90:3462](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/Eutrophication/mod_wq.f90:3462) `            WQPA(L,NAL) = ALGAES(NAL).WQPMA (IMWQZT(L)) * WQF1N(NAL)*WQF2I(NAL)*WQTDG(IWQT(L),NAL)      ! *** Nutrient & Temperature Adjusted`<br>[Eutrophication/mod_wq.f90:3468](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/Eutrophication/mod_wq.f90:3468) `            WQF4SC  = ALGAES(NAL).WQSTOX / (ALGAES(NAL).WQSTOX + SWQ(L)*SWQ(L) + 1.E-12)` | 일치 | 최대 성장률에 영양염·광·온도 제한을 곱한다. 염분 독성 옵션에서 염분 제한을 추가한다. |
| [Theory](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/manuals/pdfs/EFDC_Theory_Document_Ver_12.pdf) PDF 156쪽 / 인쇄 143쪽, 식 **8.8**<br>`\displaystyle f_1(N)=min\left(\frac{NH4+NO3}{KHN_x+NH4+NO3},\frac{PO4}{KHP_x+PO4},\frac{SAd}{KHS+SAd}\right)`<br>[판독 기록:24](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/_staging/recovery/read/EFDC/manuals/pdfs/EFDC_Theory_Document_Ver_12.pdf/p145-162.md:24) | [Eutrophication/mod_wq.f90:3317](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/Eutrophication/mod_wq.f90:3317) `            WQGN(NAL) = RNH4NO3(L) / (ALGAES(NAL).WQKHNA + RNH4NO3(L)+ 1.E-18)`<br>[Eutrophication/mod_wq.f90:3318](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/Eutrophication/mod_wq.f90:3318) `            WQGP(NAL) = PO4DWQ(L)  / (ALGAES(NAL).WQKHPA + PO4DWQ(L) + 1.E-18)`<br>[Eutrophication/mod_wq.f90:3319](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/Eutrophication/mod_wq.f90:3319) `            WQF1N(NAL) = min(WQGN(NAL), WQGP(NAL))                                  ! *** Minimum of the N/P `<br>[Eutrophication/mod_wq.f90:3343](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/Eutrophication/mod_wq.f90:3343) `              WQF1N(NAL) = min(WQF1N(NAL),WQGSD)                                    ! *** Limit: Diatoms - Minimum of the N/P/S` | 일치 | 질소·용존 인의 최소 제한을 구한다. 규소 사용 군에서 규소 제한을 추가한다. CO2 옵션은 별도 제한을 추가한다. |
| [Theory](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/manuals/pdfs/EFDC_Theory_Document_Ver_12.pdf) PDF 157쪽 / 인쇄 144쪽, 식 **8.9**<br>`\displaystyle f_2(I)=\frac{\exp(1)\ FD}{K_{ess}(ZB-ZT)}\left(\exp(-\alpha_b)-\exp(-\alpha_T)\right)`<br>[판독 기록:25](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/_staging/recovery/read/EFDC/manuals/pdfs/EFDC_Theory_Document_Ver_12.pdf/p145-162.md:25) | [Eutrophication/mod_wq.f90:3373](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/Eutrophication/mod_wq.f90:3373) `          WQTT1 = WQFD*2.718                                     ! *** EXP(1.0) = 2.718`<br>[Eutrophication/mod_wq.f90:3379](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/Eutrophication/mod_wq.f90:3379) `              WQF2I(NAL) = WQTT1/(HPK(L,K)*WQKESS)*(EXPA1 - EXPA0)` | 불일치 | Steele 함수의 깊이 적분 구조는 같다. 문서는 자연상수 e를 사용한다. 코드는 e를 2.718로 근사한다. 이 차이는 계수 근사에 해당한다. |
| [Theory](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/manuals/pdfs/EFDC_Theory_Document_Ver_12.pdf) PDF 157쪽 / 인쇄 144쪽, 식 **8.10**<br>`\displaystyle \alpha_B=\left(\frac{I_0}{FD\cdot I_{sx}}\right)\exp(-K_{ess}\ ZB)`<br>[판독 기록:25](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/_staging/recovery/read/EFDC/manuals/pdfs/EFDC_Theory_Document_Ver_12.pdf/p145-162.md:25) | [Eutrophication/mod_wq.f90:3378](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/Eutrophication/mod_wq.f90:3378) `              EXPA1 = EXP(-WQIBOT(L)  /(WQFD*WQIS(L,NAL)))              ! *** WQITOP(L,K)/WQIS(L,NAL) is the fraction of actual SR to average SR at the BOTTOM` | 일치 | EXPA1이 층 하단의 무차원 광 강도이다. |
| [Theory](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/manuals/pdfs/EFDC_Theory_Document_Ver_12.pdf) PDF 157쪽 / 인쇄 144쪽, 식 **8.11**<br>`\displaystyle \alpha_T=\left(\frac{I_0}{FD\cdot I_{sx}}\right)\exp(-K_{ess}\ ZT)`<br>[판독 기록:25](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/_staging/recovery/read/EFDC/manuals/pdfs/EFDC_Theory_Document_Ver_12.pdf/p145-162.md:25) | [Eutrophication/mod_wq.f90:3377](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/Eutrophication/mod_wq.f90:3377) `              EXPA0 = EXP(-WQITOP(L,K)/(WQFD*WQIS(L,NAL)))              ! *** WQITOP(L,K)/WQIS(L,NAL) is the fraction of actual SR to average SR at the TOP ` | 일치 | EXPA0이 층 상단의 무차원 광 강도이다. |
| [Theory](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/manuals/pdfs/EFDC_Theory_Document_Ver_12.pdf) PDF 158쪽 / 인쇄 145쪽, 식 **8.12**<br>`\displaystyle I_{sx}=\max\left(I_{0avg}\cdot\exp(-K_{ess}\ D_{optx}),I_{sxmin}\right)`<br>[판독 기록:26](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/_staging/recovery/read/EFDC/manuals/pdfs/EFDC_Theory_Document_Ver_12.pdf/p145-162.md:26) | [Eutrophication/mod_wq.f90:3359](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/Eutrophication/mod_wq.f90:3359) `                WQIS(L,NAL) = max( WQAVGIO*EXP(-WQKESS*ALGAES(NAL).WQDOP(IZ)), WQISMIN )  ` | 일치 | 광 최적값의 최소 한계를 MAX로 적용한다. |
| [Theory](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/manuals/pdfs/EFDC_Theory_Document_Ver_12.pdf) PDF 158쪽 / 인쇄 145쪽, 식 **8.13**<br>`\displaystyle I_{0avg}=CI_aI_0+CI_bI_1+CI_cI_2`<br>[판독 기록:26](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/_staging/recovery/read/EFDC/manuals/pdfs/EFDC_Theory_Document_Ver_12.pdf/p145-162.md:26) | [Eutrophication/mod_wq.f90:3032](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/Eutrophication/mod_wq.f90:3032) `    WQAVGIO = WQCIA*WQI0 + WQCIB*WQI1 + WQCIC*WQI2`<br>[Eutrophication/mod_wq.f90:3034](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/Eutrophication/mod_wq.f90:3034) `    WQAVGIO = WQCIA*WQI1 + WQCIB*WQI2 + WQCIC*WQI3` | 일치 | 현재·과거 광 강도를 입력 가중치로 합한다. |
| [Theory](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/manuals/pdfs/EFDC_Theory_Document_Ver_12.pdf) PDF 158쪽 / 인쇄 145쪽, 식 **8.14**<br>`\displaystyle f_3(T)=\begin{cases}\exp\left(-KTG1_x\cdot(T-TM1_x)^2\right),\ T\leq TM1_x\\1,\ TM1_x<T<TM2_x\\\exp\left(-KTG2_x\cdot(T-TM2_x)^2\right),\ T\geq TM2_x\end{cases}`<br>[판독 기록:26](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/_staging/recovery/read/EFDC/manuals/pdfs/EFDC_Theory_Document_Ver_12.pdf/p145-162.md:26) | [Eutrophication/mod_biota.f90:370](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/Eutrophication/mod_biota.f90:370) `      WQTDG(M,NAL) = 1.`<br>[Eutrophication/mod_biota.f90:372](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/Eutrophication/mod_biota.f90:372) `        WQTDG(M,NAL) = EXP(-ALGAES(NAL).WQKG1*(WTEMP - ALGAES(NAL).WQTM1)*(WTEMP - ALGAES(NAL).WQTM1) )`<br>[Eutrophication/mod_biota.f90:375](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/Eutrophication/mod_biota.f90:375) `        WQTDG(M,NAL) = EXP(-ALGAES(NAL).WQKG2*(WTEMP - ALGAES(NAL).WQTM2)*(WTEMP - ALGAES(NAL).WQTM2) )` | 일치 | 최적 온도 구간에서는 1을 사용한다. 구간 밖에서는 서로 다른 이차 지수식을 사용한다. |
| [Theory](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/manuals/pdfs/EFDC_Theory_Document_Ver_12.pdf) PDF 159쪽 / 인쇄 146쪽, 식 **8.15**<br>`\displaystyle f_4(S)=\frac{STOXS^2}{STOXS^2+S^2}`<br>[판독 기록:27](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/_staging/recovery/read/EFDC/manuals/pdfs/EFDC_Theory_Document_Ver_12.pdf/p145-162.md:27) | [Eutrophication/mod_biota.f90:326](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/Eutrophication/mod_biota.f90:326) `    ALGAES(NAL).WQSTOX = ALGAES(NAL).WQSTOX*ALGAES(NAL).WQSTOX`<br>[Eutrophication/mod_wq.f90:3468](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/Eutrophication/mod_wq.f90:3468) `            WQF4SC  = ALGAES(NAL).WQSTOX / (ALGAES(NAL).WQSTOX + SWQ(L)*SWQ(L) + 1.E-12)` | 일치 | 입력 STOXS를 읽은 뒤 제곱한다. 런타임 WQSTOX가 문서의 STOXS²이다. 분모에 1E−12를 추가한다. |
| [Theory](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/manuals/pdfs/EFDC_Theory_Document_Ver_12.pdf) PDF 159쪽 / 인쇄 146쪽, 식 **8.16**<br>`\displaystyle BM_x=BMR_x\exp\left(KTB_x[T-TR_x]\right)`<br>[판독 기록:27](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/_staging/recovery/read/EFDC/manuals/pdfs/EFDC_Theory_Document_Ver_12.pdf/p145-162.md:27) | [Eutrophication/mod_biota.f90:381](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/Eutrophication/mod_biota.f90:381) `      WQTDR(M,NAL) = EXP( ALGAES(NAL).WQKTB*(WTEMP - ALGAES(NAL).WQTR) ) `<br>[Eutrophication/mod_wq.f90:3463](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/Eutrophication/mod_wq.f90:3463) `            WQBM(L,NAL) = ALGAES(NAL).WQBMRA(IMWQZT(L)) * WQTDR(IWQT(L),NAL)                            ! *** Temperature Adjusted` | 일치 | 기준 기초대사율에 기준 온도와의 차이에 대한 지수함수를 곱한다. |
| [Theory](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/manuals/pdfs/EFDC_Theory_Document_Ver_12.pdf) PDF 159쪽 / 인쇄 146쪽, 식 **8.17**<br>`\displaystyle PR_x=PRR_x\left(\frac{B_x}{B_{xP}}\right)^{\alpha_P}\exp\left(KTP_x[T-TP_x]\right)`<br>[판독 기록:27](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/_staging/recovery/read/EFDC/manuals/pdfs/EFDC_Theory_Document_Ver_12.pdf/p145-162.md:27) | [Eutrophication/mod_wq.f90:3464](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/Eutrophication/mod_wq.f90:3464) `            WQPR(L,NAL) = ALGAES(NAL).WQPRRA(IMWQZT(L)) * WQTDR(IWQT(L),NAL)                            ! *** Temperature Adjusted` | 불일치 | 코드는 기초대사와 같은 WQTDR을 포식률에 곱한다. 문서의 생물량 거듭제곱과 독립 KTP·TP를 사용하지 않는다. |
| [Theory](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/manuals/pdfs/EFDC_Theory_Document_Ver_12.pdf) PDF 160쪽 / 인쇄 147쪽, 식 **8.18**<br>`\displaystyle PR_x=\frac{PA_z}{KHC_z+PA_z}\cdot RMAX_z\cdot Z_z\cdot\frac{UB_{xz}.B_x}{PA_z}\cdot f(T)`<br>[판독 기록:28](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/_staging/recovery/read/EFDC/manuals/pdfs/EFDC_Theory_Document_Ver_12.pdf/p145-162.md:28) | [Eutrophication/mod_zoopl.f90:351](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/Eutrophication/mod_zoopl.f90:351) `                  BAZ(L,NZO,NAL) = max(0., WQV(L,K,19+NAL) - ZOOPL(NZO).CTZ)`<br>[Eutrophication/mod_zoopl.f90:417](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/Eutrophication/mod_zoopl.f90:417) `                BZPAL = ZOOPL(NZO).WQGZ(L)*ZOOPL(NZO).UBZ(NAL)*BAZ(L,NZO,NAL)/(PRAZ(L,NZO) + 1.E-18)*WQV(L,K,NWQVZ+NZO)` | 불일치 | 코드는 B 대신 MAX(B−CT,0)을 섭식 분자에 사용한다. CT=0일 때 문서 형태로 줄어든다. |
| [Theory](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/manuals/pdfs/EFDC_Theory_Document_Ver_12.pdf) PDF 161쪽 / 인쇄 148쪽, 식 **8.19**<br>`\displaystyle v_s(t)=A\frac{2\pi}{86400}cos\left(\frac{2\pi}{86400}t+\varphi\right)`<br>[판독 기록:29](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/_staging/recovery/read/EFDC/manuals/pdfs/EFDC_Theory_Document_Ver_12.pdf/p145-162.md:29) | [Eutrophication/mod_biota.f90:534](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/Eutrophication/mod_biota.f90:534) `    ALGAES(NAL).SETTLING(L,K) = ALGAES(NAL).AMPLITUDE*2.*PI*COS((TIMEDAY-DAYINT)*2.*PI + ALGAES(NAL).PHASESHIFT)` | 일치 | 코드는 TIMEDAY를 사용하여 m/day 속도를 계산한다. 문서의 초·m/s 표현과 86400 변환 후 같다. |
| [Theory](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/manuals/pdfs/EFDC_Theory_Document_Ver_12.pdf) PDF 161쪽 / 인쇄 148쪽, 식 **8.20**<br>`\displaystyle v_s(t)=\begin{cases}A\frac{2\pi}{86400}cos\left(\frac{2\pi}{86400}t+\varphi\right)e^{-\alpha(H-z)},\ I_s>0\\A\frac{2\pi}{86400}cos\left(\frac{2\pi}{86400}t+\varphi\right),\ I_s\leq0\end{cases}`<br>[판독 기록:29](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/_staging/recovery/read/EFDC/manuals/pdfs/EFDC_Theory_Document_Ver_12.pdf/p145-162.md:29) | [Eutrophication/mod_biota.f90:541](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/Eutrophication/mod_biota.f90:541) `    if( RADTOP(L,KC) >= ALGAES(NAL).LIGHTMIN )then`<br>[Eutrophication/mod_biota.f90:546](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/Eutrophication/mod_biota.f90:546) `        ALGAES(NAL).SETTLING(L,K) = ALGAES(NAL).AMPLITUDE*2.*PI*COS((TIMEDAY-DAYINT)*2.*PI + ALGAES(NAL).PHASESHIFT)*EXP(-EXTC*DEPTH)` | 불일치 | 광 감쇠 항은 대응한다. 코드는 I≥LIGHTMIN을 주간 조건으로 사용한다. 문서는 Is>0을 사용한다. |
| [Theory](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/manuals/pdfs/EFDC_Theory_Document_Ver_12.pdf) PDF 161쪽 / 인쇄 148쪽, 식 **8.21**<br>`\displaystyle \frac{d\rho}{dx}=\left(\frac{N_0}{60}\right)Ie^{-I/I_0}+c,\ I\geq I_c`<br>[판독 기록:29](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/_staging/recovery/read/EFDC/manuals/pdfs/EFDC_Theory_Document_Ver_12.pdf/p145-162.md:29) | [Eutrophication/mod_biota.f90:562](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/Eutrophication/mod_biota.f90:562) `        DENDELTA = (ALGAES(NAL).C1*RADLAYER*EXP(-RADLAYER/ALGAES(NAL).LIGHTSAT) + ALGAES(NAL).C2)*DTWQ*86400.` | 불일치 | 코드는 시간에 따른 밀도 증가율을 계산한다. 문서 좌변은 dρ/dx로 인쇄되어 있다. c1=N0/60 변환은 코드에서 수행하지 않고 입력 c1을 사용한다. |
| [Theory](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/manuals/pdfs/EFDC_Theory_Document_Ver_12.pdf) PDF 161쪽 / 인쇄 148쪽, 식 **8.22**<br>`\displaystyle \frac{d\rho}{dx}=f_1\rho_i+f_2\qquad I<I_c`<br>[판독 기록:29](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/_staging/recovery/read/EFDC/manuals/pdfs/EFDC_Theory_Document_Ver_12.pdf/p145-162.md:29) | [Eutrophication/mod_biota.f90:565](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/Eutrophication/mod_biota.f90:565) `        DENDELTA = (ALGAES(NAL).F1*(CELLDENSLIGHT(L,K,NAL) + 65.) + ALGAES(NAL).F2)*DTWQ*86400.       ! *** Rate of density decrease` | 불일치 | 코드는 이전 밝은 기간의 밀도 CELLDENSLIGHT에 65를 더한다. 문서는 이전 밝은 기간의 밀도 ρi를 정의한다. 문서의 좌변 dρ/dx는 시간 미분 표기와도 다르다. |
| [Theory](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/manuals/pdfs/EFDC_Theory_Document_Ver_12.pdf) PDF 161쪽 / 인쇄 148쪽, 식 **8.23**<br>`\displaystyle \rho_i^{n+1}=\left(c_1Ie^{-I/I_0}+c_2\right)\Delta t+\rho_i^n\qquad I\geq I_c`<br>[판독 기록:29](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/_staging/recovery/read/EFDC/manuals/pdfs/EFDC_Theory_Document_Ver_12.pdf/p145-162.md:29) | [Eutrophication/mod_biota.f90:562](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/Eutrophication/mod_biota.f90:562) `        DENDELTA = (ALGAES(NAL).C1*RADLAYER*EXP(-RADLAYER/ALGAES(NAL).LIGHTSAT) + ALGAES(NAL).C2)*DTWQ*86400.`<br>[Eutrophication/mod_biota.f90:568](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/Eutrophication/mod_biota.f90:568) `      CELLDENS(L,K,NAL) = CELLDENS(L,K,NAL) + DENDELTA` | 일치 | 광 조건에서 c1·c2 속도를 초 시간간격에 곱하여 밀도를 갱신한다. 갱신 뒤 상·하한을 적용한다. |
| [Theory](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/manuals/pdfs/EFDC_Theory_Document_Ver_12.pdf) PDF 161쪽 / 인쇄 148쪽, 식 **8.24**<br>`\displaystyle \rho_i^{n+1}=\left(f_1(\rho_i^n+\rho_*)+f_2\right)\Delta t+\rho_i^n\qquad I<I_c`<br>[판독 기록:29](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/_staging/recovery/read/EFDC/manuals/pdfs/EFDC_Theory_Document_Ver_12.pdf/p145-162.md:29) | [Eutrophication/mod_biota.f90:565](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/Eutrophication/mod_biota.f90:565) `        DENDELTA = (ALGAES(NAL).F1*(CELLDENSLIGHT(L,K,NAL) + 65.) + ALGAES(NAL).F2)*DTWQ*86400.       ! *** Rate of density decrease`<br>[Eutrophication/mod_biota.f90:568](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/Eutrophication/mod_biota.f90:568) `      CELLDENS(L,K,NAL) = CELLDENS(L,K,NAL) + DENDELTA` | 불일치 | 야간 갱신의 기준은 CELLDENSLIGHT이다. 문서의 현재 ρiⁿ와 다르다. 밀도 보정 65를 고정한다. |
| [Theory](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/manuals/pdfs/EFDC_Theory_Document_Ver_12.pdf) PDF 162쪽 / 인쇄 149쪽, 식 **8.25**<br>`\displaystyle v_s(t)=2gr^2\frac{(\rho_i-\rho_w)R}{9\phi n}`<br>[판독 기록:30](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/_staging/recovery/read/EFDC/manuals/pdfs/EFDC_Theory_Document_Ver_12.pdf/p145-162.md:30) | [Eutrophication/mod_biota.f90:573](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/Eutrophication/mod_biota.f90:573) `      VISCW = ViscosityW(TEM(L,KC))                                    ! *** Dynamic viscosity of water (kg/m/s)`<br>[Eutrophication/mod_biota.f90:574](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/Eutrophication/mod_biota.f90:574) `      ALGAES(NAL).SETTLING(L,K) = 2.*G * ALGAES(NAL).CELLRAD**2 * ALGAES(NAL).CELL2COL * (CELLDENS(L,K,NAL) - RHOW(L,K)) / 9. / ALGAES(NAL).CELLDRAG / VISCW`<br>[Eutrophication/mod_biota.f90:575](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/Eutrophication/mod_biota.f90:575) `      ALGAES(NAL).SETTLING(L,K) = ALGAES(NAL).SETTLING(L,K)*86400.     ! *** Convert to m/day` | 일치 | 반경 제곱·밀도차·형상계수·점성에 의한 Stokes 침강식을 계산한다. m/s를 m/day로 변환한다. |
| [Theory](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/manuals/pdfs/EFDC_Theory_Document_Ver_12.pdf) PDF 162쪽 / 인쇄 149쪽, 식 **8.26**<br>`\displaystyle P_m=PM_m\cdot min(f_1(N),f_4(V))\cdot f_2(I)\cdot f_3(T)\cdot f_5(D)`<br>[판독 기록:30](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/_staging/recovery/read/EFDC/manuals/pdfs/EFDC_Theory_Document_Ver_12.pdf/p145-162.md:30) | [Eutrophication/mod_wq.f90:3443](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/Eutrophication/mod_wq.f90:3443) `            WQF1N(NAL) = min(WQLVF, WQF1N(NAL)) `<br>[Eutrophication/mod_wq.f90:3450](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/Eutrophication/mod_wq.f90:3450) `            WQPA(L,NAL) = ALGAES(NAL).WQPMA(IMWQZT(L))*WQF1N(NAL)*WQF2I(NAL)*WQTDG(IWQT(L),NAL)*WQLDF   ! *** Macroalgae growth rate` | 일치 | 영양염과 유속 제한의 최소값에 광·온도·생물량 제한을 곱한다. |
| [Theory](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/manuals/pdfs/EFDC_Theory_Document_Ver_12.pdf) PDF 163쪽 / 인쇄 150쪽, 식 **8.27**<br>`\displaystyle f_4(V)=\frac{U}{KMV+U}`<br>[판독 기록:13](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/_staging/recovery/read/EFDC/manuals/pdfs/EFDC_Theory_Document_Ver_12.pdf/p163-180.md:13) | [Eutrophication/mod_wq.f90:3421](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/Eutrophication/mod_wq.f90:3421) `                  WQLVF = WQVEL / (ALGAES(NAL).WQKMV(IZ) + WQVEL)  `<br>[Eutrophication/mod_wq.f90:3423](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/Eutrophication/mod_wq.f90:3423) `                  WQLVF = ALGAES(NAL).WQKMVMIN(IZ) / (ALGAES(NAL).WQKMV(IZ) + ALGAES(NAL).WQKMVMIN(IZ))  ` | 일치 | 임계 유속 위에서 U/(KMV+U)를 사용한다. 임계 유속 이하의 최소 제한값 처리는 PDF p.164 본문에 있다. |
| [Theory](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/manuals/pdfs/EFDC_Theory_Document_Ver_12.pdf) PDF 163쪽 / 인쇄 150쪽, 식 **8.28**<br>`\displaystyle f_4(V)=d+\frac{a-d}{\left[1+\left(\frac{U}{c}\right)^b\right]^e}`<br>[판독 기록:13](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/_staging/recovery/read/EFDC/manuals/pdfs/EFDC_Theory_Document_Ver_12.pdf/p163-180.md:13) | [Eutrophication/mod_wq.f90:3436](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/Eutrophication/mod_wq.f90:3436) `                XNUMER = ALGAES(NAL).WQKMVA(IZ) - ALGAES(NAL).WQKMVD(IZ)  `<br>[Eutrophication/mod_wq.f90:3437](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/Eutrophication/mod_wq.f90:3437) `                XDENOM = 1.0 + (WQVEL/ALGAES(NAL).WQKMVC(IZ))**ALGAES(NAL).WQKMVB(IZ)  `<br>[Eutrophication/mod_wq.f90:3438](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/Eutrophication/mod_wq.f90:3438) `                WQLVF = ALGAES(NAL).WQKMVD(IZ) + ( XNUMER / (XDENOM**ALGAES(NAL).WQKMVE(IZ)) )  ` | 일치 | a·b·c·d·e의 로지스틱 함수를 그대로 계산한다. |
| [Theory](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/manuals/pdfs/EFDC_Theory_Document_Ver_12.pdf) PDF 164쪽 / 인쇄 151쪽, 식 **8.29**<br>`\displaystyle f_5(D)=\frac{KBP}{KBP+MAC_m}`<br>[판독 기록:14](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/_staging/recovery/read/EFDC/manuals/pdfs/EFDC_Theory_Document_Ver_12.pdf/p163-180.md:14) | [Eutrophication/mod_wq.f90:3446](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/Eutrophication/mod_wq.f90:3446) `            XMRM = WQV(L,K,19+NAL)*HPK(L,K)          ! *** Convert WQV (gC/M3) to a density: XMRM (gC/M2)  `<br>[Eutrophication/mod_wq.f90:3447](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/Eutrophication/mod_wq.f90:3447) `            WQLDF = ALGAES(NAL).WQKBP(IZ) / (ALGAES(NAL).WQKBP(IZ) + XMRM)` | 일치 | 농도에 층 두께를 곱해 MACm의 g C/m²를 만든다. KBP/(KBP+MACm)를 계산한다. |
| [Theory](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/manuals/pdfs/EFDC_Theory_Document_Ver_12.pdf) PDF 165쪽 / 인쇄 152쪽, 식 **8.30**<br>`\displaystyle HM=min\left(\frac{\sum_{K=1}^{KC}BM}{BM_{Lim}},HM_{Max}\right)`<br>[판독 기록:15](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/_staging/recovery/read/EFDC/manuals/pdfs/EFDC_Theory_Document_Ver_12.pdf/p163-180.md:15) | [Eutrophication/mod_biota.f90:707](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/Eutrophication/mod_biota.f90:707) `        TVALHEI = min(WQV(L,K,19+NAL)/ALGAES(NAL).THRESHOLD, 1.0)              ! *** Height of macrophyte in current layer   DELME - /ALGAES(NAL).STEMDENSITY HERE AND EVERYWHERE ?`<br>[Eutrophication/mod_biota.f90:708](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/Eutrophication/mod_biota.f90:708) `        HEIGHT_MAC(L,NAL) = HEIGHT_MAC(L,NAL) + TVALHEI*HPK(L,K)               ! *** Total height of macrophyte`<br>[Eutrophication/mod_wq.f90:563](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/Eutrophication/mod_wq.f90:563) `            HEIGHT_MAC(L,NAL) = ALGAES(NAL).MAXLENGTH` | 불일치 | 코드는 층별 MIN(농도/THRESHOLD,1)에 층 두께를 곱해 높이를 누적한다. 동적 높이에서 문서의 전체 합/임계값과 HMMax 상한을 직접 사용하지 않는다. 임계값 0의 별도 분기에서 고정 MAXLENGTH를 사용한다. |
| [Theory](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/manuals/pdfs/EFDC_Theory_Document_Ver_12.pdf) PDF 165쪽 / 인쇄 152쪽, 식 **8.31**<br>`\displaystyle F_D=\rho\frac{U^2}{2}C_D\lambda`<br>[판독 기록:15](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/_staging/recovery/read/EFDC/manuals/pdfs/EFDC_Theory_Document_Ver_12.pdf/p163-180.md:15) | [calexp2t.f90:750](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/calexp2t.f90:750) `          FXVEG(L,K) = UMAGTMP*SUB3D(L,K)*FXVEG(L,K)   ![m/s] q_xC_d`<br>[calexp2t.f90:767](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/calexp2t.f90:767) `            FX(L,K) = FX(L,K) + (FXVEG(L,K)-FXVEGE(L))*U(L,K)*DXYU(L) ! *** (m^4/s^2)`<br>[caltbxy.f90:728](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/caltbxy.f90:728) `                FXVEG(L,K) = FXVEG(L,K) + 0.25*CPVEGU*(DXP(L) *(MACAD(L,NAL) *HVGTC/MVEGZ(L,NAL)) +   &          ! *** dimensionless` | 불일치 | 문서는 λ를 단위 수평 면적당 줄기 수로 정의한다. 코드는 줄기 지름을 포함한 투영면적 밀도 MACAD와 잠긴 높이, MVEGZ를 사용한다. 격자면 평균과 속도 성분 처리는 수치 구현에 해당한다. 투영면적과 잠긴 높이 항은 문서 식에 나타나지 않는다. |
| [Theory](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/manuals/pdfs/EFDC_Theory_Document_Ver_12.pdf) PDF 166쪽 / 인쇄 153쪽, 식 **8.32**<br>`\displaystyle \frac{\partial Z_z}{\partial t}=(G_z-BM_z-D_z-PR_z)Z_z+\frac{WZ_z}{V}`<br>[판독 기록:16](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/_staging/recovery/read/EFDC/manuals/pdfs/EFDC_Theory_Document_Ver_12.pdf/p163-180.md:16) | [Eutrophication/mod_zoopl.f90:400](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/Eutrophication/mod_zoopl.f90:400) `            WQACZ = (ZOOPL(NZO).WQGZ(L) - ZOOPL(NZO).WQBZ(L) - ZOOPL(NZO).WQDZ(L) - ZOOPL(NZO).WQPZ(L))*DTWQZO2`<br>[Eutrophication/mod_zoopl.f90:403](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/Eutrophication/mod_zoopl.f90:403) `            WQRCZ = WQWPSZ(L,K,NZO)*VOLWQ(L)`<br>[Eutrophication/mod_zoopl.f90:406](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/Eutrophication/mod_zoopl.f90:406) `            WQV(L,K,NWQVZ+NZO) = WQZRR(L)*WQZKK(L)` | 일치 | 성장·기초대사·저산소 사망·포식의 순속도와 외부 부하를 사다리꼴 갱신에 넣는다. |
| [Theory](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/manuals/pdfs/EFDC_Theory_Document_Ver_12.pdf) PDF 166쪽 / 인쇄 153쪽, 식 **8.33**<br>`\displaystyle G_z=\frac{PA_z}{KHC_z+PA_z}.RMAX_z.f(T)`<br>[판독 기록:16](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/_staging/recovery/read/EFDC/manuals/pdfs/EFDC_Theory_Document_Ver_12.pdf/p163-180.md:16) | [Eutrophication/mod_zoopl.f90:368](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/Eutrophication/mod_zoopl.f90:368) `              ZOOPL(NZO).WQGZ(L) = ZOOPL(NZO).RMAXZ(IMWQZT(L))*WQTDGZ(IWQZT(L),NZO)*PRAZ(L,NZO)/(PRAZ(L,NZO) + ZOOPL(NZO).KHCZ + 1.E-18)` | 일치 | 최대 섭식률에 먹이 Monod 제한과 온도 함수를 곱한다. |
| [Theory](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/manuals/pdfs/EFDC_Theory_Document_Ver_12.pdf) PDF 167쪽 / 인쇄 154쪽, 식 **8.34**<br>`\displaystyle BA_{xz}=Max(B_{xz}-CT_z,0)`<br>[판독 기록:17](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/_staging/recovery/read/EFDC/manuals/pdfs/EFDC_Theory_Document_Ver_12.pdf/p163-180.md:17) | [Eutrophication/mod_zoopl.f90:351](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/Eutrophication/mod_zoopl.f90:351) `                  BAZ(L,NZO,NAL) = max(0., WQV(L,K,19+NAL) - ZOOPL(NZO).CTZ)` | 일치 | 가용 조류 생물량은 MAX(B−CT,0)이다. |
| [Theory](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/manuals/pdfs/EFDC_Theory_Document_Ver_12.pdf) PDF 167쪽 / 인쇄 154쪽, 식 **8.35**<br>`\displaystyle PA_z=UL_z.LPOCA_z+UR_z.RPOCA_z+\sum UB_{xz}.BA_{xz}+UZ_z.ZA`<br>[판독 기록:17](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/_staging/recovery/read/EFDC/manuals/pdfs/EFDC_Theory_Document_Ver_12.pdf/p163-180.md:17) | [Eutrophication/mod_zoopl.f90:360](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/Eutrophication/mod_zoopl.f90:360) `              PRAZ(L,NZO) = UBZT + ZOOPL(NZO).URZ*RPOCAZ(L,NZO) &`<br>[Eutrophication/mod_zoopl.f90:364](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/Eutrophication/mod_zoopl.f90:364) `                PRAZ(L,NZO) = PRAZ(L,NZO) + ZOOPL(NZO).UZPL * ZPLPREY` | 불일치 | 코드는 문서의 먹이 목록에 UDZ·DOCA를 추가한다. 포식자 군에는 동물플랑크톤 먹이도 추가한다. UDZ=0이면 DOC 추가항이 사라진다. |
| [Theory](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/manuals/pdfs/EFDC_Theory_Document_Ver_12.pdf) PDF 167쪽 / 인쇄 154쪽, 식 **8.36**<br>`\displaystyle PA_z=UL_z.LPOCA_z+UR_z.RPOCA_z+\sum UB_{xz}.BA_{xz}`<br>[판독 기록:17](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/_staging/recovery/read/EFDC/manuals/pdfs/EFDC_Theory_Document_Ver_12.pdf/p163-180.md:17) | [Eutrophication/mod_zoopl.f90:360](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/Eutrophication/mod_zoopl.f90:360) `              PRAZ(L,NZO) = UBZT + ZOOPL(NZO).URZ*RPOCAZ(L,NZO) &`<br>[Eutrophication/mod_zoopl.f90:361](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/Eutrophication/mod_zoopl.f90:361) `                          + ZOOPL(NZO).ULZ*LPOCAZ(L,NZO) + ZOOPL(NZO).UDZ*DOCAZ(L,NZO)` | 불일치 | 먹이 군의 가용 먹이에도 DOC 이용항이 있다. 문서 식에는 DOC 항이 없다. |
| [Theory](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/manuals/pdfs/EFDC_Theory_Document_Ver_12.pdf) PDF 167쪽 / 인쇄 154쪽, 식 **8.37**<br>`\displaystyle f(T)=\begin{cases}\exp(-KT_{g1}\cdot(T-T_{opt1})^2),&T\leq T_{opt1}\\1,&T_{opt1}<T<T_{opt2}\\\exp(-KT_{g2}\cdot(T-T_{opt2})^2),&T\geq T_{opt2}\end{cases}`<br>[판독 기록:17](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/_staging/recovery/read/EFDC/manuals/pdfs/EFDC_Theory_Document_Ver_12.pdf/p163-180.md:17) | [Eutrophication/mod_zoopl.f90:244](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/Eutrophication/mod_zoopl.f90:244) `          WQTDGZ(M,NZO) = EXP(-ZOOPL(NZO).KTGZ1 * (WTEMPZ - ZOOPL(NZO).TMZG1) * (WTEMPZ - ZOOPL(NZO).TMZG1))`<br>[Eutrophication/mod_zoopl.f90:247](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/Eutrophication/mod_zoopl.f90:247) `          WQTDGZ(M,NZO) = EXP(-ZOOPL(NZO).KTGZ2 * (WTEMPZ - ZOOPL(NZO).TMZG2) * (WTEMPZ - ZOOPL(NZO).TMZG2))` | 일치 | 최적 온도 구간과 그 양쪽의 이차 지수함수를 사용한다. |
| [Theory](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/manuals/pdfs/EFDC_Theory_Document_Ver_12.pdf) PDF 168쪽 / 인쇄 155쪽, 식 **8.38**<br>`\displaystyle BM_z=BMR_z\cdot\exp(KTB_z\cdot(T-T_{rz}))`<br>[판독 기록:18](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/_staging/recovery/read/EFDC/manuals/pdfs/EFDC_Theory_Document_Ver_12.pdf/p163-180.md:18) | [Eutrophication/mod_zoopl.f90:251](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/Eutrophication/mod_zoopl.f90:251) `        WQTDBZ(M,NZO) = EXP(ZOOPL(NZO).KTBZ * (WTEMPZ - ZOOPL(NZO).TRZB))`<br>[Eutrophication/mod_zoopl.f90:371](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/Eutrophication/mod_zoopl.f90:371) `              ZOOPL(NZO).WQBZ(L) = ZOOPL(NZO).BMRZ(IMWQZT(L))*WQTDBZ(IWQZT(L),NZO)` | 일치 | 기초대사 기준 온도 TRZB의 지수함수를 사용한다. |
| [Theory](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/manuals/pdfs/EFDC_Theory_Document_Ver_12.pdf) PDF 168쪽 / 인쇄 155쪽, 식 **8.39**<br>`\displaystyle D_z=DZERO_z\left(1-\frac{DO_{ref}}{DOCRIT_z}\right)`<br>[판독 기록:18](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/_staging/recovery/read/EFDC/manuals/pdfs/EFDC_Theory_Document_Ver_12.pdf/p163-180.md:18) | [Eutrophication/mod_zoopl.f90:377](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/Eutrophication/mod_zoopl.f90:377) `              DOREF = min(ZOOPL(NZO).DOCRIT, WQV(L,K,IDOX))`<br>[Eutrophication/mod_zoopl.f90:378](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/Eutrophication/mod_zoopl.f90:378) `              ZOOPL(NZO).WQDZ(L) = ZOOPL(NZO).DZEROZ*(1.0 - DOREF/ZOOPL(NZO).DOCRIT)` | 불일치 | 식의 형태는 같다. 문서는 DO≥DOCRIT일 때 DOref=0으로 정의한다. 코드는 DOref=MIN(DOCRIT,DO)로 제한한다. 코드는 이 범위에서 죽음률을 0으로 만든다. 문서의 변수 정의는 같은 쪽의 저산소 죽음 설명과 충돌한다. |
| [Theory](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/manuals/pdfs/EFDC_Theory_Document_Ver_12.pdf) PDF 168쪽 / 인쇄 155쪽, 식 **8.40**<br>`\displaystyle PR_z=PRR_z\cdot\exp(KTP_z\cdot(T-T_{rz}))`<br>[판독 기록:18](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/_staging/recovery/read/EFDC/manuals/pdfs/EFDC_Theory_Document_Ver_12.pdf/p163-180.md:18) | [Eutrophication/mod_zoopl.f90:253](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/Eutrophication/mod_zoopl.f90:253) `        WQTDPZ(M,NZO) = EXP(ZOOPL(NZO).KTPZ * (WTEMPZ - ZOOPL(NZO).TRZP))`<br>[Eutrophication/mod_zoopl.f90:374](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/Eutrophication/mod_zoopl.f90:374) `              ZOOPL(NZO).WQPZ(L) = ZOOPL(NZO).PRRZ(IMWQZT(L))*WQTDPZ(IWQZT(L),NZO)` | 일치 | 포식 기준 온도 TRZP의 지수함수를 사용한다. 문서 Trz를 포식 기준 온도로 대응시킨다. |
| [Theory](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/manuals/pdfs/EFDC_Theory_Document_Ver_12.pdf) PDF 169쪽 / 인쇄 156쪽, 식 **8.41**<br>`\displaystyle \begin{aligned}\frac{\partial RPOC}{\partial t}={}&\sum_{algae}FCRP_x\cdot D_x\cdot B_x+\sum_{zoopl}\left(FCRDZ_z\cdot D_z+FCRPZ_z\cdot PR_z-\frac{UR_z\cdot RPOC}{PA_z}\cdot R_z\right)\cdot Z_z\\&-K_{RPOC}\cdot RPOC+\frac{\partial}{\partial Z}(WS_{RP}\cdot RPOC)+\frac{WRPOC}{V}\end{aligned}`<br>[판독 기록:19](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/_staging/recovery/read/EFDC/manuals/pdfs/EFDC_Theory_Document_Ver_12.pdf/p163-180.md:19) | [Eutrophication/mod_wq.f90:3817](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/Eutrophication/mod_wq.f90:3817) `              WQA4 = WQA4 + ALGAES(NAL).WQFCRP*WQPR(L,NAL)*WQO(L,K,19+NAL)`<br>[Eutrophication/mod_wq.f90:3827](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/Eutrophication/mod_wq.f90:3827) `          WQRR(L) = WQV(L,K,IROC) + DTWQ*WQR4 + DTWQO2*WQA4 + WQB4*WQVO(L,K,IROC)`<br>[Eutrophication/mod_zoopl.f90:441](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/Eutrophication/mod_zoopl.f90:441) `              RPOCZ = (ZOOPL(NZO).FCRDZ*ZOOPL(NZO).WQDZ(L) + ZOOPL(NZO).FCRPZ*ZOOPL(NZO).WQPZ(L) &`<br>[Eutrophication/mod_zoopl.f90:442](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/Eutrophication/mod_zoopl.f90:442) `                           - FRRP(L,NZO)*ZOOPL(NZO).WQGZ(L))*WQV(L,K,NWQVZ+NZO)` | 불일치 | 생성·가수분해·침강·부하는 대응한다. 동물플랑크톤 소비는 RPOC 대신 MAX(RPOC−CT,0)을 사용한다. |
| [Theory](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/manuals/pdfs/EFDC_Theory_Document_Ver_12.pdf) PDF 169쪽 / 인쇄 156쪽, 식 **8.42**<br>`\displaystyle \begin{aligned}\frac{\partial LPOC}{\partial t}={}&\sum_{algae}FCLP_x\cdot D_x\cdot B_x+\sum_{zoopl}\left(FCLDZ_z\cdot D_z+FCLPZ_z\cdot PR_z-\frac{UL_z\cdot LPOC}{PA_z}\cdot R_z\right)\cdot Z_z\\&-K_{LPOC}\cdot LPOC+\frac{\partial}{\partial Z}(WS_{LP}\cdot LPOC)+\frac{WLPOC}{V}\end{aligned}`<br>[판독 기록:19](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/_staging/recovery/read/EFDC/manuals/pdfs/EFDC_Theory_Document_Ver_12.pdf/p163-180.md:19) | [Eutrophication/mod_wq.f90:3868](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/Eutrophication/mod_wq.f90:3868) `              WQA5 = WQA5 + ALGAES(NAL).WQFCLP*WQPR(L,NAL)*WQO(L,K,19+NAL)`<br>[Eutrophication/mod_wq.f90:3877](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/Eutrophication/mod_wq.f90:3877) `          WQRR(L) = WQV(L,K,ILOC) + DTWQ*WQR5 + DTWQO2*WQA5 + WQC5*WQVO(L,K,ILOC)`<br>[Eutrophication/mod_zoopl.f90:447](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/Eutrophication/mod_zoopl.f90:447) `              LPOCZ = (ZOOPL(NZO).FCLDZ*ZOOPL(NZO).WQDZ(L) + ZOOPL(NZO).FCLPZ*ZOOPL(NZO).WQPZ(L) &`<br>[Eutrophication/mod_zoopl.f90:448](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/Eutrophication/mod_zoopl.f90:448) `                           - FRLP(L,NZO)*ZOOPL(NZO).WQGZ(L))*WQV(L,K,NWQVZ+NZO)` | 불일치 | 생성·가수분해·침강·부하는 대응한다. 동물플랑크톤 소비는 LPOC 대신 MAX(LPOC−CT,0)을 사용한다. |
| [Theory](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/manuals/pdfs/EFDC_Theory_Document_Ver_12.pdf) PDF 170쪽 / 인쇄 157쪽, 식 **8.43**<br>`\displaystyle R_z=\frac{PA_z}{KHC_z+PA_z}.RMAX_z.f(T)`<br>[판독 기록:20](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/_staging/recovery/read/EFDC/manuals/pdfs/EFDC_Theory_Document_Ver_12.pdf/p163-180.md:20) | [Eutrophication/mod_zoopl.f90:368](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/Eutrophication/mod_zoopl.f90:368) `              ZOOPL(NZO).WQGZ(L) = ZOOPL(NZO).RMAXZ(IMWQZT(L))*WQTDGZ(IWQZT(L),NZO)*PRAZ(L,NZO)/(PRAZ(L,NZO) + ZOOPL(NZO).KHCZ + 1.E-18)` | 일치 | Rz는 코드의 GROWZ와 같은 먹이·온도 제한 섭식률이다. |
| [Theory](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/manuals/pdfs/EFDC_Theory_Document_Ver_12.pdf) PDF 170쪽 / 인쇄 157쪽, 식 **8.44**<br>`\displaystyle \begin{aligned}\frac{\partial DOC}{\partial t}={}&\sum_{algae}\left[FCD_x+(1-FCD_x)\left(\frac{KHR_x}{KHR_x+DO}\right)\right]\cdot BM_x\cdot B_x+\sum_{algae}FCDP_x\cdot D_x\cdot B_x\\&+\sum_{zoopl}(FCDDZ\cdot D_z+FCDPZ\cdot PR_z)\cdot Z_z+K_{RPOC}\cdot RPOC\\&+K_{LPOC}\cdot LPOC-K_{HR}\cdot DOC-Denit\cdot DOC+\frac{WDOC}{V}\end{aligned}`<br>[판독 기록:20](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/_staging/recovery/read/EFDC/manuals/pdfs/EFDC_Theory_Document_Ver_12.pdf/p163-180.md:20) | [Eutrophication/mod_wq.f90:3921](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/Eutrophication/mod_wq.f90:3921) `              WQA6A(NAL) = ALGAES(NAL).WQFCDB + (1.0 - ALGAES(NAL).WQFCDB)*(ALGAES(NAL).WQKHRA(IZ)/(ALGAES(NAL).WQKHRA(IZ) + O2WQ(L) + 1.E-18))`<br>[Eutrophication/mod_wq.f90:3922](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/Eutrophication/mod_wq.f90:3922) `              WQA6 = WQA6 + ( WQA6A(NAL)*WQBM(L,NAL) + ALGAES(NAL).WQFCDP*WQPR(L,NAL) )*WQO(L,K,19+NAL)`<br>[Eutrophication/mod_wq.f90:3934](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/Eutrophication/mod_wq.f90:3934) `          WQRR(L) = WQV(L,K,IDOC) + DTWQ*WQR6 + WQD6*WQVO(L,K,IDOC) + DTWQO2*(WQA6 + WQKRPC(L)*WQO(L,K,IROC) + WQKLPC(L)*WQO(L,K,ILOC))`<br>[Eutrophication/mod_zoopl.f90:453](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/Eutrophication/mod_zoopl.f90:453) `              DOCZ = (ZOOPL(NZO).FCDDZ*ZOOPL(NZO).WQDZ(L) + ZOOPL(NZO).FCDPZ*ZOOPL(NZO).WQPZ(L))*WQV(L,K,NWQVZ+NZO)` | 일치 | 기초대사 DOC 배설과 비호흡 손실·POC 가수분해·DOC 산화·탈질·외부 부하를 계산한다. 동물플랑크톤 DOC 생성에는 사망·포식만 포함한다. |
| [Theory](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/manuals/pdfs/EFDC_Theory_Document_Ver_12.pdf) PDF 171쪽 / 인쇄 158쪽, 식 **8.45**<br>`\displaystyle \frac{\partial B_x}{\partial t}=-BM_xB_x`<br>[판독 기록:21](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/_staging/recovery/read/EFDC/manuals/pdfs/EFDC_Theory_Document_Ver_12.pdf/p163-180.md:21) | [Eutrophication/mod_wq.f90:3755](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/Eutrophication/mod_wq.f90:3755) `            WQAC = (WQPA(L,NAL) - WQBM(L,NAL) - WQPR(L,NAL) - WQBSETL(L,2,NAL) + WQBSETL(L,3,NAL) )*DTWQO2           ! *** Production per unit time multiplied by half time step` | 일치 | 조류 순손실 계수에 −BM을 넣는다. 이 식은 전체 조류식의 기초대사 성분이다. |
| [Theory](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/manuals/pdfs/EFDC_Theory_Document_Ver_12.pdf) PDF 171쪽 / 인쇄 158쪽, 식 **8.46**<br>`\displaystyle (1-FCD_x)BM_xB_x:\ \text{respiration}`<br>[판독 기록:21](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/_staging/recovery/read/EFDC/manuals/pdfs/EFDC_Theory_Document_Ver_12.pdf/p163-180.md:21) | [Eutrophication/mod_wq.f90:4698](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/Eutrophication/mod_wq.f90:4698) `              WQRes = (1.0 - ALGAES(NAL).WQFCDB)*O2WQ(L)*WQBM(L,NAL)/(ALGAES(NAL).WQKHRA(IZ) + O2WQ(L) + 1.E-18)       ` | 일치 | DO가 충분할 때 호흡 분율은 1−FCD로 수렴한다. 문서도 충분한 DO의 분해식으로 제시한다. |
| [Theory](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/manuals/pdfs/EFDC_Theory_Document_Ver_12.pdf) PDF 171쪽 / 인쇄 158쪽, 식 **8.47**<br>`\displaystyle FCD_xBM_xB_x:\ \text{excretion}`<br>[판독 기록:21](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/_staging/recovery/read/EFDC/manuals/pdfs/EFDC_Theory_Document_Ver_12.pdf/p163-180.md:21) | [Eutrophication/mod_wq.f90:3921](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/Eutrophication/mod_wq.f90:3921) `              WQA6A(NAL) = ALGAES(NAL).WQFCDB + (1.0 - ALGAES(NAL).WQFCDB)*(ALGAES(NAL).WQKHRA(IZ)/(ALGAES(NAL).WQKHRA(IZ) + O2WQ(L) + 1.E-18))` | 일치 | DOC 생성항의 FCD·BM·B 성분이 대응한다. |
| [Theory](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/manuals/pdfs/EFDC_Theory_Document_Ver_12.pdf) PDF 171쪽 / 인쇄 158쪽, 식 **8.48**<br>`\displaystyle (1-FCD_x)\left(\frac{DO}{KHR_x+DO}\right)BM_xB_x:\ \text{respiration}`<br>[판독 기록:21](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/_staging/recovery/read/EFDC/manuals/pdfs/EFDC_Theory_Document_Ver_12.pdf/p163-180.md:21) | [Eutrophication/mod_wq.f90:4698](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/Eutrophication/mod_wq.f90:4698) `              WQRes = (1.0 - ALGAES(NAL).WQFCDB)*O2WQ(L)*WQBM(L,NAL)/(ALGAES(NAL).WQKHRA(IZ) + O2WQ(L) + 1.E-18)       ` | 일치 | 산소 Monod 제한을 곱한 기초대사 호흡 성분을 계산한다. |
| [Theory](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/manuals/pdfs/EFDC_Theory_Document_Ver_12.pdf) PDF 171쪽 / 인쇄 158쪽, 식 **8.49**<br>`\displaystyle (1-FCD_x)\left(\frac{KHR_x}{KHR_x+DO}\right)BM_xB_x:\ \text{excretion}`<br>[판독 기록:21](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/_staging/recovery/read/EFDC/manuals/pdfs/EFDC_Theory_Document_Ver_12.pdf/p163-180.md:21) | [Eutrophication/mod_wq.f90:3921](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/Eutrophication/mod_wq.f90:3921) `              WQA6A(NAL) = ALGAES(NAL).WQFCDB + (1.0 - ALGAES(NAL).WQFCDB)*(ALGAES(NAL).WQKHRA(IZ)/(ALGAES(NAL).WQKHRA(IZ) + O2WQ(L) + 1.E-18))` | 일치 | 1−FCD에 KHR/(KHR+DO)를 곱한 추가 배설 성분을 계산한다. |
| [Theory](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/manuals/pdfs/EFDC_Theory_Document_Ver_12.pdf) PDF 172쪽 / 인쇄 159쪽, 식 **8.50**<br>`\displaystyle \left[FCD_x+(1-FCD_x)\left(\frac{KHR_x}{KHR_x+DO}\right)\right]BM_xB_x`<br>[판독 기록:22](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/_staging/recovery/read/EFDC/manuals/pdfs/EFDC_Theory_Document_Ver_12.pdf/p163-180.md:22) | [Eutrophication/mod_wq.f90:3921](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/Eutrophication/mod_wq.f90:3921) `              WQA6A(NAL) = ALGAES(NAL).WQFCDB + (1.0 - ALGAES(NAL).WQFCDB)*(ALGAES(NAL).WQKHRA(IZ)/(ALGAES(NAL).WQKHRA(IZ) + O2WQ(L) + 1.E-18))`<br>[Eutrophication/mod_wq.f90:3922](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/Eutrophication/mod_wq.f90:3922) `              WQA6 = WQA6 + ( WQA6A(NAL)*WQBM(L,NAL) + ALGAES(NAL).WQFCDP*WQPR(L,NAL) )*WQO(L,K,19+NAL)` | 일치 | 기본 배설과 산소 제한 배설을 합한다. |
| [Theory](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/manuals/pdfs/EFDC_Theory_Document_Ver_12.pdf) PDF 172쪽 / 인쇄 159쪽, 식 **8.51**<br>`\displaystyle \begin{aligned}\frac{\partial DOC}{\partial t}={}&\sum_{algae}FCDP_xPR_xB_x+K_{RPOC}RPOC+K_{LPOC}LPOC\\&-K_{HR}DOC-Denit\ DOC+\frac{WDOC}{V}\end{aligned}`<br>[판독 기록:22](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/_staging/recovery/read/EFDC/manuals/pdfs/EFDC_Theory_Document_Ver_12.pdf/p163-180.md:22) | [Eutrophication/mod_wq.f90:3921](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/Eutrophication/mod_wq.f90:3921) `              WQA6A(NAL) = ALGAES(NAL).WQFCDB + (1.0 - ALGAES(NAL).WQFCDB)*(ALGAES(NAL).WQKHRA(IZ)/(ALGAES(NAL).WQKHRA(IZ) + O2WQ(L) + 1.E-18))`<br>[Eutrophication/mod_wq.f90:3934](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/Eutrophication/mod_wq.f90:3934) `          WQRR(L) = WQV(L,K,IDOC) + DTWQ*WQR6 + WQD6*WQVO(L,K,IDOC) + DTWQO2*(WQA6 + WQKRPC(L)*WQO(L,K,IROC) + WQKLPC(L)*WQO(L,K,ILOC))` | 일치 | 문서가 전제한 FCD=0·KHR=0·동물플랑크톤 비활성 조건에서 기초대사 DOC 항이 사라진다. 이 판정은 일반 입력에 대한 식과의 일치를 뜻하지 않는다. |
| [Theory](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/manuals/pdfs/EFDC_Theory_Document_Ver_12.pdf) PDF 173쪽 / 인쇄 160쪽, 식 **8.52**<br>`\displaystyle K_{HR}=\left(\frac{DO}{KHOR_{DO}+DO}\right)K_{DOC}`<br>[판독 기록:23](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/_staging/recovery/read/EFDC/manuals/pdfs/EFDC_Theory_Document_Ver_12.pdf/p163-180.md:23) | [Eutrophication/mod_wq.f90:3511](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/Eutrophication/mod_wq.f90:3511) `        WQTT1 = WQKDOC / (WQKHORDO + O2WQ(L) + 1.E-18)  `<br>[Eutrophication/mod_wq.f90:3512](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/Eutrophication/mod_wq.f90:3512) `        WQKHR(L)   = WQTT1*O2WQ(L)  ` | 일치 | DOC 호흡 계수에 DO/(KHORDO+DO)를 곱한다. |
| [Theory](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/manuals/pdfs/EFDC_Theory_Document_Ver_12.pdf) PDF 173쪽 / 인쇄 160쪽, 식 **8.53**<br>`\displaystyle K_{RPOC}=\left(K_{RC}+K_{RCalg}\sum_{algae}B_x\right)\exp(KT_{HDR}(T-TR_{HDR}))`<br>[판독 기록:23](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/_staging/recovery/read/EFDC/manuals/pdfs/EFDC_Theory_Document_Ver_12.pdf/p163-180.md:23) | [Eutrophication/mod_wq.f90:3494](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/Eutrophication/mod_wq.f90:3494) `            WQOBTOT(L) = WQOBTOT(L) + WQV(L,K,19+NAL)           `<br>[Eutrophication/mod_wq.f90:3500](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/Eutrophication/mod_wq.f90:3500) `        WQKRPC(L) = (WQKRC + WQKRCALG*WQOBTOT(L)) * WQTDHDR(IWQT(L))        ! *** Hydrolysis:     RPOP--> DOC` | 일치 | 이동성 조류 생물량 합에 의한 가수분해 촉진과 온도 함수를 곱한다. |
| [Theory](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/manuals/pdfs/EFDC_Theory_Document_Ver_12.pdf) PDF 173쪽 / 인쇄 160쪽, 식 **8.54**<br>`\displaystyle K_{LPOC}=\left(K_{LC}+K_{LCalg}\sum_{algae}B_x\right)\exp(KT_{HDR}(T-TR_{HDR}))`<br>[판독 기록:23](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/_staging/recovery/read/EFDC/manuals/pdfs/EFDC_Theory_Document_Ver_12.pdf/p163-180.md:23) | [Eutrophication/mod_wq.f90:3494](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/Eutrophication/mod_wq.f90:3494) `            WQOBTOT(L) = WQOBTOT(L) + WQV(L,K,19+NAL)           `<br>[Eutrophication/mod_wq.f90:3501](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/Eutrophication/mod_wq.f90:3501) `        WQKLPC(L) = (WQKLC + WQKLCALG*WQOBTOT(L)) * WQTDHDR(IWQT(L))        ! *** Hydrolysis:     RPOP--> DOC` | 일치 | LPOC 가수분해에 동일한 형태를 사용한다. |
| [Theory](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/manuals/pdfs/EFDC_Theory_Document_Ver_12.pdf) PDF 173쪽 / 인쇄 160쪽, 식 **8.55**<br>`\displaystyle K_{DOC}=\left(K_{DC}+K_{DCalg}\sum_{algae}B_x\right)\exp(KT_{MIN}(T-TR_{MIN}))`<br>[판독 기록:23](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/_staging/recovery/read/EFDC/manuals/pdfs/EFDC_Theory_Document_Ver_12.pdf/p163-180.md:23) | [Eutrophication/mod_wq.f90:3496](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/Eutrophication/mod_wq.f90:3496) `            XMRM = XMRM + ALGAES(NAL).WQKDCALM(IZ) * WQV(L,K,19+NAL)  `<br>[Eutrophication/mod_wq.f90:3508](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/Eutrophication/mod_wq.f90:3508) `        WQKDOC = (WQKDC(IZ) + WQKDCALG*WQOBTOT(L) + XMRM)*WQTDMNL(IWQT(L))     ! *** Heterotrophic respiration rate of DOC at infinite DO (1/day)` | 불일치 | 코드는 이동성 조류 합 외에 비이동성 조류의 XMRM 촉진항도 DOC 무기화에 추가한다. 비이동성 조류가 없으면 문서 형태와 같다. |
| [Theory](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/manuals/pdfs/EFDC_Theory_Document_Ver_12.pdf) PDF 174쪽 / 인쇄 161쪽, 식 **8.56**<br>`\displaystyle 4NO_3^-+4H^++5CH_2O\rightarrow2N_2+7H_2O+5CO_2`<br>[판독 기록:24](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/_staging/recovery/read/EFDC/manuals/pdfs/EFDC_Theory_Document_Ver_12.pdf/p163-180.md:24) | 관련 원문 탐색 근거:<br>[Eutrophication/mod_wq.f90:3513](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/Eutrophication/mod_wq.f90:3513) `        WQDENIT(L) = WQTT1*WQAANOX*RNO3WQ(L)/(WQKHDNN + RNO3WQ(L) + 1.E-18)    ! *** Denitrification Rate  `<br>[Eutrophication/mod_wq.f90:4434](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/Eutrophication/mod_wq.f90:4434) `          WQB15 = WQV(L,K,INOX) + DTWQ*WQRR(L) + DTWQO2*( WQA15 - WQANDC*WQDENIT(L)*WQO(L,K,IDOC) + WQNIT(L)*WQO(L,K,INHX))` | 문서에만 | 화학양론 반응식은 설명식이다. 코드는 Denit·DOC와 입력 ANDC 질소/탄소 비율을 사용한다. H⁺·N2·H2O를 독립 상태변수로 풀지 않는다. |
| [Theory](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/manuals/pdfs/EFDC_Theory_Document_Ver_12.pdf) PDF 174쪽 / 인쇄 161쪽, 식 **8.57**<br>`\displaystyle Denit=\left(\frac{KHOR_{DO}}{KHOR_{DO}+DO}\right)\left(\frac{NO3}{KHDN_N+NO3}\right)AANOX\cdot K_{DOC}`<br>[판독 기록:24](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/_staging/recovery/read/EFDC/manuals/pdfs/EFDC_Theory_Document_Ver_12.pdf/p163-180.md:24) | [Eutrophication/mod_wq.f90:955](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/Eutrophication/mod_wq.f90:955) `    WQAANOX = WQAANOX*WQKHORDO`<br>[Eutrophication/mod_wq.f90:3511](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/Eutrophication/mod_wq.f90:3511) `        WQTT1 = WQKDOC / (WQKHORDO + O2WQ(L) + 1.E-18)  `<br>[Eutrophication/mod_wq.f90:3513](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/Eutrophication/mod_wq.f90:3513) `        WQDENIT(L) = WQTT1*WQAANOX*RNO3WQ(L)/(WQKHDNN + RNO3WQ(L) + 1.E-18)    ! *** Denitrification Rate  ` | 일치 | 입력 AANOX에 KHORDO를 미리 곱한다. 이후 WQTT1과 곱하면 문서의 DO 억제 분율이 복원된다. |
| [Theory](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/manuals/pdfs/EFDC_Theory_Document_Ver_12.pdf) PDF 175쪽 / 인쇄 162쪽, 식 **8.58**<br>`\displaystyle \begin{aligned}\frac{\partial RPOP}{\partial t}={}&\sum_{algae}(FPR_x\cdot BM_x+FPRP_x\cdot D_x)\cdot APC\cdot B_x+\sum_{zoopl}FPRDZ_z\cdot D_z\cdot Z_z\cdot APC_z\\&+\sum_{zoopl}\left(FPRPZ_z\cdot PR_z-\frac{UR_z\cdot RPOP}{PA_z}\cdot R_z\right)\cdot Z_z\cdot APC_z\\&-K_{RPOP}\cdot RPOP+\frac{\partial}{\partial Z}(WS_{RP}\cdot RPOP)+\frac{WRPOP}{V}\end{aligned}`<br>[판독 기록:25](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/_staging/recovery/read/EFDC/manuals/pdfs/EFDC_Theory_Document_Ver_12.pdf/p163-180.md:25) | [Eutrophication/mod_wq.f90:3968](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/Eutrophication/mod_wq.f90:3968) `              WQA7A(NAL) = (ALGAES(NAL).WQFPRB*WQBM(L,NAL) + ALGAES(NAL).WQFPRP*WQPR(L,NAL)) * WQO(L,K,19+NAL)`<br>[Eutrophication/mod_wq.f90:3980](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/Eutrophication/mod_wq.f90:3980) `          WQRR(L) = WQV(L,K,IROP) + DTWQ*WQR7 + DTWQO2*WQA7 + WQE7*WQVO(L,K,IROP) `<br>[Eutrophication/mod_zoopl.f90:428](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/Eutrophication/mod_zoopl.f90:428) `            FRLP(L,NZO)  = ZOOPL(NZO).ULZ*LPOCAZ(L,NZO)/(PRAZ(L,NZO) + 1.E-18)`<br>[Eutrophication/mod_zoopl.f90:470](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/Eutrophication/mod_zoopl.f90:470) `                           - FRRP(L,NZO)*ZOOPL(NZO).WQGZ(L))*WQV(L,K,NWQVZ+NZO)*ZOOPL(NZO).APCZ` | 불일치 | 코드는 섭식 탄소 분율 FRRP에 동물플랑크톤 APC를 곱한다. 문서의 UR·RPOP/PA 분자와 다르다. |
| [Theory](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/manuals/pdfs/EFDC_Theory_Document_Ver_12.pdf) PDF 175쪽 / 인쇄 162쪽, 식 **8.59**<br>`\displaystyle \begin{aligned}\frac{\partial LPOP}{\partial t}={}&\sum_{algae}(FPL_x\cdot BM_x+FPLP_x\cdot D_x)\cdot APC\cdot B_x+\sum_{zoopl}FPLDZ_z\cdot D_z\cdot Z_z\cdot APC_z\\&+\sum_{zoopl}\left(FPLPZ_z\cdot PR_z-\frac{UL_z\cdot LPOP}{PA_z}\cdot R_z\right)\cdot Z_z\cdot APC_z\\&-K_{LPOP}\cdot LPOP+\frac{\partial}{\partial Z}(WS_{LP}\cdot LPOP)+\frac{WLPOP}{V}\end{aligned}`<br>[판독 기록:25](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/_staging/recovery/read/EFDC/manuals/pdfs/EFDC_Theory_Document_Ver_12.pdf/p163-180.md:25) | [Eutrophication/mod_wq.f90:4021](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/Eutrophication/mod_wq.f90:4021) `              WQA8A(NAL) = (ALGAES(NAL).WQFPLB*WQBM(L,NAL) + ALGAES(NAL).WQFPLP*WQPR(L,NAL)) * WQO(L,K,19+NAL)`<br>[Eutrophication/mod_wq.f90:4033](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/Eutrophication/mod_wq.f90:4033) `          WQRR(L) = WQV(L,K,ILOP) + DTWQ*WQR8 + DTWQO2*WQA8 + WQF8*WQVO(L,K,ILOP)  `<br>[Eutrophication/mod_zoopl.f90:428](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/Eutrophication/mod_zoopl.f90:428) `            FRLP(L,NZO)  = ZOOPL(NZO).ULZ*LPOCAZ(L,NZO)/(PRAZ(L,NZO) + 1.E-18)`<br>[Eutrophication/mod_zoopl.f90:476](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/Eutrophication/mod_zoopl.f90:476) `                           - FRLP(L,NZO)*ZOOPL(NZO).WQGZ(L))*WQV(L,K,NWQVZ+NZO)*ZOOPL(NZO).APCZ` | 불일치 | 코드는 섭식 탄소 분율 FRLP에 동물플랑크톤 APC를 곱한다. 문서의 UL·LPOP/PA 분자와 다르다. |
| [Theory](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/manuals/pdfs/EFDC_Theory_Document_Ver_12.pdf) PDF 176쪽 / 인쇄 163쪽, 식 **8.60**<br>`\displaystyle \begin{aligned}\frac{\partial DOP}{\partial t}={}&\sum_{algae}(FPD_x\ BM_x+FPDP_x\ D_x)APC\ B_x+\sum_{zoopl}FPDBZ_z\cdot BM_z\cdot Z_z\cdot APC_z\\&+\sum_{zoopl}(FPDDZ\cdot D_z+FPDPZ\cdot PR_z)\cdot Z_z\cdot APC_z\\&+K_{RPOP}\cdot RPOP+K_{LPOP}\cdot LPOP-K_{DOP}\cdot DOP+\frac{WDOP}{V}\end{aligned}`<br>[판독 기록:26](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/_staging/recovery/read/EFDC/manuals/pdfs/EFDC_Theory_Document_Ver_12.pdf/p163-180.md:26) | [Eutrophication/mod_wq.f90:4073](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/Eutrophication/mod_wq.f90:4073) `              WQA9A(NAL) = (ALGAES(NAL).WQFPDB*WQBM(L,NAL) + ALGAES(NAL).WQFPDP*WQPR(L,NAL)) * WQO(L,K,19+NAL)`<br>[Eutrophication/mod_wq.f90:4085](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/Eutrophication/mod_wq.f90:4085) `          WQRR(L) = WQV(L,K,IDOP) + DTWQ*WQR9 + WQF9*WQVO(L,K,IDOP) + DTWQO2*(WQA9 + WQKRPP(L)*WQO(L,K,IROP) + WQKLPP(L)*WQO(L,K,ILOP))`<br>[Eutrophication/mod_zoopl.f90:482](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/Eutrophication/mod_zoopl.f90:482) `                           + ZOOPL(NZO).FPDBZ*ZOOPL(NZO).WQBZ(L))*WQV(L,K,NWQVZ+NZO)*ZOOPL(NZO).APCZ` | 일치 | 조류·동물플랑크톤의 DOP 생성과 입자성 인 가수분해·DOP 무기화·부하를 계산한다. |
| [Theory](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/manuals/pdfs/EFDC_Theory_Document_Ver_12.pdf) PDF 177쪽 / 인쇄 164쪽, 식 **8.61**<br>`\displaystyle \begin{aligned}\frac{\partial}{\partial t}(PO4p+PO4d)={}&\sum_{algae}(FPI_x\ BM_x+FPIP_x\ D_x-P_x)APC\cdot B_x+K_{DOP}\cdot DOP\\&+\sum_{zoopl}(FPIBZ_z\cdot BM_z+FPIDZ_z\cdot D_z+FPIPZ_z\cdot PR_z)Z_z\cdot APC_z\\&+\frac{\partial}{\partial Z}(WS_{TSS}\cdot PO4p)+\frac{BFPO4d}{\Delta Z}+\frac{WPO4p}{V}+\frac{WPO4d}{V}\end{aligned}`<br>[판독 기록:27](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/_staging/recovery/read/EFDC/manuals/pdfs/EFDC_Theory_Document_Ver_12.pdf/p163-180.md:27) | [Eutrophication/mod_wq.f90:4117](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/Eutrophication/mod_wq.f90:4117) `              WQA10A(NAL) = (ALGAES(NAL).WQFPIB*WQBM(L,NAL) + ALGAES(NAL).WQFPIP*WQPR(L,NAL) - WQPA(L,NAL))*WQO(L,K,19+NAL)`<br>[Eutrophication/mod_wq.f90:4137](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/Eutrophication/mod_wq.f90:4137) `            WQRR(L) = WQRR(L) + WQBFPO4D(L)*HPKI(L,K) ! *** Add in Benthic Flux`<br>[Eutrophication/mod_wq.f90:4152](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/Eutrophication/mod_wq.f90:4152) `          WQRR(L) = WQVO(L,K,IP4D) + DTWQ*WQRR(L) + WQH10(L)*WQVO(L,K,IP4D) + DTWQO2*(WQKK(L) + WQKDOP(L)*WQO(L,K,IDOP))`<br>[Eutrophication/mod_zoopl.f90:488](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/Eutrophication/mod_zoopl.f90:488) `                           + ZOOPL(NZO).FPIBZ*ZOOPL(NZO).WQBZ(L))*WQV(L,K,NWQVZ+NZO)*ZOOPL(NZO).APCZ` | 일치 | 대사·손실·성장에 따른 인 교환과 DOP 무기화·동물플랑크톤·흡착 인 침강·저면 플럭스·부하를 계산한다. |
| [Theory](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/manuals/pdfs/EFDC_Theory_Document_Ver_12.pdf) PDF 178쪽 / 인쇄 165쪽, 식 **8.62**<br>`\displaystyle \begin{aligned}PO4p&=\left(\frac{K_{PO4p}SORPS}{1+K_{PO4p}SORPS}\right)(PO4p+PO4d)\\PO4d&=\left(\frac{1}{1+K_{PO4p}SORPS}\right)(PO4p+PO4d)\\SORPS&=SED\ \text{or}\ TAM_P\end{aligned}`<br>[판독 기록:28](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/_staging/recovery/read/EFDC/manuals/pdfs/EFDC_Theory_Document_Ver_12.pdf/p163-180.md:28) | [Eutrophication/mod_wq.f90:4911](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/Eutrophication/mod_wq.f90:4911) `          WQPO4D(L,K) = WQV(L,K,IP4D) / (1.0 + WQKPO4P*WQTAMP(L,K))  `<br>[Eutrophication/mod_wq.f90:4920](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/Eutrophication/mod_wq.f90:4920) `          WQPO4D(L,K) = WQV(L,K,IP4D) / (1.0 + WQKPO4P*SEDT(L,K))  ` | 일치 | TAMp 또는 SED에 대한 평형 분배로 용존 인을 계산한다. 입자 인은 전체와 용존의 차이이다. |
| [Theory](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/manuals/pdfs/EFDC_Theory_Document_Ver_12.pdf) PDF 178쪽 / 인쇄 165쪽, 식 **8.63**<br>`\displaystyle K_{PO4_p}=\frac{PO4_p}{PO4_d}\frac{1}{SED}`<br>[판독 기록:28](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/_staging/recovery/read/EFDC/manuals/pdfs/EFDC_Theory_Document_Ver_12.pdf/p163-180.md:28) | 관련 원문 탐색 근거:<br>[Eutrophication/mod_wq.f90:4920](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/Eutrophication/mod_wq.f90:4920) `          WQPO4D(L,K) = WQV(L,K,IP4D) / (1.0 + WQKPO4P*SEDT(L,K))  ` | 문서에만 | KPO4p는 입력 분배계수이다. 코드는 PO4p·PO4d·SED에서 K를 역산하지 않는다. |
| [Theory](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/manuals/pdfs/EFDC_Theory_Document_Ver_12.pdf) PDF 178쪽 / 인쇄 165쪽, 식 **8.64**<br>`\displaystyle K_{PO4_p}=\frac{PO4_p}{PO4_d}\frac{1}{TAM_p}`<br>[판독 기록:28](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/_staging/recovery/read/EFDC/manuals/pdfs/EFDC_Theory_Document_Ver_12.pdf/p163-180.md:28) | 관련 원문 탐색 근거:<br>[Eutrophication/mod_wq.f90:4911](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/Eutrophication/mod_wq.f90:4911) `          WQPO4D(L,K) = WQV(L,K,IP4D) / (1.0 + WQKPO4P*WQTAMP(L,K))  ` | 문서에만 | KPO4p는 입력 분배계수이다. 코드는 PO4p·PO4d·TAMp에서 K를 역산하지 않는다. |
| [Theory](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/manuals/pdfs/EFDC_Theory_Document_Ver_12.pdf) PDF 179쪽 / 인쇄 166쪽, 식 **8.65**<br>`\displaystyle APC=(CP1_{prm}+CP2_{prm}\exp(-CP3_{prm}PO4d))^{-1}`<br>[판독 기록:29](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/_staging/recovery/read/EFDC/manuals/pdfs/EFDC_Theory_Document_Ver_12.pdf/p163-180.md:29) | [Eutrophication/mod_wq.f90:3532](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/Eutrophication/mod_wq.f90:3532) `        WQAPC(L) = 1.0/(WQCP1PRM + WQCP2PRM*EXP(-WQCP3PRM*PO4DWQ(L)))   ! *** A mean Phosphorus-to-Carbon ration for all algal groups` | 일치 | 용존 인의 지수함수로 조류 P/C 비율을 계산한다. |
| [Theory](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/manuals/pdfs/EFDC_Theory_Document_Ver_12.pdf) PDF 179쪽 / 인쇄 166쪽, 식 **8.66**<br>`\displaystyle K_{RPOP}=\left(K_{RP}+\left(\frac{KHP}{KHP+PO4d}\right)K_{RPalg}\sum_{algae}B_x\right)\exp(KT_{HDR}(T-TR_{HDR}))`<br>[판독 기록:29](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/_staging/recovery/read/EFDC/manuals/pdfs/EFDC_Theory_Document_Ver_12.pdf/p163-180.md:29) | [Eutrophication/mod_wq.f90:3533](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/Eutrophication/mod_wq.f90:3533) `        WQTT1 = WQKHP/(WQKHP + PO4DWQ(L) + 1.E-18) * WQOBTOT(L)  `<br>[Eutrophication/mod_wq.f90:3534](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/Eutrophication/mod_wq.f90:3534) `        WQKRPP(L) = (WQKRP + WQKRPALG*WQTT1) * WQTDHDR(IWQT(L))         ! *** Hydrolysis:     RPOP--> DOP` | 일치 | 평균 KHP에 의한 인 제한·조류 생물량 촉진·온도 함수를 RPOP 가수분해에 적용한다. |
| [Theory](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/manuals/pdfs/EFDC_Theory_Document_Ver_12.pdf) PDF 180쪽 / 인쇄 167쪽, 식 **8.67**<br>`\displaystyle K_{LPOP}=\left(K_{LP}+\left(\frac{KHP}{KHP+PO4d}\right)K_{LPalg}\sum_{alage}B_x\right)\exp(KT_{HDR}(T-TR_{HDR}))`<br>[판독 기록:30](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/_staging/recovery/read/EFDC/manuals/pdfs/EFDC_Theory_Document_Ver_12.pdf/p163-180.md:30) | [Eutrophication/mod_wq.f90:3533](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/Eutrophication/mod_wq.f90:3533) `        WQTT1 = WQKHP/(WQKHP + PO4DWQ(L) + 1.E-18) * WQOBTOT(L)  `<br>[Eutrophication/mod_wq.f90:3535](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/Eutrophication/mod_wq.f90:3535) `        WQKLPP(L) = (WQKLP + WQKLPALG*WQTT1) * WQTDHDR(IWQT(L))         ! *** Hydrolysis:     LPOP--> DOP` | 일치 | 동일한 인 제한 형태를 LPOP 가수분해에 적용한다. |
| [Theory](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/manuals/pdfs/EFDC_Theory_Document_Ver_12.pdf) PDF 180쪽 / 인쇄 167쪽, 식 **8.68**<br>`\displaystyle K_{DOP}=\left(K_{DP}+\left(\frac{KHP}{KHP+PO4d}\right)K_{DPalg}\sum_{algae}B_x\right)\exp(KT_{MIN}(T-TR_{MIN}))`<br>[판독 기록:30](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/_staging/recovery/read/EFDC/manuals/pdfs/EFDC_Theory_Document_Ver_12.pdf/p163-180.md:30) | [Eutrophication/mod_wq.f90:3533](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/Eutrophication/mod_wq.f90:3533) `        WQTT1 = WQKHP/(WQKHP + PO4DWQ(L) + 1.E-18) * WQOBTOT(L)  `<br>[Eutrophication/mod_wq.f90:3536](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/Eutrophication/mod_wq.f90:3536) `        WQKDOP(L) = (WQKDP + WQKDPALG*WQTT1) * WQTDMNL(IWQT(L))         ! *** Mineralization: DOP --> PO4` | 일치 | 동일한 인 제한 형태를 DOP 무기화에 적용한다. |
| [Theory](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/manuals/pdfs/EFDC_Theory_Document_Ver_12.pdf) PDF 180쪽 / 인쇄 167쪽, 식 **8.69**<br>`\displaystyle KHP=\frac{\sum_{algae}KHP_x}{number\ algae}`<br>[판독 기록:30](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/_staging/recovery/read/EFDC/manuals/pdfs/EFDC_Theory_Document_Ver_12.pdf/p163-180.md:30) | [Eutrophication/mod_wq.f90:3524](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/Eutrophication/mod_wq.f90:3524) `          WQKHP = WQKHP + ALGAES(NAL).WQKHPA                            ! *** Mean phosphorus half-saturation for algae`<br>[Eutrophication/mod_wq.f90:3528](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/Eutrophication/mod_wq.f90:3528) `      if( ALGCOUNT > 0. ) WQKHP = WQKHP/ALGCOUNT   ! *** Note - better if mass weighted` | 일치 | 이동성 조류군의 KHP 합을 그 군 수로 나눈다. |
| [Theory](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/manuals/pdfs/EFDC_Theory_Document_Ver_12.pdf) PDF 181쪽 / 인쇄 168쪽, 식 **8.70**<br>`\displaystyle \frac{\partial RPON}{\partial t}=\sum_{algae}(FNR_x\cdot BM_x+FNRP_x\cdot D_x)\cdot ANC_x\cdot B_x+\sum_{zoopl}FNRDZ_z\cdot D_z\cdot Z_z\cdot ANC_z+\sum_{zoopl}\left(FNRPZ_z\cdot PR_z-\frac{UR_z\cdot RPON}{PA_z}\cdot R_z\right)\cdot Z_z\cdot ANC_z-K_{RPON}RPON+\frac{\partial}{\partial Z}(WS_{RP}\cdot RPON)+\frac{WRPON}{V}`<br>[판독 기록:13](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/_staging/recovery/read/EFDC/manuals/pdfs/EFDC_Theory_Document_Ver_12.pdf/p181-198.md:13) | [Eutrophication/mod_wq.f90:4184](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/Eutrophication/mod_wq.f90:4184) `              WQA11A(NAL) = (ALGAES(NAL).WQFNRB*WQBM(L,NAL) + ALGAES(NAL).WQFNRP*WQPR(L,NAL))*ALGAES(NAL).WQANCA*WQO(L,K,19+NAL)  `<br>[Eutrophication/mod_zoopl.f90:429](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/Eutrophication/mod_zoopl.f90:429) `            FRRP(L,NZO)  = ZOOPL(NZO).URZ*RPOCAZ(L,NZO)/(PRAZ(L,NZO) + 1.E-18)`<br>[Eutrophication/mod_zoopl.f90:504](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/Eutrophication/mod_zoopl.f90:504) `                           - FRRP(L,NZO)*ZOOPL(NZO).WQGZ(L))*WQV(L,K,NWQVZ+NZO)*ZOOPL(NZO).ANCZ` | 불일치 | 조류 생성·가수분해·침강은 대응한다. 동물플랑크톤 섭식 손실은 RPON 대신 가용 RPOC 분율과 ANCz를 사용한다. |
| [Theory](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/manuals/pdfs/EFDC_Theory_Document_Ver_12.pdf) PDF 181쪽 / 인쇄 168쪽, 식 **8.71**<br>`\displaystyle \frac{\partial LPON}{\partial t}=\sum_{algae}(FNL_x\cdot BM_x+FNLP_x\cdot D_x)\cdot ANC_x\cdot B_x+\sum_{zoopl}FNLDZ_z\cdot D_z\cdot Z_z\cdot ANC_z+\sum_{zoopl}\left(FNLPZ_z\cdot PR_z-\frac{UL_z\cdot LPON}{PA_z}\cdot R_z\right)\cdot Z_z\cdot ANC_z-K_{LPON}LPON+\frac{\partial}{\partial Z}(WS_{LP}\cdot LPON)+\frac{WLPON}{V}`<br>[판독 기록:13](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/_staging/recovery/read/EFDC/manuals/pdfs/EFDC_Theory_Document_Ver_12.pdf/p181-198.md:13) | [Eutrophication/mod_wq.f90:4237](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/Eutrophication/mod_wq.f90:4237) `              WQA12A(NAL) = (ALGAES(NAL).WQFNLB*WQBM(L,NAL) + ALGAES(NAL).WQFNLP*WQPR(L,NAL))*ALGAES(NAL).WQANCA*WQO(L,K,19+NAL)  `<br>[Eutrophication/mod_zoopl.f90:428](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/Eutrophication/mod_zoopl.f90:428) `            FRLP(L,NZO)  = ZOOPL(NZO).ULZ*LPOCAZ(L,NZO)/(PRAZ(L,NZO) + 1.E-18)`<br>[Eutrophication/mod_zoopl.f90:510](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/Eutrophication/mod_zoopl.f90:510) `                           - FRLP(L,NZO)*ZOOPL(NZO).WQGZ(L))*WQV(L,K,NWQVZ+NZO)*ZOOPL(NZO).ANCZ` | 불일치 | 조류 생성·가수분해·침강은 대응한다. 동물플랑크톤 섭식 손실은 LPON 대신 가용 LPOC 분율과 ANCz를 사용한다. |
| [Theory](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/manuals/pdfs/EFDC_Theory_Document_Ver_12.pdf) PDF 182쪽 / 인쇄 169쪽, 식 **8.72**<br>`\displaystyle \frac{\partial DON}{\partial t}=\sum_{algae}(FND_x\,BM_x+FNDP_x\,D_x)ANC_x\,B_x+\sum_{zoopl}FNDBZ_z\cdot BM_z\cdot Z_z\cdot ANC_z+\sum_{zoopl}(FNDDZ\cdot D_z+FNDPZ\cdot PR_z)\cdot Z_z\cdot ANC_z+K_{RPON}\cdot RPON+K_{LPON}\cdot LPON-K_{DON}\cdot DON+\frac{WDON}{V}`<br>[판독 기록:14](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/_staging/recovery/read/EFDC/manuals/pdfs/EFDC_Theory_Document_Ver_12.pdf/p181-198.md:14) | [Eutrophication/mod_wq.f90:4289](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/Eutrophication/mod_wq.f90:4289) `              WQA13A(NAL) = (ALGAES(NAL).WQFNDB*WQBM(L,NAL) + ALGAES(NAL).WQFNDP*WQPR(L,NAL))*ALGAES(NAL).WQANCA*WQO(L,K,19+NAL)  `<br>[Eutrophication/mod_wq.f90:4301](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/Eutrophication/mod_wq.f90:4301) `          WQRR(L) = WQVO(L,K,IDON) + DTWQ*WQR13 + WQF13*WQVO(L,K,IDON) + DTWQO2*(WQA13 + WQKRPN(L)*WQO(L,K,IRON)  + WQKLPN(L)*WQO(L,K,ILON))`<br>[Eutrophication/mod_zoopl.f90:516](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/Eutrophication/mod_zoopl.f90:516) `                           + ZOOPL(NZO).FNDBZ*ZOOPL(NZO).WQBZ(L))*WQV(L,K,NWQVZ+NZO)*ZOOPL(NZO).ANCZ` | 일치 | 조류·동물플랑크톤의 DON 생성과 입자성 N 가수분해·DON 무기화·부하를 계산한다. |
| [Theory](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/manuals/pdfs/EFDC_Theory_Document_Ver_12.pdf) PDF 182쪽 / 인쇄 169쪽, 식 **8.73**<br>`\displaystyle \frac{\partial NH4}{\partial t}=\sum_{algae}(FNI_x\,BM_x+FNIP_x\,D_x-PN_xP_x)ANC_x\cdot B_x+K_{DON}\cdot DON+\sum_{zoopl}(FNIBZ_z\cdot BM_z+FNIDZ_z\cdot D_z+FNIPZ_z\cdot PR_z)Z_z\cdot ANC_z-KNit\,NH4+\frac{BFNH4}{\Delta Z}+\frac{WNH4}{V}`<br>[판독 기록:14](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/_staging/recovery/read/EFDC/manuals/pdfs/EFDC_Theory_Document_Ver_12.pdf/p181-198.md:14) | [Eutrophication/mod_wq.f90:4357](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/Eutrophication/mod_wq.f90:4357) `          WQF14 = - DTWQO2*WQNIT(L)  `<br>[Eutrophication/mod_wq.f90:4365](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/Eutrophication/mod_wq.f90:4365) `              WQA14A(NAL) = ALGAES(NAL).WQFNIB*WQBM(L,NAL) + ALGAES(NAL).WQFNIP*WQPR(L,NAL) - WQPN(L,NAL)*WQPA(L,NAL)  `<br>[Eutrophication/mod_wq.f90:4375](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/Eutrophication/mod_wq.f90:4375) `          WQRR(L) = WQV(L,K,INHX) + DTWQ*WQRR(L) + WQF14*WQVO(L,K,INHX) + DTWQO2*(WQA14 + WQKDON(L)*WQO(L,K,IDON))`<br>[Eutrophication/mod_zoopl.f90:522](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/Eutrophication/mod_zoopl.f90:522) `                           + ZOOPL(NZO).FNIBZ*ZOOPL(NZO).WQBZ(L))*WQV(L,K,NWQVZ+NZO)*ZOOPL(NZO).ANCZ` | 일치 | NH4 선호 성장·대사·손실·DON 무기화·질산화·저면 플럭스·부하의 구조가 대응한다. 질산화 계수 차이는 식 8.81·8.83에서 판정한다. |
| [Theory](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/manuals/pdfs/EFDC_Theory_Document_Ver_12.pdf) PDF 183쪽 / 인쇄 170쪽, 식 **8.74**<br>`\displaystyle \frac{\partial NO3}{\partial t}=-\sum_{algae}(1-PN_X)P_X\,ANC_X\,B_X+KNit\,NH4-ANDC\,Denit\,DOC+\frac{BFNO3}{\Delta Z}+\frac{WNO3}{V}`<br>[판독 기록:15](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/_staging/recovery/read/EFDC/manuals/pdfs/EFDC_Theory_Document_Ver_12.pdf/p181-198.md:15) | [Eutrophication/mod_wq.f90:4424](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/Eutrophication/mod_wq.f90:4424) `              WQA15A(NAL) = (WQPN(L,NAL) - 1.0)*WQPA(L,NAL) * ALGAES(NAL).WQANCA * WQO(L,K,19+NAL)  `<br>[Eutrophication/mod_wq.f90:4434](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/Eutrophication/mod_wq.f90:4434) `          WQB15 = WQV(L,K,INOX) + DTWQ*WQRR(L) + DTWQO2*( WQA15 - WQANDC*WQDENIT(L)*WQO(L,K,IDOC) + WQNIT(L)*WQO(L,K,INHX))` | 일치 | NO3 성장 소비·NH4 질산화 생성·탈질 소비·저면 플럭스·외부 부하의 구조가 대응한다. |
| [Theory](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/manuals/pdfs/EFDC_Theory_Document_Ver_12.pdf) PDF 184쪽 / 인쇄 171쪽, 식 **8.75**<br>`\displaystyle PN_x=NH4\frac{NO3}{(KHN_x+NH4)(KHN_x+NO3)}+NH4\frac{KHN_x}{(NH4+NO3)(KHN_x+NO3)}`<br>[판독 기록:16](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/_staging/recovery/read/EFDC/manuals/pdfs/EFDC_Theory_Document_Ver_12.pdf/p181-198.md:16) | [Eutrophication/mod_wq.f90:3590](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/Eutrophication/mod_wq.f90:3590) `          WQTTA = RNH4WQ(L)/(ALGAES(NAL).WQKHNA + RNO3WQ(L) + 1.E-18)`<br>[Eutrophication/mod_wq.f90:3591](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/Eutrophication/mod_wq.f90:3591) `          WQPN(L,NAL) = ( RNO3WQ(L)/(ALGAES(NAL).WQKHNA + RNH4WQ(L) + 1.E-18) + ALGAES(NAL).WQKHNA/(RNH4NO3(L) + 1.E-18) ) * WQTTA   ! *** Ammonium preference` | 일치 | NH4 선호도 두 항을 대수적으로 같은 형태로 계산한다. 분모에 작은 양수를 추가한다. |
| [Theory](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/manuals/pdfs/EFDC_Theory_Document_Ver_12.pdf) PDF 184쪽 / 인쇄 171쪽, 식 **8.76**<br>`\displaystyle K_{RPON}=\left(K_{RN}+\left(\frac{KHN}{KHN+NH4+NO3}\right)K_{RNalg}\sum_{algae}B_x\right)\exp\left(KT_{HDR}(T-TR_{HDR})\right)`<br>[판독 기록:16](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/_staging/recovery/read/EFDC/manuals/pdfs/EFDC_Theory_Document_Ver_12.pdf/p181-198.md:16) | [Eutrophication/mod_wq.f90:3580](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/Eutrophication/mod_wq.f90:3580) `        WQKRPN(L) = (WQKRN + WQKRNALG*WQTT1) * WQTDHDR(IWQT(L))  ! *** Hydrolysis:     RPON-->DON` | 일치 | NH4+NO3 제한과 조류 생물량 촉진을 RPON 가수분해에 적용한다. |
| [Theory](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/manuals/pdfs/EFDC_Theory_Document_Ver_12.pdf) PDF 184쪽 / 인쇄 171쪽, 식 **8.77**<br>`\displaystyle K_{LPON}=\left(K_{LN}+\left(\frac{KHN}{KHN+NH4+NO3}\right)K_{LNalg}\sum_{algae}B_x\right)\exp\left(KT_{HDR}(T-TR_{HDR})\right)`<br>[판독 기록:16](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/_staging/recovery/read/EFDC/manuals/pdfs/EFDC_Theory_Document_Ver_12.pdf/p181-198.md:16) | [Eutrophication/mod_wq.f90:3581](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/Eutrophication/mod_wq.f90:3581) `        WQKLPN(L) = (WQKLN + WQKLNALG*WQTT1) * WQTDHDR(IWQT(L))  ! *** Hydrolysis:     LPON-->DON` | 일치 | 같은 N 제한 형태를 LPON 가수분해에 적용한다. |
| [Theory](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/manuals/pdfs/EFDC_Theory_Document_Ver_12.pdf) PDF 184쪽 / 인쇄 171쪽, 식 **8.78**<br>`\displaystyle K_{DON}=\left(K_{DN}+\left(\frac{KHN}{KHN+NH4+NO3}\right)K_{DNalg}\sum_{algae}B_x\right)\exp\left(KT_{MIN}(T-TR_{MIN})\right)`<br>[판독 기록:16](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/_staging/recovery/read/EFDC/manuals/pdfs/EFDC_Theory_Document_Ver_12.pdf/p181-198.md:16) | [Eutrophication/mod_wq.f90:3582](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/Eutrophication/mod_wq.f90:3582) `        WQKDON(L) = (WQKDN + WQKDNALG*WQTT1) * WQTDMNL(IWQT(L))  ! *** Mineralization: DON -->NH4` | 일치 | 같은 N 제한 형태를 DON 무기화에 적용한다. |
| [Theory](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/manuals/pdfs/EFDC_Theory_Document_Ver_12.pdf) PDF 185쪽 / 인쇄 172쪽, 식 **8.79**<br>`\displaystyle KHN=\frac{\sum_{algae}KHN_x}{\mathit{number\ of\ algae}}`<br>[판독 기록:17](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/_staging/recovery/read/EFDC/manuals/pdfs/EFDC_Theory_Document_Ver_12.pdf/p181-198.md:17) | [Eutrophication/mod_wq.f90:3571](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/Eutrophication/mod_wq.f90:3571) `          WQKHN = WQKHN + ALGAES(NAL).WQKHNA`<br>[Eutrophication/mod_wq.f90:3575](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/Eutrophication/mod_wq.f90:3575) `      if( ALGCOUNT > 0. ) WQKHN = WQKHN/ALGCOUNT   ! *** Note - better if mass weighted` | 일치 | 이동성 조류군의 KHN 합을 그 군 수로 나눈다. |
| [Theory](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/manuals/pdfs/EFDC_Theory_Document_Ver_12.pdf) PDF 185쪽 / 인쇄 172쪽, 식 **8.80**<br>`\displaystyle NH_4^++2O_2\rightarrow NO_3^-+H_2O+2H^+`<br>[판독 기록:17](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/_staging/recovery/read/EFDC/manuals/pdfs/EFDC_Theory_Document_Ver_12.pdf/p181-198.md:17) | 관련 원문 탐색 근거:<br>[Eutrophication/mod_wq.f90:4719](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/Eutrophication/mod_wq.f90:4719) `          WQNH3 = WQAONT*WQNIT(L)*WQO(L,K,INHX)      ! *** Ammonia` | 문서에만 | 질산화 화학양론을 설명한다. 코드는 질산화 플럭스에 입력 AONT를 곱한다. H⁺·H2O를 독립적으로 갱신하지 않는다. |
| [Theory](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/manuals/pdfs/EFDC_Theory_Document_Ver_12.pdf) PDF 185쪽 / 인쇄 172쪽, 식 **8.81**<br>`\displaystyle KNit\cdot NH4=fNit(T)\left(\frac{DO}{KHNit_{DO}+DO}\right)\left(\frac{NH4}{KHNit_N+NH4}\right)Nit_m`<br>[판독 기록:17](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/_staging/recovery/read/EFDC/manuals/pdfs/EFDC_Theory_Document_Ver_12.pdf/p181-198.md:17) | [Eutrophication/mod_wq.f90:945](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/Eutrophication/mod_wq.f90:945) `    call fson_get(json_data, "nitrification.max_rate",                                 WQNITM)`<br>[Eutrophication/mod_wq.f90:3593](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/Eutrophication/mod_wq.f90:3593) `        WQNIT(L) = WQTDNIT(IWQT(L)) * O2WQ(L) / (WQKHNDO + O2WQ(L) + 1.E-18) * RNH4WQ(L) / (WQKHNN + RNH4WQ(L) + 1.E-18)`<br>[Eutrophication/mod_wq.f90:4357](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/Eutrophication/mod_wq.f90:4357) `          WQF14 = - DTWQO2*WQNIT(L)  ` | 불일치 | WQNIT의 분자에 NH4가 있다. 코드가 이 계수를 NH4 손실률로 다시 곱한다. 문서의 Nitm·NH4/(KH+NH4)와 달리 코드 총속도에는 NH4가 한 번 더 곱해진다. |
| [Theory](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/manuals/pdfs/EFDC_Theory_Document_Ver_12.pdf) PDF 185쪽 / 인쇄 172쪽, 식 **8.82**<br>`\displaystyle fNit(T)=\begin{cases}\exp\left(-KNit1(T-TNit)^2\right),&T\leq TNit\\ \exp\left(-KNit2(TNit-T)^2\right),&T>TNit\end{cases}`<br>[판독 기록:17](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/_staging/recovery/read/EFDC/manuals/pdfs/EFDC_Theory_Document_Ver_12.pdf/p181-198.md:17) | [Eutrophication/mod_wq.f90:1531](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/Eutrophication/mod_wq.f90:1531) `      WQTDNIT(M) = WQNITM*EXP(-WQKN2*(WQTNIT-WTEMP)**2)`<br>[Eutrophication/mod_wq.f90:1533](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/Eutrophication/mod_wq.f90:1533) `      WQTDNIT(M) = WQNITM*EXP(-WQKN1*(WTEMP-WQTNIT)**2)` | 일치 | 기준 온도의 양쪽에 서로 다른 이차 지수함수를 사용한다. |
| [Theory](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/manuals/pdfs/EFDC_Theory_Document_Ver_12.pdf) PDF 185쪽 / 인쇄 172쪽, 식 **8.83**<br>`\displaystyle KNit=fNit(T)\left(\frac{DO}{KHNit_{DO}+DO}\right)\left(\frac{KHNit_N}{KHNit_N+NH4}\right)KNit_m`<br>[판독 기록:17](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/_staging/recovery/read/EFDC/manuals/pdfs/EFDC_Theory_Document_Ver_12.pdf/p181-198.md:17) | [Eutrophication/mod_wq.f90:3593](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/Eutrophication/mod_wq.f90:3593) `        WQNIT(L) = WQTDNIT(IWQT(L)) * O2WQ(L) / (WQKHNDO + O2WQ(L) + 1.E-18) * RNH4WQ(L) / (WQKHNN + RNH4WQ(L) + 1.E-18)` | 불일치 | 문서의 계수 분자는 KHNitN이다. 코드의 분자는 현재 NH4이다. |
| [Theory](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/manuals/pdfs/EFDC_Theory_Document_Ver_12.pdf) PDF 186쪽 / 인쇄 173쪽, 식 **8.84**<br>`\displaystyle KNit_m=\frac{Nit_m}{KHNit_N}`<br>[판독 기록:18](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/_staging/recovery/read/EFDC/manuals/pdfs/EFDC_Theory_Document_Ver_12.pdf/p181-198.md:18) | 관련 원문 탐색 근거:<br>[Eutrophication/mod_wq.f90:945](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/Eutrophication/mod_wq.f90:945) `    call fson_get(json_data, "nitrification.max_rate",                                 WQNITM)` | 문서에만 | 코드는 max_rate를 직접 읽는다. Nitm/KHNitN 변환을 코드에서 찾지 못했다. |
| [Theory](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/manuals/pdfs/EFDC_Theory_Document_Ver_12.pdf) PDF 186쪽 / 인쇄 173쪽, 식 **8.85**<br>`\displaystyle \frac{\partial SiP}{\partial t}=(FSP_d\cdot BM_d+FSPP_d\cdot D_d)ASC_d\,B_d+\sum_{zoopl}FSPDZ_z\cdot D_z\cdot Z\cdot ASC_z\frac{UB_d\,B_d}{PA_z}+\sum_{zoopl}FSPPZ_z\cdot PR_z\cdot Z_z\cdot ASC_z\frac{UB_d\,B_d}{PA_z}-K_{SUA}\cdot SiP+\frac{\partial}{\partial Z}(WS_d\cdot SiP)+\frac{WSiP}{V}`<br>[판독 기록:18](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/_staging/recovery/read/EFDC/manuals/pdfs/EFDC_Theory_Document_Ver_12.pdf/p181-198.md:18) | [Eutrophication/mod_wq.f90:4448](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/Eutrophication/mod_wq.f90:4448) `            WQM16 = -(WQKSUA(IWQT(L)) + WQRPSET(L,1)) * DTWQO2 `<br>[Eutrophication/mod_wq.f90:4455](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/Eutrophication/mod_wq.f90:4455) `                WQA16D = WQA16D + (ALGAES(NAL).WQFSPD*WQBM(L,NAL) + ALGAES(NAL).WQFSPP*WQPR(L,NAL)) * ALGAES(NAL).WQASC * WQO(L,K,19+NAL)`<br>[Eutrophication/mod_zoopl.f90:535](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/Eutrophication/mod_zoopl.f90:535) `                if( ALGAES(NAL).ISILICA > 0 ) FRSI(L,NZO) = FRSI(L,NZO) + ZOOPL(NZO).UBZ(NAL)*BAZ(L,NZO,NAL)/(PRAZ(L,NZO) + 1.E-18)`<br>[Eutrophication/mod_zoopl.f90:547](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/Eutrophication/mod_zoopl.f90:547) `                         *WQV(L,K,NWQVZ+NZO)*ZOOPL(NZO).ASCZ*FRSI(L,NZO)` | 불일치 | 코드는 SiP 침강에 RPOC 침강계수를 사용한다. 문서는 규소 조류 WSd를 사용한다. 동물플랑크톤 먹이 분자도 가용 조류 생물량을 사용한다. |
| [Theory](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/manuals/pdfs/EFDC_Theory_Document_Ver_12.pdf) PDF 187쪽 / 인쇄 174쪽, 식 **8.86**<br>`\displaystyle \frac{\partial SiA}{\partial t}=(FSI_d\,BM_d+FSIP_d\,D_d-P_d)ASC_d\,B_d+K_{SUA}\cdot SiP+\sum_{zoopl}(BM_z+FSADZ_z\cdot D_z+FSAPZ_z\cdot PR_z)Z_z\cdot ASC_z\cdot\frac{UB_d\,B_d}{PA_z}+\frac{\partial}{\partial Z}(WS_{TSS}SiAp)+\frac{BFSiAd}{\Delta Z}+\frac{WSiA}{V}`<br>[판독 기록:19](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/_staging/recovery/read/EFDC/manuals/pdfs/EFDC_Theory_Document_Ver_12.pdf/p181-198.md:19) | [Eutrophication/mod_wq.f90:4506](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/Eutrophication/mod_wq.f90:4506) `                WQKK(L) = WQKK(L) + (ALGAES(NAL).WQFSID*WQBM(L,NAL) + ALGAES(NAL).WQFSIP*WQPR(L,NAL) - WQPA(L,NAL)) * ALGAES(NAL).WQASC * WQO(L,K,19+NAL)`<br>[Eutrophication/mod_wq.f90:4534](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/Eutrophication/mod_wq.f90:4534) `            WQRR(L) = WQV(L,K,ISAA) + DTWQ*WQRR(L) + WQN17(L)*WQVO(L,K,ISAA) + DTWQO2*( WQKK(L) + WQKSUA(IWQT(L))*WQO(L,K,ISUU))`<br>[Eutrophication/mod_zoopl.f90:535](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/Eutrophication/mod_zoopl.f90:535) `                if( ALGAES(NAL).ISILICA > 0 ) FRSI(L,NZO) = FRSI(L,NZO) + ZOOPL(NZO).UBZ(NAL)*BAZ(L,NZO,NAL)/(PRAZ(L,NZO) + 1.E-18)`<br>[Eutrophication/mod_zoopl.f90:559](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/Eutrophication/mod_zoopl.f90:559) `                         *WQV(L,K,NWQVZ+NZO)*ZOOPL(NZO).ASCZ*FRSI(L,NZO)` | 불일치 | 조류 규소 생성·소비와 용출·저면 플럭스는 대응한다. 동물플랑크톤 분자는 UBd·Bd 대신 UBd·MAX(Bd−CT,0)을 사용한다. |
| [Theory](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/manuals/pdfs/EFDC_Theory_Document_Ver_12.pdf) PDF 188쪽 / 인쇄 175쪽, 식 **8.87**<br>`\displaystyle SiAp=\left(\frac{K_{SiAp}\,SORPS}{1+K_{SiAp}\,SORPS}\right)SiA`<br>[판독 기록:20](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/_staging/recovery/read/EFDC/manuals/pdfs/EFDC_Theory_Document_Ver_12.pdf/p181-198.md:20) | [Eutrophication/mod_wq.f90:4912](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/Eutrophication/mod_wq.f90:4912) `          WQSAD(L,K)  = WQV(L,K,ISAA) / (1.0 + WQKSAP*WQTAMP(L,K))  `<br>[Eutrophication/mod_wq.f90:4921](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/Eutrophication/mod_wq.f90:4921) `          WQSAD(L,K)  = WQV(L,K,ISAA) / (1.0 + WQKSAP*SEDT(L,K))  ` | 일치 | 용존 규소를 평형 분율로 구한다. 입자성 규소는 전체 SiA에서 용존 규소를 뺀 값이다. |
