| [Theory](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/manuals/pdfs/EFDC_Theory_Document_Ver_12.pdf) PDF 188쪽 / 인쇄 175쪽, 식 **8.89**<br>`\displaystyle SORPS=SED\ \mathrm{or}\ TAM_p`<br>[판독 기록:20](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/_staging/recovery/read/EFDC/manuals/pdfs/EFDC_Theory_Document_Ver_12.pdf/p181-198.md:20) | [Eutrophication/mod_wq.f90:4912](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/Eutrophication/mod_wq.f90:4912) `          WQSAD(L,K)  = WQV(L,K,ISAA) / (1.0 + WQKSAP*WQTAMP(L,K))  `<br>[Eutrophication/mod_wq.f90:4921](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/Eutrophication/mod_wq.f90:4921) `          WQSAD(L,K)  = WQV(L,K,ISAA) / (1.0 + WQKSAP*SEDT(L,K))  `<br>[Eutrophication/mod_wq.f90:4928](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/Eutrophication/mod_wq.f90:4928) `          WQPO4D(L,K) = WQV(L,K,IP4D)  ` | 일치 | 흡착 옵션이 TAMp와 SED를 선택한다. 흡착 비활성 옵션에서는 모두 용존으로 둔다. |
| [Theory](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/manuals/pdfs/EFDC_Theory_Document_Ver_12.pdf) PDF 188쪽 / 인쇄 175쪽, 식 **8.90**<br>`\displaystyle SiA=SiAp+SiAd`<br>[판독 기록:20](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/_staging/recovery/read/EFDC/manuals/pdfs/EFDC_Theory_Document_Ver_12.pdf/p181-198.md:20) | [Eutrophication/mod_wq.f90:4912](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/Eutrophication/mod_wq.f90:4912) `          WQSAD(L,K)  = WQV(L,K,ISAA) / (1.0 + WQKSAP*WQTAMP(L,K))  ` | 일치 | 전체 가용 규소 SiA를 저장하고 용존·입자 분율을 진단한다. 합의 정의가 대응한다. |

| [Theory](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/manuals/pdfs/EFDC_Theory_Document_Ver_12.pdf) PDF 189쪽 / 인쇄 176쪽, 식 **8.92**<br>`\displaystyle \frac{\partial COD}{\partial t}=-\left(\frac{DO}{KH_{COD}+DO}\right)K_{COD}COD+\frac{BFCOD}{\Delta Z}+\frac{WCOD}{V}`<br>[판독 기록:21](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/_staging/recovery/read/EFDC/manuals/pdfs/EFDC_Theory_Document_Ver_12.pdf/p181-198.md:21) | [Eutrophication/mod_wq.f90:3627](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/Eutrophication/mod_wq.f90:3627) `        WQO18(L) = -DTWQO2*WQKCOD(IWQT(L),IZ)*O2WQ(L)/(WQKHCOD(IZ) + O2WQ(L) + 1.E-18)  `<br>[Eutrophication/mod_wq.f90:4586](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/Eutrophication/mod_wq.f90:4586) `            WQRR(L) = WQRR(L) + WQBFCOD(L)*HPKI(L,K)   ! *** Add in Benthic Flux`<br>[Eutrophication/mod_wq.f90:4602](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/Eutrophication/mod_wq.f90:4602) `          WQRR(L) = WQV(L,K,ICOD) + DTWQ*WQRR(L) + WQO18(L)*WQV(L,K,ICOD)  ` | 일치 | COD 산화·저면 COD 공급·부하를 계산한다. PDF p.189의 COD 농도 단위 g O2-equivalents/m²/day는 코드 상태농도와 다르다. 상태농도의 단위는 g O2-equivalents/m³로 해석한다. |

| [Theory](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/manuals/pdfs/EFDC_Theory_Document_Ver_12.pdf) PDF 190쪽 / 인쇄 177쪽, 식 **8.94**<br>`\displaystyle \frac{\partial DO}{\partial t}=\sum_{algae}\left((1+0.3(1-PN_x))P_x-(1-FCD_x)\frac{DO}{KHR_x+DO}\cdot BM_x\right)AOCR\,B_x-AONT\,Nit\,NH4-AOCR\,K_{HR}DOC-\frac{DO}{KH_{COD}+DO}\cdot K_{COD}COD-\sum_{zoopl}BM_z\cdot Z_z\cdot AOCR+K_R(DO_S-DO)+\frac{SOD}{\Delta Z}+\frac{WDO}{V}`<br>[판독 기록:22](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/_staging/recovery/read/EFDC/manuals/pdfs/EFDC_Theory_Document_Ver_12.pdf/p181-198.md:22) | [Eutrophication/mod_wq.f90:4698](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/Eutrophication/mod_wq.f90:4698) `              WQRes = (1.0 - ALGAES(NAL).WQFCDB)*O2WQ(L)*WQBM(L,NAL)/(ALGAES(NAL).WQKHRA(IZ) + O2WQ(L) + 1.E-18)       `<br>[Eutrophication/mod_wq.f90:4710](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/Eutrophication/mod_wq.f90:4710) `            WQA19A(NAL) = WQTTAA(NAL)*ALGAES(NAL).WQAOCRP - WQRes*ALGAES(NAL).WQAOCRR`<br>[Eutrophication/mod_wq.f90:4718](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/Eutrophication/mod_wq.f90:4718) `          WQDOC = WQAOCR*WQKHR(L)*WQO(L,K,IDOC)      ! *** DOC`<br>[Eutrophication/mod_wq.f90:4719](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/Eutrophication/mod_wq.f90:4719) `          WQNH3 = WQAONT*WQNIT(L)*WQO(L,K,INHX)      ! *** Ammonia`<br>[Eutrophication/mod_wq.f90:4721](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/Eutrophication/mod_wq.f90:4721) `          WQRR(L) = WQV(L,K,IDOX) + WQSUM  + WQCOD  + WQRea + DTWQO2*(WQA19 - WQDOC - WQNH3)` | 불일치 | 코드는 조류군의 광합성과 호흡에 서로 다른 WQAOCRP·WQAOCRR을 사용한다. 문서는 공통 AOCR을 사용한다. 질산화 차이는 식 8.81에 따른다. |

| [Theory](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/manuals/pdfs/EFDC_Theory_Document_Ver_12.pdf) PDF 191쪽 / 인쇄 178쪽, 식 **8.99**<br>`\displaystyle T_s=\log\left(\frac{298.15-T}{273.15+T}\right)`<br>[판독 기록:23](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/_staging/recovery/read/EFDC/manuals/pdfs/EFDC_Theory_Document_Ver_12.pdf/p181-198.md:23) | [Eutrophication/mod_wq.f90:5733](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/Eutrophication/mod_wq.f90:5733) `      TVAL = LOG((298.15 - TWQ(L))/(273.15 + TWQ(L)))` | 일치 | 환산 온도는 동일한 자연로그 비율이다. |
| [Theory](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/manuals/pdfs/EFDC_Theory_Document_Ver_12.pdf) PDF 191쪽 / 인쇄 178쪽, 식 **8.100**<br>`\displaystyle \ln f(T)=-\frac{8.621949\times10^{11}}{T_a^4}+\frac{1.2438\times10^{10}}{T_a^3}-\frac{6.642308\times10^7}{T_a^2}+\frac{1.575701\times10^5}{T_a}-139.34411`<br>[판독 기록:23](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/_staging/recovery/read/EFDC/manuals/pdfs/EFDC_Theory_Document_Ver_12.pdf/p181-198.md:23) | [Eutrophication/mod_wq.f90:5728](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/Eutrophication/mod_wq.f90:5728) `      RLNSAT1 = ((((-8.621949e11 * TVAL + 1.2438e10)*TVAL -6.642308e7)*TVAL + 1.575701e5)*TVAL -139.34411)` | 일치 | Chapra 온도식의 네 역거듭제곱 항과 상수항이 같다. |
| [Theory](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/manuals/pdfs/EFDC_Theory_Document_Ver_12.pdf) PDF 191쪽 / 인쇄 178쪽, 식 **8.101**<br>`\displaystyle \ln f(S)=-S\times\left(-\frac{2140.7}{T_a^2}+\frac{10.754}{T_a}+1.7674\times10^{-2}\right)`<br>[판독 기록:23](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/_staging/recovery/read/EFDC/manuals/pdfs/EFDC_Theory_Document_Ver_12.pdf/p181-198.md:23) | [Eutrophication/mod_wq.f90:5729](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/Eutrophication/mod_wq.f90:5729) `      RLNSAT2 = -SWQ(L)*(( 2140.7 * TVAL - 10.754)*TVAL + 1.7674e-2)   ! *** Salinity correction: -S*(1.7674e-2 - 10.754/Ta + 2140.7/Ta^2)` | 불일치 | 문서 괄호 안의 Ta⁻²·Ta⁻¹ 항은 각각 음·양이다. 코드의 두 항은 각각 양·음이다. PDF p.191 이미지에서 부호를 확인했다. |

Convert elevation to km`<br>[Eutrophication/mod_wq.f90:5713](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/Eutrophication/mod_wq.f90:5713) `        RLNSAT3 = (((-1.60747e-4 * ELEV + 6.10834e-3)*ELEV - 0.11988)*ELEV + 1.)` | 일치 | m 고도를 km로 바꾼 뒤 동일한 3차 다항식을 적용한다. |

| [Theory](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/manuals/pdfs/EFDC_Theory_Document_Ver_12.pdf) PDF 191쪽 / 인쇄 178쪽, 식 **8.104**<br>`\displaystyle 106CO_2+16NH_4^++H_2PO_4^-+106H_2O\rightarrow \mathit{protoplasm}+106O_2+15H^+`<br>[판독 기록:23](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/_staging/recovery/read/EFDC/manuals/pdfs/EFDC_Theory_Document_Ver_12.pdf/p181-198.md:23) | 관련 원문 탐색 근거:<br>[Eutrophication/mod_wq.f90:4682](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/Eutrophication/mod_wq.f90:4682) `                WQTTAA(NAL) = (1.3 - 0.3*WQPN(L,NAL)) * WQPA(L,NAL) ` | 문서에만 | NH4 기반 광합성의 화학양론 설명식이다. 코드는 산소/탄소 비율과 NH4 선호도를 사용한다. protoplasm·H⁺·H2O를 독립적으로 풀지 않는다. |
| [Theory](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/manuals/pdfs/EFDC_Theory_Document_Ver_12.pdf) PDF 191쪽 / 인쇄 178쪽, 식 **8.105**<br>`\displaystyle 106CO_2+16NO_3^-+H_2PO_4^-+122H_2O\rightarrow \mathit{protoplasm}+138O_2`<br>[판독 기록:23](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/_staging/recovery/read/EFDC/manuals/pdfs/EFDC_Theory_Document_Ver_12.pdf/p181-198.md:23) | 관련 원문 탐색 근거:<br>[Eutrophication/mod_wq.f90:4682](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/Eutrophication/mod_wq.f90:4682) `                WQTTAA(NAL) = (1.3 - 0.3*WQPN(L,NAL)) * WQPA(L,NAL) ` | 문서에만 | NO3 기반 광합성의 화학양론 설명식이다. 코드의 1.3−0.3PN 산소 생성비에 대응한다. |
| [Theory](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/manuals/pdfs/EFDC_Theory_Document_Ver_12.pdf) PDF 192쪽 / 인쇄 179쪽, 식 **8.106**<br>`\displaystyle CH_2O+O_2\rightarrow CO_2+H_2O`<br>[판독 기록:24](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/_staging/recovery/read/EFDC/manuals/pdfs/EFDC_Theory_Document_Ver_12.pdf/p181-198.md:24) | 관련 원문 탐색 근거:<br>[Eutrophication/mod_wq.f90:4698](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/Eutrophication/mod_wq.f90:4698) `              WQRes = (1.0 - ALGAES(NAL).WQFCDB)*O2WQ(L)*WQBM(L,NAL)/(ALGAES(NAL).WQKHRA(IZ) + O2WQ(L) + 1.E-18)       `<br>[Eutrophication/mod_wq.f90:4718](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/Eutrophication/mod_wq.f90:4718) `          WQDOC = WQAOCR*WQKHR(L)*WQO(L,K,IDOC)      ! *** DOC` | 문서에만 | 유기탄소 호흡의 화학양론 설명식이다. 코드는 입력 AOCR 계수를 사용한다. |
| [Theory](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/manuals/pdfs/EFDC_Theory_Document_Ver_12.pdf) PDF 192쪽 / 인쇄 179쪽, 식 **8.107**<br>`\displaystyle K_r(20^\circ C)=A.\frac{V^B}{H^C}`<br>[판독 기록:24](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/_staging/recovery/read/EFDC/manuals/pdfs/EFDC_Theory_Document_Ver_12.pdf/p181-198.md:24) | [Eutrophication/mod_wq.f90:3660](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/Eutrophication/mod_wq.f90:3660) `            WQVREA = WQKRO(IZ) * XMRM**0.5 / HP(L)**0.5         ! *** Multi-layer implementation  `<br>[Eutrophication/mod_wq.f90:3668](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/Eutrophication/mod_wq.f90:3668) `            WQVREA = WQKRO(IZ) * XMRM**0.67 / HP(L)**1.85  ` | 일치 | 재폭기 선택 분기에서 유속 거듭제곱과 수심 거듭제곱 경험식을 계산한다. 지수는 선택식에 따라 달라진다. |
| [Theory](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/manuals/pdfs/EFDC_Theory_Document_Ver_12.pdf) PDF 192쪽 / 인쇄 179쪽, 식 **8.108**<br>`\displaystyle K_r=K_r(20^\circ C)1.024^{T-20}`<br>[판독 기록:24](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/_staging/recovery/read/EFDC/manuals/pdfs/EFDC_Theory_Document_Ver_12.pdf/p181-198.md:24) | [Eutrophication/mod_wq.f90:1540](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/Eutrophication/mod_wq.f90:1540) `    WQTDKR(M,1) = WQKTR(1)**TT20                                 ! *** TEMPERATURE EFFECT ON REAERATION` | 일치 | 코드는 입력 KTr^(T−20)을 사용한다. KTr=1.024일 때 문서의 수치식과 같다. 1.024를 실행 코드의 고정 기본값으로 확인하지 못했다. |
| [Theory](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/manuals/pdfs/EFDC_Theory_Document_Ver_12.pdf) PDF 192쪽 / 인쇄 179쪽, 식 **8.109**<br>`\displaystyle K_r=\frac{1}{\Delta z}\left(K_{ro}\sqrt{\frac{ueq}{heq}}+W_{rea}\right)(KT_r)^{T-20}`<br>[판독 기록:24](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/_staging/recovery/read/EFDC/manuals/pdfs/EFDC_Theory_Document_Ver_12.pdf/p181-198.md:24) | [Eutrophication/mod_wq.f90:3660](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/Eutrophication/mod_wq.f90:3660) `            WQVREA = WQKRO(IZ) * XMRM**0.5 / HP(L)**0.5         ! *** Multi-layer implementation  `<br>[Eutrophication/mod_wq.f90:3661](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/Eutrophication/mod_wq.f90:3661) `            WQVREA = WQVREA * HPKI(L,K)                         ! *** Multi-layer implementation`<br>[Eutrophication/mod_wq.f90:3690](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/Eutrophication/mod_wq.f90:3690) `          WQP19(L)    = -(WQVREA + WQWREA) * WQTDKR(IWQT(L),IZ)   ! *** Reaeration rate (1/day)  ` | 일치 | O'Connor–Dobbins 선택 분기에서 문서의 유속/수심 제곱근·표층 두께·풍속·온도 보정을 적용한다. 입력 REAC=1 조건이다. |
| [Theory](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/manuals/pdfs/EFDC_Theory_Document_Ver_12.pdf) PDF 193쪽 / 인쇄 180쪽, 식 **8.110**<br>`\displaystyle W_{rea}=0.728\sqrt{U_w}-0.317U_w+0.0372U_w^2`<br>[판독 기록:25](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/_staging/recovery/read/EFDC/manuals/pdfs/EFDC_Theory_Document_Ver_12.pdf/p181-198.md:25) | [Eutrophication/mod_wq.f90:3643](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/Eutrophication/mod_wq.f90:3643) `          WINDREA = min(WINDST(L),11.)  `<br>[Eutrophication/mod_wq.f90:3644](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/Eutrophication/mod_wq.f90:3644) `          WQWREA = 0.728*SQRT(WINDREA) + (0.0372*WINDREA - 0.317)*WINDREA  ` | 일치 | 풍속 다항식의 계수가 같다. 코드가 적용 전에 풍속을 11 m/s 이하로 제한한다. |
| [Theory](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/manuals/pdfs/EFDC_Theory_Document_Ver_12.pdf) PDF 193쪽 / 인쇄 180쪽, 식 **8.111**<br>`\displaystyle \frac{\partial DO}{\partial t}=\sum_{algae}((1.3-0.3PN_x)P_x-BM_x)AOCR\,B_x-AONT\,Nit\,NH4-AOCR\,K_{HR}\,DOC-\sum_{zoopl}BM_z\cdot Z_z\cdot AOCR-\left(\frac{DO}{KH_{COD}+DO}\right)K_{COD}COD+K_R(DO_S-DO)+\frac{SOD}{\Delta Z}+\frac{WDO}{V}`<br>[판독 기록:25](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/_staging/recovery/read/EFDC/manuals/pdfs/EFDC_Theory_Document_Ver_12.pdf/p181-198.md:25) | [Eutrophication/mod_wq.f90:4698](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/Eutrophication/mod_wq.f90:4698) `              WQRes = (1.0 - ALGAES(NAL).WQFCDB)*O2WQ(L)*WQBM(L,NAL)/(ALGAES(NAL).WQKHRA(IZ) + O2WQ(L) + 1.E-18)       `<br>[Eutrophication/mod_wq.f90:4710](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/Eutrophication/mod_wq.f90:4710) `            WQA19A(NAL) = WQTTAA(NAL)*ALGAES(NAL).WQAOCRP - WQRes*ALGAES(NAL).WQAOCRR`<br>[Eutrophication/mod_wq.f90:4721](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/Eutrophication/mod_wq.f90:4721) `          WQRR(L) = WQV(L,K,IDOX) + WQSUM  + WQCOD  + WQRea + DTWQO2*(WQA19 - WQDOC - WQNH3)` | 불일치 | 코드는 일반 기초대사 DO 제한·DOC 배설식을 유지한다. FCD=0·KHR=0 및 조류군 AOCRP=AOCRR=AOCR 조건에서만 문서의 단순 호흡항으로 줄어든다. 질산화 속도 차이는 남는다. |
| [Theory](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/manuals/pdfs/EFDC_Theory_Document_Ver_12.pdf) PDF 193쪽 / 인쇄 180쪽, 식 **8.112**<br>`\displaystyle \frac{\partial TAM}{\partial t}=\left(\frac{KHbmf}{KHbmf+DO}\right)\left(\exp\left(Ktam(T-Ttam)\right)\right)\frac{BFTAM}{\Delta z}+\frac{\partial}{\partial Z}(WS_sTAMp)+\frac{WTAM}{V}`<br>[판독 기록:25](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/_staging/recovery/read/EFDC/manuals/pdfs/EFDC_Theory_Document_Ver_12.pdf/p181-198.md:25) | [Eutrophication/mod_wq.f90:1542](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/Eutrophication/mod_wq.f90:1542) `    WQTDTAM(M) = WQKHBMF * WQBFTAM * EXP( WQKTAM*(WTEMP-WQTTAM) )`<br>[Eutrophication/mod_wq.f90:4741](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/Eutrophication/mod_wq.f90:4741) `            if(K == KSZ(L)) WQR20(L) = WQR20(L) + WQTDTAM(IWQT(L))*HPKI(L,K)/(WQKHBMF + O2WQ(L) + 1.E-18)`<br>[Eutrophication/mod_wq.f90:4758](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/Eutrophication/mod_wq.f90:4758) `            WQT20 = - DTWQ*WQWSSET(L,1)    ! *** DTWQO2` | 일치 | TAM 저면 공급에 DO 제한과 온도 함수를 곱한다. 입자 TAM 침강과 부하를 함께 처리한다. |
| [Theory](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/manuals/pdfs/EFDC_Theory_Document_Ver_12.pdf) PDF 194쪽 / 인쇄 181쪽, 식 **8.113**<br>`\displaystyle TAMd=\min\left(TAM_{dmx}\mathit{exp}(-K_{dotam}\,DO),\,TAM\right)`<br>[판독 기록:26](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/_staging/recovery/read/EFDC/manuals/pdfs/EFDC_Theory_Document_Ver_12.pdf/p181-198.md:26) | [Eutrophication/mod_wq.f90:4909](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/Eutrophication/mod_wq.f90:4909) `          WQTAMD = min( WQTAMDMX*EXP(-WQKDOTAM*O2WQ(L)), WQV(L,K,ITAM) )  ` | 일치 | DO의 지수함수로 용존 TAM 상한을 정하고 전체 TAM과 최소값을 취한다. |
| [Theory](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/manuals/pdfs/EFDC_Theory_Document_Ver_12.pdf) PDF 194쪽 / 인쇄 181쪽, 식 **8.114**<br>`\displaystyle TAMp=TAM-TAMd`<br>[판독 기록:26](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/_staging/recovery/read/EFDC/manuals/pdfs/EFDC_Theory_Document_Ver_12.pdf/p181-198.md:26) | [Eutrophication/mod_wq.f90:4910](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/Eutrophication/mod_wq.f90:4910) `          WQTAMP(L,K) = WQV(L,K,ITAM) - WQTAMD  ` | 일치 | 입자 TAM은 전체 TAM에서 용존 TAM을 뺀 값이다. |
| [Theory](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/manuals/pdfs/EFDC_Theory_Document_Ver_12.pdf) PDF 194쪽 / 인쇄 181쪽, 식 **8.115**<br>`\displaystyle \frac{\partial FCB}{\partial t}=KFCB\left(TFCB^{T-20}\right)FCB+\frac{WFCB}{V}`<br>[판독 기록:26](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/_staging/recovery/read/EFDC/manuals/pdfs/EFDC_Theory_Document_Ver_12.pdf/p181-198.md:26) | [Eutrophication/mod_wq.f90:325](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/Eutrophication/mod_wq.f90:325) `        WQTT = WQKFCB * WQTFCB**TT20 * DTWQO2  `<br>[Eutrophication/mod_wq.f90:326](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/Eutrophication/mod_wq.f90:326) `        WQTD1FCB(M1) = 1.0 - WQTT  `<br>[Eutrophication/mod_wq.f90:327](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/Eutrophication/mod_wq.f90:327) `        WQTD2FCB(M1) = 1.0 / (1.0 + WQTT)  `<br>[Eutrophication/mod_wq.f90:4793](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/Eutrophication/mod_wq.f90:4793) `          WQRR(L) = WQV(L,K,IFCB)*WQTD1FCB(IWQT(L)) + DTWQ*WQR21`<br>[Eutrophication/mod_wq.f90:4794](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/Eutrophication/mod_wq.f90:4794) `          WQV(L,K,IFCB) = max(WQRR(L)*WQKK(L),0.0)` | 불일치 | 문서 반응항은 양의 KFCB·TFCB^(T−20)·FCB로 인쇄되어 있다. 코드는 (1−a)/(1+a)로 감쇠시킨다. 양의 사멸 계수에서 부호가 반대이다. PDF p.194 이미지에서 양의 부호를 확인했다. |
| [Theory](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/manuals/pdfs/EFDC_Theory_Document_Ver_12.pdf) PDF 195쪽 / 인쇄 182쪽, 식 **8.116**<br>`\displaystyle \frac{\partial PM}{\partial t}=\frac{\partial}{\partial z}(WS_{PM}PM)+PM_{SS}`<br>[판독 기록:27](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/_staging/recovery/read/EFDC/manuals/pdfs/EFDC_Theory_Document_Ver_12.pdf/p181-198.md:27) | [Eutrophication/mod_wq.f90:3245](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/Eutrophication/mod_wq.f90:3245) `        WQRPSET(L,1) = WQWSRP(IMWQZT(L))*HPKI(L,K)  ! *** Refractory POM  (1/day)`<br>[Eutrophication/mod_wq.f90:3246](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/Eutrophication/mod_wq.f90:3246) `        WQLPSET(L,1) = WQWSLP(IMWQZT(L))*HPKI(L,K)  ! *** Labile POM      (1/day)`<br>[Eutrophication/mod_wq.f90:3809](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/Eutrophication/mod_wq.f90:3809) `          WQB4 = -( WQKRPC(L) + WQRPSET(L,1))*DTWQO2`<br>[Eutrophication/mod_wq.f90:3837](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/Eutrophication/mod_wq.f90:3837) `            WQRR(L) = WQRR(L) + DTWQO2*WQRPSET(L,2)*WQVO(L,K+1,IROC)  ` | 일치 | 입자성 물질의 층 침강 손실과 위층 공급을 반응 갱신에 넣는다. |

| [Theory](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/manuals/pdfs/EFDC_Theory_Document_Ver_12.pdf) PDF 195쪽 / 인쇄 182쪽, 식 **8.118**<br>`\displaystyle \frac{\partial PM_1}{\partial t}=\frac{WS_{POM}}{\Delta Z_1}PM_2-\frac{P_{depPM}WS_{PM}}{\Delta Z_1}PM_1+\frac{E_{PM}}{\Delta Z_1}+PM_{SS1}`<br>[판독 기록:27](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/_staging/recovery/read/EFDC/manuals/pdfs/EFDC_Theory_Document_Ver_12.pdf/p181-198.md:27) | 관련 원문 탐색 근거:<br>[Eutrophication/mod_wq.f90:3245](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/Eutrophication/mod_wq.f90:3245) `        WQRPSET(L,1) = WQWSRP(IMWQZT(L))*HPKI(L,K)  ! *** Refractory POM  (1/day)`<br>[Eutrophication/mod_wq.f90:4958](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/Eutrophication/mod_wq.f90:4958) `        WQDFRC(L) = SCB(L)*WQWSRP(IMWQZ)*WQV(L,K,IROC)   !< Depositional flux of RPOC ` | 코드에서 찾지 못함 | 수질 POM의 침강·저면 퇴적은 찾았다. 문서의 PdepPM 및 EPM 재부유 항을 활성 수질 반응 경로에서 찾지 못했다. |
| [Theory](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/manuals/pdfs/EFDC_Theory_Document_Ver_12.pdf) PDF 195쪽 / 인쇄 182쪽, 식 **8.119**<br>`\displaystyle E_{PM}=\left(\frac{PM_{bed}}{SED_{bed}}\right)\max(J_{ERO},\,0)`<br>[판독 기록:27](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/_staging/recovery/read/EFDC/manuals/pdfs/EFDC_Theory_Document_Ver_12.pdf/p181-198.md:27) | 관련 원문 탐색 근거:<br>[Eutrophication/mod_wq.f90:4958](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/Eutrophication/mod_wq.f90:4958) `        WQDFRC(L) = SCB(L)*WQWSRP(IMWQZ)*WQV(L,K,IROC)   !< Depositional flux of RPOC ` | 코드에서 찾지 못함 | PMbed/SEDbed·MAX(JERO,0) 재부유 계산을 수질 POM 경로에서 찾지 못했다. 퇴적물 자체의 침식식은 이 식의 구현으로 간주하지 않았다. |
| [Theory](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/manuals/pdfs/EFDC_Theory_Document_Ver_12.pdf) PDF 196쪽 / 인쇄 183쪽, 식 **8.120**<br>`\displaystyle \frac{\partial C}{\partial t}=\boldsymbol{K}C+\frac{\partial}{\partial z}(WC)+\boldsymbol{R}`<br>[판독 기록:28](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/_staging/recovery/read/EFDC/manuals/pdfs/EFDC_Theory_Document_Ver_12.pdf/p181-198.md:28) | [Eutrophication/mod_wq.f90:3809](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/Eutrophication/mod_wq.f90:3809) `          WQB4 = -( WQKRPC(L) + WQRPSET(L,1))*DTWQO2`<br>[Eutrophication/mod_wq.f90:3827](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/Eutrophication/mod_wq.f90:3827) `          WQRR(L) = WQV(L,K,IROC) + DTWQ*WQR4 + DTWQO2*WQA4 + WQB4*WQVO(L,K,IROC)`<br>[Eutrophication/mod_wq.f90:3837](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/Eutrophication/mod_wq.f90:3837) `            WQRR(L) = WQRR(L) + DTWQO2*WQRPSET(L,2)*WQVO(L,K+1,IROC)  ` | 일치 | 반응·침강·부하를 상태변수별 갱신으로 구현한다. 별도 전체 행렬을 구성하지 않는다. |
| [Theory](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/manuals/pdfs/EFDC_Theory_Document_Ver_12.pdf) PDF 196쪽 / 인쇄 183쪽, 식 **8.121**<br>`\displaystyle \begin{aligned}\frac{C_k}{\partial t}&=\boldsymbol{K}1_k C_k+\delta_k\boldsymbol{K}2_k C_{k+1}+\boldsymbol{R}_k\\ \boldsymbol{K}1_k&=\boldsymbol{K}_k-\frac{1}{\Delta_k}W\\ \boldsymbol{K}2_k&=\frac{1}{\Delta_k}W\end{aligned}`<br>[판독 기록:28](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/_staging/recovery/read/EFDC/manuals/pdfs/EFDC_Theory_Document_Ver_12.pdf/p181-198.md:28) | [Eutrophication/mod_wq.f90:3809](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/Eutrophication/mod_wq.f90:3809) `          WQB4 = -( WQKRPC(L) + WQRPSET(L,1))*DTWQO2`<br>[Eutrophication/mod_wq.f90:3837](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/Eutrophication/mod_wq.f90:3837) `            WQRR(L) = WQRR(L) + DTWQO2*WQRPSET(L,2)*WQVO(L,K+1,IROC)  ` | 불일치 | 층 손실과 위층 공급 구조는 대응한다. 문서 첫 좌변의 ∂가 누락되어 있다. 문서가 포함한다고 한 저면 EPM 항을 코드에서 찾지 못했다. PDF p.196 이미지에서 좌변을 확인했다. |
| [Theory](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/manuals/pdfs/EFDC_Theory_Document_Ver_12.pdf) PDF 196쪽 / 인쇄 183쪽, 식 **8.122**<br>`\displaystyle C_k^{n+1}-C_k^n=\lambda\theta\left(\boldsymbol{K}1_k^n C_k^{n+1}+\delta_k\boldsymbol{K}2_k^n C_{k+1}^{n+1}+\boldsymbol{R}_k^{n+1}\right)+(1-\lambda)\theta\left(\boldsymbol{K}1_k^n C_k^n+\delta_k\boldsymbol{K}2_k^n C_{k+1}^n+\boldsymbol{R}_k^n\right)`<br>[판독 기록:28](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/_staging/recovery/read/EFDC/manuals/pdfs/EFDC_Theory_Document_Ver_12.pdf/p181-198.md:28) | [Eutrophication/mod_wq.f90:202](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/Eutrophication/mod_wq.f90:202) `    DTWQO2 = DTWQ*0.5  `<br>[Eutrophication/mod_wq.f90:3827](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/Eutrophication/mod_wq.f90:3827) `          WQRR(L) = WQV(L,K,IROC) + DTWQ*WQR4 + DTWQO2*WQA4 + WQB4*WQVO(L,K,IROC)`<br>[Eutrophication/mod_wq.f90:3837](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/Eutrophication/mod_wq.f90:3837) `            WQRR(L) = WQRR(L) + DTWQO2*WQRPSET(L,2)*WQVO(L,K+1,IROC)  ` | 불일치 | 코드는 λ=0.5의 반응 갱신을 사용한다. RPOC 위층 공급에는 WQVO만 사용하여 문서의 신·구 위층 농도 가중합과 다르다. |
| [Theory](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/manuals/pdfs/EFDC_Theory_Document_Ver_12.pdf) PDF 196쪽 / 인쇄 183쪽, 식 **8.123**<br>`\displaystyle (\boldsymbol{I}-\lambda\theta\boldsymbol{K}1_k^n)C_k^{n+1}=(\boldsymbol{I}+(1-\lambda)\theta\boldsymbol{K}1_k^n)C_k^n+\theta\delta_k\boldsymbol{K}2_k^n\left(\lambda C_{k+1}^{n+1}+(1-\lambda)C_{k+1}^n\right)+\theta\left(\lambda\boldsymbol{R}_k^{n+1}+(1-\lambda)\boldsymbol{R}_k^n\right)`<br>[판독 기록:28](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/_staging/recovery/read/EFDC/manuals/pdfs/EFDC_Theory_Document_Ver_12.pdf/p181-198.md:28) | [Eutrophication/mod_wq.f90:3827](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/Eutrophication/mod_wq.f90:3827) `          WQRR(L) = WQV(L,K,IROC) + DTWQ*WQR4 + DTWQO2*WQA4 + WQB4*WQVO(L,K,IROC)`<br>[Eutrophication/mod_wq.f90:3837](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/Eutrophication/mod_wq.f90:3837) `            WQRR(L) = WQRR(L) + DTWQO2*WQRPSET(L,2)*WQVO(L,K+1,IROC)  ` | 불일치 | RPOC 갱신에서 위층의 Cn+1를 사다리꼴 평균에 넣지 않는다. 문서의 일반 λ·위층 신·구 농도 결합식과 다르다. |

(4)  RLRPD-Loss rate for plant detritus at bottom of water column (/day)`<br>[Eutrophication/mod_rpem.f90:392](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/Eutrophication/mod_rpem.f90:392) `      WQRPD(L) = FACIMP*(WQRPD(L)+DTWQ*FRPSD*RLRPS*WQRPS(L))` | 불일치 | 문서는 생산 PRPS·RPS를 쇄설물 공급으로 사용하고 RPS에 손실률을 곱한다. 코드는 비호흡 손실 RLRPS·RPS를 공급으로 사용하고 RPD를 감쇠시킨다. PDF p.199 이미지에서 두 대상을 확인했다. |
| [Theory](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/manuals/pdfs/EFDC_Theory_Document_Ver_12.pdf) PDF 199쪽 / 인쇄 186쪽, 식 **8.128**<br>`\displaystyle P_{RPS}=PM_{RPS}\cdot f_{1W}(N)\cdot f_{1B}(N)\cdot f_2(I)\cdot f_3(T)\cdot f_4(S)\cdot f_5(RPS)`<br>[판독 기록:13](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/_staging/recovery/read/EFDC/manuals/pdfs/EFDC_Theory_Document_Ver_12.pdf/p199-216.md:13) | [Eutrophication/mod_rpem.f90:192](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/Eutrophication/mod_rpem.f90:192) `      XLIMNRPS(L) = min(TMPNIT,TMPPO4)                                ! F1(N), EQ. (6), Minimum of N or P limit`<br>[Eutrophication/mod_rpem.f90:299](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/Eutrophication/mod_rpem.f90:299) `      PRPS(L) = PMRPS*XLIMTPRPS(L)*XLIMNRPS(L)*XLIMLRPS(L)*EXP(-RKSH*WQRPS(L))  ! EQ. (5)   PRPS - Nutrient, light and temperature limited growth rate (/day)`<br>[Eutrophication/mod_rpem.f90:943](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/Eutrophication/mod_rpem.f90:943) `    call fson_get(json_data, "salinity_toxicity.salinity_effect_option", iSTOXRPE)` | 불일치 | 코드는 수주·퇴적층의 결합 영양염 제한 하나를 사용한다. 문서의 두 제한함수 곱과 다르다. 생물량 제한은 EXP(−RKSH·RPS)이다. 읽은 염분 옵션을 성장식에 적용하지 않는다. |
| [Theory](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/manuals/pdfs/EFDC_Theory_Document_Ver_12.pdf) PDF 200쪽 / 인쇄 187쪽, 식 **8.129**<br>`\displaystyle \begin{aligned}f_{1W}(N)&=\min\left(\frac{(NH4+NO3)_W}{KHN_{RPS}+(NH4+NO3)_W},\frac{PO4d_W}{KHP_{RPS}+PO4d_W}\right)\\f_{1B}(N)&=\min\left(\frac{(NH4+NO3)_B}{KHN_{RPS}+(NH4+NO3)_B},\frac{PO4d_B}{KHP_{RPS}+PO4d_B}\right)\end{aligned}`<br>[판독 기록:14](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/_staging/recovery/read/EFDC/manuals/pdfs/EFDC_Theory_Document_Ver_12.pdf/p199-216.md:14) | [Eutrophication/mod_rpem.f90:186](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/Eutrophication/mod_rpem.f90:186) `      RATION = RKHNRPS/RKHNRPR                                       ! Ratio of N half saturation constants for water/bed  `<br>[Eutrophication/mod_rpem.f90:187](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/Eutrophication/mod_rpem.f90:187) `      TOP = WQV(L,K,INHX) + WQV(L,K,INOX) + RATION*(SM2NH4(L)+SM2NO3(L))                                                           `<br>[Eutrophication/mod_rpem.f90:188](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/Eutrophication/mod_rpem.f90:188) `      TMPNIT = TOP/(TOP+RKHNRPS)`<br>[Eutrophication/mod_rpem.f90:190](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/Eutrophication/mod_rpem.f90:190) `      TOP = WQPO4D(L,K)+RATIOP*SM2PO4(L)                                                                                   `<br>[Eutrophication/mod_rpem.f90:192](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/Eutrophication/mod_rpem.f90:192) `      XLIMNRPS(L) = min(TMPNIT,TMPPO4)                                ! F1(N), EQ. (6), Minimum of N or P limit` | 불일치 | 코드는 수주 농도에 반포화상수 비로 가중한 퇴적층 농도를 더한다. 그 합에 Monod 제한을 적용한다. 수주·퇴적층의 독립 Monod 함수가 아니다. |
| [Theory](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/manuals/pdfs/EFDC_Theory_Document_Ver_12.pdf) PDF 200쪽 / 인쇄 187쪽, 식 **8.130**<br>`\displaystyle \frac{\partial I}{\partial Z_*}=-K_{ess}\cdot I`<br>[판독 기록:14](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/_staging/recovery/read/EFDC/manuals/pdfs/EFDC_Theory_Document_Ver_12.pdf/p199-216.md:14) | [Transport/mod_heat.f90:572](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/Transport/mod_heat.f90:572) `          BOT = max(-WCKESS*HPK(L,K),-40.0)`<br>[Transport/mod_heat.f90:573](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/Transport/mod_heat.f90:573) `          FRACLAYER = EXP(BOT)` | 일치 | 광의 수직 감쇠를 층별 EXP(−Ke·Δz)로 구현한다. RPEM은 계산된 RADKE를 읽는다. |
| [Theory](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/manuals/pdfs/EFDC_Theory_Document_Ver_12.pdf) PDF 201쪽 / 인쇄 188쪽, 식 **8.131**<br>`\displaystyle I=I_{ws}\exp\left(-\int_0^{Z_*}K_{ess}\cdot dZ_*\right)`<br>[판독 기록:15](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/_staging/recovery/read/EFDC/manuals/pdfs/EFDC_Theory_Document_Ver_12.pdf/p199-216.md:15) | [Transport/mod_heat.f90:572](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/Transport/mod_heat.f90:572) `          BOT = max(-WCKESS*HPK(L,K),-40.0)`<br>[Transport/mod_heat.f90:573](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/Transport/mod_heat.f90:573) `          FRACLAYER = EXP(BOT)`<br>[Eutrophication/mod_rpem.f90:222](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/Eutrophication/mod_rpem.f90:222) `        RKESSTOP(L) = RADKE(L,KSZ(L)+1)   ! EQ. (24)`<br>[Eutrophication/mod_rpem.f90:228](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/Eutrophication/mod_rpem.f90:228) `      WQKESS = RADKE(L,KSZ(L))` | 일치 | 층별 지수 감쇠는 구간별 일정 소광계수 적분에 대응한다. 광 지수는 코드에서 −40 이하로 제한한다. |
| [Theory](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/manuals/pdfs/EFDC_Theory_Document_Ver_12.pdf) PDF 201쪽 / 인쇄 188쪽, 식 **8.132**<br>`\displaystyle I_{ws}=I_o\min\left(exp\left(-K_{eme}\cdot(H_{RPS}-H)\right),1\right)`<br>[판독 기록:15](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/_staging/recovery/read/EFDC/manuals/pdfs/EFDC_Theory_Document_Ver_12.pdf/p199-216.md:15) | 관련 원문 탐색 근거:<br>[Eutrophication/mod_rpem.f90:248](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/Eutrophication/mod_rpem.f90:248) `        WQAVGIO = PARADJ*2.065*RADTOP(L,KC)                              ! RPEM의 HRPS−H와 Keme에 의한 정수성 지상부 광 감소식을 찾지 못했다. |
| [Theory](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/manuals/pdfs/EFDC_Theory_Document_Ver_12.pdf) PDF 201쪽 / 인쇄 188쪽, 식 **8.133**<br>`\displaystyle K_{essac}=Ke_b+Ke_{TSS}\cdot TSS+Ke_{VSS}\cdot VSS+Ke_{Chl}\sum_{m=1}^{M}\left(\frac{B_m}{CChl_m}\right)`<br>[판독 기록:15](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/_staging/recovery/read/EFDC/manuals/pdfs/EFDC_Theory_Document_Ver_12.pdf/p199-216.md:15) | [Transport/mod_heat.f90:1622](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/Transport/mod_heat.f90:1622) `    WCKESS = WCKESS + WQKEPOC*(WQV(L,K,IROC) + WQV(L,K,ILOC))        ! *** PARTICULATE ORGANIC MATTER`<br>[Transport/mod_heat.f90:1623](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/Transport/mod_heat.f90:1623) `    WCKESS = WCKESS + WQKEDOM*WQV(L,K,IDOC)                          ! *** DISSOLVED ORGANIC MATTER`<br>[Transport/mod_heat.f90:1638](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/Transport/mod_heat.f90:1638) `      CHLKE = WQKECHL*WQCHL(L,K)**WQKECHLE` | 불일치 | 코드는 POC·DOC의 광 감소를 따로 더한다. 엽록소에는 입력 거듭제곱 또는 비선형식을 적용한다. 문서의 VSS·선형 엽록소식과 일반적으로 다르다. |
| [Theory](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/manuals/pdfs/EFDC_Theory_Document_Ver_12.pdf) PDF 201쪽 / 인쇄 188쪽, 식 **8.134**<br>`\displaystyle K_{essic}=Ke_b+Ke_{TSS}\cdot TSS+Ke_{VSS}\cdot VSS+Ke_{Chl}\sum_{m=1}^{M}\left(\frac{B_m}{CChl_m}\right)+Ke_{RPS}\cdot RPS`<br>[판독 기록:15](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/_staging/recovery/read/EFDC/manuals/pdfs/EFDC_Theory_Document_Ver_12.pdf/p199-216.md:15) | [Transport/mod_heat.f90:1629](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/Transport/mod_heat.f90:1629) `          WCKESS = WCKESS + ALGAES(NAL).WQKEMAC*WQV(L,K,19+NAL)      ! *** MACROPHYTE MASS G/M3 TO 1/M`<br>[Eutrophication/mod_rpem.f90:228](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/Eutrophication/mod_rpem.f90:228) `      WQKESS = RADKE(L,KSZ(L))`<br>[Eutrophication/mod_rpem.f90:237](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/Eutrophication/mod_rpem.f90:237) `        RKESSBOT(L) = RKESSBOT(L) + RKERPE*Z(L,KSZ(L))*HWQ(L)*WQRPE(L)/CCHLRPE   ! EQ. (10), INCLUDE EPIPHYTES SHADING` | 불일치 | 캐노피 소광계수는 RADKE와 부착 조류 보정에서 온다. 문서의 KeRPS·RPS 항을 RPEM 광장 계산에서 찾지 못했다. 일반 비이동성 조류 WQV의 차광을 RPEM 지상부 RPS의 구현으로 간주하지 않았다. |

EQ. (11), PART OF IT, USED IF IJRPRS = 2 IN CAL_RPEM.INP C5` | 불일치 | 캐노피 상단 대신 저층 상단 Z(KSZ)를 사용한다. HRPS 높이로 캐노피 상단을 정하지 않는다. |

EQ. (8)` | 불일치 | 코드는 저층 소광계수를 전체 HWQ에 곱한 지수로 하단 광을 정한다. 문서의 캐노피 상단 광·내부 깊이 구분과 다르다. |

EQ. (10), INCLUDE EPIPHYTES SHADING` | 문서에만 | Florida Bay의 건조질량·잎 면적에서 KeRPE를 구하는 설명식이다. 코드는 RKERPE·CCHLRPE를 입력으로 사용한다. |

| [Theory](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/manuals/pdfs/EFDC_Theory_Document_Ver_12.pdf) PDF 203쪽 / 인쇄 190쪽, 식 **8.144**<br>`\displaystyle f_2(I)=\frac{I}{I_{RSPopt}}\exp\left(1-\frac{I}{I_{RSPopt}}\right)`<br>[판독 기록:17](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/_staging/recovery/read/EFDC/manuals/pdfs/EFDC_Theory_Document_Ver_12.pdf/p199-216.md:17) | [Eutrophication/mod_rpem.f90:251](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/Eutrophication/mod_rpem.f90:251) `        TMPEXP = EXP(ALPHABOT)-EXP(ALPHATOP)`<br>[Eutrophication/mod_rpem.f90:252](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/Eutrophication/mod_rpem.f90:252) `        XLIMLRPS(L) = 2.718/(RKESSAVG*Z(L,KSZ(L))*HWQ(L))*TMPEXP         ! F2(I), EQ. (7)` | 일치 | 코드의 두 지수함수 차이는 Steele 점 광 제한을 층에 적분한 형태이다. 이 판정은 함수 형태에 관한 해석이다. 광장 배치 차이는 식 8.135–8.148에서 판정한다. |
| [Theory](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/manuals/pdfs/EFDC_Theory_Document_Ver_12.pdf) PDF 203쪽 / 인쇄 190쪽, 식 **8.145**<br>`\displaystyle f_2(I)=\frac{I_{RPSA}}{I_{RSPopt}}\exp\left(1-\frac{I_{RPSA}}{I_{RSPopt}}\right)`<br>[판독 기록:17](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/_staging/recovery/read/EFDC/manuals/pdfs/EFDC_Theory_Document_Ver_12.pdf/p199-216.md:17) | 관련 원문 탐색 근거:<br>[Eutrophication/mod_rpem.f90:252](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/Eutrophication/mod_rpem.f90:252) `        XLIMLRPS(L) = 2.718/(RKESSAVG*Z(L,KSZ(L))*HWQ(L))*TMPEXP         ! F2(I), EQ. (7)` | 문서에만 | 평균 광도에 점 Steele 함수를 적용하는 경로가 없다. 코드는 광 제한함수 자체를 층에 평균한다. |

(8)`<br>[Eutrophication/mod_rpem.f90:252](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/Eutrophication/mod_rpem.f90:252) `        XLIMLRPS(L) = 2.718/(RKESSAVG*Z(L,KSZ(L))*HWQ(L))*TMPEXP         ! F2(I), EQ. (7)` | 불일치 | 코드는 저층 경계 광 강도와 평균 소광계수로 적분 결과를 계산한다. 문서의 HRPS·별도 KeRPE·RPE 광장 적분과 다르다. |
| [Theory](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/manuals/pdfs/EFDC_Theory_Document_Ver_12.pdf) PDF 204쪽 / 인쇄 191쪽, 식 **8.147**<br>`\displaystyle f_{2avg}(I)=\frac{\exp(1)}{K_{essic}\cdot H_{RPS}}\left[\exp\left(-F_2\cdot exp(-K_{essic}\cdot H_{RPS})\right)-\exp(F_2)\right]`<br>[판독 기록:18](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/_staging/recovery/read/EFDC/manuals/pdfs/EFDC_Theory_Document_Ver_12.pdf/p199-216.md:18) | [Eutrophication/mod_rpem.f90:249](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/Eutrophication/mod_rpem.f90:249) `        ALPHATOP = -(WQAVGIO/RISSO(L)) *EXP(-RKESSTOP(L)*TMP1*HWQ(L))    ! EQ. (9), ASSUME HRPS IS WITHIN THE BOTTOM LA`<br>[Eutrophication/mod_rpem.f90:251](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/Eutrophication/mod_rpem.f90:251) `        TMPEXP = EXP(ALPHABOT)-EXP(ALPHATOP)`<br>[Eutrophication/mod_rpem.f90:252](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/Eutrophication/mod_rpem.f90:252) `        XLIMLRPS(L) = 2.718/(RKESSAVG*Z(L,KSZ(L))*HWQ(L))*TMPEXP         ! | [Theory](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/manuals/pdfs/EFDC_Theory_Document_Ver_12.pdf) PDF 204쪽 / 인쇄 191쪽, 식 **8.148**<br>`\displaystyle F_2=\frac{I_{ws}}{I_{RSPopt}}\cdot\exp\left(-K_{essac}\cdot(H-H_{RPS})-Ke_{RPE}\cdot RPE\right)`<br>[판독 기록:18](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/_staging/recovery/read/EFDC/manuals/pdfs/EFDC_Theory_Document_Ver_12.pdf/p199-216.md:18) | [Eutrophication/mod_rpem.f90:237](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/Eutrophication/mod_rpem.f90:237) `        RKESSBOT(L) = RKESSBOT(L) + RKERPE*Z(L,KSZ(L))*HWQ(L)*WQRPE(L)/CCHLRPE   ! (10), INCLUDE EPIPHYTES SHADING`<br>[Eutrophication/mod_rpem.f90:249](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/Eutrophication/mod_rpem.f90:249) `        ALPHATOP = -(WQAVGIO/RISSO(L)) *EXP(-RKESSTOP(L)*TMP1*HWQ(L))    ! EQ. (9), ASSUME HRPS IS WITHIN THE BOTTOM LA` | 불일치 | 코드는 HRPS 대신 저층 상단 깊이를 사용한다. 부착 조류 감소도 F2의 독립 표면 감쇠항으로 넣지 않는다. |
| [Theory](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/manuals/pdfs/EFDC_Theory_Document_Ver_12.pdf) PDF 204쪽 / 인쇄 191쪽, 식 **8.149**<br>`\displaystyle f_3(T)=\begin{cases}\exp\left(-KTP1_{RPS}[T-TP1_{RPS}]^2\right)\quad if\ T\le TP1_{RPS}\\1\quad if\ TP1_{RPS}<T<TP2_{RPS}\\\exp\left(-KTP2_{RPS}[T-TP2_{RPS}]^2\right)\quad if\ T\ge TP1_{RPS}\end{cases}`<br>[판독 기록:18](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/_staging/recovery/read/EFDC/manuals/pdfs/EFDC_Theory_Document_Ver_12.pdf/p199-216.md:18) | [Eutrophication/mod_rpem.f90:1249](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/Eutrophication/mod_rpem.f90:1249) `    if( WTEMP < TP1RPS )then`<br>[Eutrophication/mod_rpem.f90:1252](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/Eutrophication/mod_rpem.f90:1252) `    if( WTEMP > TP2RPS )then`<br>[Eutrophication/mod_rpem.f90:1253](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/Eutrophication/mod_rpem.f90:1253) `      RPEMTPrps(NT) = EXP(-rKTP2RPS*(WTEMP-TP2RPS)*(WTEMP-TP2RPS) )` | 불일치 | 고온 분기의 함수는 대응한다. 문서는 고온 조건을 T≥TP1로 인쇄한다. 코드는 T>TP2에서 고온 함수를 적용한다. PDF p.204 이미지에서 조건을 확인했다. |
| [Theory](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/manuals/pdfs/EFDC_Theory_Document_Ver_12.pdf) PDF 204쪽 / 인쇄 191쪽, 식 **8.150**<br>`\displaystyle f_3(T)=\exp\left(KTP_{RPS}[T-TPREF_{RPS}]\right)`<br>[판독 기록:18](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/_staging/recovery/read/EFDC/manuals/pdfs/EFDC_Theory_Document_Ver_12.pdf/p199-216.md:18) | 관련 원문 탐색 근거:<br>[Eutrophication/mod_rpem.f90:1250](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/Eutrophication/mod_rpem.f90:1250) `      RPEMTPrps(NT) = EXP(-rKTP1RPS*(WTEMP-TP1RPS)*(WTEMP-TP1RPS) )`<br>[Eutrophication/mod_rpem.f90:1253](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/Eutrophication/mod_rpem.f90:1253) `      RPEMTPrps(NT) = EXP(-rKTP2RPS*(WTEMP-TP2RPS)*(WTEMP-TP2RPS) )` | 문서에만 | 지상부 생산에 대한 단일 선형 지수 온도 대안은 없다. 코드가 양쪽 Gaussian 함수만 구성한다. |

EQ. (5)   PRPS - Nutrient, light and temperature limited growth rate (/day)`<br>[Eutrophication/mod_rpem.f90:943](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/Eutrophication/mod_rpem.f90:943) `    call fson_get(json_data, "salinity_toxicity.salinity_effect_option", iSTOXRPE)`<br>[Eutrophication/mod_rpem.f90:944](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/Eutrophication/mod_rpem.f90:944) `    call fson_get(json_data, "salinity_toxicity.STOXS", STOXS)` | 코드에서 찾지 못함 | STOXS·염분 옵션은 읽는다. 성장 계산에서 STOXS²/(STOXS²+S²)를 적용하는 줄을 찾지 못했다. |

EQ. (5)   PRPS - Nutrient, light and temperature limited growth rate (/day)`<br>[Eutrophication/mod_rpem.f90:837](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/Eutrophication/mod_rpem.f90:837) `    call fson_get(json_data, "rooted_plant.growth.shoot.self_shading", rKSH)` | 불일치 | 밀도 제한은 EXP(−RKSH·RPS)이다. 문서의 종별 포화 생물량 합의 제곱을 사용하지 않는다. |

EQ. (1)`<br>[Eutrophication/mod_rpem.f90:862](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/Eutrophication/mod_rpem.f90:862) `    call fson_get(json_data, "rooted_plant.non_respiration_loss.shoot.loss_rate", rLRPS)` | 불일치 | 지상부 비호흡 손실률 RLRPS를 상수로 읽고 사용한다. 문서의 온도 지수 보정을 적용하지 않는다. |

| [Theory](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/manuals/pdfs/EFDC_Theory_Document_Ver_12.pdf) PDF 206쪽 / 인쇄 193쪽, 식 **8.156**<br>`\displaystyle RORS=\frac{RPR_{obs}}{RPS_{obs}}`<br>[판독 기록:20](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/_staging/recovery/read/EFDC/manuals/pdfs/EFDC_Theory_Document_Ver_12.pdf/p199-216.md:20) | 관련 원문 탐색 근거:<br>[Eutrophication/mod_rpem.f90:827](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/Eutrophication/mod_rpem.f90:827) `    call fson_get(json_data, "rooted_plant.growth.root_to_shoot_carbon_transport.observed_ratio", ROSR)` | 문서에만 | 관측 뿌리/지상부 비를 정의하는 식이다. 코드는 observed_ratio를 직접 읽는다. 관측 두 생물량에서 비를 계산하지 않는다. |
| [Theory](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/manuals/pdfs/EFDC_Theory_Document_Ver_12.pdf) PDF 206쪽 / 인쇄 193쪽, 식 **8.157**<br>`\displaystyle JRP_{RS}=KRP_{RS}\left(\frac{I_{SS}}{I_{SS}+I_{SSS}}\right)RPR`<br>[판독 기록:20](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/_staging/recovery/read/EFDC/manuals/pdfs/EFDC_Theory_Document_Ver_12.pdf/p199-216.md:20) | [Eutrophication/mod_rpem.f90:349](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/Eutrophication/mod_rpem.f90:349) `        RJRPRS(L) = RKRPRS*RISS(L)/(RISS(L)+RISSS+1E-18)                                                                           ` | 불일치 | 문서의 광 제한 이동률에 RPR을 곱한다. 코드는 그 생물량 인자를 곱하지 않는다. 따라서 입력 RKRPRS의 필요 단위도 문서의 1/day와 다르다. |

| [Theory](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/manuals/pdfs/EFDC_Theory_Document_Ver_12.pdf) PDF 212쪽 / 인쇄 199쪽, 식 **8.181**<br>`\displaystyle \frac{\partial DOP_B}{\partial t}=\frac{1}{B}\left(FPD_{RPR}\cdot R_{RPR}+FPDL_{RPR}\cdot L_{RPR}\right)RPRPC\cdot RPR`<br>[판독 기록:26](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/_staging/recovery/read/EFDC/manuals/pdfs/EFDC_Theory_Document_Ver_12.pdf/p199-216.md:26) | [Eutrophication/mod_rpem.f90:656](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/Eutrophication/mod_rpem.f90:656) `        SMPOP(L,1) = SMPOP(L,1) + DTDHWQ*(FPDRPR*WQRPRRP(L)+FPDLRPR*WQRPRLP(L) )` | 불일치 | 문서의 별도 퇴적층 DOP 대신 G1 SMPOP에 공급한다. |
| [Theory](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/manuals/pdfs/EFDC_Theory_Document_Ver_12.pdf) PDF 212쪽 / 인쇄 199쪽, 식 **8.182**<br>`\displaystyle \begin{aligned}\frac{\partial PO4t_W}{\partial t}&=\frac{1}{H}\left(FPI_{RPS}\cdot R_{RPS}+(1-F_{RPSD})\cdot FPIL_{RPS}\cdot L_{RPS}\right)\cdot RPSPC\cdot RPS\\&\quad+\frac{1}{H}\left(FPI_{RPE}\cdot R_{RPE}+FPIL_{RPE}\cdot L_{RPE}\right)\cdot RPEPC\cdot RPE\\&\quad+\frac{1}{H}FCIL_{RPD}\cdot L_{RPD}\cdot RPSPC\cdot RPD-\frac{1}{H}F_{RPSPW}\cdot R_{RPS}\cdot RPSPC\cdot RPS\\&\quad-\frac{1}{H}P_{RPE}\cdot RPEPC\cdot RPE\end{aligned}`<br>[판독 기록:26](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/_staging/recovery/read/EFDC/manuals/pdfs/EFDC_Theory_Document_Ver_12.pdf/p199-216.md:26) | [Eutrophication/mod_rpem.f90:479](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/Eutrophication/mod_rpem.f90:479) `                                      + FPIRPE*WQRPERP(L) + FPILRPE*WQRPELP(L) + FPILRPD*WQRPDLP(L) )`<br>[Eutrophication/mod_rpem.f90:518](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/Eutrophication/mod_rpem.f90:518) `        WQV(L,K,IP4D) = WQV(L,K,IP4D) - DTDHWQ*( RPEPC*PRPE(L)*WQRPE(L) + RPSPC*FRPSPW(L)*PRPS(L)*WQRPS(L) )  ! PO4T, EQ. COMPLETED` | 불일치 | 문서 지상부 흡수항은 RRPS를 사용한다. 코드는 생산 PRPS를 사용한다. 쇄설물 분율도 문서 FCIL 대신 FPIL이다. 코드는 저층 두께로 나눈다. |
| [Theory](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/manuals/pdfs/EFDC_Theory_Document_Ver_12.pdf) PDF 212쪽 / 인쇄 199쪽, 식 **8.183**<br>`\displaystyle \begin{aligned}\frac{\partial PO4t_B}{\partial t}&=\frac{1}{B}\left(FPI_{RPR}\cdot R_{RPR}+FPIL_{RPR}\cdot L_{RPR}\right)RPRPC\cdot RPR-\\&\quad\frac{1}{H}(1-F_{RPSPW})P_{RPS}\cdot RPRPC\cdot RPS\end{aligned}`<br>[판독 기록:26](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/_staging/recovery/read/EFDC/manuals/pdfs/EFDC_Theory_Document_Ver_12.pdf/p199-216.md:26) | [Eutrophication/mod_rpem.f90:633](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/Eutrophication/mod_rpem.f90:633) `        DTDHWQ = DTWQ/SMHSED(1)`<br>[Eutrophication/mod_rpem.f90:659](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/Eutrophication/mod_rpem.f90:659) `        SM2PO4(L) = SM2PO4(L) + DTDHWQ*(FPIRPR*WQRPRRP(L)+FPILRPR*WQRPRLP(L) )             ! EQ. (37B), INCOMPLETE`<br>[Eutrophication/mod_rpem.f90:684](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/Eutrophication/mod_rpem.f90:684) `        DTDHWQ = DTWQ/(DZC(L,K)*HWQ(L))`<br>[Eutrophication/mod_rpem.f90:685](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/Eutrophication/mod_rpem.f90:685) `        SM2PO4(L) = SM2PO4(L)-DTDHWQ*RPRPC*(1.-FRPSPW(L))*PRPS(L)*WQRPS(L)    ! EQ. (37B), COMPLETED` | 일치 | 뿌리 공급항은 퇴적층 두께로 나눈다. 흡수항은 문서와 같이 수주 반응 두께로 나눈다. 이 두께 혼용의 물리적 타당성을 이 판정으로 보증하지 않는다. |
| [Theory](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/manuals/pdfs/EFDC_Theory_Document_Ver_12.pdf) PDF 212쪽 / 인쇄 199쪽, 식 **8.184**<br>`\displaystyle F_{RPSPW}=\frac{KHP_{RPR}P04d_w}{KHP_{RPR}PO4d_w+KHP_{RPS}P04d_b}`<br>[판독 기록:26](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/_staging/recovery/read/EFDC/manuals/pdfs/EFDC_Theory_Document_Ver_12.pdf/p199-216.md:26) | [Eutrophication/mod_rpem.f90:508](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/Eutrophication/mod_rpem.f90:508) `        TMPWAT = RKHPRPR*WQV(L,K,10)                                    ! ??, BUT (38) MEANS PO4DW, DISSOLVED ONLY, NO`<br>[Eutrophication/mod_rpem.f90:509](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/Eutrophication/mod_rpem.f90:509) `        TMPBED = RKHPRPS*SM2PO4(L)                                                                                           `<br>[Eutrophication/mod_rpem.f90:510](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/Eutrophication/mod_rpem.f90:510) `        FRPSPW(L) = TMPWAT/(TMPWAT+TMPBED+1E-18)                        ! EQ. (38)` | 불일치 | 문서는 용존 수주 PO4d를 사용한다. 코드는 WQV(L,K,10)의 전체 인산염을 사용한다. 개발자 주석도 용존/전체 차이를 지적한다. |
| [Theory](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/manuals/pdfs/EFDC_Theory_Document_Ver_12.pdf) PDF 213쪽 / 인쇄 200쪽, 식 **8.185**<br>`\displaystyle \begin{aligned}\frac{\partial RPON_W}{\partial t}&=\frac{1}{H}\left(FNR_{RPS}\cdot R_{RPS}+(1-F_{RPSD})\cdot FNRL_{RPS}\cdot L_{RPS}\right)\cdot RPSNC\cdot RPS\\&\quad+\frac{1}{H}\left(FNR_{RPE}\cdot R_{RPE}+FNRL_{RPE}\cdot L_{RPE}\right)\cdot RPENC\cdot RPE\\&\quad+\frac{1}{H}FNRL_{RPD}\cdot L_{RPD}\cdot RPSNC\cdot RPD\end{aligned}`<br>[판독 기록:27](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/_staging/recovery/read/EFDC/manuals/pdfs/EFDC_Theory_Document_Ver_12.pdf/p199-216.md:27) | [Eutrophication/mod_rpem.f90:424](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/Eutrophication/mod_rpem.f90:424) `        WQRPSRN(L) = RPSNC*WQRPSR(L)                                    ! *** WQRPSRN - Shoot nitrogen biomass loss due to respiration`<br>[Eutrophication/mod_rpem.f90:425](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/Eutrophication/mod_rpem.f90:425) `        WQRPSLN(L) = RPSNC*WQRPSL(L)                                    ! *** WQRPSLN - Shoot nitrogen biomass loss due to non-respiration processes                                        `<br>[Eutrophication/mod_rpem.f90:483](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/Eutrophication/mod_rpem.f90:483) `                                      + FNRRPE*WQRPERN(L) + FNRLRPE*WQRPELN(L) + FNRLRPD*WQRPDLN(L) )`<br>[Eutrophication/mod_rpem.f90:1379](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/Eutrophication/mod_rpem.f90:1379) `  WQRPDLN  = 0` | 불일치 | 문서 쇄설물 N 공급에는 RPSNC·LRPD·RPD가 있다. 코드가 쓰는 WQRPDLN은 0으로 초기화한 뒤 갱신하지 않는다. 저층 두께도 전체 H와 다르다. |

EQ. (42B), COMPLETED` | 불일치 | 문서는 PNRPE·(1−FRPSPW)를 NH4 저면 흡수에 사용한다. 코드는 PNRPS·(1−FRPSNW)를 사용한다. P 선호 분율과 N 선호 분율이 다르다. |
| [Theory](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/manuals/pdfs/EFDC_Theory_Document_Ver_12.pdf) PDF 214쪽 / 인쇄 201쪽, 식 **8.193**<br>`\displaystyle \begin{aligned}\frac{\partial NO3_W}{\partial t}&=-\frac{1}{H}(1-PN_{RPS})F_{RPSNW}\cdot P_{RPS}\cdot RPSNC\cdot RPS\\&\quad-\frac{1}{H}(1-PN_{RPE})P_{RPE}\cdot RPENC\cdot RPE\end{aligned}`<br>[판독 기록:28](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/_staging/recovery/read/EFDC/manuals/pdfs/EFDC_Theory_Document_Ver_12.pdf/p199-216.md:28) | [Eutrophication/mod_rpem.f90:575](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/Eutrophication/mod_rpem.f90:575) `        WQV(L,K,INOX) = WQV(L,K,INOX) - DTDHWQ*(RPENC*TMPNO3E*PRPE(L)*WQRPE(L) + RPSNC*TMPNO3S*FRPSNW(L)*PRPS(L)*WQRPS(L))  ! NO3, EQ. COMPLETED` | 불일치 | 성장·NH4 선호도·수주 흡수 분율의 구조는 같다. 코드는 전체 H 대신 저층 두께를 사용한다. |
| [Theory](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/manuals/pdfs/EFDC_Theory_Document_Ver_12.pdf) PDF 214쪽 / 인쇄 201쪽, 식 **8.194**<br>`\displaystyle \frac{\partial NO3_B}{\partial t}=-\frac{1}{H}(1-PN_{RPS})(1-F_{RPSNW})P_{RPS}\cdot RPSNC\cdot RPS`<br>[판독 기록:28](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/_staging/recovery/read/EFDC/manuals/pdfs/EFDC_Theory_Document_Ver_12.pdf/p199-216.md:28) | [Eutrophication/mod_rpem.f90:693](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/Eutrophication/mod_rpem.f90:693) `        DTDHWQ    = DTWQ/(DZC(L,K)*HWQ(L))`<br>[Eutrophication/mod_rpem.f90:695](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/Eutrophication/mod_rpem.f90:695) `        TMPNO3S   = 1. - PNRPS(L)                                                                                                `<br>[Eutrophication/mod_rpem.f90:696](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/Eutrophication/mod_rpem.f90:696) `        TMPBED    = 1. - FRPSNW(L)                                                                                                `<br>[Eutrophication/mod_rpem.f90:698](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/Eutrophication/mod_rpem.f90:698) `        SM2NO3(L) = SM2NO3(L) - DTDHWQ*RPSNC*TMPNO3S*TMPBED*PRPS(L)*WQRPS(L)   ! EQ. (43B)` | 일치 | 지상부의 NO3 선호·저면 N 흡수 분율·생산량을 곱한다. 코드의 수주 반응 두께를 문서 H에 대응시킨다. |
| [Theory](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/manuals/pdfs/EFDC_Theory_Document_Ver_12.pdf) PDF 214쪽 / 인쇄 201쪽, 식 **8.195**<br>`\displaystyle PN_{RPS}=\frac{NH4\cdot NO3}{(KHNP_{RPS}+NH4)(KHNP_{RPS}+NO3)}+\frac{NH4\cdot KHNP_{RPS}}{(NH4+NO3)(KHNP_{RPS}+NO3)}`<br>[판독 기록:28](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/_staging/recovery/read/EFDC/manuals/pdfs/EFDC_Theory_Document_Ver_12.pdf/p199-216.md:28) | [Eutrophication/mod_rpem.f90:536](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/Eutrophication/mod_rpem.f90:536) `        TOP1 = (WQV(L,K,INHX) + SM2NH4(L))*(WQV(L,K,INOX) + SM2NO3(L))                ! WHY USE WATER COLUMN AND BED, BOTH OF THEM?`<br>[Eutrophication/mod_rpem.f90:537](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/Eutrophication/mod_rpem.f90:537) `        TOP2 = RKHNPRPS*(WQV(L,K,INHX) + SM2NH4(L))                                                                              `<br>[Eutrophication/mod_rpem.f90:538](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/Eutrophication/mod_rpem.f90:538) `        BOT1 = RKHNPRPS + (WQV(L,K,INHX) + SM2NH4(L))                                                                              `<br>[Eutrophication/mod_rpem.f90:540](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/Eutrophication/mod_rpem.f90:540) `        BOT3 = WQV(L,K,INHX) + SM2NH4(L) + (WQV(L,K,INOX) + SM2NO3(L))     `<br>[Eutrophication/mod_rpem.f90:544](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/Eutrophication/mod_rpem.f90:544) `        PNRPS(L) = TOP1/(BOT1*BOT2) + TOP2/(BOT2*BOT3)                           ! EQ. (44A)` | 불일치 | 코드는 수주와 퇴적층 NH4·NO3를 합한 값을 선호도에 넣는다. 문서의 단일 NH4·NO3 농도 정의를 그대로 사용하지 않는다. |
| [Theory](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/manuals/pdfs/EFDC_Theory_Document_Ver_12.pdf) PDF 214쪽 / 인쇄 201쪽, 식 **8.196**<br>`\displaystyle PN_{RPE}=\frac{NH4\cdot NO3}{(KHNP_{RPE}+NH4)(KHNP_{RPE}+NO3)}+\frac{NH4\cdot KHNP_{RPE}}{(NH4+NO3)(KHNP_{RPE}+NO3)}`<br>[판독 기록:28](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/_staging/recovery/read/EFDC/manuals/pdfs/EFDC_Theory_Document_Ver_12.pdf/p199-216.md:28) | [Eutrophication/mod_rpem.f90:553](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/Eutrophication/mod_rpem.f90:553) `          TOP1 = WQV(L,K,INHX)*WQV(L,K,INOX)                                                                                       `<br>[Eutrophication/mod_rpem.f90:554](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/Eutrophication/mod_rpem.f90:554) `          TOP2 = RKHNPRPE*WQV(L,K,INHX)                                                                                          `<br>[Eutrophication/mod_rpem.f90:561](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/Eutrophication/mod_rpem.f90:561) `          PNRPE(L) = TOP1/(BOT1*BOT2)+TOP2/(BOT2*BOT3)                    ! EQ. (44B)                                                                    ` | 일치 | 부착 조류 NH4 선호도에 수주 농도의 두 항을 사용한다. 분모를 1E−12 이상으로 제한한다. |
| [Theory](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/manuals/pdfs/EFDC_Theory_Document_Ver_12.pdf) PDF 214쪽 / 인쇄 201쪽, 식 **8.197**<br>`\displaystyle F_{RPSNW}=\frac{KHN_{RPR}(NH4+NO3)_w}{KHN_{RPR}(NH4+NO3)_w+KHN_{RPS}(NH4+NO3)_b}`<br>[판독 기록:28](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/_staging/recovery/read/EFDC/manuals/pdfs/EFDC_Theory_Document_Ver_12.pdf/p199-216.md:28) | [Eutrophication/mod_rpem.f90:527](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/Eutrophication/mod_rpem.f90:527) `        TMPWAT = RKHNRPR*(WQV(L,K,INHX) + WQV(L,K,INOX))`<br>[Eutrophication/mod_rpem.f90:528](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/Eutrophication/mod_rpem.f90:528) `        TMPBED = RKHNRPS*(SM2NH4(L)+SM2NO3(L))                                                                               `<br>[Eutrophication/mod_rpem.f90:529](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/Eutrophication/mod_rpem.f90:529) `        FRPSNW(L) = TMPWAT/(TMPWAT+TMPBED+1E-18)                              ! EQ. (45)` | 일치 | 반포화상수로 가중한 수주·퇴적층 DIN에서 수주 흡수 분율을 계산한다. |
