| [Theory](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/manuals/pdfs/EFDC_Theory_Document_Ver_12.pdf) PDF 217쪽 / 인쇄 204쪽, 식 **8.198**<br>`H=H_1+H_2\approx H_2`<br>[판독 기록:13](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/_staging/recovery/read/EFDC/manuals/pdfs/EFDC_Theory_Document_Ver_12.pdf/p217-234.md:13) | [Eutrophication/mod_diagen.f90:281](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/Eutrophication/mod_diagen.f90:281) `      call fson_get(item, "total_diagenesis_sediment_thickness", SMHSED(IZN))`<br>[Eutrophication/mod_diagen.f90:297](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/Eutrophication/mod_diagen.f90:297) `      SMDTOH(IZN)   = DTWQ/SMHSED(IZN)                           ! *** Assumes fixed DT`<br>[Eutrophication/mod_diagen.f90:303](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/Eutrophication/mod_diagen.f90:303) `      SM1DIFT(IZN)  = SMDIFT * SMDTOH(IZN)/(SMHSED(IZN)+ 1.E-18)` | 일치 | 코드는 총 활성 퇴적층 두께 SMHSED를 하층 두께로 사용한다. 산소성 상층을 정상상태로 풀어 H≈H2의 근사를 적용한다. |
| [Theory](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/manuals/pdfs/EFDC_Theory_Document_Ver_12.pdf) PDF 219쪽 / 인쇄 206쪽, 식 **8.199**<br>`J_{POC,i}=FCLP_i\cdot WS_{LP}\cdot LPOC^N+FCRP_i\cdot WS_{RP}\cdot RPOC^N+\sum_{algae}FCB_{x,i}\cdot WS_x\cdot B_x^N`<br>[판독 기록:15](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/_staging/recovery/read/EFDC/manuals/pdfs/EFDC_Theory_Document_Ver_12.pdf/p217-234.md:15) | [Eutrophication/mod_diagen.f90:775](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/Eutrophication/mod_diagen.f90:775) `          SMDFCA = SMDFCA + SMFCBA(NAL,M)*WQDFB(L,NAL)                     ! *** RPOC`<br>[Eutrophication/mod_diagen.f90:780](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/Eutrophication/mod_diagen.f90:780) `        SMDFC(L,M) = SMDFCA + SMFCR(ISMZMAP(L),M)*WQDFRC(L)                ! *** RPOC`<br>[Eutrophication/mod_diagen.f90:788](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/Eutrophication/mod_diagen.f90:788) `      SMDFC(L,1) = SMDFC(L,1) + WQDFLC(L)  ! *** LPOC` | 불일치 | 조류·난분해 POC의 G군 분배는 대응한다. 쉽게 분해되는 LPOC는 G1에만 공급한다. 문서의 일반 FCLPi 분배를 입력으로 사용하지 않는다. |
| [Theory](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/manuals/pdfs/EFDC_Theory_Document_Ver_12.pdf) PDF 219쪽 / 인쇄 206쪽, 식 **8.200**<br>`J_{PON,i}=FNLP_i\cdot WS_{LP}\cdot LPON^N+FNRP_i\cdot WS_{RP}\cdot RPON^N+\sum_{algae}FNB_{x,i}\cdot ANC_x\cdot WS_x\cdot B_x^N`<br>[판독 기록:15](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/_staging/recovery/read/EFDC/manuals/pdfs/EFDC_Theory_Document_Ver_12.pdf/p217-234.md:15) | [Eutrophication/mod_diagen.f90:773](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/Eutrophication/mod_diagen.f90:773) `          SMDFNA = SMDFNA + SMFNBA(NAL,M)*ALGAES(NAL).WQANCA*WQDFB(L,NAL)  ! *** RPON`<br>[Eutrophication/mod_diagen.f90:778](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/Eutrophication/mod_diagen.f90:778) `        SMDFN(L,M) = SMDFNA + SMFNR(ISMZMAP(L),M)*WQDFRN(L)                ! *** RPON`<br>[Eutrophication/mod_diagen.f90:786](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/Eutrophication/mod_diagen.f90:786) `      SMDFN(L,1) = SMDFN(L,1) + WQDFLN(L)  ! *** LPON` | 불일치 | 조류 N/C·난분해 PON의 G군 분배는 대응한다. LPON은 G1에만 공급한다. 문서의 일반 FNLPi 분배를 입력으로 사용하지 않는다. |
| [Theory](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/manuals/pdfs/EFDC_Theory_Document_Ver_12.pdf) PDF 219쪽 / 인쇄 206쪽, 식 **8.201**<br>`J_{POP,i}=FPLP_i\cdot WS_{LP}\cdot LPOP^N+FPRP_i\cdot WS_{RP}\cdot RPOP^N+\sum_{algae}FPB_{x,i}\cdot APC\cdot WS_x\cdot B_x^N+\gamma_i\cdot WS_{TSS}\cdot PO4_p^N`<br>[판독 기록:15](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/_staging/recovery/read/EFDC/manuals/pdfs/EFDC_Theory_Document_Ver_12.pdf/p217-234.md:15) | [Eutrophication/mod_wq.f90:4988](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/Eutrophication/mod_wq.f90:4988) `          WQDFLP(L) = SCB(L)*(WQDFLP(L) + WQWSS(IMWQZ)*( WQV(L,KSZ(L),IP4D) - WQPO4D(L,KSZ(L))) )           ! *** Add PO4 flux to LOP flux`<br>[Eutrophication/mod_diagen.f90:774](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/Eutrophication/mod_diagen.f90:774) `          SMDFPA = SMDFPA + SMFPBA(NAL,M)*WQAPC(L)*WQDFB(L,NAL)            ! *** RPOP`<br>[Eutrophication/mod_diagen.f90:779](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/Eutrophication/mod_diagen.f90:779) `        SMDFP(L,M) = SMDFPA + SMFPR(ISMZMAP(L),M)*WQDFRP(L)                ! *** RPOP`<br>[Eutrophication/mod_diagen.f90:787](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/Eutrophication/mod_diagen.f90:787) `      SMDFP(L,1) = SMDFP(L,1) + WQDFLP(L)  ! *** LPOP` | 불일치 | 흡착 인을 WQDFLP에 합친 뒤 G1에만 공급한다. 문서의 일반 γi 및 FPLPi 분배와 다르다. γ=(1,0,0)인 경우 흡착 인 분배가 대응한다. |
| [Theory](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/manuals/pdfs/EFDC_Theory_Document_Ver_12.pdf) PDF 219쪽 / 인쇄 206쪽, 식 **8.202**<br>`J_{PSi}=WS_d\cdot PSi^N+ASC_d\cdot WS_d\cdot B_d^N+WS_{TSS}\cdot SA_p^N`<br>[판독 기록:15](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/_staging/recovery/read/EFDC/manuals/pdfs/EFDC_Theory_Document_Ver_12.pdf/p217-234.md:15) | [Eutrophication/mod_wq.f90:4975](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/Eutrophication/mod_wq.f90:4975) `              WQDFSI(L) = WQDFSI(L) + SCB(L)*ALGAES(NAL).SETTLING(L,K)*WQV(L,K,ISUU)  `<br>[Eutrophication/mod_wq.f90:4989](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/Eutrophication/mod_wq.f90:4989) `          if( IWQSI == 1 ) WQDFSI(L) = (WQDFSI(L) + WQWSS(IMWQZ)*(WQV(L,KSZ(L),ISAA) - WQSAD(L,KSZ(L))))  `<br>[Eutrophication/mod_diagen.f90:1035](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/Eutrophication/mod_diagen.f90:1035) `            if( ALGAES(NAL).ISILICA > 0 ) SMDFSI(L) = SMDFSI(L) + (ALGAES(NAL).WQASC*WQDFB(L,NAL) + WQDFSI(L) + SMJDSI) * SMDTOH(ISMZMAP(L))` | 불일치 | 코드는 규소 사용 조류군을 반복하면서 전체 WQDFSI를 각 군에서 더한다. 규소군이 둘 이상이면 전체 입자 규소·흡착 규소 공급을 반복한다. 문서는 단일 WSd의 공급식이다. |
| [Theory](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/manuals/pdfs/EFDC_Theory_Document_Ver_12.pdf) PDF 220쪽 / 인쇄 207쪽, 식 **8.203**<br>`H_2\frac{\partial G_{POM,i}}{\partial t}=-K_{POM,i}\cdot\theta_{POM,i}^{T-20}\cdot G_{POM,i}\cdot H_2-W\cdot G_{POM,i}+J_{POM,i}`<br>[판독 기록:16](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/_staging/recovery/read/EFDC/manuals/pdfs/EFDC_Theory_Document_Ver_12.pdf/p217-234.md:16) | [Eutrophication/mod_diagen.f90:814](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/Eutrophication/mod_diagen.f90:814) `        SMPOC(L,M) = (SMPOC(L,M) + SMDFC(L,M)*SMDTOH(ISMZMAP(L))) / (SMW2DTOH(ISMZMAP(L)) + SMTDCD(ISMT(L),M)*DTWQ + 1.E-18)`<br>[Eutrophication/mod_diagen.f90:815](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/Eutrophication/mod_diagen.f90:815) `        SMPON(L,M) = (SMPON(L,M) + SMDFN(L,M)*SMDTOH(ISMZMAP(L))) / (SMW2DTOH(ISMZMAP(L)) + SMTDND(ISMT(L),M)*DTWQ + 1.E-18)`<br>[Eutrophication/mod_diagen.f90:816](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/Eutrophication/mod_diagen.f90:816) `        SMPOP(L,M) = (SMPOP(L,M) + SMDFP(L,M)*SMDTOH(ISMZMAP(L))) / (SMW2DTOH(ISMZMAP(L)) + SMTDPD(ISMT(L),M)*DTWQ + 1.E-18)` | 일치 | G군 POM에 퇴적 공급을 더하고 온도별 분해·매몰 소멸을 음해 분모로 처리한다. |
| [Theory](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/manuals/pdfs/EFDC_Theory_Document_Ver_12.pdf) PDF 220쪽 / 인쇄 207쪽, 식 **8.204**<br>`J_M=\sum_{i=1}^{2}K_{POM,i}\cdot\theta_{POM,i}^{T-20}\cdot G_{POM,i}\cdot H_2`<br>[판독 기록:16](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/_staging/recovery/read/EFDC/manuals/pdfs/EFDC_Theory_Document_Ver_12.pdf/p217-234.md:16) | [Eutrophication/mod_diagen.f90:822](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/Eutrophication/mod_diagen.f90:822) `      SMDGFN(L) = SMHSED(ISMZMAP(L)) * (SMTDND(ISMT(L),1)*SMPON(L,1)  + SMTDND(ISMT(L),2)*SMPON(L,2))`<br>[Eutrophication/mod_diagen.f90:823](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/Eutrophication/mod_diagen.f90:823) `      SMDGFP(L) = SMHSED(ISMZMAP(L)) * (SMTDPD(ISMT(L),1)*SMPOP(L,1)  + SMTDPD(ISMT(L),2)*SMPOP(L,2))`<br>[Eutrophication/mod_diagen.f90:824](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/Eutrophication/mod_diagen.f90:824) `      SMDGFC(L) = SMHSED(ISMZMAP(L)) * (SMTDCD(ISMT(L),1)*SMPOC(L,1)  + SMTDCD(ISMT(L),2)*SMPOC(L,2))` | 일치 | G1+G2의 분해속도·농도에 하층 두께를 곱해 C·N·P 플럭스를 구한다. |
| [Theory](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/manuals/pdfs/EFDC_Theory_Document_Ver_12.pdf) PDF 221쪽 / 인쇄 208쪽, 식 **8.205**<br>`H_1\frac{\partial Ct_1}{\partial t}=0=s(fd_0\cdot Ct_0-fd_1\cdot Ct_1)+KL(fd_2\cdot Ct_2-fd_1\cdot Ct_1)+\omega(fp_2\cdot Ct_2-fp_1\cdot Ct_1)-W\cdot Ct_1-\frac{K_1^2}{s}Ct_1+J_1`<br>[판독 기록:17](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/_staging/recovery/read/EFDC/manuals/pdfs/EFDC_Theory_Document_Ver_12.pdf/p217-234.md:17) | [Eutrophication/mod_diagen.f90:935](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/Eutrophication/mod_diagen.f90:935) `        A1NH4SM  = SMKL12(L)*SMFD1NH4 + SMW12(L)*SMFP1NH4 + SMW2(IZ)`<br>[Eutrophication/mod_diagen.f90:936](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/Eutrophication/mod_diagen.f90:936) `        A2NH4SM  = SMKL12(L)*SMFD2NH4 + SMW12(L)*SMFP2NH4`<br>[Eutrophication/mod_diagen.f90:1179](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/Eutrophication/mod_diagen.f90:1179) `  A11NH4 = RSMSS*SMFD1NH4 + A1NH4SM + RRNH4`<br>[Eutrophication/mod_diagen.f90:1183](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/Eutrophication/mod_diagen.f90:1183) `  call SOLVSMBE(RSM1NH4,RSM2NH4,A11NH4,A22NH4SM,A1NH4SM,A2NH4SM,B11NH4,B22NH4)` | 일치 | 상층 저장항 0의 두 층 전달·매몰·반응 계수를 선형계에 넣는다. 일반식 구조가 대응한다. |
| [Theory](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/manuals/pdfs/EFDC_Theory_Document_Ver_12.pdf) PDF 222쪽 / 인쇄 209쪽, 식 **8.206**<br>`J_{aq}=s(fd_1\cdot Ct_1-fd_0\cdot Ct_0)`<br>[판독 기록:18](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/_staging/recovery/read/EFDC/manuals/pdfs/EFDC_Theory_Document_Ver_12.pdf/p217-234.md:18) | [Eutrophication/mod_diagen.f90:996](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/Eutrophication/mod_diagen.f90:996) `        WQBFNH4(L)  = SMSS(L) * (SMFD1NH4*SM1NH4(L) - WQV(L,KSZ(L),INHX))   ! *** Ammonium flux  (g NH4/m2/day)`<br>[Eutrophication/mod_diagen.f90:997](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/Eutrophication/mod_diagen.f90:997) `        WQBFNO3(L)  = SMSS(L) * (SM1NO3(L) - WQV(L,KSZ(L),INOX))            ! *** Nitrate flux   (g NO3/m2/day)`<br>[Eutrophication/mod_diagen.f90:1023](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/Eutrophication/mod_diagen.f90:1023) `        WQBFPO4D(L) = SMSS(L) * (SMD1PO4(L) - WQPO4D(L,KSZ(L)))`<br>[Eutrophication/mod_diagen.f90:1058](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/Eutrophication/mod_diagen.f90:1058) `          WQBFSAD(L) = SMSS(L) * (SMD1SI(L) - WQSAD(L,KSZ(L)))` | 일치 | 수주로 양인 s·(상층 용존 농도−수주 용존 농도)를 계산한다. |
| [Theory](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/manuals/pdfs/EFDC_Theory_Document_Ver_12.pdf) PDF 222쪽 / 인쇄 209쪽, 식 **8.207**<br>`H_2\frac{\partial Ct_2}{\partial t}=-KL(fd_2\cdot Ct_2-fd_1\cdot Ct_1)-\omega(fp_2\cdot Ct_2-fp_1\cdot Ct_1)+W(Ct_1-Ct_2)-K_2\cdot Ct_2+J_2`<br>[판독 기록:18](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/_staging/recovery/read/EFDC/manuals/pdfs/EFDC_Theory_Document_Ver_12.pdf/p217-234.md:18) | [Eutrophication/mod_diagen.f90:937](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/Eutrophication/mod_diagen.f90:937) `        A22NH4SM = A2NH4SM + SMW2PHODT(IZ)`<br>[Eutrophication/mod_diagen.f90:939](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/Eutrophication/mod_diagen.f90:939) `        B2NH4SM  = SMDGFN(L) + SMHODT(IZ)*SM2NH4(L)`<br>[Eutrophication/mod_diagen.f90:1016](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/Eutrophication/mod_diagen.f90:1016) `        A22PO4SM = A2PO4SM + SMW2PHODT(ISMZMAP(L))`<br>[Eutrophication/mod_diagen.f90:1018](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/Eutrophication/mod_diagen.f90:1018) `        B22PO4SM = SMDGFP(L) + SMHODT(ISMZMAP(L))*SM2PO4(L)` | 일치 | 하층 저장·혼합·매몰·반응·생성항을 음해 계수와 우변에 넣는다. |
| [Theory](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/manuals/pdfs/EFDC_Theory_Document_Ver_12.pdf) PDF 222쪽 / 인쇄 209쪽, 식 **8.208**<br>`fd_1=\frac{1}{1+m_1\cdot\pi_1}\qquad fp_1=1-fd_1`<br>[판독 기록:18](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/_staging/recovery/read/EFDC/manuals/pdfs/EFDC_Theory_Document_Ver_12.pdf/p217-234.md:18) | [Eutrophication/mod_diagen.f90:259](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/Eutrophication/mod_diagen.f90:259) `    SMFD1NH4 = 1.0 / (1.0 + SMM1*SMP1NH4)`<br>[Eutrophication/mod_diagen.f90:260](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/Eutrophication/mod_diagen.f90:260) `    SMFP1NH4 = 1.0 - SMFD1NH4`<br>[Eutrophication/mod_diagen.f90:267](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/Eutrophication/mod_diagen.f90:267) `    SMFD1H2S = 1.0 / (1.0 + SMM1*SMP1H2S)`<br>[Eutrophication/mod_diagen.f90:268](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/Eutrophication/mod_diagen.f90:268) `    SMFP1H2S = 1.0 - SMFD1H2S`<br>[Eutrophication/mod_diagen.f90:1011](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/Eutrophication/mod_diagen.f90:1011) `        SMFD1PO4 = 1.0 / (1.0 + SMM1*SMP1PO4)`<br>[Eutrophication/mod_diagen.f90:1012](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/Eutrophication/mod_diagen.f90:1012) `        SMFP1PO4 = 1.0 - SMFD1PO4` | 일치 | 상층의 용존 분율 1/(1+m1·π1)과 입자 분율 1−fd1을 사용한다. |
| [Theory](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/manuals/pdfs/EFDC_Theory_Document_Ver_12.pdf) PDF 222쪽 / 인쇄 209쪽, 식 **8.209**<br>`fd_2=\frac{1}{1+m_2\cdot\pi_2}\qquad fp_2=1-fd_2`<br>[판독 기록:18](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/_staging/recovery/read/EFDC/manuals/pdfs/EFDC_Theory_Document_Ver_12.pdf/p217-234.md:18) | [Eutrophication/mod_diagen.f90:261](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/Eutrophication/mod_diagen.f90:261) `    SMFD2NH4 = 1.0 / (1.0 + SMM2*SMP2NH4)`<br>[Eutrophication/mod_diagen.f90:262](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/Eutrophication/mod_diagen.f90:262) `    SMFP2NH4 = 1.0 - SMFD2NH4`<br>[Eutrophication/mod_diagen.f90:269](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/Eutrophication/mod_diagen.f90:269) `    SMFD2H2S = 1.0 / (1.0 + SMM2*SMP2H2S)`<br>[Eutrophication/mod_diagen.f90:270](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/Eutrophication/mod_diagen.f90:270) `    SMFP2H2S = 1.0 - SMFD2H2S`<br>[Eutrophication/mod_diagen.f90:273](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/Eutrophication/mod_diagen.f90:273) `    SMFD2SI = 1.0 / (1.0 + SMM2*SMP2SI)`<br>[Eutrophication/mod_diagen.f90:274](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/Eutrophication/mod_diagen.f90:274) `    SMFP2SI = 1.0 - SMFD2SI` | 일치 | 하층의 용존·입자 분율을 같은 형태로 계산한다. |
| [Theory](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/manuals/pdfs/EFDC_Theory_Document_Ver_12.pdf) PDF 223쪽 / 인쇄 210쪽, 식 **8.210**<br>`s=\frac{D_1}{H_1}=\frac{SOD}{DO_0}`<br>[판독 기록:19](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/_staging/recovery/read/EFDC/manuals/pdfs/EFDC_Theory_Document_Ver_12.pdf/p217-234.md:19) | [Eutrophication/mod_diagen.f90:828](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/Eutrophication/mod_diagen.f90:828) `      XSMO20(L) = max( WQV(L,KSZ(L),IDOX), 3.0 )`<br>[Eutrophication/mod_diagen.f90:1175](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/Eutrophication/mod_diagen.f90:1175) `  RSMSS = SMSOD1 / (XSMO20(L)+ 1.E-18)` | 불일치 | s=SOD/DO의 폐합 구조는 같다. 코드는 실제 DO 대신 MAX(DO,3.0 g O2/m³)를 분모로 사용한다. DO<3이면 문서 정의와 다르다. |
| [Theory](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/manuals/pdfs/EFDC_Theory_Document_Ver_12.pdf) PDF 223쪽 / 인쇄 210쪽, 식 **8.211**<br>`\omega=\frac{D_p\cdot\theta_{D_p}^{T-20}}{H_2}\frac{G_{POC,1}}{G_{POC,R}}\frac{DO_0}{KM_{D_p}+DO_0}`<br>[판독 기록:19](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/_staging/recovery/read/EFDC/manuals/pdfs/EFDC_Theory_Document_Ver_12.pdf/p217-234.md:19) | [Eutrophication/mod_diagen.f90:299](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/Eutrophication/mod_diagen.f90:299) `      SMDP(IZN)     = SMDP(IZN) / (SMHSED(IZN)*SMPOCR+ 1.E-18)`<br>[Eutrophication/mod_diagen.f90:828](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/Eutrophication/mod_diagen.f90:828) `      XSMO20(L) = max( WQV(L,KSZ(L),IDOX), 3.0 )`<br>[Eutrophication/mod_diagen.f90:885](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/Eutrophication/mod_diagen.f90:885) `      SMW12(L)  = SMDP(IZ)*SMTDDP(ISMT(L)) * SMPOC(L,1) * XSMO20(L) * (1.0-SMKBST*SMBST(L)) / (SMKMDP+XSMO20(L)+ 1.E-18) + SMDPMIN(IZ)` | 불일치 | 기본 입자 혼합 함수에 스트레스 보정과 최소 혼합을 더한다. 코드의 DO는 3.0 g O2/m³ 이상으로 제한한다. 스트레스 0·최소 혼합 0·DO≥3 조건에서 문서 기본식과 같다. |
| [Theory](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/manuals/pdfs/EFDC_Theory_Document_Ver_12.pdf) PDF 224쪽 / 인쇄 211쪽, 식 **8.212**<br>`\frac{\partial ST}{\partial t}=\begin{cases}-K_{ST}\cdot ST+\left(1-\frac{DO_0}{KM_{D_p}}\right),&\text{if }DO_0<KM_{D_p}\\-K_{ST}\cdot ST,&\text{if }DO_0>KM_{D_p}\end{cases}`<br>[판독 기록:20](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/_staging/recovery/read/EFDC/manuals/pdfs/EFDC_Theory_Document_Ver_12.pdf/p217-234.md:20) | [Eutrophication/mod_diagen.f90:828](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/Eutrophication/mod_diagen.f90:828) `      XSMO20(L) = max( WQV(L,KSZ(L),IDOX), 3.0 )`<br>[Eutrophication/mod_diagen.f90:832](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/Eutrophication/mod_diagen.f90:832) `        SMBST(L)  = (SMTMP(L) +DTWQ*(1.0-XSMO20(L)*SM1OKMDP)) * SMBST1`<br>[Eutrophication/mod_diagen.f90:834](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/Eutrophication/mod_diagen.f90:834) `        SMBST(L) = SMBST(L)*SMBST1` | 불일치 | 스트레스의 생성·감쇠 구조는 같다. 코드는 DO를 3.0 g O2/m³ 이상으로 제한한 뒤 저산소 조건을 판단한다. |
| [Theory](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/manuals/pdfs/EFDC_Theory_Document_Ver_12.pdf) PDF 224쪽 / 인쇄 211쪽, 식 **8.213**<br>`\omega=\frac{D_p\cdot\theta_{D_p}^{T-20}}{H_2}\frac{G_{POC,1}}{G_{POC,R}}\frac{DO_0}{KM_{D_p}+DO_0}f(ST)+\frac{D_{p_{min}}}{H_2}`<br>[판독 기록:20](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/_staging/recovery/read/EFDC/manuals/pdfs/EFDC_Theory_Document_Ver_12.pdf/p217-234.md:20) | [Eutrophication/mod_diagen.f90:299](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/Eutrophication/mod_diagen.f90:299) `      SMDP(IZN)     = SMDP(IZN) / (SMHSED(IZN)*SMPOCR+ 1.E-18)`<br>[Eutrophication/mod_diagen.f90:307](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/Eutrophication/mod_diagen.f90:307) `      SMDPMIN(IZN)  = XSMDPMIN / (SMHSED(IZN)+ 1.E-18)`<br>[Eutrophication/mod_diagen.f90:828](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/Eutrophication/mod_diagen.f90:828) `      XSMO20(L) = max( WQV(L,KSZ(L),IDOX), 3.0 )`<br>[Eutrophication/mod_diagen.f90:885](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/Eutrophication/mod_diagen.f90:885) `      SMW12(L)  = SMDP(IZ)*SMTDDP(ISMT(L)) * SMPOC(L,1) * XSMO20(L) * (1.0-SMKBST*SMBST(L)) / (SMKMDP+XSMO20(L)+ 1.E-18) + SMDPMIN(IZ)` | 불일치 | 문서 본문의 f(ST)=1−KST·ST와 최소 혼합항을 사용한다. 실제 DO 대신 MAX(DO,3)을 넣는 차이가 남는다. |
| [Theory](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/manuals/pdfs/EFDC_Theory_Document_Ver_12.pdf) PDF 226쪽 / 인쇄 213쪽, 식 **8.214**<br>`KL=\frac{D_d\cdot\theta_{D_d}^{T-20}}{H_2}+R_{BI,BT}\cdot\omega`<br>[판독 기록:22](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/_staging/recovery/read/EFDC/manuals/pdfs/EFDC_Theory_Document_Ver_12.pdf/p217-234.md:22) | [Eutrophication/mod_diagen.f90:300](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/Eutrophication/mod_diagen.f90:300) `      SMDD(IZN)     = SMDD(IZN) / (SMHSED(IZN)+ 1.E-18)`<br>[Eutrophication/mod_diagen.f90:886](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/Eutrophication/mod_diagen.f90:886) `      SMKL12(L) = SMDD(IZ)*SMTDDD(ISMT(L)) + SMRBIBT*SMW12(L)` | 일치 | Dd/H2 온도 보정에 생물관개/교란 비·입자 혼합속도를 더한다. |
| [Theory](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/manuals/pdfs/EFDC_Theory_Document_Ver_12.pdf) PDF 226쪽 / 인쇄 213쪽, 식 **8.215**<br>`J_{1,NH4}=0\qquad J_{2,NH4}=J_N`<br>[판독 기록:22](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/_staging/recovery/read/EFDC/manuals/pdfs/EFDC_Theory_Document_Ver_12.pdf/p217-234.md:22) | [Eutrophication/mod_diagen.f90:938](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/Eutrophication/mod_diagen.f90:938) `        B1NH4SM  = WQV(L,KSZ(L),INHX)`<br>[Eutrophication/mod_diagen.f90:939](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/Eutrophication/mod_diagen.f90:939) `        B2NH4SM  = SMDGFN(L) + SMHODT(IZ)*SM2NH4(L)`<br>[Eutrophication/mod_diagen.f90:1180](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/Eutrophication/mod_diagen.f90:1180) `  B11NH4 = RSMSS*B1NH4SM`<br>[Eutrophication/mod_diagen.f90:1181](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/Eutrophication/mod_diagen.f90:1181) `  B22NH4 = B2NH4SM` | 일치 | 상층의 별도 diagenesis 생성은 없다. 하층 NH4에는 JN을 더한다. 상층 우변은 수주 경계 공급이다. |
| [Theory](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/manuals/pdfs/EFDC_Theory_Document_Ver_12.pdf) PDF 226쪽 / 인쇄 213쪽, 식 **8.216**<br>`K_{1,NH4}^{2}=\frac{DO_0}{2\cdot KM_{NH4,O2}+DO_0}\frac{KM_{NH4}}{KM_{NH4}+NH4_1}K_{NH4}^{2}\cdot\theta_{NH4}^{T-20}`<br>[판독 기록:22](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/_staging/recovery/read/EFDC/manuals/pdfs/EFDC_Theory_Document_Ver_12.pdf/p217-234.md:22) | [Eutrophication/mod_diagen.f90:263](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/Eutrophication/mod_diagen.f90:263) `    SMKMO2N  = SMKMO2N * 2.0`<br>[Eutrophication/mod_diagen.f90:301](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/Eutrophication/mod_diagen.f90:301) `      SMKNH4(IZN)   = SMKNH4(IZN)*SMKNH4(IZN) * SMKMNH4`<br>[Eutrophication/mod_diagen.f90:828](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/Eutrophication/mod_diagen.f90:828) `      XSMO20(L) = max( WQV(L,KSZ(L),IDOX), 3.0 )`<br>[Eutrophication/mod_diagen.f90:934](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/Eutrophication/mod_diagen.f90:934) `        SK1NH4SM = ( SMKNH4(IZ)*SMTDNH4(ISMT(L)) * XSMO20(L) ) / ( (SMKMO2N+XSMO20(L)+ 1.E-12) * (SMKMNH4+SM1NH4(L)) )` | 불일치 | 입력 O2 반포화상수 2배 및 KNH4²·KMNH4 선변환을 고려하면 함수 형태가 같다. DO<3에서 실제 DO를 쓰지 않는다. NH4 제한 계수에는 이전 상층 농도를 사용한다. |
| [Theory](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/manuals/pdfs/EFDC_Theory_Document_Ver_12.pdf) PDF 226쪽 / 인쇄 213쪽, 식 **8.217**<br>`J_{Nit}=\frac{K_{1,NH4}^{2}}{s}\cdot NH4_1`<br>[판독 기록:22](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/_staging/recovery/read/EFDC/manuals/pdfs/EFDC_Theory_Document_Ver_12.pdf/p217-234.md:22) | [Eutrophication/mod_diagen.f90:1178](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/Eutrophication/mod_diagen.f90:1178) `  RRNH4 = SK1NH4SM/(RSMSS+ 1.E-18)`<br>[Eutrophication/mod_diagen.f90:1185](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/Eutrophication/mod_diagen.f90:1185) `  RJNITSM = RRNH4 * RSM1NH4` | 일치 | KNH4²/s에 새 상층 NH4 농도를 곱해 질산화 플럭스를 계산한다. |
| [Theory](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/manuals/pdfs/EFDC_Theory_Document_Ver_12.pdf) PDF 226쪽 / 인쇄 213쪽, 식 **8.218**<br>`K_{2,NH4}=0`<br>[판독 기록:22](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/_staging/recovery/read/EFDC/manuals/pdfs/EFDC_Theory_Document_Ver_12.pdf/p217-234.md:22) | [Eutrophication/mod_diagen.f90:937](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/Eutrophication/mod_diagen.f90:937) `        A22NH4SM = A2NH4SM + SMW2PHODT(IZ)` | 일치 | 하층 NH4의 반응 소멸계수는 0이다. 하층 분모에는 혼합·매몰·시간 저장만 있다. |
| [Theory](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/manuals/pdfs/EFDC_Theory_Document_Ver_12.pdf) PDF 227쪽 / 인쇄 214쪽, 식 **8.219**<br>`\begin{aligned}J_{1,NO3}&=J_{Nit}\\J_{2,NO3}&=0\end{aligned}`<br>[판독 기록:23](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/_staging/recovery/read/EFDC/manuals/pdfs/EFDC_Theory_Document_Ver_12.pdf/p217-234.md:23) | [Eutrophication/mod_diagen.f90:948](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/Eutrophication/mod_diagen.f90:948) `        B2NO3SM  = SMHODT(IZ)*SM2NO3(L)`<br>[Eutrophication/mod_diagen.f90:1190](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/Eutrophication/mod_diagen.f90:1190) `  B11NO3 = RJNITSM + RSMSS*B1NO3SM`<br>[Eutrophication/mod_diagen.f90:1191](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/Eutrophication/mod_diagen.f90:1191) `  B22NO3 = B2NO3SM` | 일치 | 상층 NO3 생성은 질산화 플럭스이다. 하층 NO3에는 별도 diagenesis 생성항이 없다. |
| [Theory](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/manuals/pdfs/EFDC_Theory_Document_Ver_12.pdf) PDF 227쪽 / 인쇄 214쪽, 식 **8.220**<br>`K_{1,NO3}^{2}=K_{NO3,1}^{2}\cdot\theta_{NO3}^{T-20}`<br>[판독 기록:23](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/_staging/recovery/read/EFDC/manuals/pdfs/EFDC_Theory_Document_Ver_12.pdf/p217-234.md:23) | [Eutrophication/mod_diagen.f90:302](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/Eutrophication/mod_diagen.f90:302) `      SMK1NO3(IZN)  = SMK1NO3(IZN)*SMK1NO3(IZN)`<br>[Eutrophication/mod_diagen.f90:942](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/Eutrophication/mod_diagen.f90:942) `        SK1NO3SM = SMK1NO3(IZ)*SMTDNO3(ISMT(L))` | 일치 | 상층 탈질 속도를 입력 때 제곱한 뒤 온도계수를 곱한다. |
| [Theory](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/manuals/pdfs/EFDC_Theory_Document_Ver_12.pdf) PDF 227쪽 / 인쇄 214쪽, 식 **8.221**<br>`K_{2,NO3}=K_{NO3,2}^{2}\cdot\theta_{NO3}^{T-20}`<br>[판독 기록:23](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/_staging/recovery/read/EFDC/manuals/pdfs/EFDC_Theory_Document_Ver_12.pdf/p217-234.md:23) | [Eutrophication/mod_diagen.f90:294](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/Eutrophication/mod_diagen.f90:294) `      call fson_get(item, "layer_2.reaction_velocity_for_denitrification_at_20_degC", SMK2NO3(IZN))`<br>[Eutrophication/mod_diagen.f90:945](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/Eutrophication/mod_diagen.f90:945) `        RK2NO3SM = SMK2NO3(IZ)*SMTDNO3(ISMT(L))` | 불일치 | 문서가 하층 입력 속도 KNO3,2를 제곱한다. 코드는 SMK2NO3를 제곱하지 않고 온도계수만 곱한다. PDF p.227 이미지에서 문서의 제곱을 확인했다. |
| [Theory](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/manuals/pdfs/EFDC_Theory_Document_Ver_12.pdf) PDF 227쪽 / 인쇄 214쪽, 식 **8.222**<br>`J_{N2(g)}=\frac{K_{1,NO3}^{2}}{s}NO3_1+K_{2,NO3}\cdot NO3_2`<br>[판독 기록:23](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/_staging/recovery/read/EFDC/manuals/pdfs/EFDC_Theory_Document_Ver_12.pdf/p217-234.md:23) | [Eutrophication/mod_diagen.f90:1188](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/Eutrophication/mod_diagen.f90:1188) `  RRNO3 = SK1NO3SM/(RSMSS+ 1.E-18)`<br>[Eutrophication/mod_diagen.f90:1195](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/Eutrophication/mod_diagen.f90:1195) `  RJDENSM = RRNO3*RSM1NO3 + RK2NO3SM*RSM2NO3` | 일치 | 상층·하층의 계산된 탈질 플럭스를 합한다. 하층 계수 정의의 차이는 식 8.221에서 판정한다. |
| [Theory](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/manuals/pdfs/EFDC_Theory_Document_Ver_12.pdf) PDF 228쪽 / 인쇄 215쪽, 식 **8.223**<br>`\begin{aligned}J_{1,PO4}&=0\\J_{2,PO4}&=J_P\end{aligned}`<br>[판독 기록:24](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/_staging/recovery/read/EFDC/manuals/pdfs/EFDC_Theory_Document_Ver_12.pdf/p217-234.md:24) | [Eutrophication/mod_diagen.f90:1017](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/Eutrophication/mod_diagen.f90:1017) `        B11PO4SM = SMSS(L) * WQPO4D(L,KSZ(L))`<br>[Eutrophication/mod_diagen.f90:1018](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/Eutrophication/mod_diagen.f90:1018) `        B22PO4SM = SMDGFP(L) + SMHODT(ISMZMAP(L))*SM2PO4(L)` | 일치 | 상층 인산염에 별도 생성항이 없다. 하층 생성항은 POP diagenesis JP이다. |
| [Theory](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/manuals/pdfs/EFDC_Theory_Document_Ver_12.pdf) PDF 228쪽 / 인쇄 215쪽, 식 **8.224**<br>`\pi_{1,PO4}=\pi_{2,PO4}\cdot(\Delta\pi_{PO4,1})\qquad DO_0>(DO_0)_{crit,PO4}`<br>[판독 기록:24](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/_staging/recovery/read/EFDC/manuals/pdfs/EFDC_Theory_Document_Ver_12.pdf/p217-234.md:24) | [Eutrophication/mod_diagen.f90:828](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/Eutrophication/mod_diagen.f90:828) `      XSMO20(L) = max( WQV(L,KSZ(L),IDOX), 3.0 )`<br>[Eutrophication/mod_diagen.f90:1006](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/Eutrophication/mod_diagen.f90:1006) `        if( XSMO20(L) < SMCO2PO4 )then`<br>[Eutrophication/mod_diagen.f90:1009](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/Eutrophication/mod_diagen.f90:1009) `          SMP1PO4 = SMP2PO4 * SMDP1PO4(ISMZMAP(L))` | 불일치 | 임계 DO 이상에서 분배계수 배수는 대응한다. 임계 분기 판단에 실제 DO 대신 MAX(DO,3)을 사용한다. |
| [Theory](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/manuals/pdfs/EFDC_Theory_Document_Ver_12.pdf) PDF 228쪽 / 인쇄 215쪽, 식 **8.225**<br>`\pi_{1,PO4}=\pi_{2,PO4}\cdot(\Delta\pi_{PO4,1})^{\frac{DO_0}{(DO_0)_{crit,PO4}}}\qquad DO_0\le(DO_0)_{crit,PO4}`<br>[판독 기록:24](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/_staging/recovery/read/EFDC/manuals/pdfs/EFDC_Theory_Document_Ver_12.pdf/p217-234.md:24) | [Eutrophication/mod_diagen.f90:828](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/Eutrophication/mod_diagen.f90:828) `      XSMO20(L) = max( WQV(L,KSZ(L),IDOX), 3.0 )`<br>[Eutrophication/mod_diagen.f90:1007](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/Eutrophication/mod_diagen.f90:1007) `          SMP1PO4 = SMP2PO4 * SMDP1PO4(ISMZMAP(L))**(XSMO20(L)/(SMCO2PO4+ 1.E-18))` | 불일치 | DO 의존 거듭제곱 형태는 같다. 코드는 MAX(DO,3)을 지수에 사용한다. DO→0에서 문서의 π1→π2 극한을 일반적으로 재현하지 않는다. |
| [Theory](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/manuals/pdfs/EFDC_Theory_Document_Ver_12.pdf) PDF 228쪽 / 인쇄 215쪽, 식 **8.226**<br>`\kappa_{1,PO4}=\kappa_{2,PO4}=0`<br>[판독 기록:24](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/_staging/recovery/read/EFDC/manuals/pdfs/EFDC_Theory_Document_Ver_12.pdf/p217-234.md:24) | [Eutrophication/mod_diagen.f90:1015](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/Eutrophication/mod_diagen.f90:1015) `        A11PO4SM = SMSS(L)*SMFD1PO4 + A1PO4SM`<br>[Eutrophication/mod_diagen.f90:1016](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/Eutrophication/mod_diagen.f90:1016) `        A22PO4SM = A2PO4SM + SMW2PHODT(ISMZMAP(L))` | 일치 | 양층 PO4 계수에 별도 화학반응 소멸항이 없다. |
| [Theory](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/manuals/pdfs/EFDC_Theory_Document_Ver_12.pdf) PDF 229쪽 / 인쇄 216쪽, 식 **8.227**<br>`\begin{aligned}J_{1,H2S}&=0\\J_{2,H2S}&=a_{O2,C}\cdot J_C-a_{O2,NO3}\cdot J_{N2(g)}\end{aligned}`<br>[판독 기록:25](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/_staging/recovery/read/EFDC/manuals/pdfs/EFDC_Theory_Document_Ver_12.pdf/p217-234.md:25) | [Eutrophication/mod_diagen.f90:958](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/Eutrophication/mod_diagen.f90:958) `          B1H2SSM  = 0.0`<br>[Eutrophication/mod_diagen.f90:951](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/Eutrophication/mod_diagen.f90:951) `        SMO2JC = SMO2C*SMDGFC(L)`<br>[Eutrophication/mod_diagen.f90:1202](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/Eutrophication/mod_diagen.f90:1202) `  SMJ2H2S = max(SMO2JC - SMO2NO3*RJDENSM, 0.0)`<br>[Eutrophication/mod_diagen.f90:1208](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/Eutrophication/mod_diagen.f90:1208) `    B22H2S = B2H2SSM + SMJ2H2S` | 불일치 | 하층 탄소 생성에서 탈질 산소당량을 뺀다. 코드는 결과를 MAX(...,0)로 제한한다. 문서 식에는 비음수 제한이 없다. |
| [Theory](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/manuals/pdfs/EFDC_Theory_Document_Ver_12.pdf) PDF 229쪽 / 인쇄 216쪽, 식 **8.228**<br>`K_{1,H2S}^{2}=\left(K_{H2S,d1}^{2}\cdot fd_{1,H2S}+K_{H2S,p1}^{2}\cdot fp_{1,H2S}\right)\theta_{H2S}^{T-20}\frac{DO_0}{2\cdot KM_{H2S,O2}}`<br>[판독 기록:25](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/_staging/recovery/read/EFDC/manuals/pdfs/EFDC_Theory_Document_Ver_12.pdf/p217-234.md:25) | [Eutrophication/mod_diagen.f90:271](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/Eutrophication/mod_diagen.f90:271) `    XSMK1H2S = (SMKD1HS*SMKD1HS*SMFD1H2S + SMKP1HS*SMKP1HS*SMFP1H2S) / (2.0*SMKMH2S)`<br>[Eutrophication/mod_diagen.f90:491](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/Eutrophication/mod_diagen.f90:491) `    SMK1H2S(IT)  = XSMK1H2S * SMTHH2S**TT20`<br>[Eutrophication/mod_diagen.f90:828](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/Eutrophication/mod_diagen.f90:828) `      XSMO20(L) = max( WQV(L,KSZ(L),IDOX), 3.0 )`<br>[Eutrophication/mod_diagen.f90:954](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/Eutrophication/mod_diagen.f90:954) `          SK1H2SSM = SMK1H2S(ISMT(L)) * XSMO20(L)` | 불일치 | 용존·입자 반응속도의 제곱 분율과 2·KMO2 분모는 대응한다. 산소 인자에는 실제 DO 대신 MAX(DO,3)을 사용한다. |
| [Theory](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/manuals/pdfs/EFDC_Theory_Document_Ver_12.pdf) PDF 229쪽 / 인쇄 216쪽, 식 **8.229**<br>`K_{2,H2S}^{2}=0`<br>[판독 기록:25](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/_staging/recovery/read/EFDC/manuals/pdfs/EFDC_Theory_Document_Ver_12.pdf/p217-234.md:25) | [Eutrophication/mod_diagen.f90:957](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/Eutrophication/mod_diagen.f90:957) `          A22H2SSM = A2H2SSM + SMW2PHODT(IZ)` | 일치 | 하층 황화물에 별도 반응 소멸계수는 없다. |
| [Theory](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/manuals/pdfs/EFDC_Theory_Document_Ver_12.pdf) PDF 230쪽 / 인쇄 217쪽, 식 **8.230**<br>`SOD=CSOD+NSOD=\frac{K_{1,H2S}^{2}}{s}H2S_1+a_{O2,NH4}\cdot J_{Nit}`<br>[판독 기록:26](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/_staging/recovery/read/EFDC/manuals/pdfs/EFDC_Theory_Document_Ver_12.pdf/p217-234.md:26) | [Eutrophication/mod_diagen.f90:1213](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/Eutrophication/mod_diagen.f90:1213) `    CSODSM = RRH2S*RSM1H2S`<br>[Eutrophication/mod_diagen.f90:1230](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/Eutrophication/mod_diagen.f90:1230) `  RNSODSM = SMO2NH4*RJNITSM`<br>[Eutrophication/mod_diagen.f90:1231](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/Eutrophication/mod_diagen.f90:1231) `  SMSOD = CSODSM + RNSODSM` | 일치 | 탄소성 SOD와 NH4 질산화의 질소성 SOD를 합한다. 수주 전달 시 부호를 음으로 바꾸고 SODMULT를 곱한다. |
| [Theory](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/manuals/pdfs/EFDC_Theory_Document_Ver_12.pdf) PDF 230쪽 / 인쇄 217쪽, 식 **8.231**<br>`J_{aq,H2S}=s(fd_{1,H2S}\cdot H2S_1-COD)`<br>[판독 기록:26](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/_staging/recovery/read/EFDC/manuals/pdfs/EFDC_Theory_Document_Ver_12.pdf/p217-234.md:26) | [Eutrophication/mod_diagen.f90:1212](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/Eutrophication/mod_diagen.f90:1212) `    AQJH2SSM = SMTT1*RSM1H2S`<br>[Eutrophication/mod_diagen.f90:998](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/Eutrophication/mod_diagen.f90:998) `        WQBFCOD(L)  = SMJAQH2S(L) - SMSS(L)*WQV(L,KSZ(L),ICOD)              ! *** COD flux       (g COD/m2/day)` | 일치 | 상층 용존 황화물 플럭스를 계산한 뒤 수주 COD의 역방향 s·COD를 뺀다. 메탄 분기에서는 그 수주 COD 차감을 함께 처리한다. |
| [Theory](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/manuals/pdfs/EFDC_Theory_Document_Ver_12.pdf) PDF 230쪽 / 인쇄 217쪽, 식 **8.232**<br>`\begin{aligned}J_{1,CH4}&=0\\J_{2,CH4}&=a_{O2,C}\cdot J_C-a_{O2,NO3}\cdot J_{N2(g)}\end{aligned}`<br>[판독 기록:26](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/_staging/recovery/read/EFDC/manuals/pdfs/EFDC_Theory_Document_Ver_12.pdf/p217-234.md:26) | [Eutrophication/mod_diagen.f90:1202](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/Eutrophication/mod_diagen.f90:1202) `  SMJ2H2S = max(SMO2JC - SMO2NO3*RJDENSM, 0.0)` | 불일치 | 메탄 생성도 탄소 산소당량−탈질 산소당량이다. 코드는 비음수 MAX를 추가한다. |
| [Theory](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/manuals/pdfs/EFDC_Theory_Document_Ver_12.pdf) PDF 230쪽 / 인쇄 217쪽, 식 **8.233**<br>`J_{2,CH4}=CSOD+J_{aq,CH4}+J_{CH4(g)}`<br>[판독 기록:26](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/_staging/recovery/read/EFDC/manuals/pdfs/EFDC_Theory_Document_Ver_12.pdf/p217-234.md:26) | [Eutrophication/mod_diagen.f90:1225](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/Eutrophication/mod_diagen.f90:1225) `    AQJCH4SM = CSODMSM*SMSECH`<br>[Eutrophication/mod_diagen.f90:1226](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/Eutrophication/mod_diagen.f90:1226) `    CSODSM = CSODMSM - AQJCH4SM`<br>[Eutrophication/mod_diagen.f90:1227](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/Eutrophication/mod_diagen.f90:1227) `    GJCH4SM = SMJ2H2S - CSODMSM` | 일치 | 수용성 메탄·탄소성 SOD·기체 메탄의 합이 하층 생성 플럭스이다. |
| [Theory](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/manuals/pdfs/EFDC_Theory_Document_Ver_12.pdf) PDF 231쪽 / 인쇄 218쪽, 식 **8.234**<br>`CSOD=CSOD_{max}\cdot\left(1-\operatorname{sech}\left[\frac{K_{CH4}\cdot\theta_{CH4}^{T-20}}{s}\right]\right)`<br>[판독 기록:27](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/_staging/recovery/read/EFDC/manuals/pdfs/EFDC_Theory_Document_Ver_12.pdf/p217-234.md:27) | [Eutrophication/mod_diagen.f90:1218](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/Eutrophication/mod_diagen.f90:1218) `    SMTT2 = SMK1CH4 / (RSMSS+ 1.E-18)`<br>[Eutrophication/mod_diagen.f90:1221](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/Eutrophication/mod_diagen.f90:1221) `      SMSECH = 2.0 / (SMTT3 + 1.0/SMTT3)`<br>[Eutrophication/mod_diagen.f90:1226](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/Eutrophication/mod_diagen.f90:1226) `    CSODSM = CSODMSM - AQJCH4SM` | 일치 | sech를 두 지수함수로 계산한다. CSODmax·(1−sech)를 적용한다. 지수 인자가 80 이상이면 sech=0으로 둔다. |
| [Theory](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/manuals/pdfs/EFDC_Theory_Document_Ver_12.pdf) PDF 231쪽 / 인쇄 218쪽, 식 **8.235**<br>`CSOD_{max}=\mathit{minimum}\left\{\sqrt{2\cdot KL\cdot CH4_{sat}\cdot J_{2,CH4}},\ J_{2,CH4}\right\}`<br>[판독 기록:27](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/_staging/recovery/read/EFDC/manuals/pdfs/EFDC_Theory_Document_Ver_12.pdf/p217-234.md:27) | [Eutrophication/mod_diagen.f90:962](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/Eutrophication/mod_diagen.f90:962) `          SMCH4S  = (10.0 + HP(L) + SMHSED(IZ)) * SMTD1CH4(ISMT(L)) * SMKL12(L)`<br>[Eutrophication/mod_diagen.f90:1217](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/Eutrophication/mod_diagen.f90:1217) `    CSODMSM = min( SQRT(SMCH4S*SMJ2H2S), SMJ2H2S )` | 일치 | SMCH4S에 2·KL·CH4sat를 합쳐 넣는다. MIN(제곱근 공급 제한, 총 생성)을 사용한다. |
| [Theory](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/manuals/pdfs/EFDC_Theory_Document_Ver_12.pdf) PDF 231쪽 / 인쇄 218쪽, 식 **8.236**<br>`CH4_{sat}=100\left(1+\frac{h+H_2}{10}\right)1.024^{20-T}`<br>[판독 기록:27](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/_staging/recovery/read/EFDC/manuals/pdfs/EFDC_Theory_Document_Ver_12.pdf/p217-234.md:27) | [Eutrophication/mod_diagen.f90:492](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/Eutrophication/mod_diagen.f90:492) `    SMTD1CH4(IT) = 0.97656**TT20 * 20.0`<br>[Eutrophication/mod_diagen.f90:962](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/Eutrophication/mod_diagen.f90:962) `          SMCH4S  = (10.0 + HP(L) + SMHSED(IZ)) * SMTD1CH4(ISMT(L)) * SMKL12(L)` | 불일치 | 수 깊이·퇴적층 두께의 함수 구조는 같다. 코드는 1/1.024를 0.97656으로 근사한다. SMCH4S는 CH4sat 단독값이 아니라 식 8.235의 2·KL까지 포함한 계수이다. 이 차이는 계수 근사에 해당한다. |
| [Theory](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/manuals/pdfs/EFDC_Theory_Document_Ver_12.pdf) PDF 231쪽 / 인쇄 218쪽, 식 **8.237**<br>`J_{aq,CH4}=CSOD_{max}\cdot\operatorname{sech}\left[\frac{K_{CH4}\cdot\theta_{CH4}^{T-20}}{s}\right]=CSOD_{max}-CSOD`<br>[판독 기록:27](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/_staging/recovery/read/EFDC/manuals/pdfs/EFDC_Theory_Document_Ver_12.pdf/p217-234.md:27) | [Eutrophication/mod_diagen.f90:1225](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/Eutrophication/mod_diagen.f90:1225) `    AQJCH4SM = CSODMSM*SMSECH`<br>[Eutrophication/mod_diagen.f90:1226](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/Eutrophication/mod_diagen.f90:1226) `    CSODSM = CSODMSM - AQJCH4SM` | 일치 | 수용성 메탄은 CSODmax·sech이다. CSOD는 CSODmax에서 그 플럭스를 뺀 값이다. |
| [Theory](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/manuals/pdfs/EFDC_Theory_Document_Ver_12.pdf) PDF 231쪽 / 인쇄 218쪽, 식 **8.238**<br>`H_2\frac{\partial PSi}{\partial t}=-S_{Si}\cdot H_2-W\cdot PSi+J_{PSi}+J_{DSi}`<br>[판독 기록:27](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/_staging/recovery/read/EFDC/manuals/pdfs/EFDC_Theory_Document_Ver_12.pdf/p217-234.md:27) | [Eutrophication/mod_diagen.f90:1035](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/Eutrophication/mod_diagen.f90:1035) `            if( ALGAES(NAL).ISILICA > 0 ) SMDFSI(L) = SMDFSI(L) + (ALGAES(NAL).WQASC*WQDFB(L,NAL) + WQDFSI(L) + SMJDSI) * SMDTOH(ISMZMAP(L))`<br>[Eutrophication/mod_diagen.f90:1037](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/Eutrophication/mod_diagen.f90:1037) `          WQTT = DTWQ * SMTDSI(ISMT(L)) * (SMSISAT-SMFD2SI*SM2SI(L)) / (SMPSI(L)+SMKMPSI+ 1.E-18)`<br>[Eutrophication/mod_diagen.f90:1038](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/Eutrophication/mod_diagen.f90:1038) `          SMPSI(L) = (SMPSI(L)+SMDFSI(L)) / (SMW2DTOH(ISMZMAP(L))+WQTT+ 1.E-18)` | 불일치 | 분해·매몰·퇴적 공급의 음해 구조는 대응한다. 규소 조류군 반복문이 JPSi·JDSi 공급을 군마다 반복한다. 규소군 수가 1이면 이 차이가 사라진다. |
| [Theory](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/manuals/pdfs/EFDC_Theory_Document_Ver_12.pdf) PDF 232쪽 / 인쇄 219쪽, 식 **8.239**<br>`S_{Si}=K_{Si}\cdot\theta_{Si}^{T-20}\frac{PSi}{PSi+KH_{PSi}}(Si_{sat}-fd_{2,Si}\cdot Si_2)`<br>[판독 기록:28](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/_staging/recovery/read/EFDC/manuals/pdfs/EFDC_Theory_Document_Ver_12.pdf/p217-234.md:28) | [Eutrophication/mod_diagen.f90:494](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/Eutrophication/mod_diagen.f90:494) `    SMTDSI(IT)   = SMKSI * SMTHSI**TT20`<br>[Eutrophication/mod_diagen.f90:1037](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/Eutrophication/mod_diagen.f90:1037) `          WQTT = DTWQ * SMTDSI(ISMT(L)) * (SMSISAT-SMFD2SI*SM2SI(L)) / (SMPSI(L)+SMKMPSI+ 1.E-18)`<br>[Eutrophication/mod_diagen.f90:1038](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/Eutrophication/mod_diagen.f90:1038) `          SMPSI(L) = (SMPSI(L)+SMDFSI(L)) / (SMW2DTOH(ISMZMAP(L))+WQTT+ 1.E-18)` | 일치 | 온도 보정·PSi Monod 제한·용존 규소 포화 부족량을 사용한다. KHPSi와 KMPSi를 같은 입력 SMKMPSI에 대응시킨다. |
| [Theory](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/manuals/pdfs/EFDC_Theory_Document_Ver_12.pdf) PDF 232쪽 / 인쇄 219쪽, 식 **8.240**<br>`J_{1,Si}=\kappa_{1,Si}=0`<br>[판독 기록:28](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/_staging/recovery/read/EFDC/manuals/pdfs/EFDC_Theory_Document_Ver_12.pdf/p217-234.md:28) | [Eutrophication/mod_diagen.f90:1048](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/Eutrophication/mod_diagen.f90:1048) `          A11SISM = SMSS(L)*SMFD1SI + A1SISM`<br>[Eutrophication/mod_diagen.f90:1052](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/Eutrophication/mod_diagen.f90:1052) `          B11SISM = SMSS(L) * WQSAD(L,KSZ(L))` | 일치 | 상층 규소에는 별도 생성·반응 소멸항이 없다. 우변은 수주 경계 공급이다. |
| [Theory](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/manuals/pdfs/EFDC_Theory_Document_Ver_12.pdf) PDF 232쪽 / 인쇄 219쪽, 식 **8.241**<br>`J_{2,Si}=K_{Si}\cdot\theta_{Si}^{T-20}\frac{PSi}{PSi+KM_{PSi}}Si_{sat}\cdot H_2`<br>[판독 기록:28](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/_staging/recovery/read/EFDC/manuals/pdfs/EFDC_Theory_Document_Ver_12.pdf/p217-234.md:28) | [Eutrophication/mod_diagen.f90:1049](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/Eutrophication/mod_diagen.f90:1049) `          WQTT = SMTDSI(ISMT(L)) * SMPSI(L) * SMHSED(ISMZMAP(L)) / (SMPSI(L)+SMKMPSI+ 1.E-18)`<br>[Eutrophication/mod_diagen.f90:1050](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/Eutrophication/mod_diagen.f90:1050) `          SMJ2SI = WQTT * SMSISAT` | 일치 | 하층 PSi 용출 계수에 포화 농도와 하층 두께를 곱해 생성항을 계산한다. |
| [Theory](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/manuals/pdfs/EFDC_Theory_Document_Ver_12.pdf) PDF 232쪽 / 인쇄 219쪽, 식 **8.242**<br>`\kappa_{2,Si}=K_{Si}\cdot\theta_{Si}^{T-20}\frac{PSi}{PSi+KM_{PSi}}f_{d2,Si}\cdot H_2`<br>[판독 기록:28](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/_staging/recovery/read/EFDC/manuals/pdfs/EFDC_Theory_Document_Ver_12.pdf/p217-234.md:28) | [Eutrophication/mod_diagen.f90:1049](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/Eutrophication/mod_diagen.f90:1049) `          WQTT = SMTDSI(ISMT(L)) * SMPSI(L) * SMHSED(ISMZMAP(L)) / (SMPSI(L)+SMKMPSI+ 1.E-18)`<br>[Eutrophication/mod_diagen.f90:1051](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/Eutrophication/mod_diagen.f90:1051) `          A22SISM = A2SISM + SMW2PHODT(ISMZMAP(L)) + WQTT*SMFD2SI` | 일치 | 하층 규소 제거계수는 용출 계수·용존 분율·하층 두께이다. |
| [Theory](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/manuals/pdfs/EFDC_Theory_Document_Ver_12.pdf) PDF 232쪽 / 인쇄 219쪽, 식 **8.243**<br>`\pi_{1,Si}=\pi_{2,Si}\cdot(\Delta\pi_{Si,1})\qquad DO_0>(DO_0)_{crit,Si}`<br>[판독 기록:28](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/_staging/recovery/read/EFDC/manuals/pdfs/EFDC_Theory_Document_Ver_12.pdf/p217-234.md:28) | [Eutrophication/mod_diagen.f90:828](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/Eutrophication/mod_diagen.f90:828) `      XSMO20(L) = max( WQV(L,KSZ(L),IDOX), 3.0 )`<br>[Eutrophication/mod_diagen.f90:1039](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/Eutrophication/mod_diagen.f90:1039) `          if( XSMO20(L) < SMCO2SI )then`<br>[Eutrophication/mod_diagen.f90:1042](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/Eutrophication/mod_diagen.f90:1042) `            SMP1SI = SMP2SI * SMDP1SI` | 불일치 | 임계 DO 이상의 분배 배수는 같다. 코드가 분기 판단에 MAX(DO,3)을 사용한다. |
| [Theory](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/manuals/pdfs/EFDC_Theory_Document_Ver_12.pdf) PDF 233쪽 / 인쇄 220쪽, 식 **8.244**<br>`\pi_{1,Si}=\pi_{2,Si}\cdot(\Delta\pi_{Si,1})^{\frac{DO_0}{(DO_0)_{crit,Si}}}\qquad DO_0\le(DO_0)_{crit,Si}`<br>[판독 기록:29](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/_staging/recovery/read/EFDC/manuals/pdfs/EFDC_Theory_Document_Ver_12.pdf/p217-234.md:29) | [Eutrophication/mod_diagen.f90:828](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/Eutrophication/mod_diagen.f90:828) `      XSMO20(L) = max( WQV(L,KSZ(L),IDOX), 3.0 )`<br>[Eutrophication/mod_diagen.f90:1040](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/Eutrophication/mod_diagen.f90:1040) `            SMP1SI = SMP2SI * SMDP1SI**(XSMO20(L)/(SMCO2SI+ 1.E-18))` | 불일치 | DO 비의 지수 분배 형태는 같다. 코드가 지수에 MAX(DO,3)을 사용한다. |
| [Theory](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/manuals/pdfs/EFDC_Theory_Document_Ver_12.pdf) PDF 233쪽 / 인쇄 220쪽, 식 **8.245**<br>`\frac{\partial T}{\partial t}=\frac{D_T}{H^2}(T_W-T)`<br>[판독 기록:29](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/_staging/recovery/read/EFDC/manuals/pdfs/EFDC_Theory_Document_Ver_12.pdf/p217-234.md:29) | [Eutrophication/mod_diagen.f90:303](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/Eutrophication/mod_diagen.f90:303) `      SM1DIFT(IZN)  = SMDIFT * SMDTOH(IZN)/(SMHSED(IZN)+ 1.E-18)`<br>[Eutrophication/mod_diagen.f90:304](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/Eutrophication/mod_diagen.f90:304) `      SM2DIFT(IZN)  = 1.0 / (1.0 + SM1DIFT(IZN))`<br>[Eutrophication/mod_diagen.f90:742](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/Eutrophication/mod_diagen.f90:742) `      SMT(L) = (SMT(L) + SM1DIFT(IZ)*TEM(L,KSZ(L))) * SM2DIFT(IZ)` | 일치 | 수주 최하층 온도와 퇴적층 온도의 차이에 DT/H² 열 전달을 적용한다. 식 8.252의 음해 형태로 계산한다. |
| [Theory](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/manuals/pdfs/EFDC_Theory_Document_Ver_12.pdf) PDF 233쪽 / 인쇄 220쪽, 식 **8.246**<br>`0=s\left(fd_0\cdot Ct_o'-fd_1\cdot Ct_1'\right)+KL\left(fd_2\cdot Ct_2'-fd_1\cdot Ct_1'\right)+\omega\left(fp_2\cdot Ct_2'-fp_1\cdot Ct_1'\right)-W\cdot Ct_1'-\frac{K_1^2}{s}Ct_1'+J_1'`<br>[판독 기록:29](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/_staging/recovery/read/EFDC/manuals/pdfs/EFDC_Theory_Document_Ver_12.pdf/p217-234.md:29) | [Eutrophication/mod_diagen.f90:1179](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/Eutrophication/mod_diagen.f90:1179) `  A11NH4 = RSMSS*SMFD1NH4 + A1NH4SM + RRNH4`<br>[Eutrophication/mod_diagen.f90:1180](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/Eutrophication/mod_diagen.f90:1180) `  B11NH4 = RSMSS*B1NH4SM`<br>[Eutrophication/mod_diagen.f90:1183](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/Eutrophication/mod_diagen.f90:1183) `  call SOLVSMBE(RSM1NH4,RSM2NH4,A11NH4,A22NH4SM,A1NH4SM,A2NH4SM,B11NH4,B22NH4)` | 일치 | 상층 정상상태 계수와 현재 수주 경계 농도를 사용한다. |
| [Theory](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/manuals/pdfs/EFDC_Theory_Document_Ver_12.pdf) PDF 233쪽 / 인쇄 220쪽, 식 **8.247**<br>`0=-KL\left(fd_2\cdot Ct_2'-fd_1\cdot Ct_1'\right)-\omega\left(fp_2\cdot Ct_2'-fp_1\cdot Ct_1'\right)+W\left(Ct_1'-Ct_2'\right)-\left(K_2+\frac{H_2}{\theta}\right)Ct_2'+\left(J_2'+\frac{H_2}{\theta}Ct_2\right)`<br>[판독 기록:29](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/_staging/recovery/read/EFDC/manuals/pdfs/EFDC_Theory_Document_Ver_12.pdf/p217-234.md:29) | [Eutrophication/mod_diagen.f90:937](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/Eutrophication/mod_diagen.f90:937) `        A22NH4SM = A2NH4SM + SMW2PHODT(IZ)`<br>[Eutrophication/mod_diagen.f90:939](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/Eutrophication/mod_diagen.f90:939) `        B2NH4SM  = SMDGFN(L) + SMHODT(IZ)*SM2NH4(L)` | 일치 | 하층의 H2/시간간격 저장항을 대각계수와 이전 농도 우변에 더한다. |
| [Theory](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/manuals/pdfs/EFDC_Theory_Document_Ver_12.pdf) PDF 234쪽 / 인쇄 221쪽, 식 **8.248**<br>`\begin{bmatrix}s\cdot fd_1+a_1+\frac{K_1^2}{s}&-a_2\\-a_1&a_2+W+K_2+\frac{H_2}{\theta}\end{bmatrix}\begin{bmatrix}Ct_1'\\Ct_2'\end{bmatrix}=\begin{bmatrix}J_1'+s\cdot fd_0\cdot Ct_0'\\J_2'+\frac{H_2}{\theta}Ct_2\end{bmatrix}`<br>[판독 기록:30](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/_staging/recovery/read/EFDC/manuals/pdfs/EFDC_Theory_Document_Ver_12.pdf/p217-234.md:30) | [Eutrophication/mod_diagen.f90:935](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/Eutrophication/mod_diagen.f90:935) `        A1NH4SM  = SMKL12(L)*SMFD1NH4 + SMW12(L)*SMFP1NH4 + SMW2(IZ)`<br>[Eutrophication/mod_diagen.f90:936](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/Eutrophication/mod_diagen.f90:936) `        A2NH4SM  = SMKL12(L)*SMFD2NH4 + SMW12(L)*SMFP2NH4`<br>[Eutrophication/mod_diagen.f90:937](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/Eutrophication/mod_diagen.f90:937) `        A22NH4SM = A2NH4SM + SMW2PHODT(IZ)`<br>[Eutrophication/mod_diagen.f90:1179](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/Eutrophication/mod_diagen.f90:1179) `  A11NH4 = RSMSS*SMFD1NH4 + A1NH4SM + RRNH4`<br>[Eutrophication/mod_diagen.f90:1127](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/Eutrophication/mod_diagen.f90:1127) `  SMA12 = -SMA2  `<br>[Eutrophication/mod_diagen.f90:1128](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/Eutrophication/mod_diagen.f90:1128) `  SMA21 = -SMA1  `<br>[Eutrophication/mod_diagen.f90:1136](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/Eutrophication/mod_diagen.f90:1136) `  SMV1 = (SMB11*SMA22 - SMB22*SMA12) * SMDET  `<br>[Eutrophication/mod_diagen.f90:1137](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/Eutrophication/mod_diagen.f90:1137) `  SMV2 = (SMB22*SMA11 - SMB11*SMA21) * SMDET  ` | 일치 | 두 층의 2×2 계수행렬과 우변을 구성한다. Cramer 형태로 해를 계산한다. |
| [Theory](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/manuals/pdfs/EFDC_Theory_Document_Ver_12.pdf) PDF 234쪽 / 인쇄 221쪽, 식 **8.249**<br>`\begin{aligned}a_1&=KL\cdot fd_1+\omega\cdot fp_1+W\\a_2&=KL\cdot fd_2+\omega\cdot fp_2\end{aligned}`<br>[판독 기록:30](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/_staging/recovery/read/EFDC/manuals/pdfs/EFDC_Theory_Document_Ver_12.pdf/p217-234.md:30) | [Eutrophication/mod_diagen.f90:935](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/Eutrophication/mod_diagen.f90:935) `        A1NH4SM  = SMKL12(L)*SMFD1NH4 + SMW12(L)*SMFP1NH4 + SMW2(IZ)`<br>[Eutrophication/mod_diagen.f90:936](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/Eutrophication/mod_diagen.f90:936) `        A2NH4SM  = SMKL12(L)*SMFD2NH4 + SMW12(L)*SMFP2NH4` | 일치 | a1에는 용존·입자 혼합과 매몰을 넣는다. a2에는 용존·입자 혼합만 넣는다. |
| [Theory](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/manuals/pdfs/EFDC_Theory_Document_Ver_12.pdf) PDF 234쪽 / 인쇄 221쪽, 식 **8.250**<br>`G_{POM,i}'=\left(G_{POM,i}+\frac{\theta}{H_2}J_{POM,i}\right)\left(1+\theta\cdot K_{POM,i}\cdot\theta_{POM,i}^{T-20}+\frac{\theta}{H_2}W\right)^{-1}`<br>[판독 기록:30](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/_staging/recovery/read/EFDC/manuals/pdfs/EFDC_Theory_Document_Ver_12.pdf/p217-234.md:30) | [Eutrophication/mod_diagen.f90:305](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/Eutrophication/mod_diagen.f90:305) `      SMW2DTOH(IZN) = 1.0 + SMW2(IZN)*SMDTOH(IZN)`<br>[Eutrophication/mod_diagen.f90:814](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/Eutrophication/mod_diagen.f90:814) `        SMPOC(L,M) = (SMPOC(L,M) + SMDFC(L,M)*SMDTOH(ISMZMAP(L))) / (SMW2DTOH(ISMZMAP(L)) + SMTDCD(ISMT(L),M)*DTWQ + 1.E-18)`<br>[Eutrophication/mod_diagen.f90:815](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/Eutrophication/mod_diagen.f90:815) `        SMPON(L,M) = (SMPON(L,M) + SMDFN(L,M)*SMDTOH(ISMZMAP(L))) / (SMW2DTOH(ISMZMAP(L)) + SMTDND(ISMT(L),M)*DTWQ + 1.E-18)`<br>[Eutrophication/mod_diagen.f90:816](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/Eutrophication/mod_diagen.f90:816) `        SMPOP(L,M) = (SMPOP(L,M) + SMDFP(L,M)*SMDTOH(ISMZMAP(L))) / (SMW2DTOH(ISMZMAP(L)) + SMTDPD(ISMT(L),M)*DTWQ + 1.E-18)` | 일치 | 입력 공급 θ/H2와 분해 θ·K·온도함수 및 매몰 θ·W/H2가 코드의 분자·분모에 대응한다. |
| [Theory](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/manuals/pdfs/EFDC_Theory_Document_Ver_12.pdf) PDF 234쪽 / 인쇄 221쪽, 식 **8.251**<br>`PSi'=\left(PSi+\frac{\theta}{H_2}(J_{PSi}+J_{DSi})\right)\left(1+\theta\cdot K_{Si}\cdot\theta_{Si}^{T-20}\frac{Si_{sat}-f_{d2,Si}\cdot Si_2}{PSi+KM_{PSi}}+\frac{\theta}{H_2}W\right)^{-1}`<br>[판독 기록:30](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/_staging/recovery/read/EFDC/manuals/pdfs/EFDC_Theory_Document_Ver_12.pdf/p217-234.md:30) | [Eutrophication/mod_diagen.f90:1035](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/Eutrophication/mod_diagen.f90:1035) `            if( ALGAES(NAL).ISILICA > 0 ) SMDFSI(L) = SMDFSI(L) + (ALGAES(NAL).WQASC*WQDFB(L,NAL) + WQDFSI(L) + SMJDSI) * SMDTOH(ISMZMAP(L))`<br>[Eutrophication/mod_diagen.f90:1037](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/Eutrophication/mod_diagen.f90:1037) `          WQTT = DTWQ * SMTDSI(ISMT(L)) * (SMSISAT-SMFD2SI*SM2SI(L)) / (SMPSI(L)+SMKMPSI+ 1.E-18)`<br>[Eutrophication/mod_diagen.f90:1038](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/Eutrophication/mod_diagen.f90:1038) `          SMPSI(L) = (SMPSI(L)+SMDFSI(L)) / (SMW2DTOH(ISMZMAP(L))+WQTT+ 1.E-18)` | 불일치 | 용출 계수와 매몰 분모는 같다. 코드가 수주 입자 규소·독립 JDSi 공급을 규소 조류군 수만큼 반복한다. 문서는 한 번 더한다. |
| [Theory](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/manuals/pdfs/EFDC_Theory_Document_Ver_12.pdf) PDF 234쪽 / 인쇄 221쪽, 식 **8.252**<br>`T'=\left(T+\frac{\theta}{H^2}D_T\cdot T_W\right)\left(1+\frac{\theta}{H^2}D_T\right)^{-1}`<br>[판독 기록:30](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/_staging/recovery/read/EFDC/manuals/pdfs/EFDC_Theory_Document_Ver_12.pdf/p217-234.md:30) | [Eutrophication/mod_diagen.f90:303](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/Eutrophication/mod_diagen.f90:303) `      SM1DIFT(IZN)  = SMDIFT * SMDTOH(IZN)/(SMHSED(IZN)+ 1.E-18)`<br>[Eutrophication/mod_diagen.f90:304](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/Eutrophication/mod_diagen.f90:304) `      SM2DIFT(IZN)  = 1.0 / (1.0 + SM1DIFT(IZN))`<br>[Eutrophication/mod_diagen.f90:742](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/Eutrophication/mod_diagen.f90:742) `      SMT(L) = (SMT(L) + SM1DIFT(IZ)*TEM(L,KSZ(L))) * SM2DIFT(IZ)` | 일치 | θ·DT/H²를 SM1DIFT로 계산한다. 같은 음해 온도 갱신식이다. |

### 2.4 독성물질

| 문서 식·쪽·LaTeX 원문 | 구현 코드 줄 원문 | 판정 | 이유 |
|---|---|---|---|
| [Theory](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/manuals/pdfs/EFDC_Theory_Document_Ver_12.pdf) PDF 124쪽 / 인쇄 111쪽, 식 **7.1**<br>`\begin{aligned}&\dfrac{\partial}{\partial t}(mHC_w)+\dfrac{\partial}{\partial x}(m_yHuC_w)+\dfrac{\partial}{\partial y}(m_xHvC_w)+\dfrac{\partial}{\partial z}(mwC_w)=\\&\quad\dfrac{\partial}{\partial z}\left(m\dfrac{A_b}{H}\dfrac{\partial}{\partial z}C_w\right)+mH\left(\sum_i\left(K_{dS}^iS^i\chi_S^i\right)+\sum_j\left(K_{dD}^jD^j\chi_D^j\right)\right)\\&\quad-mH\sum_i\left(K_{aS}^iS^i\right)\left(\psi_w\dfrac{C_w}{\phi}\right)\left(\hat{\chi}_S^i-\chi_S^i\right)\\&\quad-mH\sum_j\left(K_{aD}^jD^j\right)\left(\psi_w\dfrac{C_w}{\phi}\right)\left(\hat{\chi}_D^j-\chi_D^j\right)-mH\gamma C_w\end{aligned}`<br>[판독 기록:28](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/_staging/recovery/read/EFDC/manuals/pdfs/EFDC_Theory_Document_Ver_12.pdf/p109-126.md:28) | 관련 원문 탐색 근거:<br>[ChemFate/caltox.f90:306](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/ChemFate/caltox.f90:306) `            TOXPFW(L,K,NS,NT) = TOXPFW(L,K,NS,NT)/(1. + TOXPFTW(L,K,NT))`<br>[varinit.f90:314](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/varinit.f90:314) `      WCV(NACTIVEWC).VAL0 => TOX(:,:,NT)` | 문서에만 | 문서는 자유 용존상과 담체별 흡착상을 독립 상태로 풀며 흡착·탈착 속도 및 포화 흡착량을 사용한다. 코드는 총농도 TOX를 이송하고 평형 분율을 대수적으로 계산한다. 대응하는 유한속도 흡착 방정식은 없다. |
| [Theory](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/manuals/pdfs/EFDC_Theory_Document_Ver_12.pdf) PDF 125쪽 / 인쇄 112쪽, 식 **7.2**<br>`\begin{aligned}&\dfrac{\partial}{\partial t}\left(mHD^j\chi_D^j\right)+\dfrac{\partial}{\partial x}\left(m_yHuD^j\chi_D^j\right)\\&\quad+\dfrac{\partial}{\partial y}\left(m_xHvD^j\chi_D^j\right)+\dfrac{\partial}{\partial z}\left(mwD^j\chi_D^j\right)\\&=\dfrac{\partial}{\partial z}\left(m\dfrac{A_b}{H}\dfrac{\partial}{\partial z}\left(D^j\chi_D^j\right)\right)+mH\left(K_{sD}^jD^j\right)\left(\psi_w\dfrac{C_w}{\phi}\right)\left(\hat{\chi}_D^j-\chi_D^j\right)\\&\quad-mH\left(K_{dD}^j+\gamma\right)\left(D^j\chi_D^j\right)\end{aligned}`<br>[판독 기록:29](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/_staging/recovery/read/EFDC/manuals/pdfs/EFDC_Theory_Document_Ver_12.pdf/p109-126.md:29) | 관련 원문 탐색 근거:<br>[ChemFate/caltox.f90:306](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/ChemFate/caltox.f90:306) `            TOXPFW(L,K,NS,NT) = TOXPFW(L,K,NS,NT)/(1. + TOXPFTW(L,K,NT))`<br>[varinit.f90:314](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/varinit.f90:314) `      WCV(NACTIVEWC).VAL0 => TOX(:,:,NT)` | 문서에만 | 문서는 자유 용존상과 담체별 흡착상을 독립 상태로 풀며 흡착·탈착 속도 및 포화 흡착량을 사용한다. 코드는 총농도 TOX를 이송하고 평형 분율을 대수적으로 계산한다. 대응하는 유한속도 흡착 방정식은 없다. |
| [Theory](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/manuals/pdfs/EFDC_Theory_Document_Ver_12.pdf) PDF 125쪽 / 인쇄 112쪽, 식 **7.3**<br>`\begin{aligned}&\dfrac{\partial}{\partial t}\left(mHS^i\chi_S^i\right)+\dfrac{\partial}{\partial x}\left(m_yHuS^i\chi_S^i\right)\\&\quad+\dfrac{\partial}{\partial y}\left(m_xHvS^i\chi_S^i\right)+\dfrac{\partial}{\partial z}\left(mwS^i\chi_S^i\right)\\&=\dfrac{\partial}{\partial z}\left(m\dfrac{A_b}{H}\dfrac{\partial}{\partial z}\left(S^i\chi_S^i\right)\right)+mH\left(K_{aS}^iS^i\right)\left(\psi_w\dfrac{C_w}{\phi}\right)\left(\hat{\chi}_S^i-\chi_S^i\right)\\&\quad-mH\left(K_{aS}^i+\gamma\right)\left(S^i\chi_S^i\right)\end{aligned}`<br>[판독 기록:29](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/_staging/recovery/read/EFDC/manuals/pdfs/EFDC_Theory_Document_Ver_12.pdf/p109-126.md:29) | 관련 원문 탐색 근거:<br>[ChemFate/caltox.f90:306](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/ChemFate/caltox.f90:306) `            TOXPFW(L,K,NS,NT) = TOXPFW(L,K,NS,NT)/(1. + TOXPFTW(L,K,NT))`<br>[varinit.f90:314](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/varinit.f90:314) `      WCV(NACTIVEWC).VAL0 => TOX(:,:,NT)` | 문서에만 | 문서는 자유 용존상과 담체별 흡착상을 독립 상태로 풀며 흡착·탈착 속도 및 포화 흡착량을 사용한다. 코드는 총농도 TOX를 이송하고 평형 분율을 대수적으로 계산한다. 대응하는 유한속도 흡착 방정식은 없다. |
| [Theory](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/manuals/pdfs/EFDC_Theory_Document_Ver_12.pdf) PDF 126쪽 / 인쇄 113쪽, 식 **7.4**<br>`C_D^j=D^j\chi_D^j`<br>[판독 기록:30](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/_staging/recovery/read/EFDC/manuals/pdfs/EFDC_Theory_Document_Ver_12.pdf/p109-126.md:30) | [ChemFate/caltox.f90:233](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/ChemFate/caltox.f90:233) `              TOXPFW(L,K,NFD,NT) = STDOCW(L,K)*TOXPARWC(1,NT)`<br>[ChemFate/caltox.f90:306](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/ChemFate/caltox.f90:306) `            TOXPFW(L,K,NS,NT) = TOXPFW(L,K,NS,NT)/(1. + TOXPFTW(L,K,NT))` | 일치 | DOC 농도와 분배계수의 곱을 정규화하여 DOC 결합 분율을 구한다. 총농도에 이 분율을 곱하면 문서의 DOC 결합 농도가 된다. 독립 DOC 결합 상태변수는 없다. |
| [Theory](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/manuals/pdfs/EFDC_Theory_Document_Ver_12.pdf) PDF 126쪽 / 인쇄 113쪽, 식 **7.5**<br>`C_S^i=S^i\chi_S^i`<br>[판독 기록:30](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/_staging/recovery/read/EFDC/manuals/pdfs/EFDC_Theory_Document_Ver_12.pdf/p109-126.md:30) | [ChemFate/caltox.f90:146](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/ChemFate/caltox.f90:146) `                  TOXPFW(L,K,NS,NT) = SED(L,K,NS)*TOXPARW(L,NS,NT)`<br>[ChemFate/caltox.f90:306](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/ChemFate/caltox.f90:306) `            TOXPFW(L,K,NS,NT) = TOXPFW(L,K,NS,NT)/(1. + TOXPFTW(L,K,NT))` | 일치 | 퇴적물 농도와 분배계수의 곱을 정규화하여 입자별 분율을 구한다. 총농도에 이 분율을 곱하면 문서의 입자 결합 농도가 된다. |
| [Theory](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/manuals/pdfs/EFDC_Theory_Document_Ver_12.pdf) PDF 126쪽 / 인쇄 113쪽, 식 **7.6**<br>`\left(K_{sD}^jD^j\right)\left(\psi_w\dfrac{C_w}{\phi}\right)\left(\hat{\chi}_D^j-\chi_D^j\right)=K_{dD}^jC_D^j`<br>[판독 기록:30](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/_staging/recovery/read/EFDC/manuals/pdfs/EFDC_Theory_Document_Ver_12.pdf/p109-126.md:30) | 관련 원문 탐색 근거:<br>[ChemFate/caltox.f90:306](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/ChemFate/caltox.f90:306) `            TOXPFW(L,K,NS,NT) = TOXPFW(L,K,NS,NT)/(1. + TOXPFTW(L,K,NT))` | 문서에만 | 문서는 유한속도 흡착식의 평형 조건을 유도한다. 코드는 입력 분배계수를 직접 사용한다. 흡착 속도와 탈착 속도의 평형 관계를 계산하지 않는다. |
| [Theory](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/manuals/pdfs/EFDC_Theory_Document_Ver_12.pdf) PDF 126쪽 / 인쇄 113쪽, 식 **7.7**<br>`\left(K_{aS}^iS^i\right)\left(\psi_w\dfrac{C_w}{\phi}\right)\left(\hat{\chi}_S^i-\chi_S^i\right)=K_{dS}^iC_S^i`<br>[판독 기록:30](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/_staging/recovery/read/EFDC/manuals/pdfs/EFDC_Theory_Document_Ver_12.pdf/p109-126.md:30) | 관련 원문 탐색 근거:<br>[ChemFate/caltox.f90:306](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/ChemFate/caltox.f90:306) `            TOXPFW(L,K,NS,NT) = TOXPFW(L,K,NS,NT)/(1. + TOXPFTW(L,K,NT))` | 문서에만 | 문서는 유한속도 흡착식의 평형 조건을 유도한다. 코드는 입력 분배계수를 직접 사용한다. 흡착 속도와 탈착 속도의 평형 관계를 계산하지 않는다. |
| [Theory](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/manuals/pdfs/EFDC_Theory_Document_Ver_12.pdf) PDF 127쪽 / 인쇄 114쪽, 식 **7.8**<br>`\displaystyle \begin{aligned}\frac{C_D^j}{C_w}&=\frac{f_D^j}{f_w}=P_D^j\frac{D^j}{\phi}\\P_D^j&=P_{Do}^j\left(1+P_{Do}^j\left(\frac{C_w}{\widehat{\chi}_D^j\phi}\right)\right)^{-1}\\P_{Do}^j&=\frac{\psi_w K_{aD}^j\widehat{\chi}_D^j}{K_{dD}^j}\end{aligned}`<br>[판독 기록:13](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/_staging/recovery/read/EFDC/manuals/pdfs/EFDC_Theory_Document_Ver_12.pdf/p127-144.md:13) | 관련 원문 탐색 근거:<br>[ChemFate/caltox.f90:126](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/ChemFate/caltox.f90:126) `                  TMPVAL = SED(L,K,NS)**TMPEXP`<br>[ChemFate/caltox.f90:128](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/ChemFate/caltox.f90:128) `                  TOXPFW(L,K,NS,NT) = TMPVAL*SED(L,K,NS)*STFPOCW(L,K,NS)*TOXPARW(L,NS,NT)`<br>[ChemFate/caltox.f90:244](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/ChemFate/caltox.f90:244) `              if( STDOCW(L,K) > 0.) TMPVAL = STDOCW(L,K)**TMPEXP` | 문서에만 | 문서의 Langmuir 분배계수는 자유 용존 농도와 포화 흡착량에 의존한다. 코드의 선택적 비선형 분배는 담체 농도의 거듭제곱에 의존한다. 문서의 농도 의존 Langmuir 식은 구현하지 않는다. |
| [Theory](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/manuals/pdfs/EFDC_Theory_Document_Ver_12.pdf) PDF 127쪽 / 인쇄 114쪽, 식 **7.9**<br>`\displaystyle \begin{aligned}\frac{C_S^i}{C_w}&=\frac{f_S^i}{f_w}=P_S^i\frac{S^i}{\phi}\\P_S^i&=P_{So}^i\left(1+P_{So}^i\left(\frac{C_w}{\widehat{\chi}_S^i\phi}\right)\right)^{-1}\\P_{So}^i&=\frac{\psi_w K_{aS}^i\widehat{\chi}_S^i}{K_{dS}^i}\end{aligned}`<br>[판독 기록:13](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/_staging/recovery/read/EFDC/manuals/pdfs/EFDC_Theory_Document_Ver_12.pdf/p127-144.md:13) | 관련 원문 탐색 근거:<br>[ChemFate/caltox.f90:126](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/ChemFate/caltox.f90:126) `                  TMPVAL = SED(L,K,NS)**TMPEXP`<br>[ChemFate/caltox.f90:128](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/ChemFate/caltox.f90:128) `                  TOXPFW(L,K,NS,NT) = TMPVAL*SED(L,K,NS)*STFPOCW(L,K,NS)*TOXPARW(L,NS,NT)`<br>[ChemFate/caltox.f90:244](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/ChemFate/caltox.f90:244) `              if( STDOCW(L,K) > 0.) TMPVAL = STDOCW(L,K)**TMPEXP` | 문서에만 | 문서의 Langmuir 분배계수는 자유 용존 농도와 포화 흡착량에 의존한다. 코드의 선택적 비선형 분배는 담체 농도의 거듭제곱에 의존한다. 문서의 농도 의존 Langmuir 식은 구현하지 않는다. |
| [Theory](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/manuals/pdfs/EFDC_Theory_Document_Ver_12.pdf) PDF 127쪽 / 인쇄 114쪽, 식 **7.10**<br>`\displaystyle f_w+\sum_i f_S^i+\sum_j f_D^j=1`<br>[판독 기록:13](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/_staging/recovery/read/EFDC/manuals/pdfs/EFDC_Theory_Document_Ver_12.pdf/p127-144.md:13) | [ChemFate/caltox.f90:290](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/ChemFate/caltox.f90:290) `            TOXPFTW(L,K,NT) = TOXPFTW(L,K,NT) + TOXPFW(L,K,NS,NT)`<br>[ChemFate/caltox.f90:306](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/ChemFate/caltox.f90:306) `            TOXPFW(L,K,NS,NT) = TOXPFW(L,K,NS,NT)/(1. + TOXPFTW(L,K,NT))`<br>[ChemFate/caltox.f90:318](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/ChemFate/caltox.f90:318) `            TOXFDFW(L,K,NT) = 1./(1. + TOXPFTW(L,K,NT))` | 일치 | 정규화 전 담체별 비율의 합을 r로 두면 자유 용존 분율은 1/(1+r)이다. 담체별 분율의 합은 r/(1+r)이다. 두 분율의 합은 1이다. 이후 별도 총입자상 분율 제한은 §3에 적었다. |
| [Theory](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/manuals/pdfs/EFDC_Theory_Document_Ver_12.pdf) PDF 127쪽 / 인쇄 114쪽, 식 **7.11**<br>`\displaystyle \begin{aligned}f_w&=\frac{C_w}{C}=\frac{\phi}{\phi+\sum_i P_S^i S^i+\sum_j P_D^j D^j}\\f_D^j&=\frac{C_D^j}{C}=\frac{P_D^j D^j}{\phi+\sum_i P_S^i S^i+\sum_j P_D^j D^j}\\f_S^i&=\frac{C_S^i}{C}=\frac{P_S^i S^i}{\phi+\sum_i P_S^i S^i+\sum_j P_D^j D^j}\end{aligned}`<br>[판독 기록:13](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/_staging/recovery/read/EFDC/manuals/pdfs/EFDC_Theory_Document_Ver_12.pdf/p127-144.md:13) | [ChemFate/caltox.f90:306](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/ChemFate/caltox.f90:306) `            TOXPFW(L,K,NS,NT) = TOXPFW(L,K,NS,NT)/(1. + TOXPFTW(L,K,NT))`<br>[ChemFate/caltox.f90:318](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/ChemFate/caltox.f90:318) `            TOXFDFW(L,K,NT) = 1./(1. + TOXPFTW(L,K,NT))`<br>[ChemFate/caltoxb.f90:472](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/ChemFate/caltoxb.f90:472) `            TOXFDFB(L,K,NT) = PORBED(L,K)*HBED(L,K) /(PORBED(L,K)*HBED(L,K) + TOXPFTB(L,K,NT))` | 일치 | 수주에서는 공극률을 1로 취급한다. 퇴적층에서는 분모에 공극수 두께 PORBED·HBED를 사용한다. 담체별 평형 분율의 구조가 일치한다. |
| [Theory](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/manuals/pdfs/EFDC_Theory_Document_Ver_12.pdf) PDF 127쪽 / 인쇄 114쪽, 식 **7.12**<br>`\displaystyle \begin{aligned}C_{w:w}&=\frac{C_w}{\phi}\\C_{D:w}^j&=\frac{C_D^j}{\phi}\\D_{:w}^{J}&=\frac{D^j}{\phi}\end{aligned}`<br>[판독 기록:13](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/_staging/recovery/read/EFDC/manuals/pdfs/EFDC_Theory_Document_Ver_12.pdf/p127-144.md:13) | [ChemFate/caltoxb.f90:412](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/ChemFate/caltoxb.f90:412) `          TOXPFB(L,K,NFD,NT) = PORBED(L,K)*HBED(L,K)*STDOCB(L,K)*TOXPARBC(1,NT)`<br>[ChemFate/caltoxb.f90:472](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/ChemFate/caltoxb.f90:472) `            TOXFDFB(L,K,NT) = PORBED(L,K)*HBED(L,K) /(PORBED(L,K)*HBED(L,K) + TOXPFTB(L,K,NT))` | 일치 | 퇴적층 DOC 담체량은 공극수 두께와 DOCBC의 곱이다. 자유 용존상 농도는 전체 퇴적층 농도를 공극률로 나눈 값으로 환산한다. TOXB는 면적당 질량이므로 층 두께 환산이 필요하다. |
| [Theory](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/manuals/pdfs/EFDC_Theory_Document_Ver_12.pdf) PDF 128쪽 / 인쇄 115쪽, 식 **7.13**<br>`\displaystyle \begin{aligned}\frac{C_{w:w}}{C}&=\frac{1}{\phi+\sum_i P_S^i S^i+\sum_j P_D^j\phi D_{:w}^j}\\\frac{C_{D:w}^j}{C}&=\frac{P_D^j D_{:w}^j}{\phi+\sum_i P_S^i S^i+\sum_j P_D^j\phi D_{:w}^j}\\\frac{C_S^i}{C}&=\frac{P_S^i S^i}{\phi+\sum_i P_S^i S^i+\sum_j P_D^j\phi D_{:w}^j}\end{aligned}`<br>[판독 기록:14](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/_staging/recovery/read/EFDC/manuals/pdfs/EFDC_Theory_Document_Ver_12.pdf/p127-144.md:14) | [ChemFate/caltoxb.f90:412](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/ChemFate/caltoxb.f90:412) `          TOXPFB(L,K,NFD,NT) = PORBED(L,K)*HBED(L,K)*STDOCB(L,K)*TOXPARBC(1,NT)`<br>[ChemFate/caltoxb.f90:454](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/ChemFate/caltoxb.f90:454) `          TMPVAL = PORBED(L,K)*HBED(L,K) + TOXPFTB(L,K,NT)`<br>[ChemFate/caltoxb.f90:456](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/ChemFate/caltoxb.f90:456) `            TOXPFB(L,K,NS,NT) = TOXPFB(L,K,NS,NT)/TMPVAL`<br>[ChemFate/caltoxb.f90:472](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/ChemFate/caltoxb.f90:472) `            TOXFDFB(L,K,NT) = PORBED(L,K)*HBED(L,K) /(PORBED(L,K)*HBED(L,K) + TOXPFTB(L,K,NT))` | 일치 | 공극수 부피를 기준으로 한 DOC와 전체층 부피를 기준으로 한 고형물을 같은 분모에 결합한다. 코드의 면적당 담체량 표현을 HBED로 나누면 문서의 부피 기준 분율이 된다. |
| [Theory](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/manuals/pdfs/EFDC_Theory_Document_Ver_12.pdf) PDF 128쪽 / 인쇄 115쪽, 식 **7.14**<br>`\displaystyle \begin{aligned}&\frac{\partial}{\partial t}(mHC_w)+\frac{\partial}{\partial x}(m_yHuC_w)+\frac{\partial}{\partial y}(m_xHvC_w)+\frac{\partial}{\partial z}(m_xm_ywC_w)\\&=\frac{\partial}{\partial z}\left(m_xm_y\frac{A_b}{H}\frac{\partial}{\partial z}(C_w)\right)+mH\left(\sum_i\left(K_{dS}^j C_S^i\right)+\sum_j\left(K_{dD}^j C_D^i\right)\right)\\&\quad-mH\left(\sum_i\left(K_{aS}^i S^i\right)\left(\psi_w\frac{C_w}{\phi}\right)\left(\widehat{\chi}_S^i-\chi_S^i\right)+\sum_j\left(K_{aD}^j D^j\right)\left(\psi_w\frac{C_w}{\phi}\right)\left(\widehat{\chi}_D^j-\chi_D^j\right)+\gamma C_w\right)\end{aligned}`<br>[판독 기록:14](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/_staging/recovery/read/EFDC/manuals/pdfs/EFDC_Theory_Document_Ver_12.pdf/p127-144.md:14) | 관련 원문 탐색 근거:<br>[ChemFate/caltox.f90:306](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/ChemFate/caltox.f90:306) `            TOXPFW(L,K,NS,NT) = TOXPFW(L,K,NS,NT)/(1. + TOXPFTW(L,K,NT))`<br>[varinit.f90:314](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/varinit.f90:314) `      WCV(NACTIVEWC).VAL0 => TOX(:,:,NT)` | 문서에만 | 문서는 자유 용존상과 담체별 흡착상을 독립 상태로 풀며 흡착·탈착 속도 및 포화 흡착량을 사용한다. 코드는 총농도 TOX를 이송하고 평형 분율을 대수적으로 계산한다. 대응하는 유한속도 흡착 방정식은 없다. |
| [Theory](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/manuals/pdfs/EFDC_Theory_Document_Ver_12.pdf) PDF 128쪽 / 인쇄 115쪽, 식 **7.15**<br>`\displaystyle \begin{aligned}&\frac{\partial}{\partial t}\left(mHC_D^j\right)+\frac{\partial}{\partial x}\left(m_yHuC_D^j\right)+\frac{\partial}{\partial y}\left(m_xHvC_D^j\right)+\frac{\partial}{\partial z}\left(mwC_D^j\right)\\&=\frac{\partial}{\partial z}\left[m\frac{A_b}{H}\frac{\partial}{\partial z}\left(C_D^j\right)\right]+mH\left(K_{sD}^j D^j\right)\left(\psi_w\frac{C_w}{\phi}\right)\left(\widehat{\chi}_D^j-\chi_D^j\right)\\&\quad-mH\left(K_{dD}^j+\gamma\right)C_D^j\end{aligned}`<br>[판독 기록:14](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/_staging/recovery/read/EFDC/manuals/pdfs/EFDC_Theory_Document_Ver_12.pdf/p127-144.md:14) | 관련 원문 탐색 근거:<br>[ChemFate/caltox.f90:306](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/ChemFate/caltox.f90:306) `            TOXPFW(L,K,NS,NT) = TOXPFW(L,K,NS,NT)/(1. + TOXPFTW(L,K,NT))`<br>[varinit.f90:314](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/varinit.f90:314) `      WCV(NACTIVEWC).VAL0 => TOX(:,:,NT)` | 문서에만 | 문서는 자유 용존상과 담체별 흡착상을 독립 상태로 풀며 흡착·탈착 속도 및 포화 흡착량을 사용한다. 코드는 총농도 TOX를 이송하고 평형 분율을 대수적으로 계산한다. 대응하는 유한속도 흡착 방정식은 없다. |
| [Theory](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/manuals/pdfs/EFDC_Theory_Document_Ver_12.pdf) PDF 128쪽 / 인쇄 115쪽, 식 **7.16**<br>`\displaystyle \begin{aligned}&\frac{\partial}{\partial t}\left(mHC_S^i\right)+\frac{\partial}{\partial x}\left(m_yHuC_S^i\right)+\frac{\partial}{\partial y}\left(m_xHvC_S^i\right)+\frac{\partial}{\partial z}\left(mwC_S^i\right)+\frac{\partial}{\partial z}\left(mw_S^i C_S^i\right)\\&=\frac{\partial}{\partial z}\left(m\frac{A_b}{H}\frac{\partial}{\partial z}C_S^i\right)+mH\left(K_{aS}^i S^i\right)\left(\psi_w\frac{C_w}{\phi}\right)\left(\widehat{\chi}_S^i-\chi_S^i\right)\\&\quad-mH\left(K_{dS}^i+\gamma\right)C_S^i\end{aligned}`<br>[판독 기록:14](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/_staging/recovery/read/EFDC/manuals/pdfs/EFDC_Theory_Document_Ver_12.pdf/p127-144.md:14) | 관련 원문 탐색 근거:<br>[ChemFate/caltox.f90:306](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/ChemFate/caltox.f90:306) `            TOXPFW(L,K,NS,NT) = TOXPFW(L,K,NS,NT)/(1. + TOXPFTW(L,K,NT))`<br>[varinit.f90:314](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/varinit.f90:314) `      WCV(NACTIVEWC).VAL0 => TOX(:,:,NT)` | 문서에만 | 문서는 자유 용존상과 담체별 흡착상을 독립 상태로 풀며 흡착·탈착 속도 및 포화 흡착량을 사용한다. 코드는 총농도 TOX를 이송하고 평형 분율을 대수적으로 계산한다. 대응하는 유한속도 흡착 방정식은 없다. |
| [Theory](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/manuals/pdfs/EFDC_Theory_Document_Ver_12.pdf) PDF 129쪽 / 인쇄 116쪽, 식 **7.17**<br>`\displaystyle \begin{aligned}&\frac{\partial}{\partial t}(mHC)+\frac{1}{m}\frac{\partial}{\partial x}(m_yHuC)+\frac{1}{m}\frac{\partial}{\partial y}(m_xvC)\\&\quad+\frac{\partial}{\partial z}(m_xm_ywC)-\frac{\partial}{\partial z}\left(m\sum_i w_S^i f_S^i C\right)\\&=\frac{\partial}{\partial z}\left(m\frac{A_b}{H}\frac{\partial C}{\partial z}\right)-mH\gamma C\end{aligned}`<br>[판독 기록:15](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/_staging/recovery/read/EFDC/manuals/pdfs/EFDC_Theory_Document_Ver_12.pdf/p127-144.md:15) | [Transport/caltran.f90:170](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/Transport/caltran.f90:170) `                                                     FUHUD(L,K,IW)-FUHUD(LEC(L),K,IW) + FVHUD(L,K,IW)-FVHUD(LNC(L),K,IW) ) * DXYIP(L)   &`<br>[Transport/caltran.f90:171](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/Transport/caltran.f90:171) `                                                  + (FWUU(L,K-1,IW)-FWUU(L,K,IW)) ) `<br>[ChemFate/caltox.f90:490](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/ChemFate/caltox.f90:490) `            TOXF(L,K,NT) = TOXF(L,K,NT)/(1. + TOXPFTW(L,K+1,NT))`<br>[varinit.f90:314](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/varinit.f90:314) `      WCV(NACTIVEWC).VAL0 => TOX(:,:,NT)` | 불일치 | 총농도 이송·침강·수직 확산·반응 처리는 존재한다. 인쇄 식은 x·y 미분 앞에 추가 1/m을 두고 y 이송항에서 H를 빠뜨린다. 코드는 면적·실제 층 두께로 보존량을 환산한다. 인쇄 식 그대로의 계량항은 아니다. |
