| [Theory](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/manuals/pdfs/EFDC_Theory_Document_Ver_12.pdf) PDF 130쪽 / 인쇄 117쪽, 식 **7.21**<br>`\displaystyle \begin{aligned}&-\frac{A_b}{H}\frac{\partial C}{\partial z}-\sum_i w_S^i f_S^i C\\&=\sum_i\left(\left(\max\left(J_{SBS}^i\frac{C_S^i}{S^i},0\right)+(1+\varepsilon)\max\left(\frac{J_{SB}^i}{\rho_S^i},0\right)\right)\left(C_w+\sum_j C_D^j\right)\right)_{SB}\\&\quad+\sum_i\left(\left(\min\left(J_{SBS}^i\frac{C_S^i}{S^i},0\right)+(1+\varepsilon_{dep})\min\left(\frac{J_{SBS}^i}{\rho_S^i},0\right)\right)\left(C_w+\sum_j C_D^j\right)\right)_{WC}\\&\quad+\sum_i\left(\left((1+\varepsilon)\max\left(\frac{J_{SBB}^i}{\rho_S^i},0\right)\right)\left(C_w+\sum_j C_D^j\right)\right)_{SB}\\&\quad+\sum_i\left(\left((1+\varepsilon_{dep})\min\left(\frac{j_{SBB}^i}{\rho_S^i},0\right)\right)\left(C_w+\sum_j C_D^j\right)\right)_{WC}\\&\quad+\left(\left(\max(q_w,0)+q_{dif}\right)\frac{1}{\phi}\left(C_w+\sum_j C_D^j\right)\right)_{SB}\\&\quad+\left(\left(\min(q_w,0)-q_{dif}\right)\frac{1}{\phi}\left(C_w+\sum_j C_D^j\right)\right)_{WC}\end{aligned}`<br>[판독 기록:16](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/_staging/recovery/read/EFDC/manuals/pdfs/EFDC_Theory_Document_Ver_12.pdf/p127-144.md:16) | [ChemFate/caltox.f90:1016](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/ChemFate/caltox.f90:1016) `        TOXF(L,0,NT) = TOXF(L,0,NT) + TMPVAL*(1.-TOXPFTW(L,KSZ(L),NT))`<br>[ChemFate/caltox.f90:1030](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/ChemFate/caltox.f90:1030) `        TOXFB(L,NT) = TOXFB(L,NT) + TMPVAL*(1.-TOXPFTB(L,K,NT))`<br>[ChemFate/caltoxb.f90:195](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/ChemFate/caltoxb.f90:195) `        ALOW(L,KBTP1,NT) = -MAX(QWTRBED(L,KBT(L)),0.) - DIFBWFAC*DIFTOXBW(L,NT)*PORBED(L,KBT(L))       ! *** Surface diffusion/flux` | 불일치 | 공극수 유량을 용존 분율과 결합하는 처리는 존재한다. 인쇄 식은 입자상 J·CS/S 항을 다시 용존 농도로 곱한다. 코드는 입자상 플럭스와 용존상 플럭스를 분리한다. |
| [Theory](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/manuals/pdfs/EFDC_Theory_Document_Ver_12.pdf) PDF 130쪽 / 인쇄 117쪽, 식 **7.22**<br>`\displaystyle S^i=\frac{F^i\rho_s^i}{1+\varepsilon}`<br>[판독 기록:16](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/_staging/recovery/read/EFDC/manuals/pdfs/EFDC_Theory_Document_Ver_12.pdf/p127-144.md:16) | [SedTran-Original/ssedtox.f90:188](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/SedTran-Original/ssedtox.f90:188) `              VFRBED(L,K,NS)  = SDEN(NS)*SEDB(L,K,NS)`<br>[SedTran-Original/ssedtox.f90:236](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/SedTran-Original/ssedtox.f90:236) `                VFRBED(L,K,NS) = VFRBED(L,K,NS)/BEDLINIT(L,K)`<br>[SedTran-Original/ssedtox.f90:1055](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/SedTran-Original/ssedtox.f90:1055) `          VDRBED(L,K) = PORBED(L,K)/(1. - PORBED(L,K))` | 일치 | SEDB는 면적당 건조 질량이다. SDEN은 입자 밀도의 역수이다. 입자별 고체 부피를 정규화한 VFRBED와 공극비를 사용하면 문서의 전체층 부피 기준 농도 관계가 된다. |
| [Theory](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/manuals/pdfs/EFDC_Theory_Document_Ver_12.pdf) PDF 130쪽 / 인쇄 117쪽, 식 **7.23**<br>`\displaystyle F^i=\left(\sum_i\left(\frac{S^i}{\rho_s^i}\right)\right)^{-1}\left(\frac{S^i}{\rho_s^i}\right)`<br>[판독 기록:16](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/_staging/recovery/read/EFDC/manuals/pdfs/EFDC_Theory_Document_Ver_12.pdf/p127-144.md:16) | [SedTran-Original/ssedtox.f90:188](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/SedTran-Original/ssedtox.f90:188) `              VFRBED(L,K,NS)  = SDEN(NS)*SEDB(L,K,NS)`<br>[SedTran-Original/ssedtox.f90:212](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/SedTran-Original/ssedtox.f90:212) `              BEDLINIT(L,K) = BEDLINIT(L,K)+VFRBED(L,K,NS)`<br>[SedTran-Original/ssedtox.f90:236](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/SedTran-Original/ssedtox.f90:236) `                VFRBED(L,K,NS) = VFRBED(L,K,NS)/BEDLINIT(L,K)` | 일치 | 입자별 질량·밀도 역수의 곱을 모든 입자의 고체 부피 합으로 나눈다. HBED는 분자와 분모에서 소거된다. |
| [Theory](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/manuals/pdfs/EFDC_Theory_Document_Ver_12.pdf) PDF 131쪽 / 인쇄 118쪽, 식 **7.24**<br>`\displaystyle \begin{aligned}&-\frac{A_b}{H}\frac{\partial C}{\partial z}-\sum_i w_S^i f_S^i C\\&=\sum_i\left(\max\left(\frac{J_{SBS}^i f_S^i}{S^i},0\right)C+\max\left(\frac{F^i J_{SBS}^i}{S^i},0\right)\left(f_w+\sum_j f_D^j\right)C\right)_{SB}\\&\quad+\sum_i\left(\min\left(\frac{J_{SBS}^i f_S^i}{S^i},0\right)C+\min\left(\frac{F_{dep}^i J_{SBS}^i}{S_{dep}^i},0\right)\left(f_w+\sum_j f_D^j\right)C\right)_{WC}\\&\quad+\sum_i\left(\left((1+\varepsilon)\max\left(\frac{J_{SBB}^i}{\rho_S^i},0\right)\right)\left(C_w+\sum_j C_D^j\right)\right)_{SB}\\&\quad+\sum_i\left(\left((1+\varepsilon_{dep})\min\left(\frac{J_{SBB}^i}{\rho_S^i},0\right)\right)\left(C_w+\sum_j C_D^j\right)\right)_{WC}\\&\quad+\left(\left(\max(q_w,0)+q_{dif}\right)\frac{1}{\phi}\left(f_w+\sum_j f_D^j\right)C\right)_{SB}\\&\quad+\left(\left(\min(q_w,0)-q_{dif}\right)\frac{1}{\phi}\left(f_w+\sum_j f_D^j\right)C\right)_{WC}\end{aligned}`<br>[판독 기록:17](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/_staging/recovery/read/EFDC/manuals/pdfs/EFDC_Theory_Document_Ver_12.pdf/p127-144.md:17) | [ChemFate/caltox.f90:794](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/ChemFate/caltox.f90:794) `            TOXPFTB(L,K,NT) = ( TOXPFTB(L,K,NT)/( PORBED(L,K)*HBED(L,K) + TOXPFTB(L,K,NT) ) ) - TOXPFB(L,K,NFD,NT)`<br>[ChemFate/caltox.f90:795](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/ChemFate/caltox.f90:795) `            TOXPFTB(L,K,NT) = max(TOXPFTB(L,K,NT),SORBMIN)`<br>[ChemFate/caltox.f90:1016](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/ChemFate/caltox.f90:1016) `        TOXF(L,0,NT) = TOXF(L,0,NT) + TMPVAL*(1.-TOXPFTW(L,KSZ(L),NT))`<br>[ChemFate/caltox.f90:1030](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/ChemFate/caltox.f90:1030) `        TOXFB(L,NT) = TOXFB(L,NT) + TMPVAL*(1.-TOXPFTB(L,K,NT))`<br>[ChemFate/caltoxb.f90:195](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/ChemFate/caltoxb.f90:195) `        ALOW(L,KBTP1,NT) = -MAX(QWTRBED(L,KBT(L)),0.) - DIFBWFAC*DIFTOXBW(L,NT)*PORBED(L,KBT(L))       ! *** Surface diffusion/flux` | 불일치 | 문서의 고형물 플럭스와 용존 분율 플럭스 분리는 코드에 존재한다. 퇴적층의 총입자상 분율은 코드에서 최소 0.99로 제한한다. 따라서 침식 공극수에 곱하는 1−TOXPFTB는 문서의 평형 용존 분율과 일반적으로 다르다. |
| [Theory](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/manuals/pdfs/EFDC_Theory_Document_Ver_12.pdf) PDF 131쪽 / 인쇄 118쪽, 식 **7.25**<br>`\displaystyle (HC)^{n+1/4}-(HC)^n+\frac{\Delta t}{m}\frac{\partial}{\partial x}(m_yHuC)+\frac{\Delta t}{m}\frac{\partial}{\partial y}(m_xHvC)+\Delta t\frac{\partial(wC)}{\partial z}=0`<br>[판독 기록:17](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/_staging/recovery/read/EFDC/manuals/pdfs/EFDC_Theory_Document_Ver_12.pdf/p127-144.md:17) | [Transport/caltran.f90:170](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/Transport/caltran.f90:170) `                                                     FUHUD(L,K,IW)-FUHUD(LEC(L),K,IW) + FVHUD(L,K,IW)-FVHUD(LNC(L),K,IW) ) * DXYIP(L)   &`<br>[Transport/caltran.f90:171](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/Transport/caltran.f90:171) `                                                  + (FWUU(L,K-1,IW)-FWUU(L,K,IW)) ) `<br>[Transport/caltran.f90:185](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/Transport/caltran.f90:185) `                                                  + (FWUU(L,K-1,IW)-FWUU(L,K,IW))  )  ` | 일치 | 수평·수직 면 플럭스 차를 보존량에 더하고 실제 층 두께로 농도로 환산한다. 문서의 n+1/4는 연산 분할 단계 표기이다. 코드가 Δt/4로 적분한다는 뜻으로 해석하지 않았다. |
| [Theory](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/manuals/pdfs/EFDC_Theory_Document_Ver_12.pdf) PDF 132쪽 / 인쇄 119쪽, 식 **7.26**<br>`\displaystyle wC=0:\ z=0,1`<br>[판독 기록:18](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/_staging/recovery/read/EFDC/manuals/pdfs/EFDC_Theory_Document_Ver_12.pdf/p127-144.md:18) | 관련 원문 탐색 근거:<br>[Transport/caltran.f90:156](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/Transport/caltran.f90:156) `        FWUU(L,K,IW) = W2(L,K)*CON1(L,KUPW(L,K))  `<br>[Transport/caltran.f90:171](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/Transport/caltran.f90:171) `                                                  + (FWUU(L,K-1,IW)-FWUU(L,K,IW)) ) ` | 코드에서 찾지 못함 | CALTRAN은 W2가 만드는 수직 이송 플럭스를 사용한다. 이 함수에서 자유수면·하상의 W2를 동시에 0으로 지정하는 원문 줄은 찾지 못했다. 유체역학 전체의 경계조건 증명으로 범위를 확장하지 않았다. |
| [Theory](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/manuals/pdfs/EFDC_Theory_Document_Ver_12.pdf) PDF 132쪽 / 인쇄 119쪽, 식 **7.27**<br>`\displaystyle (H)^{n+1/2}-(HC)^{n+1/4}=\Delta t\frac{\partial}{\partial z}\left(\sum_i w_S^i f_S^i C\right)`<br>[판독 기록:18](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/_staging/recovery/read/EFDC/manuals/pdfs/EFDC_Theory_Document_Ver_12.pdf/p127-144.md:18) | [ChemFate/caltox.f90:1199](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/ChemFate/caltox.f90:1199) `          TOX(L,K,NT) = CRIGHT/CLEFT`<br>[ChemFate/caltox.f90:1208](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/ChemFate/caltox.f90:1208) `            TOX(L,K,NT) = CRIGHT/CLEFT                                    ! *** MG/M3` | 불일치 | 코드는 농도와 실제 층 두께를 사용하는 침강 갱신을 수행한다. 인쇄 좌변 첫 항은 (H)이며 C가 빠져 있다. 인쇄 식 그대로는 농도 보존량 식이 아니다. |
| [Theory](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/manuals/pdfs/EFDC_Theory_Document_Ver_12.pdf) PDF 132쪽 / 인쇄 119쪽, 식 **7.28**<br>`\displaystyle \begin{aligned}-\sum_i w_S^i f_S^i C&=\sum_i\left(\max\left(\frac{J_{SBS}^i f_S^i}{S^i},0\right)C+\max\left(\frac{F^i J_{SBS}^i}{S^i},0\right)\left(f_w+\sum_j f_D^j\right)C\right)_{SB}\\&\quad+\sum_i\left(\min\left(\frac{J_{SBS}^i f_S^i}{S^i},0\right)C+\min\left(\frac{F_{dep}^i J_{SBS}^i}{S_{dep}^i},0\right)\left(f_w+\sum_j f_D^j\right)C\right)_{WC}\\&\quad+\sum_i\left(\left((1+\varepsilon)\max\left(\frac{J_{SBB}^i}{\rho_S^i},0\right)\right)\left(C_w+\sum_j C_D^j\right)\right)_{SB}\\&=\sum_i\left(\left((1+\varepsilon_{dep})\min\left(\frac{J_{SBB}^i}{\rho_S^i},0\right)\right)\left(C_w+\sum_j C_D^j\right)\right)_{WC}:\ z=0\end{aligned}`<br>[판독 기록:18](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/_staging/recovery/read/EFDC/manuals/pdfs/EFDC_Theory_Document_Ver_12.pdf/p127-144.md:18) | [ChemFate/caltox.f90:615](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/ChemFate/caltox.f90:615) `        TOXF(L,0,NT) = TOXF(L,0,NT)/(1. + TOXPFTW(L,KSZ(L),NT))`<br>[ChemFate/caltox.f90:694](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/ChemFate/caltox.f90:694) `          TOXFB(L,NT) = TOXFB(L,NT)/TMPVAL`<br>[ChemFate/caltox.f90:1016](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/ChemFate/caltox.f90:1016) `        TOXF(L,0,NT) = TOXF(L,0,NT) + TMPVAL*(1.-TOXPFTW(L,KSZ(L),NT))`<br>[ChemFate/caltox.f90:1030](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/ChemFate/caltox.f90:1030) `        TOXFB(L,NT) = TOXFB(L,NT) + TMPVAL*(1.-TOXPFTB(L,K,NT))` | 불일치 | 침식·퇴적·공극수 동반 플럭스는 존재한다. 인쇄 식 마지막 두 플럭스 사이에는 더하기 대신 등호가 있다. 코드의 두 플럭스는 합산 항이다. 퇴적층 분율 제한의 차이도 적용된다. |
| [Theory](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/manuals/pdfs/EFDC_Theory_Document_Ver_12.pdf) PDF 132쪽 / 인쇄 119쪽, 식 **7.29**<br>`\displaystyle w_S^i f_S^i C=0:\ z=1`<br>[판독 기록:18](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/_staging/recovery/read/EFDC/manuals/pdfs/EFDC_Theory_Document_Ver_12.pdf/p127-144.md:18) | [ChemFate/caltox.f90:360](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/ChemFate/caltox.f90:360) `          TOXF(L,K,NT) = 0.`<br>[ChemFate/caltox.f90:487](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/ChemFate/caltox.f90:487) `        do K = 1,KS` | 일치 | TOXF는 모든 층에서 0으로 초기화한다. 침강 계수는 1부터 KS까지만 만든다. 표면 KC의 침강 계수는 0이다. |
| [Theory](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/manuals/pdfs/EFDC_Theory_Document_Ver_12.pdf) PDF 132쪽 / 인쇄 119쪽, 식 **7.30**<br>`\displaystyle \begin{aligned}\Delta_k(HC)_k^{n+\frac12}-\Delta_k(HC)_k^{n+\frac14}&=\Delta t\sum_i\left(\frac{(w_S^i S^i)_{k+}}{H}\left(\frac{f_S^i}{S^i}\right)_{k+1}\right)^{n+\frac12}(HC)_{k+1}^{n+\frac12}\\&\quad-\Delta t\sum_i\left(\frac{(w_S^i S^i)_{k-}}{H}\left(\frac{f_S^i}{S^i}\right)_k\right)^{n+\frac12}(HC)_k^{n+\frac12}\end{aligned}`<br>[판독 기록:18](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/_staging/recovery/read/EFDC/manuals/pdfs/EFDC_Theory_Document_Ver_12.pdf/p127-144.md:18) | [ChemFate/caltox.f90:490](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/ChemFate/caltox.f90:490) `            TOXF(L,K,NT) = TOXF(L,K,NT)/(1. + TOXPFTW(L,K+1,NT))`<br>[ChemFate/caltox.f90:1142](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/ChemFate/caltox.f90:1142) `          AA11 = TOXF(L,KSZ(L),NT)*TOX(L,KSZ(L)+1,NT)       ! *** SETTLING FROM TOP OF BOTTOM LAYER,        MG/M2/S`<br>[ChemFate/caltox.f90:1164](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/ChemFate/caltox.f90:1164) `          TOX(L,KSZ(L),NT) = TOX(L,KSZ(L),NT) + DTSED*( BB11 + BB22 - AA11 )*HPKI(L,KSZ(L))`<br>[ChemFate/caltox.f90:1206](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/ChemFate/caltox.f90:1206) `            CLEFT = WVEL - TOXF(L,K-1,NT)                                 ! *** M/S`<br>[ChemFate/caltox.f90:1208](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/ChemFate/caltox.f90:1208) `            TOX(L,K,NT) = CRIGHT/CLEFT                                    ! *** MG/M3` | 불일치 | KC≥3의 중간층은 갱신한 위층 농도를 쓰는 음해 침강식이다. KC=2는 위층의 기존 농도로 최하층 유입을 구한다. 문서는 모든 내부층 플럭스에 n+1/2 농도를 사용한다. 따라서 모든 수직 격자 설정에 일치하지 않는다. |
| [Theory](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/manuals/pdfs/EFDC_Theory_Document_Ver_12.pdf) PDF 133쪽 / 인쇄 120쪽, 식 **7.31**<br>`\displaystyle \begin{aligned}&\Delta_1(HC)_1^{n+\frac12}-\Delta_1(HC)_1^{n+\frac14}\\&=\Delta t\sum_i\left((w_S^i S^i)_{1+}\left(\frac{f_S^i}{S^i}\right)_2\right)^{n+\frac12}C_2^{n+\frac12}\\&\quad+\Delta t\sum_i\left(\max\left(\frac{J_{SBS}^i f_S^i}{S^i},0\right)+\max\left(\frac{J_{SBS}^i F^i}{S^i},0\right)\left(f_w+\sum_j f_D^j\right)\right)_{sb}^{n+\frac12}C_{sb}^{n+\frac12}\\&\quad+\Delta t\sum_i\left(\min\left(\frac{J_{SBS}^i f_S^i}{S^i},0\right)+\min\left(\frac{J_{SBS}^i F^i}{S^i},0\right)\left(f_w+\sum_j f_D^j\right)\right)_1^{n+\frac12}C_1^{n+\frac12}\\&\quad+\Delta t\sum_i\left((1+\varepsilon)\max\left(\frac{J_{SBB}^i}{\rho_S^i},0\right)\left(f_w+\sum_j f_D^j\right)\right)_{sb}^{n+\frac12}C_{sb}^{n+\frac12}\\&\quad+\Delta t\sum_i\left((1+\varepsilon)\min\left(\frac{J_{SBB}^i}{\rho_S^i},0\right)\left(f_w+\sum_j f_D^j\right)\right)_1^{n+\frac12}C_1^{n+\frac12}\end{aligned}`<br>[판독 기록:19](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/_staging/recovery/read/EFDC/manuals/pdfs/EFDC_Theory_Document_Ver_12.pdf/p127-144.md:19) | [ChemFate/caltox.f90:1142](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/ChemFate/caltox.f90:1142) `          AA11 = TOXF(L,KSZ(L),NT)*TOX(L,KSZ(L)+1,NT)       ! *** SETTLING FROM TOP OF BOTTOM LAYER,        MG/M2/S`<br>[ChemFate/caltox.f90:1143](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/ChemFate/caltox.f90:1143) `          BB11 = TOXFB(L,NT)*TOXB(L,KBT(L),NT)              ! *** EROSIONAL FLUX TO THE WATER COLUMN,       MG/M2/S`<br>[ChemFate/caltox.f90:1144](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/ChemFate/caltox.f90:1144) `          BB22 = TOXF(L,0,NT)*TOX(L,KSZ(L),NT)              ! *** DEPOSITIONAL FLUX FROM THE WATER COLUMN,  MG/M2/S`<br>[ChemFate/caltox.f90:1164](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/ChemFate/caltox.f90:1164) `          TOX(L,KSZ(L),NT) = TOX(L,KSZ(L),NT) + DTSED*( BB11 + BB22 - AA11 )*HPKI(L,KSZ(L))` | 불일치 | 최하층 침식·퇴적 플럭스는 갱신 전 TOXB와 TOX로 구한다. 코드는 가용 질량으로 플럭스를 제한한 뒤 명시적으로 갱신한다. 문서의 n+1/2 수주·퇴적층 동시 음해 결합과 다르다. |
| [Theory](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/manuals/pdfs/EFDC_Theory_Document_Ver_12.pdf) PDF 133쪽 / 인쇄 120쪽, 식 **7.32**<br>`\displaystyle \frac{f_S^i}{S^i}=\frac{P_S^i}{\phi+\sum_i P_S^i S^i+\sum_j P_D^j D^j}`<br>[판독 기록:19](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/_staging/recovery/read/EFDC/manuals/pdfs/EFDC_Theory_Document_Ver_12.pdf/p127-144.md:19) | [ChemFate/caltox.f90:146](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/ChemFate/caltox.f90:146) `                  TOXPFW(L,K,NS,NT) = SED(L,K,NS)*TOXPARW(L,NS,NT)`<br>[ChemFate/caltox.f90:306](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/ChemFate/caltox.f90:306) `            TOXPFW(L,K,NS,NT) = TOXPFW(L,K,NS,NT)/(1. + TOXPFTW(L,K,NT))`<br>[ChemFate/caltox.f90:409](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/ChemFate/caltox.f90:409) `                    TOXF(L,K,NT) = TOXF(L,K,NT) + SEDF(L,K,NS)*TOXPARW(L,NS,NT)`<br>[ChemFate/caltox.f90:490](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/ChemFate/caltox.f90:490) `            TOXF(L,K,NT) = TOXF(L,K,NT)/(1. + TOXPFTW(L,K+1,NT))` | 일치 | 입자 농도와 Kd의 곱을 공통 분모로 나눈 뒤 침강 플럭스에 Kd/분모를 적용한다. 비선형·유기탄소 기반 옵션에서는 해당 유효 Kd를 사용한다. |
| [Theory](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/manuals/pdfs/EFDC_Theory_Document_Ver_12.pdf) PDF 133쪽 / 인쇄 120쪽, 식 **7.33**<br>`\displaystyle (HC)^{n+\frac34}-(HC)^{n+\frac12}=\Delta t\frac{\partial}{\partial z}\left(\frac{A_b}{H}\frac{\partial}{\partial z}C\right)`<br>[판독 기록:19](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/_staging/recovery/read/EFDC/manuals/pdfs/EFDC_Theory_Document_Ver_12.pdf/p127-144.md:19) | [Transport/calconc.f90:354](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/Transport/calconc.f90:354) `        CCUBTMP = RCDZKK*HPI(L)*AB(L,KSZ(L))`<br>[Transport/calconc.f90:366](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/Transport/calconc.f90:366) `          CCLBTMP(L,K) = RCDZKMK*HPI(L)*AB(L,K-1)`<br>[Transport/calconc.f90:393](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/Transport/calconc.f90:393) `          WCV(IW).VAL0(L,K) = WCV(IW).VAL0(L,K)*EEB(L,K)`<br>[Transport/calconc.f90:400](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/Transport/calconc.f90:400) `            WCV(IW).VAL0(L,K) = ( WCV(IW).VAL0(L,K) - CCLBTMP(L,K)*WCV(IW).VAL0(L,K-1) )*EEB(L,K)`<br>[Transport/calconc.f90:408](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/Transport/calconc.f90:408) `            WCV(IW).VAL0(L,K) = WCV(IW).VAL0(L,K) - VCU(L,K)*WCV(IW).VAL0(L,K+1)` | 일치 | 총농도를 수직 삼중대각 행렬의 음해법으로 갱신한다. 층별 계면 계수는 AB와 실제 수직 격자 계수를 사용한다. |
| [Theory](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/manuals/pdfs/EFDC_Theory_Document_Ver_12.pdf) PDF 133쪽 / 인쇄 120쪽, 식 **7.34**<br>`\displaystyle \begin{aligned}-\frac{A_b}{H}\frac{\partial C}{\partial z}&=\left(\left(\max(q_w,0)+q_{dif}\right)\frac{1}{\phi}\left(f_w+\sum_j f_D^j\right)C\right)_{SB}\\&\quad+\left(\left(\min(q_w,0)-q_{dif}\right)\frac{1}{\phi_{dep}}\left(f_w+\sum_j f_D^j\right)C\right)_{WC}:\ z=0\end{aligned}`<br>[판독 기록:19](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/_staging/recovery/read/EFDC/manuals/pdfs/EFDC_Theory_Document_Ver_12.pdf/p127-144.md:19) | [ChemFate/caltoxb.f90:190](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/ChemFate/caltoxb.f90:190) `        CUPP(L,KBT(L),NT) = min(QWTRBED(L,KBT(L)),0.) - DIFBWFAC*DIFTOXBW(L,NT)*PORBED(L,KBT(L))       ! *** Surface diffusion/flux`<br>[ChemFate/caltoxb.f90:195](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/ChemFate/caltoxb.f90:195) `        ALOW(L,KBTP1,NT) = -MAX(QWTRBED(L,KBT(L)),0.) - DIFBWFAC*DIFTOXBW(L,NT)*PORBED(L,KBT(L))       ! *** Surface diffusion/flux`<br>[ChemFate/caltoxb.f90:198](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/ChemFate/caltoxb.f90:198) `          BMNN(L,K,NT) = TOXTIMEI*HBED(L,K)*PORBED(L,K)/(1.-TOXPFTB(L,K,NT))`<br>[ChemFate/caltoxb.f90:200](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/ChemFate/caltoxb.f90:200) `        BMNN(L,KBTP1,NT) = TOXTIMEI*HPK(L,KSZ(L))/(1.-TOXPFTW(L,KSZ(L),NT))                         ! *** Bottom layer of water column` | 불일치 | 공극수 이류·확산과 최하층 수주 농도를 결합한다. 코드는 총입자상 분율을 최소 0.99로 제한한 퇴적층 용존 분율을 사용한다. 문서의 평형 분율을 제한 없이 사용하는 식과 다르다. |
| [Theory](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/manuals/pdfs/EFDC_Theory_Document_Ver_12.pdf) PDF 134쪽 / 인쇄 121쪽, 식 **7.35**<br>`\displaystyle -\frac{A_b}{H}\frac{\partial C}{\partial z}=0:\ z=1`<br>[판독 기록:20](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/_staging/recovery/read/EFDC/manuals/pdfs/EFDC_Theory_Document_Ver_12.pdf/p127-144.md:20) | [Transport/calconc.f90:379](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/Transport/calconc.f90:379) `        CCLBTMP(L,K) = RCDZKMK*HPI(L)*AB(L,K-1)`<br>[Transport/calconc.f90:380](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/Transport/calconc.f90:380) `        CCMBTMP      = 1._8-CCLBTMP(L,K)` | 일치 | 수직 확산의 표면 행은 하부 계면 계수만 포함한다. 표면 상부 확산 플럭스 항은 없다. |
| [Theory](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/manuals/pdfs/EFDC_Theory_Document_Ver_12.pdf) PDF 134쪽 / 인쇄 121쪽, 식 **7.36**<br>`\displaystyle \begin{aligned}(HC)_1^{n+3/4}-(HC)_1^{n+1/2}&=\frac{\Delta t}{\Delta_1}\left(\frac{A_b}{H}\frac{\partial C}{\partial z}\right)_{1+}^{n+\frac34}\\&\quad+\frac{\Delta t}{\Delta_1}\left(\max(q_w,0)+q_{dif}\right)\left(\left(f_w+\sum_j f_D^j\right)\frac{1}{\phi}\right)_{SB}^{n+\frac12}C_{SB}^{n+\frac34}\\&\quad+\frac{\Delta t}{\Delta_1}\left(\min(q_w,0)-q_{dif}\right)\left(\left(f_w+\sum_j f_D^j\right)\frac{1}{\phi_{dep}}\right)_1^{n+\frac12}C_1^{n+\frac12}\end{aligned}`<br>[판독 기록:20](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/_staging/recovery/read/EFDC/manuals/pdfs/EFDC_Theory_Document_Ver_12.pdf/p127-144.md:20) | [ChemFate/caltoxb.f90:195](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/ChemFate/caltoxb.f90:195) `        ALOW(L,KBTP1,NT) = -MAX(QWTRBED(L,KBT(L)),0.) - DIFBWFAC*DIFTOXBW(L,NT)*PORBED(L,KBT(L))       ! *** Surface diffusion/flux`<br>[ChemFate/caltoxb.f90:200](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/ChemFate/caltoxb.f90:200) `        BMNN(L,KBTP1,NT) = TOXTIMEI*HPK(L,KSZ(L))/(1.-TOXPFTW(L,KSZ(L),NT))                         ! *** Bottom layer of water column`<br>[ChemFate/caltoxb.f90:222](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/ChemFate/caltoxb.f90:222) `        BMNN(L,KBTP1,NT) = BMNN(L,KBTP1,NT) - min(QWTRBED(L,KBT(L)),0.) + DIFBWFAC*DIFTOXBW(L,NT)*PORBED(L,KBT(L))       ! *** Surface diffusion/flux`<br>[ChemFate/caltoxb.f90:246](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/ChemFate/caltoxb.f90:246) `          TOXTMP(L,KK,NT) = (RRHS(L,KK,NT) - ALOW(L,KK,NT)*TOXTMP(L,KK+KINC,NT))/BETTMP` | 불일치 | 문서는 수주 유출항에 n+1/2 농도를 사용한다. 코드는 퇴적층과 최하층 수주를 함께 삼중대각 행렬에 넣어 새 용존 농도로 푼다. 수주 수직 확산은 별도 CALCONC 단계에서 푼다. |
| [Theory](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/manuals/pdfs/EFDC_Theory_Document_Ver_12.pdf) PDF 134쪽 / 인쇄 121쪽, 식 **7.37**<br>`\displaystyle (HC)_k^{n+\frac34}-(HC)_k^{n+\frac12}=\frac{\Delta t}{\Delta_1}\left(\frac{A_b}{H}\frac{\partial C}{\partial z}\right)_{k+}^{n+\frac34}-\frac{\Delta t}{\Delta_1}\left(\frac{A_b}{H}\frac{\partial C}{\partial z}\right)_{k-}^{n+\frac34}`<br>[판독 기록:20](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/_staging/recovery/read/EFDC/manuals/pdfs/EFDC_Theory_Document_Ver_12.pdf/p127-144.md:20) | [Transport/calconc.f90:366](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/Transport/calconc.f90:366) `          CCLBTMP(L,K) = RCDZKMK*HPI(L)*AB(L,K-1)`<br>[Transport/calconc.f90:367](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/Transport/calconc.f90:367) `          CCUBTMP      = RCDZKK*HPI(L)*AB(L,K)`<br>[Transport/calconc.f90:400](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/Transport/calconc.f90:400) `            WCV(IW).VAL0(L,K) = ( WCV(IW).VAL0(L,K) - CCLBTMP(L,K)*WCV(IW).VAL0(L,K-1) )*EEB(L,K)` | 불일치 | 인쇄 식은 임의 k층에도 Δ1을 분모로 사용한다. 코드는 k층과 인접층에 맞는 CDZKMK·CDZKK 계수를 사용한다. 층 두께가 다르면 인쇄 식의 계수와 다르다. |
| [Theory](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/manuals/pdfs/EFDC_Theory_Document_Ver_12.pdf) PDF 134쪽 / 인쇄 121쪽, 식 **7.38**<br>`\displaystyle (HC)_k^{n+1}-(H)_k^{n+3/4}=-\Delta t\gamma(HC)_k^{n+1}`<br>[판독 기록:20](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/_staging/recovery/read/EFDC/manuals/pdfs/EFDC_Theory_Document_Ver_12.pdf/p127-144.md:20) | [ChemFate/caltox_kinetics.f90:192](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/ChemFate/caltox_kinetics.f90:192) `            TOX(L,K,NT) = TOX(L,K,NT)*(1.0 - TOXTIME*CDECAYW(L,K))` | 불일치 | 문서는 새 농도에 γ를 곱하는 음해 반응식이다. 코드는 기존 농도에 1−Δt·K를 곱하는 명시 반응식이다. 인쇄 좌변 두 번째 항에는 C도 빠져 있다. |
| [Theory](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/manuals/pdfs/EFDC_Theory_Document_Ver_12.pdf) PDF 135쪽 / 인쇄 122쪽, 식 **7.39**<br>`\displaystyle \begin{aligned}\frac{\partial}{\partial t}(BC)_k&=-\gamma(BC)_k\\&\quad-\delta(k,kt)\sum_i\left(\max\left(\frac{J_{SBS}^i f_S^i}{BS^i},0\right)+\max\left(\frac{J_{SBS}^i F^i}{BS^i},0\right)\left(f_w+\sum_j f_D^j\right)\right)_{kt}(BC)_{kt}\\&\quad-\delta(k,kt)\sum_i\left(\min\left(\frac{J_{SBS}^i f_S^i}{S^i},0\right)C+\min\left(\frac{J_{SBS}^i F_{dep}^i}{S_{dep}^i},0\right)\left(f_w+\sum_j f_D^j\right)C\right)_{WC}\\&\quad-\delta(k,kt)\sum_i(\min(J_{SBB}^i\chi_{SBL}^i,0)-\delta(k,kt)\sum_i\left((1+\varepsilon)\max\left(\frac{J_{SBB}^i}{B\rho_S^i},0\right)\left(f_w+\sum_j f_D^j\right)\right)_{kt}(BC)_{kt}\\&\quad-\delta(k,kt)\sum_i\left((1+\varepsilon_{dep})\min\left(\frac{J_{SBB}^i}{\rho_S^i},0\right)\left(f_w+\sum_j f_D^j\right)C\right)_{WC}\\&\quad-\left(\left(\max(q_w,0)+q_{dif}\right)_{k+}-\left(\min(q_w,0)-q_{dif}\right)_{k-}\right)\left(\frac{1}{\phi B}\left(f_w+\sum_j f_D^j\right)\right)_k(BC)_k\\&\quad-\delta(k,kt)\left(\min(q_w,0)-q_{dif}\right)_{kt+}\left(\frac{1}{\phi}\left(f_w+\sum_j f_D^j\right)C\right)_{WC}\\&\quad-(1-\delta(k,kt))\left(\min(q_w,0)-q_{dif}\right)_{k+}\left(\frac{1}{\phi B}\left(f_w+\sum_j f_D^j\right)\right)_{k+1}(BC)_{k+1}\\&\quad+\left(\max(q_w,0)+q_{dif}\right)_{k-}\left(\frac{1}{\phi B}\left(f_w+\sum_j f_D^j\right)\right)_{k-1}(BC)_{k-1}\end{aligned}`<br>[판독 기록:21](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/_staging/recovery/read/EFDC/manuals/pdfs/EFDC_Theory_Document_Ver_12.pdf/p127-144.md:21) | [ChemFate/caltox.f90:1173](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/ChemFate/caltox.f90:1173) `          TOXB(L,KBT(L),NT) = TOXB(L,KBT(L),NT) - DTSED*( BB22 + BB11 + TOXFBL(L,NT) )`<br>[ChemFate/caltoxb.f90:188](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/ChemFate/caltoxb.f90:188) `          CUPP(L,K,NT) = min(QWTRBED(L,K),0.) - (DIFTOX(NT) + PARTDIF(L,K,NT))*(PORBED(L,K) + PORBED(L,K-KINC))/(HBED(L,K) + HBED(L,K-KINC))`<br>[ChemFate/caltoxb.f90:193](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/ChemFate/caltoxb.f90:193) `          ALOW(L,K,NT) = -MAX(QWTRBED(L,K+KINC),0.)   - (DIFTOX(NT) + PARTDIF(L,K+KINC,NT))*(PORBED(L,K+KINC) + PORBED(L,K))/(HBED(L,K + KINC) + HBED(L,K))`<br>[ChemFate/caltoxb.f90:246](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/ChemFate/caltoxb.f90:246) `          TOXTMP(L,KK,NT) = (RRHS(L,KK,NT) - TOXTIME*CDECAYB(L,K))` | 불일치 | 입자상 교환·공극수 이송·분해 항은 존재한다. 코드는 명시 입자상 교환, 음해 공극수 이송, 명시 분해를 나누어 적용한다. 총입자상 분율 하한과 입자 혼합은 문서 식에 없다. 인쇄 식의 소류사 부분에는 괄호 누락도 있다. |
| [Theory](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/manuals/pdfs/EFDC_Theory_Document_Ver_12.pdf) PDF 135쪽 / 인쇄 122쪽, 식 **7.40**<br>`\displaystyle \delta(k,kt)=\begin{cases}1:\ k=kt\\0:\ k\ne kt\end{cases}`<br>[판독 기록:21](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/_staging/recovery/read/EFDC/manuals/pdfs/EFDC_Theory_Document_Ver_12.pdf/p127-144.md:21) | [ChemFate/caltoxb.f90:213](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/ChemFate/caltoxb.f90:213) `        K = KBT(L)`<br>[ChemFate/caltoxb.f90:215](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/ChemFate/caltoxb.f90:215) `          BMNN(L,K,NT) = BMNN(L,K,NT) + max(QWTRBED(L,K),0.)      + DIFBWFAC*DIFTOXBW(L,NT)*PORBED(L,KBT(L))`<br>[ChemFate/caltox.f90:1173](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/ChemFate/caltox.f90:1173) `          TOXB(L,KBT(L),NT) = TOXB(L,KBT(L),NT) - DTSED*( BB22 + BB11 + TOXFBL(L,NT) )` | 일치 | 수주 교환은 KBT 최상층에만 적용한다. 퇴적층 내부 행렬과 표면 행렬을 분기하는 조건이 Kronecker δ의 역할을 한다. |
| [Theory](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/manuals/pdfs/EFDC_Theory_Document_Ver_12.pdf) PDF 135쪽 / 인쇄 122쪽, 식 **7.41**<br>`\displaystyle \begin{aligned}(f_w)_k&=\left(\frac{BC_w}{BC}\right)_k=\left(\frac{B\phi}{B\phi+\sum_i P_S^i BS^i+\sum_j P_D^j BD^j}\right)_k\\(f_D^j)_k&=\left(\frac{BC_D^j}{BC}\right)_k=\left(\frac{P_D^j BD^j}{B\phi+\sum_i P_S^i BS^i+\sum_j P_D^j BD^j}\right)_k\\(f_S^i)_k&=\left(\frac{BC_S^i}{BC}\right)_k=\left(\frac{P_S^i BS^i}{B\phi+\sum_i P_S^i BS^i+\sum_j P_D^j BD^j}\right)_k\end{aligned}`<br>[판독 기록:21](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/_staging/recovery/read/EFDC/manuals/pdfs/EFDC_Theory_Document_Ver_12.pdf/p127-144.md:21) | [ChemFate/caltoxb.f90:454](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/ChemFate/caltoxb.f90:454) `          TMPVAL = PORBED(L,K)*HBED(L,K) + TOXPFTB(L,K,NT)`<br>[ChemFate/caltoxb.f90:456](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/ChemFate/caltoxb.f90:456) `            TOXPFB(L,K,NS,NT) = TOXPFB(L,K,NS,NT)/TMPVAL`<br>[ChemFate/caltoxb.f90:472](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/ChemFate/caltoxb.f90:472) `            TOXFDFB(L,K,NT) = PORBED(L,K)*HBED(L,K) /(PORBED(L,K)*HBED(L,K) + TOXPFTB(L,K,NT))`<br>[ChemFate/caltoxb.f90:473](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/ChemFate/caltoxb.f90:473) `            TOXCDFB(L,K,NT) = TOXPFB(L,K,NFD,NT)` | 일치 | 면적당 담체량과 공극수 두께를 합친 분모로 담체별 분율과 자유 용존 분율을 구한다. 문서의 BC 표현과 일치한다. 이후 총입자상 분율 하한은 별도 처리이다. |
| [Theory](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/manuals/pdfs/EFDC_Theory_Document_Ver_12.pdf) PDF 136쪽 / 인쇄 123쪽, 식 **7.42**<br>`\displaystyle mJ_{SBB}^i=\frac{\partial}{\partial x}\left(m_yQ_{SBLx}^i\right)+\frac{\partial}{\partial x}\left(m_xQ_{SBLy}^i\right)`<br>[판독 기록:22](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/_staging/recovery/read/EFDC/manuals/pdfs/EFDC_Theory_Document_Ver_12.pdf/p127-144.md:22) | [ChemFate/caltox.f90:860](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/ChemFate/caltox.f90:860) `            TOXFBL(L,NT) = TOXFBL(L,NT) + DXYIP(L)*( SUB(LE)*MAX(QSBDLDX(LE,NX),0.)*TMPTOXC + SUB(LE)*MIN(QSBDLDX(LE,NX),0.)*TMPTOXE  &`<br>[ChemFate/caltox.f90:861](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/ChemFate/caltox.f90:861) `                                                   - SUB(L) *MAX(QSBDLDX(L,NX),0.) *TMPTOXW - SUB(L) *MIN(QSBDLDX(L,NX),0.) *TMPTOXC  &`<br>[ChemFate/caltox.f90:862](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/ChemFate/caltox.f90:862) `                                                   + SVB(LN)*MAX(QSBDLDY(LN,NX),0.)*TMPTOXC + SVB(LN)*MIN(QSBDLDY(LN,NX),0.)*TMPTOXN  &`<br>[ChemFate/caltox.f90:863](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/ChemFate/caltox.f90:863) `                                                   - SVB(L) *MAX(QSBDLDY(L,NX),0.) *TMPTOXS - SVB(L) *MIN(QSBDLDY(L,NX),0.) *TMPTOXC )` | 불일치 | 인쇄 식은 x·y 플럭스의 두 미분을 모두 ∂/∂x로 적는다. 코드는 동·서 면에 QSBDLDX를 사용한다. 코드는 북·남 면에 QSBDLDY를 사용한다. 인쇄된 두 번째 미분과 다르다. |

| [Theory](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/manuals/pdfs/EFDC_Theory_Document_Ver_12.pdf) PDF 136쪽 / 인쇄 123쪽, 식 **7.44**<br>`\displaystyle \frac{\partial}{\partial x}\left(m_yQ_{SBLx}^i\frac{f_S^i}{S^i}C\right)+\frac{\partial}{\partial x}\left(m_xQ_{SBLy}^i\frac{f_S^i}{S^i}C\right)=mJ_{SBB}^i\chi_{SBL}^i`<br>[판독 기록:22](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/_staging/recovery/read/EFDC/manuals/pdfs/EFDC_Theory_Document_Ver_12.pdf/p127-144.md:22) | [ChemFate/caltox.f90:860](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/ChemFate/caltox.f90:860) `            TOXFBL(L,NT) = TOXFBL(L,NT) + DXYIP(L)*( SUB(LE)*MAX(QSBDLDX(LE,NX),0.)*TMPTOXC + SUB(LE)*MIN(QSBDLDX(LE,NX),0.)*TMPTOXE  &`<br>[ChemFate/caltox.f90:861](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/ChemFate/caltox.f90:861) `                                                   - SUB(L) *MAX(QSBDLDX(L,NX),0.) *TMPTOXW - SUB(L) *MIN(QSBDLDX(L,NX),0.) *TMPTOXC  &`<br>[ChemFate/caltox.f90:862](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/ChemFate/caltox.f90:862) `                                                   + SVB(LN)*MAX(QSBDLDY(LN,NX),0.)*TMPTOXC + SVB(LN)*MIN(QSBDLDY(LN,NX),0.)*TMPTOXN  &`<br>[ChemFate/caltox.f90:863](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/ChemFate/caltox.f90:863) `                                                   - SVB(L) *MAX(QSBDLDY(L,NX),0.) *TMPTOXS - SVB(L) *MIN(QSBDLDY(L,NX),0.) *TMPTOXC )` | 불일치 | 인쇄 식은 x·y 플럭스의 두 미분을 모두 ∂/∂x로 적는다. 코드는 동·서 면에 QSBDLDX를 사용한다. 코드는 북·남 면에 QSBDLDY를 사용한다. 인쇄된 두 번째 미분과 다르다. |
| [Theory](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/manuals/pdfs/EFDC_Theory_Document_Ver_12.pdf) PDF 136쪽 / 인쇄 123쪽, 식 **7.45**<br>`\displaystyle \begin{aligned}mJ_{SB}^i\chi_{SBL}^i&=\max\left(m_yQ_{SBLx}^i\right)_E\left(\frac{f_S^i}{S^i}C\right)_C+\min\left(m_yQ_{SBLx}^i\right)_E\left(\frac{f_S^i}{S^i}C\right)_E\\&\quad-\max\left(m_yQ_{SBLx}^i\right)_W\left(\frac{f_S^i}{S^i}C\right)_W-\min\left(m_yQ_{SBLx}^i\right)_W\left(\frac{f_S^i}{S^i}C\right)_C\\&\quad+\max\left(m_xQ_{SBLy}^i\right)_N\left(\frac{f_S^i}{S^i}\right)_C+\min\left(m_xQ_{SBy}^i\right)_N\left(\frac{f_S^i}{S^i}C\right)_N\\&\quad-\max\left(m_xQ_{SBLy}^i\right)_S\left(\frac{f_S^i}{S^i}C\right)_S-\min\left(m_xQ_{SBLy}^i\right)_S\left(\frac{f_S^i}{S^i}C\right)_C\end{aligned}`<br>[판독 기록:22](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/_staging/recovery/read/EFDC/manuals/pdfs/EFDC_Theory_Document_Ver_12.pdf/p127-144.md:22) | [ChemFate/caltox.f90:840](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/ChemFate/caltox.f90:840) `              TMPTOXC = TOXPFB(L,K,NS,NT)*TOXB(L,K,NT)/SNDB1(L,K,NX) `<br>[ChemFate/caltox.f90:852](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/ChemFate/caltox.f90:852) `              TMPTOXN = TOXPFB(LN,K,NS,NT)*TOXB(LN,K,NT)/SNDB1(LN,K,NX)`<br>[ChemFate/caltox.f90:862](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/ChemFate/caltox.f90:862) `                                                   + SVB(LN)*MAX(QSBDLDY(LN,NX),0.)*TMPTOXC + SVB(LN)*MIN(QSBDLDY(LN,NX),0.)*TMPTOXN  &` | 불일치 | 북쪽 양의 소류사 플럭스 항에서 인쇄 식은 중앙셀 C를 빠뜨린다. 코드는 중앙셀의 TOXPFB·TOXB/SNDB1을 곱한다. 나머지 동·서·북·남 풍상 분기는 대응한다. |
| [Theory](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/manuals/pdfs/EFDC_Theory_Document_Ver_12.pdf) PDF 137쪽 / 인쇄 124쪽, 식 **7.46**<br>`\displaystyle \begin{aligned}&(BC)_{kt}^{n+\frac12}-(BC)_{kt}^n\\&=-\Delta t\sum_i\left(\max\left(\frac{J_{SBS}^i f_S^i}{BS^i},0\right)+\max\left(\frac{J_{SBS}^i F^i}{BS^i},0\right)\left(f_w+\sum_j f_D^j\right)\right)_{kt}^{n+\frac12}(BC)_{kt}^{n+\frac12}\\&\quad-\Delta t\sum_i\left(\min\left(\frac{J_{SBS}^i f_S^i}{S^i},0\right)C+\min\left(\frac{J_{SBS}^i F_{dep}^i}{S_{dep}^i},0\right)\left(f_w+\sum_j f_D^j\right)C\right)_{WC}^{n+\frac12}\\&\quad-\Delta t\sum_i\left(J_{SBB}^i\chi_{SBL}^i,0\right)\\&\quad-\Delta t\sum_i\left((1+\varepsilon)\max\left(\frac{J_{SBB}^i}{B\rho_S^i},0\right)\left(f_w+\sum_j f_D^j\right)\right)_{kt}^{n+\frac12}(BC)_{kt}^{n+\frac12}\\&\quad-\Delta t\sum_i\left((1+\varepsilon_{dep})\min\left(\frac{J_{SBB}^i}{\rho_S^i},0\right)\left(f_w+\sum_j f_D^j\right)C\right)_{WC}^{n+\frac12}\end{aligned}`<br>[판독 기록:23](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/_staging/recovery/read/EFDC/manuals/pdfs/EFDC_Theory_Document_Ver_12.pdf/p127-144.md:23) | [ChemFate/caltox.f90:1143](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/ChemFate/caltox.f90:1143) `          BB11 = TOXFB(L,NT)*TOXB(L,KBT(L),NT)              ! *** EROSIONAL FLUX TO THE WATER COLUMN,       MG/M2/S`<br>[ChemFate/caltox.f90:1144](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/ChemFate/caltox.f90:1144) `          BB22 = TOXF(L,0,NT)*TOX(L,KSZ(L),NT)              ! *** DEPOSITIONAL FLUX FROM THE WATER COLUMN,  MG/M2/S`<br>[ChemFate/caltox.f90:1173](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/ChemFate/caltox.f90:1173) `          TOXB(L,KBT(L),NT) = TOXB(L,KBT(L),NT) - DTSED*( BB22 + BB11 + TOXFBL(L,NT) )` | 불일치 | 문서는 최상층 퇴적량을 새 시간 농도에 결합한다. 코드는 기존 수주·퇴적층 농도로 구한 BB11·BB22와 소류사 플럭스를 명시적으로 적용한다. |
| [Theory](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/manuals/pdfs/EFDC_Theory_Document_Ver_12.pdf) PDF 137쪽 / 인쇄 124쪽, 식 **7.47**<br>`\displaystyle \begin{bmatrix}a_{11}&a_{12}\\a_{21}&a_{22}\end{bmatrix}\left\{\begin{array}{c}(BC)_{kt}^{n+\frac12}\\(HC)_1^{n+\frac12}\end{array}\right\}\left\{\begin{array}{c}(BC)_{kt}^n-\Delta t\sum_i\left(J_S^i\chi_{SBL}^i\right)^{n+\frac12}\\i=ib\\\Delta_1C_1^{n+\frac14}+\Delta t\sum_i\left((w_S^i S^i)_{1+}\left(\frac{f_S^i}{S^i}\right)_2\right)^{n+\frac12}C_2^{n+\frac12}\end{array}\right\}`<br>[판독 기록:23](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/_staging/recovery/read/EFDC/manuals/pdfs/EFDC_Theory_Document_Ver_12.pdf/p127-144.md:23) | [ChemFate/caltox.f90:1143](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/ChemFate/caltox.f90:1143) `          BB11 = TOXFB(L,NT)*TOXB(L,KBT(L),NT)              ! *** EROSIONAL FLUX TO THE WATER COLUMN,       MG/M2/S`<br>[ChemFate/caltox.f90:1144](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/ChemFate/caltox.f90:1144) `          BB22 = TOXF(L,0,NT)*TOX(L,KSZ(L),NT)              ! *** DEPOSITIONAL FLUX FROM THE WATER COLUMN,  MG/M2/S`<br>[ChemFate/caltox.f90:1164](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/ChemFate/caltox.f90:1164) `          TOX(L,KSZ(L),NT) = TOX(L,KSZ(L),NT) + DTSED*( BB11 + BB22 - AA11 )*HPKI(L,KSZ(L))`<br>[ChemFate/caltox.f90:1173](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/ChemFate/caltox.f90:1173) `          TOXB(L,KBT(L),NT) = TOXB(L,KBT(L),NT) - DTSED*( BB22 + BB11 + TOXFBL(L,NT) )` | 불일치 | 코드는 수주·퇴적층 2×2 음해 행렬을 만들지 않는다. AA11은 위층 침강 유입 플럭스이다. 인쇄 식은 행렬식의 등호와 우변 수주 항의 H를 빠뜨린다. |
| [Theory](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/manuals/pdfs/EFDC_Theory_Document_Ver_12.pdf) PDF 137쪽 / 인쇄 124쪽, 식 **7.48**<br>`\displaystyle \begin{aligned}a_{11}&=1+\Delta t\sum_i\left(\max\left(\frac{J_{SBS}^i f_S^i}{BS^i},0\right)+\max\left(\frac{J_{SBS}^i F^i}{BS^i},0\right)\left(f_w+\sum_j f_D^j\right)\right)_{kt}^{n+\frac12}\\&\quad+\Delta t\sum_i\left((1+\varepsilon)\max\left(\frac{J_{SBB}^i}{B\rho_S^i},0\right)\left(f_w+\sum_j f_D^j\right)\right)_{kt}^{n+\frac12}\end{aligned}`<br>[판독 기록:23](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/_staging/recovery/read/EFDC/manuals/pdfs/EFDC_Theory_Document_Ver_12.pdf/p127-144.md:23) | [ChemFate/caltox.f90:1143](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/ChemFate/caltox.f90:1143) `          BB11 = TOXFB(L,NT)*TOXB(L,KBT(L),NT)              ! *** EROSIONAL FLUX TO THE WATER COLUMN,       MG/M2/S`<br>[ChemFate/caltox.f90:1173](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/ChemFate/caltox.f90:1173) `          TOXB(L,KBT(L),NT) = TOXB(L,KBT(L),NT) - DTSED*( BB22 + BB11 + TOXFBL(L,NT) )` | 불일치 | 문서의 a11은 퇴적층의 새 질량에 곱하는 침식 계수이다. 코드는 침식 계수에 기존 TOXB를 곱해 BB11을 만들고 직접 뺀다. |
| [Theory](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/manuals/pdfs/EFDC_Theory_Document_Ver_12.pdf) PDF 137쪽 / 인쇄 124쪽, 식 **7.49**<br>`\displaystyle \begin{aligned}a_{12}&=\frac{\Delta t}{H}\sum_i\left(\min\left(\frac{J_{SBS}^i f_S^i}{S^i},0\right)+\min\left(\frac{J_{SBS}^i F_{dep}^i}{S_{dep}^i},0\right)\left(f_w+\sum_j f_D^j\right)\right)_1^{n+\frac12}\\&\quad+\frac{\Delta t}{H}\sum_i\left((1+\varepsilon_{dep})\min\left(\frac{J_{SBB}^i}{\rho_S^i},0\right)\left(f_w+\sum_j f_D^j\right)\right)_1^{n+\frac12}\end{aligned}`<br>[판독 기록:23](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/_staging/recovery/read/EFDC/manuals/pdfs/EFDC_Theory_Document_Ver_12.pdf/p127-144.md:23) | [ChemFate/caltox.f90:1144](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/ChemFate/caltox.f90:1144) `          BB22 = TOXF(L,0,NT)*TOX(L,KSZ(L),NT)              ! *** DEPOSITIONAL FLUX FROM THE WATER COLUMN,  MG/M2/S`<br>[ChemFate/caltox.f90:1173](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/ChemFate/caltox.f90:1173) `          TOXB(L,KBT(L),NT) = TOXB(L,KBT(L),NT) - DTSED*( BB22 + BB11 + TOXFBL(L,NT) )` | 불일치 | 문서의 a12는 새 수주 농도를 퇴적층 행렬에 결합한다. 코드는 기존 TOX에 퇴적 계수를 곱한 BB22를 퇴적층 갱신에 사용한다. |
| [Theory](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/manuals/pdfs/EFDC_Theory_Document_Ver_12.pdf) PDF 138쪽 / 인쇄 125쪽, 식 **7.50**<br>`\displaystyle \begin{aligned}a_{21}&=-\Delta t\sum_i\left(\max\left(\frac{J_{SBS}^i f_S^i}{BS^i},0\right)+\max\left(\frac{J_{SBS}^i F^i}{BS^i},0\right)\left(f_w+\sum_j f_D^j\right)\right)_{kt}^{n+\frac12}\\&\quad-\Delta t\sum_i\left((1+\varepsilon)\max\left(\frac{J_{SBB}^i}{B\rho_S^i},0\right)\left(f_w+\sum_j f_D^j\right)\right)_{kt}^{n+\frac12}\end{aligned}`<br>[판독 기록:24](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/_staging/recovery/read/EFDC/manuals/pdfs/EFDC_Theory_Document_Ver_12.pdf/p127-144.md:24) | [ChemFate/caltox.f90:1143](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/ChemFate/caltox.f90:1143) `          BB11 = TOXFB(L,NT)*TOXB(L,KBT(L),NT)              ! *** EROSIONAL FLUX TO THE WATER COLUMN,       MG/M2/S`<br>[ChemFate/caltox.f90:1164](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/ChemFate/caltox.f90:1164) `          TOX(L,KSZ(L),NT) = TOX(L,KSZ(L),NT) + DTSED*( BB11 + BB22 - AA11 )*HPKI(L,KSZ(L))` | 불일치 | 문서의 a21은 새 퇴적층 질량을 수주 행렬에 결합한다. 코드는 기존 TOXB로 구한 BB11을 수주에 더한다. |
| [Theory](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/manuals/pdfs/EFDC_Theory_Document_Ver_12.pdf) PDF 138쪽 / 인쇄 125쪽, 식 **7.51**<br>`\displaystyle \begin{aligned}a_{22}&=\Delta_1-\frac{\Delta t}{H}\sum_i\left(\min\left(\frac{J_{SBS}^i f_S^i}{S^i},0\right)+\min\left(\frac{J_{SBS}^i F^i}{S^i},0\right)\left(f_w+\sum_j f_D^j\right)\right)_1^{n+\frac12}\\&\quad-\frac{\Delta t}{H}\sum_i\left((1+\varepsilon)\min\left(\frac{J_{SBB}^i}{\rho_S^i},0\right)\left(f_w+\sum_j f_D^j\right)\right)_1^{n+\frac12}\end{aligned}`<br>[판독 기록:24](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/_staging/recovery/read/EFDC/manuals/pdfs/EFDC_Theory_Document_Ver_12.pdf/p127-144.md:24) | [ChemFate/caltox.f90:1144](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/ChemFate/caltox.f90:1144) `          BB22 = TOXF(L,0,NT)*TOX(L,KSZ(L),NT)              ! *** DEPOSITIONAL FLUX FROM THE WATER COLUMN,  MG/M2/S`<br>[ChemFate/caltox.f90:1147](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/ChemFate/caltox.f90:1147) `          TMPVAL = TOX(L,KSZ(L),NT) + DTSED*( BB22 - AA11 )*HPKI(L,KSZ(L))`<br>[ChemFate/caltox.f90:1164](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/ChemFate/caltox.f90:1164) `          TOX(L,KSZ(L),NT) = TOX(L,KSZ(L),NT) + DTSED*( BB11 + BB22 - AA11 )*HPKI(L,KSZ(L))` | 불일치 | 문서의 a22는 새 수주 보존량의 대각 계수이다. 코드는 기존 TOX로 구한 BB22를 직접 적용하고 가용 수주 질량으로 제한한다. |
| [Theory](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/manuals/pdfs/EFDC_Theory_Document_Ver_12.pdf) PDF 138쪽 / 인쇄 125쪽, 식 **7.52**<br>`\displaystyle \begin{aligned}(BC)_{kt}^{n+\frac12}+\Delta_1(HC)_1^{n+\frac12}&=(BC)_{kt}^n+\Delta_1(HC)_1^{n+\frac14}\\&\quad+\Delta t\sum_i\left((w_S^i S^i)_{1+}\left(\frac{f_S^i}{S^i}\right)_2\right)^{n+\frac12}C_2^{n+\frac12}\\&\quad-\Delta t\sum_i\left(J_S^i\chi_{SBL}^i\right)^{n+\frac12}\end{aligned}`<br>[판독 기록:24](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/_staging/recovery/read/EFDC/manuals/pdfs/EFDC_Theory_Document_Ver_12.pdf/p127-144.md:24) | [ChemFate/caltox.f90:1142](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/ChemFate/caltox.f90:1142) `          AA11 = TOXF(L,KSZ(L),NT)*TOX(L,KSZ(L)+1,NT)       ! *** SETTLING FROM TOP OF BOTTOM LAYER,        MG/M2/S`<br>[ChemFate/caltox.f90:1164](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/ChemFate/caltox.f90:1164) `          TOX(L,KSZ(L),NT) = TOX(L,KSZ(L),NT) + DTSED*( BB11 + BB22 - AA11 )*HPKI(L,KSZ(L))`<br>[ChemFate/caltox.f90:1173](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/ChemFate/caltox.f90:1173) `          TOXB(L,KBT(L),NT) = TOXB(L,KBT(L),NT) - DTSED*( BB22 + BB11 + TOXFBL(L,NT) )` | 불일치 | 코드의 BB11·BB22는 수주와 퇴적층 합에서 상쇄된다. KC=2에서 위층 침강 유입 AA11은 갱신 전 농도로 계산한다. 문서는 유입에 새 n+1/2 농도를 사용한다. 두 질량수지의 시간 수준이 다르다. |
| [Theory](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/manuals/pdfs/EFDC_Theory_Document_Ver_12.pdf) PDF 138쪽 / 인쇄 125쪽, 식 **7.53**<br>`\displaystyle \begin{aligned}(BC)_{kt}^{n+3/4}&=(BC)_{kt}^{n+1/2}\\&\quad-\Delta t\left(\max(q_w,0)+q_{dif}\right)_{kt+}\left(\frac{1}{\phi B}\left(f_w+\sum_j f_D^j\right)\right)_{kt}^{n+1/2}(BC)_{kt}^{n+3/4}\\&\quad+\Delta t\left(\min(q_w,0)-q_{dif}\right)_{kt-}\left(\frac{1}{\phi B}\left(f_w+\sum_j f_D^j\right)\right)_{kt}^{n+1/2}(BC)_{kt}^{n+3/4}\\&\quad-\Delta t\left(\min(q_w,0)-q_{dif}\right)_{kt+}\left(\frac{1}{\phi H}\left(f_w+\sum_j f_D^j\right)\right)_1^{n+1/2}(HC)_1^{n+1/2}\\&\quad+\Delta t\left(\max(q_w,0)+q_{dif}\right)_{kt-}\left(\frac{1}{\phi B}\left(f_w+\sum_j f_D^j\right)\right)_{kt-1}^{n+1/2}(BC)_{kt-1}^{n+1/2}\end{aligned}`<br>[판독 기록:24](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/_staging/recovery/read/EFDC/manuals/pdfs/EFDC_Theory_Document_Ver_12.pdf/p127-144.md:24) | [ChemFate/caltoxb.f90:188](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/ChemFate/caltoxb.f90:188) `          CUPP(L,K,NT) = min(QWTRBED(L,K),0.) - (DIFTOX(NT) + PARTDIF(L,K,NT))*(PORBED(L,K) + PORBED(L,K-KINC))/(HBED(L,K) + HBED(L,K-KINC))`<br>[ChemFate/caltoxb.f90:195](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/ChemFate/caltoxb.f90:195) `        ALOW(L,KBTP1,NT) = -MAX(QWTRBED(L,KBT(L)),0.) - DIFBWFAC*DIFTOXBW(L,NT)*PORBED(L,KBT(L))       ! *** Surface diffusion/flux`<br>[ChemFate/caltoxb.f90:246](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/ChemFate/caltoxb.f90:246) `          TOXTMP(L,KK,NT) = (RRHS(L,KK,NT) - ALOW(L,KK,NT)*TOXTMP(L,KK+KINC,NT))/BETTMP` | 불일치 | 문서는 수주와 하부 퇴적층의 기존 단계 농도로 최상층을 갱신한다. 코드는 모든 퇴적층과 최하층 수주를 같은 삼중대각 행렬에 넣어 새 농도로 함께 푼다. |
| [Theory](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/manuals/pdfs/EFDC_Theory_Document_Ver_12.pdf) PDF 139쪽 / 인쇄 126쪽, 식 **7.54**<br>`\displaystyle \begin{aligned}\Delta_1(HC)_1^{n+3/4}&=\Delta_1(HC)_1^{n+1/2}+\Delta t\left(\frac{A_b}{H}\frac{\partial C}{\partial z}\right)_{1+}^{n+3/4}\\&\quad+\Delta t\left(\max(q_w,0)+q_{dif}\right)_{kt+}\left(\frac{1}{\phi B}\left(f_w+\sum_j f_D^j\right)\right)_{SB}^{n+1/2}(BC)_{kt}^{n+3/4}\\&\quad+\Delta t\left(\min(q_w,0)-q_{dif}\right)_{kt+}\left(\frac{1}{\phi H}\left(f_w+\sum_j f_D^j\right)\right)_1^{n+1/2}(HC)_1^{n+1/2}\end{aligned}`<br>[판독 기록:25](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/_staging/recovery/read/EFDC/manuals/pdfs/EFDC_Theory_Document_Ver_12.pdf/p127-144.md:25) | [ChemFate/caltoxb.f90:195](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/ChemFate/caltoxb.f90:195) `        ALOW(L,KBTP1,NT) = -MAX(QWTRBED(L,KBT(L)),0.) - DIFBWFAC*DIFTOXBW(L,NT)*PORBED(L,KBT(L))       ! *** Surface diffusion/flux`<br>[ChemFate/caltoxb.f90:200](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/ChemFate/caltoxb.f90:200) `        BMNN(L,KBTP1,NT) = TOXTIMEI*HPK(L,KSZ(L))/(1.-TOXPFTW(L,KSZ(L),NT))                         ! *** Bottom layer of water column`<br>[ChemFate/caltoxb.f90:222](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/ChemFate/caltoxb.f90:222) `        BMNN(L,KBTP1,NT) = BMNN(L,KBTP1,NT) - min(QWTRBED(L,KBT(L)),0.) + DIFBWFAC*DIFTOXBW(L,NT)*PORBED(L,KBT(L))       ! *** Surface diffusion/flux`<br>[ChemFate/caltoxb.f90:246](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/ChemFate/caltoxb.f90:246) `          TOXTMP(L,KK,NT) = (RRHS(L,KK,NT) - ALOW(L,KK,NT)*TOXTMP(L,KK+KINC,NT))/BETTMP` | 불일치 | 문서의 수주 유출항은 기존 n+1/2 농도이다. 코드의 수주 행은 새 용존 농도를 미지수로 사용한다. 수주 내부 수직 확산은 이 행렬과 별도로 적용한다. |
| [Theory](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/manuals/pdfs/EFDC_Theory_Document_Ver_12.pdf) PDF 139쪽 / 인쇄 126쪽, 식 **7.55**<br>`\displaystyle \begin{aligned}&(BC)_{kt}^{n+3/4}+\Delta_1(HC)_1^{n+3/4}\\&=(BC)_{kt}^{n+1/2}+\Delta_1(HC)_1^{n+1/2}+\Delta t\left(\frac{A_b}{H}\partial_z C\right)_{1+}^{n+3/4}\\&\quad+\Delta t\left(\min(q_w,0)-q_{dif}\right)_{kt-}\left(\frac{1}{\phi B}\left(f_w+\sum_j f_D^j\right)\right)_{kt}^{n+1/2}(BC)_{kt}^{n+3/4}\\&\quad+\Delta t\left(\max(q_w,0)+q_{dif}\right)_{kt-}\left(\frac{1}{\phi B}\left(f_w+\sum_j f_D^j\right)\right)_{kt-1}^{n+1/2}(BC)_{kt-1}^{n+3/4}\end{aligned}`<br>[판독 기록:25](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/_staging/recovery/read/EFDC/manuals/pdfs/EFDC_Theory_Document_Ver_12.pdf/p127-144.md:25) | [ChemFate/caltoxb.f90:208](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/ChemFate/caltoxb.f90:208) `          BMNN(L,K,NT) = BMNN(L,K,NT) + max(QWTRBED(L,K),0.)      + (DIFTOX(NT) + PARTDIF(L,K,NT))     *(PORBED(L,K-KINC) + PORBED(L,K))/(HBED(L,K-KINC) + HBED(L,K)) &`<br>[ChemFate/caltoxb.f90:246](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/ChemFate/caltoxb.f90:246) `          TOXTMP(L,KK,NT) = (RRHS(L,KK,NT) - ALOW(L,KK,NT)*TOXTMP(L,KK+KINC,NT))/BETTMP`<br>[ChemFate/caltoxb.f90:279](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/ChemFate/caltoxb.f90:279) `          ERRW = ERRT - ERRB`<br>[ChemFate/caltoxb.f90:280](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/ChemFate/caltoxb.f90:280) `          TOX(L,KSZ(L),NT) = TOX(L,KSZ(L),NT) - ERRW/HPK(L,KSZ(L))` | 불일치 | 코드는 전체 퇴적층과 최하층 수주의 질량 오차를 계산하고 보정한다. 문서는 최상층·수주 두 층의 합을 제시한다. 식 (7.53)의 기존 하부 농도와 이 식의 새 하부 농도도 서로 다르다. 코드의 전체 행렬·오차 보정과 동일한 단계식으로 볼 수 없다. |
| [Theory](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/manuals/pdfs/EFDC_Theory_Document_Ver_12.pdf) PDF 139쪽 / 인쇄 126쪽, 식 **7.56**<br>`\displaystyle \begin{aligned}(BC)_k^{n+3/4}&=(BC)_k^{n+1/2}\\&\quad-\Delta t\left(\min(q_w,0)-q_{dif}\right)_{k+}\left(\frac{1}{\phi B}\left(f_w+\sum_j f_D^j\right)\right)_{k+1}^{n+1/2}(BC)_{k+1}^{n+3/4}\\&\quad-\Delta t\left(\max(q_w,0)+q_{dif}\right)_{k+}\left(\frac{1}{\phi B}\left(f_w+\sum_j f_D^j\right)\right)_k^{n+1/2}(BC)_k^{n+3/4}\\&\quad+\Delta t\left(\min(q_w,0)-q_{dif}\right)_{k-}\left(\frac{1}{\phi B}\left(f_w+\sum_j f_D^j\right)\right)_k^{n+1/2}(BC)_k^{n+3/4}\\&\quad+\Delta t\left(\max(q_w,0)+q_{dif}\right)_{k-}\left(\frac{1}{\phi B}\left(f_w+\sum_j f_D^j\right)\right)_{k-1}^{n+1/2}(BC)_{k-1}^{n+3/4}\end{aligned}`<br>[판독 기록:25](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/_staging/recovery/read/EFDC/manuals/pdfs/EFDC_Theory_Document_Ver_12.pdf/p127-144.md:25) | [ChemFate/caltoxb.f90:188](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/ChemFate/caltoxb.f90:188) `          CUPP(L,K,NT) = min(QWTRBED(L,K),0.) - (DIFTOX(NT) + PARTDIF(L,K,NT))*(PORBED(L,K) + PORBED(L,K-KINC))/(HBED(L,K) + HBED(L,K-KINC))`<br>[ChemFate/caltoxb.f90:193](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/ChemFate/caltoxb.f90:193) `          ALOW(L,K,NT) = -MAX(QWTRBED(L,K+KINC),0.)   - (DIFTOX(NT) + PARTDIF(L,K+KINC,NT))*(PORBED(L,K+KINC) + PORBED(L,K))/(HBED(L,K + KINC) + HBED(L,K))`<br>[ChemFate/caltoxb.f90:208](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/ChemFate/caltoxb.f90:208) `          BMNN(L,K,NT) = BMNN(L,K,NT) + max(QWTRBED(L,K),0.)      + (DIFTOX(NT) + PARTDIF(L,K,NT))     *(PORBED(L,K-KINC) + PORBED(L,K))/(HBED(L,K-KINC) + HBED(L,K)) &`<br>[ChemFate/caltoxb.f90:246](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/ChemFate/caltoxb.f90:246) `          TOXTMP(L,KK,NT) = (RRHS(L,KK,NT) - ALOW(L,KK,NT)*TOXTMP(L,KK+KINC,NT))/BETTMP`<br>[ChemFate/caltoxb.f90:259](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/ChemFate/caltoxb.f90:259) `          TOXB(L,K,NT) = HBED(L,K)*PORBED(L,K)*TOXTMP(L,K,NT)/(1.0-TOXPFTB(L,K,NT))` | 일치 | 내부 퇴적층에서 풍상 공극수 이류와 양방향 확산을 새 농도로 푼다. 면적당 총질량에서 공극수 용존 농도로 변수 변환한다. 총입자상 분율 하한과 입자 혼합은 추가 처리이다. |
| [Theory](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/manuals/pdfs/EFDC_Theory_Document_Ver_12.pdf) PDF 140쪽 / 인쇄 127쪽, 식 **7.57**<br>`\displaystyle (BC)_k^{n+1}-(BC)_k^{n+3/4}=-\Delta t\gamma(BC)_k^{n+1}`<br>[판독 기록:26](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/_staging/recovery/read/EFDC/manuals/pdfs/EFDC_Theory_Document_Ver_12.pdf/p127-144.md:26) | [ChemFate/caltox_kinetics.f90:208](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/ChemFate/caltox_kinetics.f90:208) `            TOXB(L,K,NT) = TOXB(L,K,NT)*(1.0 - TOXTIME*CDECAYB(L,K))` | 불일치 | 문서는 새 BC에 반응 계수를 곱하는 음해법이다. 코드는 기존 TOXB에 1−Δt·K를 곱하는 명시법이다. |
| [Theory](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/manuals/pdfs/EFDC_Theory_Document_Ver_12.pdf) PDF 140쪽 / 인쇄 127쪽, 식 **7.58**<br>`\displaystyle \frac{dC_k}{dt}=-KC_k`<br>[판독 기록:26](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/_staging/recovery/read/EFDC/manuals/pdfs/EFDC_Theory_Document_Ver_12.pdf/p127-144.md:26) | [ChemFate/caltox_kinetics.f90:99](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/ChemFate/caltox_kinetics.f90:99) `              CDECAYW(L,K) = TOXS(NT).BLK_KW`<br>[ChemFate/caltox_kinetics.f90:192](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/ChemFate/caltox_kinetics.f90:192) `            TOX(L,K,NT) = TOX(L,K,NT)*(1.0 - TOXTIME*CDECAYW(L,K))` | 일치 | 수주에 일정한 1차 분해율을 적용한다. 연속식의 항이 일치한다. 시간 적분은 명시 Euler이다. 초 단위 적분과 1/day 코드 주석의 충돌은 매개변수 표에 적었다. |
| [Theory](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/manuals/pdfs/EFDC_Theory_Document_Ver_12.pdf) PDF 140쪽 / 인쇄 127쪽, 식 **7.59**<br>`\displaystyle \frac{dC_{b,k}}{dt}=-KC_{b,k},\quad\text{for}\quad\sum_k^{kt}H_{bed,k}\le D_{max}`<br>[판독 기록:26](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/_staging/recovery/read/EFDC/manuals/pdfs/EFDC_Theory_Document_Ver_12.pdf/p127-144.md:26) | [ChemFate/caltox_kinetics.f90:114](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/ChemFate/caltox_kinetics.f90:114) `            DEPTH = 0.0`<br>[ChemFate/caltox_kinetics.f90:115](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/ChemFate/caltox_kinetics.f90:115) `            do K = KBT(L),KBOT,KINC`<br>[ChemFate/caltox_kinetics.f90:118](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/ChemFate/caltox_kinetics.f90:118) `              CDECAYB(L,K) = TOXS(NT).BLK_KB`<br>[ChemFate/caltox_kinetics.f90:208](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/ChemFate/caltox_kinetics.f90:208) `            TOXB(L,K,NT) = TOXB(L,K,NT)*(1.0 - TOXTIME*CDECAYB(L,K))` | 일치 | 누적 층 두께가 BLK_MXD 이하인 층에만 일정한 분해율을 적용한다. 경계 층을 부분 두께로 나누지 않는다. 코드 상태는 mg/m²의 TOXB이다. 같은 층 질량 기준에서 1차 상대 감소율은 동일하다. |
| [Theory](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/manuals/pdfs/EFDC_Theory_Document_Ver_12.pdf) PDF 141쪽 / 인쇄 128쪽, 식 **7.60**<br>`\displaystyle \frac{dC_k}{dt}=-K_{w,bio}C_k`<br>[판독 기록:27](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/_staging/recovery/read/EFDC/manuals/pdfs/EFDC_Theory_Document_Ver_12.pdf/p127-144.md:27) | [ChemFate/caltox_kinetics.f90:136](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/ChemFate/caltox_kinetics.f90:136) `              COEFF = TOXS(NT).BIO_KW*TOXS(NT).BIO_Q10W**( 0.1*(TEM(L,K) - TOXS(NT).BIO_TW) )`<br>[ChemFate/caltox_kinetics.f90:137](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/ChemFate/caltox_kinetics.f90:137) `              CDECAYW(L,K) = CDECAYW(L,K) + COEFF`<br>[ChemFate/caltox_kinetics.f90:192](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/ChemFate/caltox_kinetics.f90:192) `            TOX(L,K,NT) = TOX(L,K,NT)*(1.0 - TOXTIME*CDECAYW(L,K))` | 일치 | 온도 보정한 생분해율을 수주 1차 분해율에 더한다. 시간 적분은 명시 Euler이다. |
| [Theory](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/manuals/pdfs/EFDC_Theory_Document_Ver_12.pdf) PDF 141쪽 / 인쇄 128쪽, 식 **7.61**<br>`\displaystyle \frac{dC_{b,k}}{dt}=-K_{b,bio}C_{b,k},\quad\text{for}\quad\sum_k^{kt}H_{bed,k}\le D_{bio}`<br>[판독 기록:27](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/_staging/recovery/read/EFDC/manuals/pdfs/EFDC_Theory_Document_Ver_12.pdf/p127-144.md:27) | [ChemFate/caltox_kinetics.f90:156](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/ChemFate/caltox_kinetics.f90:156) `                DEPTH = DEPTH + HBED(L,K)`<br>[ChemFate/caltox_kinetics.f90:158](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/ChemFate/caltox_kinetics.f90:158) `                COEFF = TOXS(NT).BIO_KB * TOXS(NT).BIO_Q10B**( 0.1*(TEMB(L) - TOXS(NT).BIO_TB) )`<br>[ChemFate/caltox_kinetics.f90:175](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/ChemFate/caltox_kinetics.f90:175) `                COEFF = TOXS(NT).BIO_KB*TOXS(NT).BIO_Q10B**( 0.1*(TEM(L,KSZ(L)) - TOXS(NT).BIO_TB) )`<br>[ChemFate/caltox_kinetics.f90:208](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/ChemFate/caltox_kinetics.f90:208) `            TOXB(L,K,NT) = TOXB(L,K,NT)*(1.0 - TOXTIME*CDECAYB(L,K))` | 일치 | BIO_MXD 이하의 누적 깊이에 생분해율을 적용한다. 퇴적층 온도를 쓸 수 없으면 최하층 수온을 사용한다. 상태 단위는 면적당 질량이다. |
| [Theory](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/manuals/pdfs/EFDC_Theory_Document_Ver_12.pdf) PDF 141쪽 / 인쇄 128쪽, 식 **7.62**<br>`\displaystyle K_{bio}=K_{bio,ref}Q_{10}^{(T-20)/10}`<br>[판독 기록:27](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/_staging/recovery/read/EFDC/manuals/pdfs/EFDC_Theory_Document_Ver_12.pdf/p127-144.md:27) | [ChemFate/caltox_kinetics.f90:136](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/ChemFate/caltox_kinetics.f90:136) `              COEFF = TOXS(NT).BIO_KW*TOXS(NT).BIO_Q10W**( 0.1*(TEM(L,K) - TOXS(NT).BIO_TW) )`<br>[ChemFate/caltox_kinetics.f90:158](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/ChemFate/caltox_kinetics.f90:158) `                COEFF = TOXS(NT).BIO_KB * TOXS(NT).BIO_Q10B**( 0.1*(TEMB(L) - TOXS(NT).BIO_TB) )`<br>[input.f90:2326](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/input.f90:2326) `          TOXS(NT).BIO_Q10W, TOXS(NT).BIO_Q10B, TOXS(NT).BIO_TW,   TOXS(NT).BIO_TB`<br>[mod_var_global.f90:798](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/mod_var_global.f90:798) `    real :: BIO_TW   = 0.0          !< Temperature - Water (C)` | 불일치 | 문서는 기준온도를 20°C로 고정한다. 코드는 BIO_TW와 BIO_TB 입력을 각각 기준온도로 사용한다. 구조체 초기값은 0°C이다. 입력을 20°C로 설정할 때만 문서 식으로 환원된다. |
| [Theory](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/manuals/pdfs/EFDC_Theory_Document_Ver_12.pdf) PDF 142쪽 / 인쇄 129쪽, 식 **7.63**<br>`\displaystyle \left.\frac{\partial C}{\partial t}\right\rvert_{volat}=\frac{K_v}{H_{KC}}\left(f_dC-\frac{C_a}{\frac{H_L}{RT_K}}\right)`<br>[판독 기록:28](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/_staging/recovery/read/EFDC/manuals/pdfs/EFDC_Theory_Document_Ver_12.pdf/p127-144.md:28) | [ChemFate/caltox.f90:781](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/ChemFate/caltox.f90:781) `          TOXPFTW(L,K,NT) = ( TOXPFTW(L,K,NT)/(1. + TOXPFTW(L,K,NT)) ) - TOXPFW(L,K,NFD,NT)`<br>[ChemFate/caltox_kinetics.f90:224](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/ChemFate/caltox_kinetics.f90:224) `          PFTWC = TOXPFTW(L,KC,NT)              ! *** Solids partitioning fraction`<br>[Transport/calvolterm.f90:129](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/Transport/calvolterm.f90:129) `  DISFRAC = 1./(1. + PFTW)                                         ! *** Dissolved fraction`<br>[Transport/calvolterm.f90:133](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/Transport/calvolterm.f90:133) `  VOLTERM = MULT*KV*HPKCI*( CONC - AIRCON*0.001 )`<br>[ChemFate/caltox_kinetics.f90:229](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/ChemFate/caltox_kinetics.f90:229) `          TOX(L,KC,NT) = TOX(L,KC,NT) - VOLTERM*TOXTIME` | 불일치 | 문서의 인쇄 우변은 양의 변화율이다. 코드는 휘발량을 뺀다. PFTW는 이미 정규화한 입자상 분율이지만 코드는 용존 분율을 1/(1+PFTW)로 다시 만든다. 대기 농도 항은 Henry 비로 나누지 않는다. MULT 입력도 추가한다. 농도·시간 단위 차이는 매개변수 표에 적었다. |
| [Theory](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/manuals/pdfs/EFDC_Theory_Document_Ver_12.pdf) PDF 142쪽 / 인쇄 129쪽, 식 **7.64**<br>`\displaystyle K_v=(R_L+R_G)^{-1}=\left[K_L^{-1}+\left(K_G\frac{H_L}{RT_K}\right)^{-1}\right]^{-1}`<br>[판독 기록:28](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/_staging/recovery/read/EFDC/manuals/pdfs/EFDC_Theory_Document_Ver_12.pdf/p127-144.md:28) | [Transport/calvolterm.f90:61](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/Transport/calvolterm.f90:61) `  TKW = TEM_KC + 273.15                                            ! *** Temperature (Kelvin)`<br>[Transport/calvolterm.f90:62](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/Transport/calvolterm.f90:62) `  TKA = TATMT + 273.15                                             ! *** Temperature (Kelvin)`<br>[Transport/calvolterm.f90:122](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/Transport/calvolterm.f90:122) `  HE = C_HE/R_CONST/TKA                                            ! *** Henry's term (dimensionless)`<br>[Transport/calvolterm.f90:123](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/Transport/calvolterm.f90:123) `  KV = 1.0 / ( 1.0/K_L + 1.0/(K_G*HE) )                            ! *** Mass transfer rate at 20 degC (m/s)` | 불일치 | 두 막의 직렬 저항 역수 구조는 일치한다. 문서는 TK를 수온으로 정의한다. 코드는 Henry 비에서 기온 TKA를 사용한다. 수온과 기온이 다르면 Kv가 다르다. |
| [Theory](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/manuals/pdfs/EFDC_Theory_Document_Ver_12.pdf) PDF 143쪽 / 인쇄 130쪽, 식 **7.65**<br>`\displaystyle K_{v,T}=K_v\Theta^{T-20}`<br>[판독 기록:29](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/_staging/recovery/read/EFDC/manuals/pdfs/EFDC_Theory_Document_Ver_12.pdf/p127-144.md:29) | [Transport/calvolterm.f90:124](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/Transport/calvolterm.f90:124) `  KV = KV*TCOEFF**(TEM_KC-20.)                                     ! *** Mass transfer rate at water temperature (m/s)` | 일치 | 수온의 20°C 기준 거듭제곱 보정을 Kv에 곱한다. 문서의 Θ에 코드 TCOEFF가 대응한다. |
| [Theory](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/manuals/pdfs/EFDC_Theory_Document_Ver_12.pdf) PDF 143쪽 / 인쇄 130쪽, 식 **7.66**<br>`\displaystyle \begin{bmatrix}H>H_{max},\ \mathit{Lake\ conditions}\\H\le H_{max},\ \mathit{River\ conditions}\end{bmatrix}`<br>[판독 기록:29](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/_staging/recovery/read/EFDC/manuals/pdfs/EFDC_Theory_Document_Ver_12.pdf/p127-144.md:29) | [Transport/calvolterm.f90:63](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/Transport/calvolterm.f90:63) `  if( VEL_AVG > VEL_MAX .or. HP < DEP_MIN )then` | 불일치 | 문서는 H≤Hmax를 하천 조건으로 분류한다. 코드는 HP<DEP_MIN을 하천 조건으로 분류한다. HP=DEP_MIN에서 유속이 임계값 이하이면 코드는 호수 조건을 사용한다. |
| [Theory](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/manuals/pdfs/EFDC_Theory_Document_Ver_12.pdf) PDF 143쪽 / 인쇄 130쪽, 식 **7.67**<br>`\displaystyle \begin{bmatrix}U\le U_{max},\ \mathit{Lake\ conditions}\\U>U_{max},\ \mathit{River\ conditions}\end{bmatrix}`<br>[판독 기록:29](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/_staging/recovery/read/EFDC/manuals/pdfs/EFDC_Theory_Document_Ver_12.pdf/p127-144.md:29) | [Transport/calvolterm.f90:57](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/Transport/calvolterm.f90:57) `  U_AVG = STCUV*UHE/HU`<br>[Transport/calvolterm.f90:58](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/Transport/calvolterm.f90:58) `  V_AVG = STCUV*VHE/HV`<br>[Transport/calvolterm.f90:59](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/Transport/calvolterm.f90:59) `  VEL_AVG = SQRT( U_AVG*U_AVG + V_AVG*V_AVG )`<br>[Transport/calvolterm.f90:63](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/Transport/calvolterm.f90:63) `  if( VEL_AVG > VEL_MAX .or. HP < DEP_MIN )then` | 일치 | 수심 평균 유속 크기가 VOL_VEL_MAX보다 클 때 하천 식을 선택한다. 유속이 임계값과 같으면 수심 조건이 하천을 선택하지 않는 한 호수 식을 사용한다. |
| [Theory](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/manuals/pdfs/EFDC_Theory_Document_Ver_12.pdf) PDF 144쪽 / 인쇄 131쪽, 식 **7.68**<br>`\displaystyle K_L=\frac{5.35}{86400}\frac{U^{0.67}}{H^{1.85}}H`<br>[판독 기록:30](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/_staging/recovery/read/EFDC/manuals/pdfs/EFDC_Theory_Document_Ver_12.pdf/p127-144.md:30) | [Transport/calvolterm.f90:67](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/Transport/calvolterm.f90:67) `    if( HP < 0.61 )then`<br>[Transport/calvolterm.f90:69](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/Transport/calvolterm.f90:69) `      K_L = 5.35*VEL_AVG**0.667 * HPI**1.85                         ! *** Compute reaeration rate (1/day)`<br>[Transport/calvolterm.f90:70](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/Transport/calvolterm.f90:70) `      K_L = K_L * HP / 86400.                                      ! *** Multiply reaeration rate with depth to get transfer rate, then convert from m/day to m/s` | 불일치 | 0.61 m 미만의 Owens 분기와 5.35·H/86400은 일치한다. 유속 지수는 문서 0.67과 코드 0.667로 다르다. 이를 수치 상수 차이로 판정했다. |
| [Theory](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/manuals/pdfs/EFDC_Theory_Document_Ver_12.pdf) PDF 144쪽 / 인쇄 131쪽, 식 **7.69**<br>`\displaystyle K_L=\frac{(D_wU)^{0.5}}{H^{1.5}}H`<br>[판독 기록:30](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/_staging/recovery/read/EFDC/manuals/pdfs/EFDC_Theory_Document_Ver_12.pdf/p127-144.md:30) | [Transport/calvolterm.f90:65](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/Transport/calvolterm.f90:65) `    HPU = 3.45*VEL_AVG**2.5`<br>[Transport/calvolterm.f90:71](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/Transport/calvolterm.f90:71) `    elseif( HP > HPU )then`<br>[Transport/calvolterm.f90:74](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/Transport/calvolterm.f90:74) `      K_L = (DW*VEL_AVG)**0.5 * HPI**1.5                           ! *** Compute reaeration rate (1/s), because all units are already in m and s, no need to /86400`<br>[Transport/calvolterm.f90:75](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/Transport/calvolterm.f90:75) `      K_L = K_L * HP                                               ! *** Multiply reaeration rate with depth to get transfer rate (m/s)` | 일치 | O'Connor–Dobbins 분기는 HP>3.45U^2.5에서 선택한다. sqrt(Dw·U)·H^−1.5·H를 계산한다. Dw를 m²/s로 사용하여 추가 일 단위 변환을 하지 않는다. |
| [Theory](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/manuals/pdfs/EFDC_Theory_Document_Ver_12.pdf) PDF 145쪽 / 인쇄 132쪽, 식 **7.70**<br>`\displaystyle D_w=\frac{22\cdot10^{-9}}{M_w^{0.6667}}`<br>[판독 기록:13](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/_staging/recovery/read/EFDC/manuals/pdfs/EFDC_Theory_Document_Ver_12.pdf/p145-162.md:13) | [ChemFate/caltox_kinetics.f90:83](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/ChemFate/caltox_kinetics.f90:83) `    if( TOXS(NT).VOL.MW > 0. ) TOXMW = 1./TOXS(NT).VOL.MW**0.66667`<br>[Transport/calvolterm.f90:73](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/Transport/calvolterm.f90:73) `      DW = 22.E-9*MOLW_I                                           ! *** Diffusivity of toxic in water (m^2/s)`<br>[Transport/calvolterm.f90:89](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/Transport/calvolterm.f90:89) `    DW = 22.E-9*MOLW_I                                             ! *** Diffusivity of toxic in water (m^2/s)` | 불일치 | 상수 22×10^−9는 일치한다. 분자량 지수는 문서 0.6667과 코드 0.66667로 다르다. 이를 수치 상수 차이로 판정했다. |
| [Theory](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/manuals/pdfs/EFDC_Theory_Document_Ver_12.pdf) PDF 145쪽 / 인쇄 132쪽, 식 **7.71**<br>`\displaystyle K_L=\frac{5.049}{86400}\frac{U^{0.969}}{H^{1.673}}H`<br>[판독 기록:13](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/_staging/recovery/read/EFDC/manuals/pdfs/EFDC_Theory_Document_Ver_12.pdf/p145-162.md:13) | [Transport/calvolterm.f90:78](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/Transport/calvolterm.f90:78) `      K_L = 5.026*VEL_AVG**0.969 * HPI**1.673                       ! *** Compute reaeration rate (1/day)`<br>[Transport/calvolterm.f90:79](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/Transport/calvolterm.f90:79) `      K_L = K_L * HP / 86400.                                      ! *** Multiply reaeration rate with depth to get transfer rate, then convert from m/day to m/s` | 불일치 | Churchill 유속·수심 지수와 m/day→m/s 변환은 일치한다. 앞 계수는 문서 5.049와 코드 5.026으로 다르다. |
| [Theory](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/manuals/pdfs/EFDC_Theory_Document_Ver_12.pdf) PDF 145쪽 / 인쇄 132쪽, 식 **7.72**<br>`\displaystyle K_L=u_*\left(\frac{\rho_a}{\rho_w}\right)^{0.5}\frac{\kappa^{0.33}}{\lambda_2}S_{cw}^{-0.67}`<br>[판독 기록:13](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/_staging/recovery/read/EFDC/manuals/pdfs/EFDC_Theory_Document_Ver_12.pdf/p145-162.md:13) | [Transport/calvolterm.f90:116](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/Transport/calvolterm.f90:116) `      K_L = 0.0462*USTR*(DENA/DENW)**.5/SCW**0.6667                ! *** .0462 = k^0.33/LAMDA`<br>[Transport/calvolterm.f90:119](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/Transport/calvolterm.f90:119) `    K_G = AMAX1(K_G,1.0E-06)                                       ! *** Gas mass transfer (m/s)`<br>[Transport/calvolterm.f90:121](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/Transport/calvolterm.f90:121) `  K_L = AMAX1(K_L,1.0E-9)                                          ! *** Liquid mass transfer (m/s)                                                            ` | 불일치 | 코드는 κ^0.33/λ2를 고정값 0.0462로 사용한다. Schmidt 수 지수는 문서 −0.67과 코드 −0.6667로 다르다. 코드에는 전달계수 하한도 있다. 문서는 κ·λ2의 수치값을 이 쪽에서 지정하지 않는다. |

Schmidt 수 지수는 문서 −0.67과 코드 −0.6667로 다르다. 코드에는 전달계수 하한도 있다. 문서는 κ·λ2의 수치값을 이 쪽에서 지정하지 않는다. |

| [Theory](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/manuals/pdfs/EFDC_Theory_Document_Ver_12.pdf) PDF 245쪽 / 인쇄 232쪽, 식 **9.11**<br>`\displaystyle x_{n+1}=x_n+\frac{1}{6}(\Delta x_1+2\Delta x_2+2\Delta x_3+\Delta x_4)`<br>[판독 기록:23](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/_staging/recovery/read/EFDC/manuals/pdfs/EFDC_Theory_Document_Ver_12.pdf/p235-252.md:23) | [Drifter/mod_drifter.f90:402](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/Drifter/mod_drifter.f90:402) `      KWEIGHT = 6.0`<br>[Drifter/mod_drifter.f90:467](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/Drifter/mod_drifter.f90:467) `    XLA(NP) = XLA1 + (KDX1 + 2.0*KDX2 + 2.0*KDX3 + KDX4)/KWEIGHT` | 일치 | RK 분기의 증분 가중치는 1, 2, 2, 1이고 분모는 6이다. 이 판정은 가중합 식에 한정한다. |
| [Theory](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/manuals/pdfs/EFDC_Theory_Document_Ver_12.pdf) PDF 245쪽 / 인쇄 232쪽, 식 **9.12**<br>`\displaystyle y_{n+1}=y_n+\frac{1}{6}(\Delta y_1+2\Delta y_2+2\Delta y_3+\Delta y_4)`<br>[판독 기록:23](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/_staging/recovery/read/EFDC/manuals/pdfs/EFDC_Theory_Document_Ver_12.pdf/p235-252.md:23) | [Drifter/mod_drifter.f90:402](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/Drifter/mod_drifter.f90:402) `      KWEIGHT = 6.0`<br>[Drifter/mod_drifter.f90:468](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/Drifter/mod_drifter.f90:468) `    YLA(NP) = YLA1 + (KDY1 + 2.0*KDY2 + 2.0*KDY3 + KDY4)/KWEIGHT` | 일치 | RK 분기의 증분 가중치는 1, 2, 2, 1이고 분모는 6이다. 이 판정은 가중합 식에 한정한다. |
| [Theory](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/manuals/pdfs/EFDC_Theory_Document_Ver_12.pdf) PDF 245쪽 / 인쇄 232쪽, 식 **9.13**<br>`\displaystyle z_{n+1}=z_n+\frac{1}{6}(\Delta z_1+2\Delta z_2+2\Delta z_3+\Delta z_4)`<br>[판독 기록:23](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/_staging/recovery/read/EFDC/manuals/pdfs/EFDC_Theory_Document_Ver_12.pdf/p235-252.md:23) | [Drifter/mod_drifter.f90:402](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/Drifter/mod_drifter.f90:402) `      KWEIGHT = 6.0`<br>[Drifter/mod_drifter.f90:471](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/Drifter/mod_drifter.f90:471) `      ZLA(NP) = ZLA1 + (KDZ1 + 2.0*KDZ2 + 2.0*KDZ3 + KDZ4)/KWEIGHT` | 일치 | RK 분기의 증분 가중치는 1, 2, 2, 1이고 분모는 6이다. 이 판정은 가중합 식에 한정한다. |
| [Theory](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/manuals/pdfs/EFDC_Theory_Document_Ver_12.pdf) PDF 245쪽 / 인쇄 232쪽, 식 **9.14**<br>`\displaystyle \Delta x_1=u(t_n,x_n,y_n,z_n)\Delta t`<br>[판독 기록:23](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/_staging/recovery/read/EFDC/manuals/pdfs/EFDC_Theory_Document_Ver_12.pdf/p235-252.md:23) | [Drifter/mod_drifter.f90:410](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/Drifter/mod_drifter.f90:410) `    KDX1 = DELTD*(U2NP + DAHX1)` | 일치 | 첫 증분은 보간 속도와 시간간격의 곱이다. 확산계수 기울기를 0으로 두고 수직 침강속도를 0으로 두는 조건이다. |
| [Theory](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/manuals/pdfs/EFDC_Theory_Document_Ver_12.pdf) PDF 245쪽 / 인쇄 232쪽, 식 **9.15**<br>`\displaystyle \Delta y_1=v(t_n,x_n,y_n,z_n)\Delta t`<br>[판독 기록:23](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/_staging/recovery/read/EFDC/manuals/pdfs/EFDC_Theory_Document_Ver_12.pdf/p235-252.md:23) | [Drifter/mod_drifter.f90:411](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/Drifter/mod_drifter.f90:411) `    KDY1 = DELTD*(V2NP + DAHY1)` | 일치 | 첫 증분은 보간 속도와 시간간격의 곱이다. 확산계수 기울기를 0으로 두고 수직 침강속도를 0으로 두는 조건이다. |
| [Theory](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/manuals/pdfs/EFDC_Theory_Document_Ver_12.pdf) PDF 245쪽 / 인쇄 232쪽, 식 **9.16**<br>`\displaystyle \Delta z_1=w(t_n,x_n,y_n,z_n)\Delta t`<br>[판독 기록:23](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/_staging/recovery/read/EFDC/manuals/pdfs/EFDC_Theory_Document_Ver_12.pdf/p235-252.md:23) | [Drifter/mod_drifter.f90:415](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/Drifter/mod_drifter.f90:415) `      KDZ1 = DELTD*(W2NP - GRPWS(NW) + DAVZ1)` | 일치 | 첫 증분은 보간 속도와 시간간격의 곱이다. 확산계수 기울기를 0으로 두고 수직 침강속도를 0으로 두는 조건이다. |
| [Theory](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/manuals/pdfs/EFDC_Theory_Document_Ver_12.pdf) PDF 245쪽 / 인쇄 232쪽, 식 **9.17**<br>`\displaystyle \Delta x_2=u\left(t_n+\frac{1}{2}\Delta t,x_n+\frac{1}{2}\Delta x_1,y_n+\frac{1}{2}\Delta y_1,z_n+\frac{1}{2}\Delta z_1\right)\Delta t`<br>[판독 기록:23](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/_staging/recovery/read/EFDC/manuals/pdfs/EFDC_Theory_Document_Ver_12.pdf/p235-252.md:23) | [Drifter/mod_drifter.f90:412](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/Drifter/mod_drifter.f90:412) `    XLA(NP)  = XLA1 + 0.5*KDX1`<br>[Drifter/mod_drifter.f90:428](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/Drifter/mod_drifter.f90:428) `    KDX2 = DELTD*(U2NP + DAHX2)`<br>[Drifter/mod_drifter.f90:1917](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/Drifter/mod_drifter.f90:1917) `      UKB  = 0.5*STCUV(L)*( RSSBCE(L)*U2(LE,K1) + RSSBCW(L)*U2(L,K1) )` | 불일치 | 공간 위치의 중간 단계는 구현한다. 각 단계는 같은 U2·V2·W2 유동장을 읽으며 문서의 단계별 시간 인수를 갱신하지 않는다. 정상 유동에서는 이 차이가 사라질 수 있다. |
