FMHK에는 DENMHK, 층 점유율, 밀도 보정과 DXMHK 역수가 추가된다. |
| [Theory](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/manuals/pdfs/EFDC_Theory_Document_Ver_12.pdf) PDF 247쪽 / 인쇄 234쪽, 식 **10.2**<br>`\displaystyle P_M=\frac{1}{2}C_TA_M\rho U^3`<br>[판독 기록:25](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/_staging/recovery/read/EFDC/manuals/pdfs/EFDC_Theory_Document_Ver_12.pdf/p235-252.md:25) | [mhkpwr.f90:212](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/mhkpwr.f90:212) `        FMHK = (B(L,K)+1.0)/DXMHK*0.5*THRSTCOEF*VELUP*VELUP*HP(L)*DZC(L,K)*WIDTHMHK(M)*LAYFRACM(K)*DENMHK(M)  ! area is ASSUMED square`<br>[mhkpwr.f90:214](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/mhkpwr.f90:214) `        PMHK(L,K) = FMHK*FLOWSPEED(K) !ThrustCoef*\|u\|u^2*area [m^5/s^3] (will yield different power outputs depending on UPSTREAM)`<br>[mhkpwr.f90:223](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/mhkpwr.f90:223) `        PMHK(L,K) = PMHK(L,K)*(B(L,K)+1.0)*1000.0` | 불일치 | 코드는 보정한 FMHK에 국소 속도를 곱한다. 코드는 출력에서 밀도 보정을 다시 곱한다. 따라서 문서의 단일 밀도 인자를 갖는 출력식과 동일하지 않다. |
| [Theory](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/manuals/pdfs/EFDC_Theory_Document_Ver_12.pdf) PDF 248쪽 / 인쇄 235쪽, 식 **10.3**<br>`\displaystyle S_K=\frac{1}{2}C_TA_M\left(\beta_pU^3-\beta_dUK\right)`<br>[판독 기록:26](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/_staging/recovery/read/EFDC/manuals/pdfs/EFDC_Theory_Document_Ver_12.pdf/p235-252.md:26) | [calqq2t.f90:229](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/calqq2t.f90:229) `            TMPQQI = 0.5*BETAMHK_P`<br>[calqq2t.f90:230](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/calqq2t.f90:230) `            TMPQQE = 0.5*BETAMHK_D`<br>[calqq2t.f90:231](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/calqq2t.f90:231) `            PQQMHKI(L,K) = TMPQQI*(FXMHK(L,K)+FXMHK(L,K+1)+FYMHK(L,K)+FYMHK(L,K+1))`<br>[calqq2t.f90:232](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/calqq2t.f90:232) `            PQQMHKE(L,K) = TMPQQE*(FXMHK(L,K)*U(L,K)*U(L,K) + FXMHK(L,K+1)*U(L,K+1)*U(L,K+1) &` | 불일치 | 코드는 BETAMHK_P를 음해 소멸계수에 쓴다. 코드는 BETAMHK_D를 명시 생성항에 쓴다. 문서의 βp 생성·βd 소멸 배치와 반대이다. |
| [Theory](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/manuals/pdfs/EFDC_Theory_Document_Ver_12.pdf) PDF 248쪽 / 인쇄 235쪽, 식 **10.4**<br>`\displaystyle S_e=C_{e4}\frac{e}{K}S_K`<br>[판독 기록:26](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/_staging/recovery/read/EFDC/manuals/pdfs/EFDC_Theory_Document_Ver_12.pdf/p235-252.md:26) | 관련 원문 탐색 근거:<br>[calqq2t.f90:356](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/calqq2t.f90:356) `                  + DELT*HPI(L)*(CE4VEG*PQQVEGI(L,1)+CE4MHK*PQQMHKI(L,1)+CE4SUP*PQQSUPI(L,1))` | 문서에만 | 문서는 K와 ε 식으로 유도한다. 코드는 q²l 식의 생성항과 음해계수에 CE4MHK를 적용한다. 독립 ε 방정식은 이 구현에 없다. |
| [Theory](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/manuals/pdfs/EFDC_Theory_Document_Ver_12.pdf) PDF 248쪽 / 인쇄 235쪽, 식 **10.5**<br>`\displaystyle \frac{\partial}{\partial t}\left(m_xm_y\rho H\Delta_k\frac{u^2+v^2}{2}\right)=-\frac{1}{2}\rho C_TA_M(u^2+v^2)^{\frac{3}{2}}=-P_M`<br>[판독 기록:26](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/_staging/recovery/read/EFDC/manuals/pdfs/EFDC_Theory_Document_Ver_12.pdf/p235-252.md:26) | [mhkpwr.f90:214](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/mhkpwr.f90:214) `        PMHK(L,K) = FMHK*FLOWSPEED(K) !ThrustCoef*\|u\|u^2*area [m^5/s^3] (will yield different power outputs depending on UPSTREAM)`<br>[mhkpwr.f90:223](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/mhkpwr.f90:223) `        PMHK(L,K) = PMHK(L,K)*(B(L,K)+1.0)*1000.0` | 불일치 | 문서의 PM은 단순 국소 추력의 에너지 추출률이다. 코드 PMHK는 추력계수 변환과 면 방향 길이 보정을 포함한다. |
| [Theory](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/manuals/pdfs/EFDC_Theory_Document_Ver_12.pdf) PDF 248쪽 / 인쇄 235쪽, 식 **10.6**<br>`\displaystyle A_M=W_MH\Delta_k`<br>[판독 기록:26](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/_staging/recovery/read/EFDC/manuals/pdfs/EFDC_Theory_Document_Ver_12.pdf/p235-252.md:26) | [mhkpwr.f90:212](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/mhkpwr.f90:212) `        FMHK = (B(L,K)+1.0)/DXMHK*0.5*THRSTCOEF*VELUP*VELUP*HP(L)*DZC(L,K)*WIDTHMHK(M)*LAYFRACM(K)*DENMHK(M)  ! area is ASSUMED square` | 일치 | 코드의 HP*DZC*WIDTHMHK가 문서의 전면 면적이다. Sum layer force magnitudes for external mode solution (need absolute value of forces)`<br>[mhkpwr.f90:301](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/mhkpwr.f90:301) `    FXMHKE(L) = FXMHKE(L)*DXYIU(L)*HUI(L)  !external mode solution units of [1/s] divide by water-column volume: Turbine X base calculations on U face so DXYIP --> DXYIU and HPI --> HUI` | 불일치 | 코드는 면별 힘의 절댓값을 VELUP으로 나누고 면 수체적의 역수를 곱한다. 문서는 PM을 국소 속도 제곱과 셀 면적으로 나눈다. |

Sum layer force magnitudes for external mode solution (need absolute value of forces)`<br>[mhkpwr.f90:267](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/mhkpwr.f90:267) `    FXMHKA = SUM(FXMHK(L,KSZ(L):KC)*DZC(L,KSZ(L):KC)) ` | 불일치 | 외부 계수는 힘의 절댓값을 속도로 나눈 합이다. 내부 평균은 FXMHK의 DZC 가중합이다. 문서의 단일 Q 평균과 계산을 구분해야 한다. |
| [Theory](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/manuals/pdfs/EFDC_Theory_Document_Ver_12.pdf) PDF 250쪽 / 인쇄 237쪽, 식 **10.19**<br>`\displaystyle \frac{\partial}{\partial t}(H\hat{u})=-g\frac{H}{m_x}\frac{\partial\zeta}{\partial x}-\sum_{k=1}^{KC}\Delta_k(Q_k-\hat{Q})u_k-\hat{Q}\hat{u}`<br>[판독 기록:28](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/_staging/recovery/read/EFDC/manuals/pdfs/EFDC_Theory_Document_Ver_12.pdf/p235-252.md:28) | [mhkpwr.f90:272](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/mhkpwr.f90:272) `       FXTEMP(L,K) = BLOCKAGE_RATIO*( FXMHK(L,K)-FXMHKA + FXSUP(L,K)-FXSUPA )*FLOAT(KC-KSZ(L)+1)  ! Pull x-force out of MHK/support layer for internal mode - push forces in other layers`<br>[calpuv2c.f90:246](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/calpuv2c.f90:246) `        RCX(L) = 1./( 1. + DELT*FXVEGE(L) )  ! *** dimensionless` | 불일치 | 외부 음해 항력의 형태는 구현한다. 내부 잔차의 계수는 문서 Qk 잔차와 다르다. |
| [Theory](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/manuals/pdfs/EFDC_Theory_Document_Ver_12.pdf) PDF 250쪽 / 인쇄 237쪽, 식 **10.20**<br>`\displaystyle \hat{u}=\sum_{k=1}^{KC}\Delta_ku_k`<br>[판독 기록:28](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/_staging/recovery/read/EFDC/manuals/pdfs/EFDC_Theory_Document_Ver_12.pdf/p235-252.md:28) | 관련 원문 탐색 근거:<br>[mhkpwr.f90:267](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/mhkpwr.f90:267) `    FXMHKA = SUM(FXMHK(L,KSZ(L):KC)*DZC(L,KSZ(L):KC)) ` | 문서에만 | 식은 수심 평균 속도의 정의이다. 이 MHK 루틴은 같은 DZC 가중 구조로 힘을 평균한다. 독립 속도 평균 대입의 직접 대응은 이 루틴에 없다. |
| [Theory](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/manuals/pdfs/EFDC_Theory_Document_Ver_12.pdf) PDF 250쪽 / 인쇄 237쪽, 식 **10.21**<br>`\displaystyle \left(1+\frac{\hat{Q}}{H}\Delta t\right)(H\hat{u})^{n+1}+\frac{\Delta t}{2}g\frac{H}{m_x}\frac{\partial\zeta^{n+1}}{\partial x}=(H\hat{u})^n-\frac{\Delta t}{2}g\frac{H}{m_x}\frac{\partial\zeta^n}{\partial x}-\Delta t\sum_{k=1}^{KC}\left[\Delta_k(Q_k-\hat{Q})u_k\right]^n`<br>[판독 기록:28](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/_staging/recovery/read/EFDC/manuals/pdfs/EFDC_Theory_Document_Ver_12.pdf/p235-252.md:28) | [calpuv2c.f90:224](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/calpuv2c.f90:224) `      FUHDYE(L) = UHDYE(L) - DELTD2*SUB(L)*HRUO(L)*HU(L)*(P(L)-P(LW)) + SUB(L)*DELT*DXIU(L)*(DXYU(L)*(TSX(L)-RITB1*TBX(L)) + FCAXE(L) + FPGXE(L) - SNLT*FXE(L))   ! *** m3/s  `<br>[calpuv2c.f90:246](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/calpuv2c.f90:246) `        RCX(L) = 1./( 1. + DELT*FXVEGE(L) )  ! *** dimensionless`<br>[calpuv2c.f90:256](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/calpuv2c.f90:256) `        FUHDYE(L) = FUHDYE(L)*RCX(L)` | 불일치 | 코드는 압력의 사다리꼴 처리와 외부 항력의 음해 처리를 한다. 항력계수는 문서 Q 평균 대신 면별 FXVEGE를 쓴다. |
| [Theory](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/manuals/pdfs/EFDC_Theory_Document_Ver_12.pdf) PDF 250쪽 / 인쇄 237쪽, 식 **10.22**<br>`\displaystyle \frac{\partial}{\partial t}(Hu_{k+1})=-g\frac{H}{m_x}\frac{\partial\zeta}{\partial x}-(Q_{k+1}-\hat{Q})u_{k+1}-\hat{Q}u_{k+1}`<br>[판독 기록:28](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/_staging/recovery/read/EFDC/manuals/pdfs/EFDC_Theory_Document_Ver_12.pdf/p235-252.md:28) | [mhkpwr.f90:272](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/mhkpwr.f90:272) `       FXTEMP(L,K) = BLOCKAGE_RATIO*( FXMHK(L,K)-FXMHKA + FXSUP(L,K)-FXSUPA )*FLOAT(KC-KSZ(L)+1)  ! Pull x-force out of MHK/support layer for internal mode - push forces in other layers`<br>[mhkpwr.f90:298](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/mhkpwr.f90:298) `    FX(L,KSZ(L):KC) = FX(L,KSZ(L):KC) + FXTEMP(L,KSZ(L):KC)` | 불일치 | 코드는 보정한 내부 힘을 FX에 더한다. 문서의 인접층 Q 잔차를 그대로 계산하는 MHK 대입은 없다. |

### 2.7 식생·Propwash

| [Propwash](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/manuals/pdfs/EFDC+_Propwash_WhitePaper.pdf) PDF 18쪽 / 인쇄 2-3쪽, 식 **2.3**<br>`\displaystyle V_w = \lvert V_a - V_g\rvert`<br>[판독 기록:30](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/_staging/recovery/read/EFDC/manuals/pdfs/EFDC+_Propwash_WhitePaper.pdf/p001-018.md:30) | [Propwash/Mod_Active_Ship.f90:853](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/Propwash/Mod_Active_Ship.f90:853) `      EP = 23.57*power**0.974 - 2.3*(self.speed*2.237)**2 * sqrt(power)                ! *** Thrust Toutant Eq [lbs-force]  -  Ignores ambient current`<br>[Propwash/Mod_Active_Ship.f90:1086](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/Propwash/Mod_Active_Ship.f90:1086) `        vel2 = bot_velocity                                                    ! *** Ignore ambient velocities` | 불일치 | 코드는 self.speed를 mph로 바꿔 쓴다. 코드는 주변 유속 Va를 빼지 않는다. |
| [Propwash](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/manuals/pdfs/EFDC+_Propwash_WhitePaper.pdf) PDF 19쪽 / 인쇄 2-4쪽, 식 **2.4**<br>`\displaystyle T(\mathrm{N})=4.45\times EP(\mathrm{lbf})`<br>[판독 기록:13](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/_staging/recovery/read/EFDC/manuals/pdfs/EFDC+_Propwash_WhitePaper.pdf/p019-036.md:13) | [Propwash/Mod_Active_Ship.f90:862](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/Propwash/Mod_Active_Ship.f90:862) `    EP = 4.448*EP                                                                      ! *** Thrust [Newtons]` | 불일치 | 문서는 lbf를 N으로 바꾸는 계수 4.45를 쓴다. 코드는 4.448을 쓴다. 차이는 약 0.045%이며 두 인용 값의 비로 계산했다. |
| [Propwash](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/manuals/pdfs/EFDC+_Propwash_WhitePaper.pdf) PDF 19쪽 / 인쇄 2-4쪽, 식 **2.5**<br>`\displaystyle V_0=1.13\times\frac{1}{D_0}\times\sqrt{\frac{T}{\rho_w}}`<br>[판독 기록:13](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/_staging/recovery/read/EFDC/manuals/pdfs/EFDC+_Propwash_WhitePaper.pdf/p019-036.md:13) | [Propwash/Mod_Active_Ship.f90:854](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/Propwash/Mod_Active_Ship.f90:854) `      C1 = 0.71                                                                        ! *** Contraction coefficient from Maynord`<br>[Propwash/Mod_Active_Ship.f90:859](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/Propwash/Mod_Active_Ship.f90:859) `      C1 = 1.0                                                                         ! *** Contraction coefficient from Maynord`<br>[Propwash/Mod_Active_Ship.f90:872](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/Propwash/Mod_Active_Ship.f90:872) `    efflux_vel = 1.13/C1/ship.prop_diam * sqrt(EP/rho)` | 일치 | D0=C1*Dp로 치환하면 식이 같다. 비덕트 C1=0.71이고 덕트 C1=1이다. |
| [Propwash](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/manuals/pdfs/EFDC+_Propwash_WhitePaper.pdf) PDF 20쪽 / 인쇄 2-5쪽, 식 **2.6**<br>`\displaystyle \frac{V_{x,max}}{V_0}=1.51-0.175\left(\frac{x}{D_p}\right)-0.46P'`<br>[판독 기록:14](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/_staging/recovery/read/EFDC/manuals/pdfs/EFDC+_Propwash_WhitePaper.pdf/p019-036.md:14) | [Propwash/Mod_Active_Ship.f90:902](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/Propwash/Mod_Active_Ship.f90:902) `    vel_max_ax  = efflux_vel *(1.51 - 0.175 * (ax / ship.prop_diam) - 0.46 * ship.pitch_ratio)` | 일치 | 형성 구역의 최대 축방향 속도식을 그대로 쓴다. |
| [Propwash](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/manuals/pdfs/EFDC+_Propwash_WhitePaper.pdf) PDF 20쪽 / 인쇄 2-5쪽, 식 **2.7**<br>`\displaystyle \frac{V_{x,r}}{V_{x,max}}=\exp\left[-\frac{1}{2}\left(\frac{r-R_{mo}}{\sigma}\right)^2\right]`<br>[판독 기록:14](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/_staging/recovery/read/EFDC/manuals/pdfs/EFDC+_Propwash_WhitePaper.pdf/p019-036.md:14) | [Propwash/Mod_Active_Ship.f90:913](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/Propwash/Mod_Active_Ship.f90:913) `    bot_velocity = vel_max_ax * exp( -0.5*(radius - radius_vel_max)**2 / sigma**2 )` | 일치 | 정규분포의 형태가 같다. σ 값의 차이는 식 2.8에 따로 판정했다. |
| [Propwash](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/manuals/pdfs/EFDC+_Propwash_WhitePaper.pdf) PDF 20쪽 / 인쇄 2-5쪽, 식 **2.8**<br>`\displaystyle \sigma=\begin{cases}0.5R_{mo}&\text{for }x/D_p<0.5\\0.5R_{mo}+0.075\left(x+0.5D_p\right)&\text{for }x/D_p\geq0.5\end{cases}`<br>[판독 기록:14](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/_staging/recovery/read/EFDC/manuals/pdfs/EFDC+_Propwash_WhitePaper.pdf/p019-036.md:14) | [Propwash/Mod_Active_Ship.f90:911](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/Propwash/Mod_Active_Ship.f90:911) `      sigma = 0.5*radius_vel_max + 0.075*(ax - radius_prop)` | 불일치 | PDF 20쪽 이미지는 x+0.5Dp를 인쇄한다. 코드는 ax-0.5Dp를 사용한다. |
| [Propwash](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/manuals/pdfs/EFDC+_Propwash_WhitePaper.pdf) PDF 20쪽 / 인쇄 2-5쪽, 식 **2.9**<br>`\displaystyle R_{mo}=0.67\left(R_p-R_h\right)`<br>[판독 기록:14](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/_staging/recovery/read/EFDC/manuals/pdfs/EFDC+_Propwash_WhitePaper.pdf/p019-036.md:14) | [Propwash/Mod_Active_Ship.f90:905](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/Propwash/Mod_Active_Ship.f90:905) `    radius_vel_max = 0.67 * (radius_prop - 0.5*ship.prop_hub_diam)                    ! *** Radius of max vel at efflux plane (Rm0)` | 일치 | 직경의 절반을 반경으로 바꿔 같은 Rmo를 계산한다. |
| [Propwash](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/manuals/pdfs/EFDC+_Propwash_WhitePaper.pdf) PDF 21쪽 / 인쇄 2-6쪽, 식 **2.10**<br>`\displaystyle \frac{V_{x,max}}{V_0}=A'\left(\frac{x}{D_p}\right)^{B'}`<br>[판독 기록:15](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/_staging/recovery/read/EFDC/manuals/pdfs/EFDC+_Propwash_WhitePaper.pdf/p019-036.md:15) | [Propwash/Mod_Active_Ship.f90:921](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/Propwash/Mod_Active_Ship.f90:921) `      vel_max_ax = efflux_vel * Aprime * (ax / ship.prop_diam)**Bprime` | 일치 | Aprime>1인 분기에서 같은 거듭제곱 형태를 쓴다. Bprime의 차이는 식 2.12에 따로 판정했다. |
| [Propwash](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/manuals/pdfs/EFDC+_Propwash_WhitePaper.pdf) PDF 21쪽 / 인쇄 2-6쪽, 식 **2.11**<br>`\displaystyle A'=-11.4C_t+6.65\beta+2.16P'`<br>[판독 기록:15](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/_staging/recovery/read/EFDC/manuals/pdfs/EFDC+_Propwash_WhitePaper.pdf/p019-036.md:15) | [Propwash/Mod_Active_Ship.f90:917](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/Propwash/Mod_Active_Ship.f90:917) `    Aprime = -11.4 * ship.thrust_coeff + 6.65 * ship.blade_area_ratio + 2.16 * ship.pitch_ratio;    ! eqn (2.32)` | 일치 | Aprime의 세 계수와 기호 대응이 같다. |
| [Propwash](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/manuals/pdfs/EFDC+_Propwash_WhitePaper.pdf) PDF 21쪽 / 인쇄 2-6쪽, 식 **2.12**<br>`\displaystyle B'=-C_t^{-0.216}\times\beta^{1.024}\times P'^{-1.87}`<br>[판독 기록:15](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/_staging/recovery/read/EFDC/manuals/pdfs/EFDC+_Propwash_WhitePaper.pdf/p019-036.md:15) | [Propwash/Mod_Active_Ship.f90:919](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/Propwash/Mod_Active_Ship.f90:919) `      Bprime = -(ship.thrust_coeff**(-0.216)) * ship.blade_area_ratio**1.024 / ship.pitch_ratio;    ! eqn (2.33)` | 불일치 | PDF 21쪽 이미지는 피치비의 -1.87승을 인쇄한다. 코드는 피치비로 한 번 나누므로 -1승이다. |
| [Propwash](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/manuals/pdfs/EFDC+_Propwash_WhitePaper.pdf) PDF 21쪽 / 인쇄 2-6쪽, 식 **2.13**<br>`\displaystyle \frac{V_{x,r}}{V_{x,max}}=\exp\left[-22.2\left(\frac{r}{x}\right)^2\right]`<br>[판독 기록:15](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/_staging/recovery/read/EFDC/manuals/pdfs/EFDC+_Propwash_WhitePaper.pdf/p019-036.md:15) | [Propwash/Mod_Active_Ship.f90:928](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/Propwash/Mod_Active_Ship.f90:928) `    bot_velocity = vel_max_ax * exp(-22.2*(radius/ax)**2)` | 일치 | 확립 구역의 횡방향 지수식이 같다. |
| [Propwash](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/manuals/pdfs/EFDC+_Propwash_WhitePaper.pdf) PDF 21쪽 / 인쇄 2-6쪽, 식 **2.14**<br>`\displaystyle x=X_p`<br>[판독 기록:15](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/_staging/recovery/read/EFDC/manuals/pdfs/EFDC+_Propwash_WhitePaper.pdf/p019-036.md:15) | [Propwash/Mod_Active_Ship.f90:1022](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/Propwash/Mod_Active_Ship.f90:1022) `    ax = ((x_ax - pt.x_pos) **2 + (y_ax - pt.y_pos)**2)**(0.5)` | 일치 | 축선 위치에서 선박 위치까지의 거리로 x를 계산한다. |
| [Propwash](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/manuals/pdfs/EFDC+_Propwash_WhitePaper.pdf) PDF 21쪽 / 인쇄 2-6쪽, 식 **2.15**<br>`\displaystyle r=\sqrt{Y_P^2+H_P^2}`<br>[판독 기록:15](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/_staging/recovery/read/EFDC/manuals/pdfs/EFDC+_Propwash_WhitePaper.pdf/p019-036.md:15) | [Propwash/Mod_Active_Ship.f90:1057](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/Propwash/Mod_Active_Ship.f90:1057) `        radius = sqrt( d1*d1 + d2*d2 )` | 일치 | 수평 성분과 수직 성분의 제곱합 제곱근을 사용한다. 수직 거리 하한은 별도 처리이다. |
| [Theory](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/manuals/pdfs/EFDC_Theory_Document_Ver_12.pdf) PDF 30쪽 / 인쇄 17쪽, 식 **2.32**<br>`\begin{aligned}&\partial_t(m_xm_yHq^2)+\partial_x(m_yHuq^2)+\partial_y(m_xHvq^2)+\partial_z(m_xm_ywq^2)\\&=\partial_z\left(m_xm_y\frac{A_q}{H}\partial_zq^2\right)-2m_xm_y\frac{Hq^3}{B_1l}\\&+2m_xm_y\left(\frac{A_v}{H}\left((\partial_zu)^2+(\partial_zv)^2\right)+gK_v\partial_zb+\partial_pc_pD_p(u^2+v^2)^{3/2}\right)+Q_q\end{aligned}`<br>[판독 기록:27](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/_staging/recovery/read/EFDC/manuals/pdfs/EFDC_Theory_Document_Ver_12.pdf/p019-036.md:27) | [calqq2t.f90:221](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/calqq2t.f90:221) `          TMPQQI = 0.25*BETAVEG_P`<br>[calqq2t.f90:224](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/calqq2t.f90:224) `          PQQVEGE(L,K) = TMPQQE*( FXVEG(L ,K)*U(L ,K)*U(L ,K) + FXVEG(L ,K+1)*U(L ,K+1)*U(L ,K+1) &`<br>[calqq2t.f90:278](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/calqq2t.f90:278) `          PQQ  = DELT*( PQQB+PQQU+PQQV+PQQVEGE(L,K)+PQQMHKE(L,K)+PQQSUPE(L,K) )`<br>[calqq2t.f90:354](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/calqq2t.f90:354) `                  + DELT*HPI(L)*(PQQVEGI(L,1)+PQQMHKI(L,1)+PQQSUPI(L,1))` | 불일치 | 문서는 식생에 의한 순 생성항을 하나의 효율 인자로 표현한다. 코드는 BETAVEG_D가 곱한 유속 제곱 항을 명시 생성원에 더한다. 코드는 BETAVEG_P가 곱한 저항 계수를 음해 대각항에 더한다. 단일 순 생성항과 두 항의 계산이 다르다. |
| [Theory](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/manuals/pdfs/EFDC_Theory_Document_Ver_12.pdf) PDF 30쪽 / 인쇄 17쪽, 식 **2.33**<br>`\begin{aligned}&\partial_t(m_xm_yHq^2l)+\partial_x(m_yHuq^2l)+\partial_y(m_xHvq^2l)+\partial_z(m_xm_ywq^2l)\\&=\partial_z\left(m_xm_y\frac{A_{ql}}{H}\partial_z(q^2l)\right)-m_xm_yE_2\frac{Hlq^3}{lB_1}\left(1+E_4\left(\frac{l}{\kappa Hz}\right)^2+E_5\left(\frac{l}{\kappa H(1-z)}\right)^2\right)\\&+m_xm_yl\left(E_1\frac{A_v}{H}\left((\partial_zu)^2+(\partial_zv)^2\right)+E_3gK_v\partial_zb+E_1\eta_pc_pD_p(u^2+v^2)^{3/2}\right)+Q_l\end{aligned}`<br>[판독 기록:27](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/_staging/recovery/read/EFDC/manuals/pdfs/EFDC_Theory_Document_Ver_12.pdf/p019-036.md:27) | [calqq2t.f90:280](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/calqq2t.f90:280) `          PQQL = DELT*HP(L)*(CTE3TMP*PQQB + CTE1*(PQQU+PQQV) + CE4VEG*PQQVEGE(L,K) + CE4MHK*PQQMHKE(L,K) + CE4SUP*PQQSUPE(L,K))`<br>[calqq2t.f90:355](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/calqq2t.f90:355) `        CMQLTMP = 1. - CLQLTMP-CUQLTMP+DELT*(QQSQR(L,1)/(CTURBB1(L,1)*DML(L,1)*HP(L)))*(1.+CTE4*DML(L,1)*DML(L,1)*FPROX(L,1)) &`<br>[calqq2t.f90:356](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/calqq2t.f90:356) `                  + DELT*HPI(L)*(CE4VEG*PQQVEGI(L,1)+CE4MHK*PQQMHKI(L,1)+CE4SUP*PQQSUPI(L,1))`<br>[varinit.f90:242](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/varinit.f90:242) `  CE4VEG = 0.9` | 불일치 | 문서는 식생 생성항에 E1을 곱한다. 코드는 별도 CE4VEG를 생성원과 음해 대각항에 곱한다. CE4VEG 초기값은 0.9이다. PDF 원문 E4항의 분모는 κHz이다. 판독 기록의 κKz 전사는 이 표에서 PDF 이미지에 맞췄다. 쪽 이미지의 기호를 적용했다. 판독 기록의 전사는 `\begin{aligned}&\partial_t(m_xm_yHq^2l)+\partial_x(m_yHuq^2l)+\partial_y(m_xHvq^2l)+\partial_z(m_xm_ywq^2l)\\&=\partial_z\left(m_xm_y\frac{A_{ql}}{H}\partial_z(q^2l)\right)-m_xm_yE_2\frac{Hlq^3}{lB_1}\left(1+E_4\left(\frac{l}{\kappa Kz}\right)^2+E_5\left(\frac{l}{\kappa H(1-z)}\right)^2\right)\\&+m_xm_yl\left(E_1\frac{A_v}{H}\left((\partial_zu)^2+(\partial_zv)^2\right)+E_3gK_v\partial_zb+E_1\eta_pc_pD_p(u^2+v^2)^{3/2}\right)+Q_l\end{aligned}`이다. |
| [Theory](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/manuals/pdfs/EFDC_Theory_Document_Ver_12.pdf) PDF 31쪽 / 인쇄 18쪽, 식 **2.34**<br>`\tilde C_D=2.0-67ad`<br>[판독 기록:28](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/_staging/recovery/read/EFDC/manuals/pdfs/EFDC_Theory_Document_Ver_12.pdf/p019-036.md:28) | 관련 원문 탐색 근거:<br>[input.f90:7323](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/input.f90:7323) `        read(1,*,ERR = 3120)IDUM, NVEGSERV(M), RDLPSQ(M), BPVEG(M), HPVEG(M), ALPVEG(M), SCVEG(M)`<br>[input.f90:7324](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/input.f90:7324) `        BDLTMP   = BPVEG(M)*BPVEG(M)*RDLPSQ(M)          ! *** "ad" - dimensionless population density`<br>[caltbxy.f90:757](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/caltbxy.f90:757) `            CPVEGU = SCVEG(M)*CPVEGU  ` | 코드에서 찾지 못함 | 코드는 SCVEG를 입력하고 필요하면 유속·줄기 지름으로 항력계수를 보정한다. 식생 계산과 입력 코드에서 2.0−67ad 관계를 찾지 못했다. 문서의 예시 회귀를 코드의 자동 계산으로 판정하지 않았다. 쪽 이미지의 기호를 적용했다. 판독 기록의 전사는 `\bar C_D=2.0-67ad`이다. |

### 2.8 매개변수 기본값·단위 대조

문서 열은 표의 항목·값·단위 또는 식의 전사이다. 묶어서 표시한 값은 원문 표의 항목 순서를 따른다. 입력문 인용은 해당 값의 입력 경로를 뜻한다. 선언의 초기값은 파일을 읽기 전의 초기값이다. 이 초기값을 물성의 권장값으로 해석하지 않는다.

| 대상 | 문서 항목·값·단위 | 코드 줄 원문 | 확인·판정 |
|---|---|---|---|

| LPT 확산·풍압·슬립 | [Theory](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/manuals/pdfs/EFDC_Theory_Document_Ver_12.pdf) PDF 243쪽 / 인쇄 230쪽<br>`\displaystyle \frac{\partial C}{\partial t}+\frac{\partial(uC)}{\partial x}+\frac{\partial(vC)}{\partial y}+\frac{\partial(wC)}{\partial z}=\frac{\partial}{\partial x}\left(A_H\frac{\partial C}{\partial x}\right)+\frac{\partial}{\partial y}\left(A_H\frac{\partial C}{\partial y}\right)+\frac{\partial}{\partial z}\left(A_b\frac{\partial C}{\partial z}\right)` | [Drifter/mod_drifter.f90:816](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/Drifter/mod_drifter.f90:816) `    read(ULOC,*) LA_ZCAL, LA_PRAN, LA_DIFOP, LA_HORDIF, LA_VERDIF, DEPOP, ADJVERP, SLIPFACTOR, IOSWD, OSWDA, OSWDB`<br>[Drifter/mod_drifter.f90:2041](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/Drifter/mod_drifter.f90:2041) `      COEF = SQRT(2.*AH(L,K)*DELTD)*LA_HORDIF`<br>[Drifter/mod_drifter.f90:2046](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/Drifter/mod_drifter.f90:2046) `    XLA(NP) = XLA(NP) + (2.*RAND(0)-1.)*COEF`<br>[Drifter/mod_drifter.f90:2079](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/Drifter/mod_drifter.f90:2079) `    COEF = OSWDA * WSPD + OSWDB ` | 코드는 확산 선택지와 확산 계수·배율을 입력받는다. 모델 확산 선택지의 LA_HORDIF는 sqrt(2AHΔt)에 곱하는 배율이다. 상수 확산 선택지의 LA_HORDIF는 sqrt(2LA_HORDIFΔt)에 들어가는 확산계수이다. 같은 입력 이름의 차원은 선택지에 따라 달라진다. |

### 2.9 Confluence 모듈 설명 대조

아래 식은 Confluence가 제시한 번호 없는 수심 보정식도 포함한다. Confluence의 EE 기능과 Fortran의 입력·계산 기능은 같은 층위가 아니다. EE의 파일 작성 과정이 필요하면 확인 한계를 이유에 적는다.

| 대상 | Confluence 파일:줄·원문 발췌 | 코드 줄 원문 | 판정·이유 |
|---|---|---|---|
| 문서의 버전 경고 | [Water_Quality_EEMS12.md:12](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/manuals/confluence/spaces/EK/pages/EFDC_Explorer_12_Knowledge_Base/EFDC_Explorer_12_User_Guide/Model_Control_Form/Modules/Water_Quality_EEMS12.md:12) `The water quality module in EE provides access to several sub-menu items, including [*Biota*](/wiki/spaces/EK/pages/2218263101/Biota+EEMS12)*,*[*Kinetics*](/wiki/spaces/EK/pages/2218262572/Kinetics+EEMS12)*,* [*Nutrients*](/wiki/spaces/EK/pages/2219147265/Nutrients+EEMS12)*,* [*Sediment Fluxes*](/wiki/spaces/EK/pages/2220425217/Sediment+Fluxes+EEMS12)*,*[*Rooted Plants & Epiphytes*](/wiki/spaces/EK/pages/2220785665/Rooted+Macrophytes+Options+EEMS12), and [*General Options*](/wiki/spaces/EK/pages/2217607194/General+Options+EEMS12)*.* The report of the configuration can be viewed with a LMC on each item, and a RMC will open the *Water Quality* form to modify the settings as shown in Figure 1 below. These menu items are described on the pages following. Note that the implementation of the water quality varies between versions of EFDC+, so the user should be aware of what is contained in the source code for their particular version of EFDC+. What is described below relates to EEMS12.` | [Eutrophication/mod_wq.f90:335](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/Eutrophication/mod_wq.f90:335) `    if( ISWQLVL == 1 ) CALL WQSKE1 ! *** Extension of CEQUAL-ICM for unlimited algae + zooplankton` | 일치. 문서는 모델·버전마다 구현이 달라질 수 있다고 설명한다. 이 비교는 현재 저장소의 EFDC+ 원문만 판정한다. |
| 수질 반응 선택지 | [Kinetics_EEMS12.md:10](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/manuals/confluence/spaces/EK/pages/EFDC_Explorer_12_Knowledge_Base/EFDC_Explorer_12_User_Guide/Model_Control_Form/Modules/Water_Quality_EEMS12/Kinetics_EEMS12.md:10) `The *WQ - Kinetics* form is shown in [Figure 1, below](https://eemodelingsystem.atlassian.net/wiki/spaces/EK/pages/2218262572#Kinetics(EEMS12)-Figure1). Some of the user options include setting the *Global Kinetic Options* from one of several modules. For the EFDC\_GVC version, the standard kinetic model is contained in Module 3 (ISWQLVL=3). While the other kinetic modules may be used (with care) when using the EFDC\_GVC version in the standard sigma mode if the GVC option is selected, ONLY module 3 works. For the EFDC+ version, the standard full kinetic module is Module 1 (ISWQLVL=1). Once the kinetic option has been chosen, the user needs to select which parameters will be varied/simulated within the selected kinetic option. Clicking on the*Params* button will bring up the list of parameters used for simulation (i.e. advection and dispersion), then the user can switch on or off (type 1 to activate a parameter and 0 to deactivate a parameter, [Figure 2](https://eemodelingsystem.atlassian.net/wiki/pages/resumedraft.action?draftId=1034354693#Kinetics-Figure2)). However, even if a parameter is not simulated, as it is still part of the kinetics, the IC concentration is used for all of the kinetic calculations. It should be noted that parameters for algae or zooplankton will be updated automatically depending on how many groups of these organisms need to be simulated in the model.` | [Eutrophication/mod_wq.f90:334](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/Eutrophication/mod_wq.f90:334) `    if( ISWQLVL == 0 ) CALL WQSKE0  `<br>[Eutrophication/mod_wq.f90:335](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/Eutrophication/mod_wq.f90:335) `    if( ISWQLVL == 1 ) CALL WQSKE1 ! *** Extension of CEQUAL-ICM for unlimited algae + zooplankton`<br>[Eutrophication/mod_wq.f90:2622](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/Eutrophication/mod_wq.f90:2622) `  call STOPP('BAD KINETICS OPTION: ISWQLVL = 0 Does not work!  Contact DSI')   ! delme` | 일치. Confluence는 EFDC+의 전체 반응 선택지를 ISWQLVL=1로 설명한다. 코드도 1에서 WQSKE1을 호출한다. 코드는 WQSKE0에서 중단한다. |
| 수질 초기화의 기본값 | [General_Options_EEMS12.md:22](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/manuals/confluence/spaces/EK/pages/EFDC_Explorer_12_Knowledge_Base/EFDC_Explorer_12_User_Guide/Model_Control_Form/Modules/Water_Quality_EEMS12/General_Options_EEMS12.md:22) `EE will automatically initialize the water quality module using values in the code. However, it is often convenient to initialize a new model using data from another EFDC+ Explorer model with similar conditions. This is done using the *Initialize WQ Parameters from File* in the *Miscellaneous* frame as shown in [General Options (EEMS12)#Figure 5](https://eemodelingsystem.atlassian.net/wiki/spaces/EK/pages/2217607194#GeneralOptions(EEMS12)-Figure5). This operation should only be conducted once at the start of the water quality model construction. This process overwrites all of the current model's settings.` | [Eutrophication/mod_wq.f90:913](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/Eutrophication/mod_wq.f90:913) `    call fson_get(json_data, "hydrolysis.carbon.minimum_rate.RPOC",                    WQKRC)`<br>[Eutrophication/mod_wq.f90:944](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/Eutrophication/mod_wq.f90:944) `    call fson_get(json_data, "nitrification.mass_NO3_reduces_per_DOC_oxidized",        WQANDC)`<br>[Eutrophication/mod_wq.f90:945](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/Eutrophication/mod_wq.f90:945) `    call fson_get(json_data, "nitrification.max_rate",                                 WQNITM)` | 코드에서 찾지 못함. 문서는 EE의 초기화 기능을 설명한다. Fortran은 이 값을 파일에서 읽는다. EE의 초기화 값 전체를 Fortran의 하드코딩 기본값으로 확인하지 못했다. |
| 수질 공간 구역 | [Kinetics_EEMS12.md:24](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/manuals/confluence/spaces/EK/pages/EFDC_Explorer_12_Knowledge_Base/EFDC_Explorer_12_User_Guide/Model_Control_Form/Modules/Water_Quality_EEMS12/Kinetics_EEMS12.md:24) `EFDC+ allows users to assign any of the water quality parameters for water column zones. Check the box *Use Zones for Kinetics* and then click on the *Define Zones* ([Kinetics (EEMS12)#Figure 3](https://eemodelingsystem.atlassian.net/wiki/spaces/EK/pages/2218262572#Kinetics(EEMS12)-Figure3)) button brings up the *Apply Cell Properties via Polygons* form (see [Apply Cell Properties via Polygons](/wiki/spaces/EK/pages/240419005/Bathymetry)). The user must first enter the maximum number of desired zones in the *Number of Kinetic Zones* box. This number may be changed at any time during the model building process.`<br>[Kinetics_EEMS12.md:30](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/manuals/confluence/spaces/EK/pages/EFDC_Explorer_12_Knowledge_Base/EFDC_Explorer_12_User_Guide/Model_Control_Form/Modules/Water_Quality_EEMS12/Kinetics_EEMS12.md:30) `Zones are assigned by the water column layer and cell, so it is possible for the same cell to have more than one zone assigned to it if KC>1. The parameters that vary by zone are shown in the *Water Quality Kinetics*frame. There are three groups of parameters that can be varied by zone: Kinetics, Settling, and Algal Dynamics. For each group, the use of zones or not is separately controlled using the checkboxes for that group. The specific parameters for the *Current Zone* for each group are modified by clicking on the *Modify* button.  When the kinetic zones are enabled, these values are used in preference to the default values that will still be displayed under COD parameters under the *Nutrients* form.` | [Eutrophication/mod_wq.f90:1278](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/Eutrophication/mod_wq.f90:1278) `      call fson_get(json_data, "COD_decay.oxygen_half_sat_const_for_COD_decay", WQKHCOD)  ! EE WQZoneP[6]`<br>[Eutrophication/mod_wq.f90:1280](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/Eutrophication/mod_wq.f90:1280) `      call fson_get(json_data, "settling_velocity.RPOM",                        WQWSRP)   ! EE WQZoneP[8]`<br>[Eutrophication/mod_wq.f90:1281](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/Eutrophication/mod_wq.f90:1281) `      call fson_get(json_data, "settling_velocity.LPOM",                        WQWSLP)   ! | 재포기·DO 포화 선택지 | [General_Options_EEMS12.md:28](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/manuals/confluence/spaces/EK/pages/EFDC_Explorer_12_Knowledge_Base/EFDC_Explorer_12_User_Guide/Model_Control_Form/Modules/Water_Quality_EEMS12/General_Options_EEMS12.md:28) `The user can also configure *reaeration options (*[*General Options (EEMS12)#Figure 6*](https://eemodelingsystem.atlassian.net/wiki/spaces/EK/pages/2217607194#GeneralOptions(EEMS12)-Figure6)*)* in this frame. The drop-down menu provides 5 options, including constant (WQKRO), constant + wind-generated, O'Connor-Dobbins (1958), Owens & Gibbs (1964), and Owens & Gibbs (modified). Each option will assign a default reaeration value.`<br>[General_Options_EEMS12.md:34](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/manuals/confluence/spaces/EK/pages/EFDC_Explorer_12_Knowledge_Base/EFDC_Explorer_12_User_Guide/Model_Control_Form/Modules/Water_Quality_EEMS12/General_Options_EEMS12.md:34) `The saturation concentration of DO in water increases as pressure increases. This means that water at lower altitudes can hold more dissolved oxygen than water at higher altitudes. Allowance for the effect of elevation on the DO of the waterbody, can be set in *DO Saturation Options* frame ([General Options (EEMS12)#Figure 7](https://eemodelingsystem.atlassian.net/wiki/spaces/EK/pages/2217607194#GeneralOptions(EEMS12)-Figure7)) The user can select different options from the drop-down list of the *Formulation*option. Options include Garcia and Gordon (1992), Chapra et al. (1997), and Genet et al. (1974). There are three options in the *Elevation Adjustment*drop-down, including not using the elevation adjustment, as well as the Chapra et al. (1997), and Zison et al. (1978) adjustments.` | [Eutrophication/mod_wq.f90:900](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/Eutrophication/mod_wq.f90:900) `    call fson_get(json_data, "reaeration.reaeration_option",                               IWQKA(1))`<br>[Eutrophication/mod_wq.f90:5728](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/Eutrophication/mod_wq.f90:5728) `      RLNSAT1 = ((((-8.621949e11 * TVAL + 1.2438e10)*TVAL -6.642308e7)*TVAL + 1.575701e5)*TVAL -139.34411)`<br>[Eutrophication/mod_wq.f90:5729](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/Eutrophication/mod_wq.f90:5729) `      RLNSAT2 = -SWQ(L)*(( 2140.7 * TVAL - 10.754)*TVAL + 1.7674e-2)   ! *** Salinity correction: -S*(1.7674e-2 - 10.754/Ta + 2140.7/Ta^2)`<br>[Eutrophication/mod_wq.f90:5734](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/Eutrophication/mod_wq.f90:5734) `      RLNSAT1 = (((((1.41575*TVAL + 1.01567)*TVAL + 4.93845)*TVAL + 4.11890)*TVAL + 3.20684)*TVAL + 5.80818)`<br>[Eutrophication/mod_wq.f90:5735](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/Eutrophication/mod_wq.f90:5735) `      RLNSAT2 = -SWQ(L)*( 1.32412e-7*SWQ(L) + (((5.54491e-3*TVAL + 7.93334E-3)*TVAL + 7.25958e-3)*TVAL +7.01211e-3) )` | 불일치. 재포기와 포화식의 선택 기능은 대응한다. Chapra와 Garcia–Gordon의 실제 포화식은 Theory 인쇄식과 다르다. 식 8.98과 8.100의 판정을 적용한다. Confluence는 선택지 이름만으로 이 차이를 드러내지 않는다. |
| 영양염 흡착 선택지 | [Nutrients_EEMS12.md:10](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/manuals/confluence/spaces/EK/pages/EFDC_Explorer_12_Knowledge_Base/EFDC_Explorer_12_User_Guide/Model_Control_Form/Modules/Water_Quality_EEMS12/Nutrients_EEMS12.md:10) `Within the *Nutrient Options and Parameters* form ([Figure 1](#Nutrients(EEMS12)-Figure1)) settings for many of the basic nutrient parameters may be made. The nutrient parameters have been grouped by nutrient type and can be edited by clicking on the respective button. For example, the user would click on the *Nitrogen* button to edit the nitrogen default settings. If the user implements kinetic zones, then those values will be used in place of these values. However, the default values are still displayed in this form. For example, this can mean that the COD decay rate may be different in two places. In this case, the kinetic zone value will take precedence.`<br>[Nutrients_EEMS12.md:15](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/manuals/confluence/spaces/EK/pages/EFDC_Explorer_12_Knowledge_Base/EFDC_Explorer_12_User_Guide/Model_Control_Form/Modules/Water_Quality_EEMS12/Nutrients_EEMS12.md:15) `The user may also specify the *Sorption Options*. | 생물군 수·침강 선택지 | [Biota_EEMS12.md:10](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/manuals/confluence/spaces/EK/pages/EFDC_Explorer_12_Knowledge_Base/EFDC_Explorer_12_User_Guide/Model_Control_Form/Modules/Water_Quality_EEMS12/Biota_EEMS12.md:10) `EEMS allows users to simulate an unlimited number of algal and macrophyte groups, with each group assigned different parameters, such as half-saturation constants and uptake rates for nutrients according to the aquatic ecosystem model. To open the *Macrophyte and Algae Options* frame, RMC on the *Biota* option under *Water Quality* *Model Control* ([Figure 1](https://eemodelingsystem.atlassian.net/wiki/pages/resumedraft.action?draftId=1034485782#AlgaeandMacrophyteOptions-Figure1)).  In the  *Macrophyte and Algae Options* frame, the algae and macrophyte parameters are classified following their kinetics processes. Depending on what is being simulated, the following tabs are displayed: *General, Growth, Basal Metabolism, Predation, Diagenesis Linkage,*and *Vertical Migration*, each of which is summarized below.`<br>[Biota_EEMS12.md:54](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/manuals/confluence/spaces/EK/pages/EFDC_Explorer_12_Knowledge_Base/EFDC_Explorer_12_User_Guide/Model_Control_Form/Modules/Water_Quality_EEMS12/Biota_EEMS12.md:54) `Figure 6a shows the *Vertical Migration* subtab with the *Constant Velocity* approach, which is set as the default option in EE.` | [Eutrophication/mod_biota.f90:70](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/Eutrophication/mod_biota.f90:70) `      call fson_get(item, "mobility_flag",                                  ISMOB)                      ! *** `<br>[Eutrophication/mod_biota.f90:74](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/Eutrophication/mod_biota.f90:74) `      call fson_get(item, "settling_velocity",                              ALGAES(NAL).WQWS(1))        ! *** `<br>[Eutrophication/mod_biota.f90:171](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/Eutrophication/mod_biota.f90:171) `      call fson_get(item, "variable_settling",                              ALGAES(NAL).ISVARSETTLE)    ! *** Option for variable algae settling.  `<br>[Eutrophication/mod_biota.f90:176](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/Eutrophication/mod_biota.f90:176) `      if( .not. ALGAES(NAL).ISMOBILE ) ALGAES(NAL).ISVARSETTLE = 0` | 일치. 코드는 그룹별 성질을 입력받는다. 코드는 고정 생물군의 침강 선택지를 0으로 놓는다. Confluence의 Constant Velocity 기본값은 EE 기본값이다. 이동성 그룹에서 Fortran이 이 선택지를 하드코딩하는 근거는 이 입력문에 없다. |
| 고정 생물군 높이 제한 | [Biota_EEMS12.md:80](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/manuals/confluence/spaces/EK/pages/EFDC_Explorer_12_Knowledge_Base/EFDC_Explorer_12_User_Guide/Model_Control_Form/Modules/Water_Quality_EEMS12/Biota_EEMS12.md:80) `From EFDC+ 11, the kinetic of immobile or attached algae (macrophyte) is handled to grow upward from the bed layer through model layers. This feature provides an extension from the modeling of submerged macrophytes, which is originally attached and exists only at the bottom layer, to a suspended canopy such as suspended aquaculture farms. The attached algae parameter is presented in the *Fixed Biota* tab ( as shown in [Algae and Macrophyte Options#Figure 7](https://eemodelingsystem.atlassian.net/wiki/pages/resumedraft.action?draftId=1034485782#AlgaeandMacrophyteOptions-Figure7)). A layered threshold value of biomass concentration is specified. The macrophyte growth upward is accomplished by moving the biomass of a layer to the layer above if the concentration in the layer is greater than a threshold concentration and the concentration in the upper layer is less than the same threshold concentration. Note that the *Maximum Length/Height* is only considered when the *Threshold to Allow Growth Between Layers* is set to zero.` | [Eutrophication/mod_wq.f90:556](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/Eutrophication/mod_wq.f90:556) `      if( ALGAES(NAL).THRESHOLD /= 0 )then`<br>[Eutrophication/mod_wq.f90:557](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/Eutrophication/mod_wq.f90:557) `        call Macro_Veg(NAL)`<br>[Eutrophication/mod_wq.f90:563](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/Eutrophication/mod_wq.f90:563) `            HEIGHT_MAC(L,NAL) = ALGAES(NAL).MAXLENGTH` | 일치. 코드는 THRESHOLD가 0이 아닌 생체량 이동 경로와 지정 높이 경로를 구분한다. 코드의 MAXLENGTH 적용은 지정 높이 경로에 있다. Confluence의 Threshold가 0일 때만 최대 높이를 적용한다는 설명과 대응한다. |
| 생물군 유동 피드백 | [Biota_EEMS12.md:82](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/manuals/confluence/spaces/EK/pages/EFDC_Explorer_12_Knowledge_Base/EFDC_Explorer_12_User_Guide/Model_Control_Form/Modules/Water_Quality_EEMS12/Biota_EEMS12.md:82) `The feedback of macrophyte growth and its density on the hydrodynamics is also optionally considered by checking the box *Use Feedback to the Hydrodynamics* in *Hydrodynamic Feedback* frame.` | [Eutrophication/mod_biota.f90:162](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/Eutrophication/mod_biota.f90:162) `        call fson_get(item, "use_hydro_feedback",                           ALGAES(NAL).ISDRAG)      `<br>[Eutrophication/mod_biota.f90:165](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/Eutrophication/mod_biota.f90:165) `        call fson_get(item, "hydro_feedback.dragcoefficient",               ALGAES(NAL).DRAGCOEFF)      ! *** Vegetagive drag coefficient.  Includes stems and leaves`<br>[Eutrophication/mod_biota.f90:166](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/Eutrophication/mod_biota.f90:166) `        call fson_get(item, "hydro_feedback.stemdiameter",                  ALGAES(NAL).MINDIAMETER)    ! *** Minimum stem diameter. As growth occurs, stemdiameter is updated based on % dry matter, stemdensity and concentration (m)`<br>[Eutrophication/mod_biota.f90:167](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/Eutrophication/mod_biota.f90:167) `        call fson_get(item, "hydro_feedback.stemdensity",                   ALGAES(NAL).STEMDENSITY)    ! *** As growth occurs, stemdensity is constant (# stems/m2)`<br>[Eutrophication/mod_biota.f90:168](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/Eutrophication/mod_biota.f90:168) `        call fson_get(item, "hydro_feedback.blocking_factor",               ALGAES(NAL).ALPMAC)         ! *** Macrophyte cell blocking factor` | 일치. 코드는 유동 피드백 여부와 식생 항력·지름·밀도·차단계수를 별도로 입력받는다. |
| 동물플랑크톤 그룹·분배 | [Zooplankton_Module_EEMS12.md:20](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/manuals/confluence/spaces/EK/pages/EFDC_Explorer_12_Knowledge_Base/EFDC_Explorer_12_User_Guide/Model_Control_Form/Modules/Water_Quality_EEMS12/Zooplankton_Module_EEMS12.md:20) `RMC on the  *Zooplankton*sub-menu items and then select *Setting* to go to the *Zooplankton* form ([Figure 2](#ZooplanktonModule(EEMS12)-Figure2)). The user can enter the number of zooplankton groups and put the name for each one.  Here, users can also add or remove *Zooplankton* groups by clicking the *Add New* or *Delete* button respectively.`<br>[Zooplankton_Module_EEMS12.md:25](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/manuals/confluence/spaces/EK/pages/EFDC_Explorer_12_Knowledge_Base/EFDC_Explorer_12_User_Guide/Model_Control_Form/Modules/Water_Quality_EEMS12/Zooplankton_Module_EEMS12.md:25) `In the *Genera*l sub-tab ([Figure 3](#ZooplanktonModule(EEMS12)-Figure3)), the user is able to set the general parameter for zooplankton. If there are more than two zooplankton groups, the user can check the predator or prey flag.`<br>[Zooplankton_Module_EEMS12.md:31](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/manuals/confluence/spaces/EK/pages/EFDC_Explorer_12_Knowledge_Base/EFDC_Explorer_12_User_Guide/Model_Control_Form/Modules/Water_Quality_EEMS12/Zooplankton_Module_EEMS12.md:31) `A zooplankton’s parameters are classified flowing its kinetics processes: *grazing, metabolism, predation, death*. The user should configure the values in the *Grazing *([Figure 4](#ZooplanktonModule(EEMS12)-Figure4))*, Basal Metabolism *([Figure 5](#ZooplanktonModule(EEMS12)-Figure5))*, Predation *([Figure 6](#ZooplanktonModule(EEMS12)-Figure6))**, and *Death ([Figure 7](#ZooplanktonModule(EEMS12)-Figure7))* tabs.` | [Eutrophication/mod_zoopl.f90:112](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/Eutrophication/mod_zoopl.f90:112) `        call fson_get(item, "predation.fraction_of_silica.SU", ZOOPL(NZO).FSPPZ)`<br>[Eutrophication/mod_zoopl.f90:113](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/Eutrophication/mod_zoopl.f90:113) `        call fson_get(item, "predation.fraction_of_silica.SU", ZOOPL(NZO).FSAPZ)`<br>[Eutrophication/mod_zoopl.f90:116](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/Eutrophication/mod_zoopl.f90:116) `        call fson_get(item, "death.critical_DO", ZOOPL(NZO).DOCRIT)`<br>[Eutrophication/mod_zoopl.f90:117](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/Eutrophication/mod_zoopl.f90:117) `        call fson_get(item, "death.zero_DO_rate", ZOOPL(NZO).DZEROZ)` | 불일치. 그룹별 포식·사망 입력 기능은 대응한다. 코드는 입자상 실리카와 용존 실리카의 포식 반환율에 같은 SU 입력 키를 사용한다. 두 반환율을 독립 입력하는 해석은 이 경로와 다르다. |
| RPEM 결합·입력 파일 | [Rooted_Macrophytes_Options_EEMS12.md:10](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/manuals/confluence/spaces/EK/pages/EFDC_Explorer_12_Knowledge_Base/EFDC_Explorer_12_User_Guide/Model_Control_Form/Modules/Water_Quality_EEMS12/Rooted_Macrophytes_Options_EEMS12.md:10) `When the water quality module is activated, the RPEM option will also be activated. Right-click on RPEM sub-menu items to set the option calculation and coefficients. The *General* tab in the *RPEM* form ([Rooted Macrophytes Options (EEMS12)#Figure 1](https://eemodelingsystem.atlassian.net/wiki/spaces/EK/pages/2220785665#RootedMacrophytesOptions(EEMS12)-Figure1)) allows the user to enable or disable a variety of combinations for RPEM, including enabling the simulation of rooted plants or epiphytes; enabling epiphytes growing on rooted plants; enabling *RPEM – Water Column Nutrient Interaction*; and enabling *RPEM – Sediment Diagenesis Interaction.*  For details on the theory behind this sub-model, please refer to the [EFDC+ Theory](/wiki/spaces/ETG) document.`<br>[Rooted_Macrophytes_Options_EEMS12.md:74](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/manuals/confluence/spaces/EK/pages/EFDC_Explorer_12_Knowledge_Base/EFDC_Explorer_12_User_Guide/Model_Control_Form/Modules/Water_Quality_EEMS12/Rooted_Macrophytes_Options_EEMS12.md:74) `Values of the RPEM settings are saved in the file "wq\_rpem.jnp" of the model folder.` | [Eutrophication/mod_rpem.f90:758](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/Eutrophication/mod_rpem.f90:758) `    json_data => fson_parse("wq_rpem.jnp")`<br>[Eutrophication/mod_rpem.f90:763](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/Eutrophication/mod_rpem.f90:763) `    call fson_get(json_data, "water_column_nutrient_linkage", IRPEMWC)`<br>[Eutrophication/mod_rpem.f90:764](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/Eutrophication/mod_rpem.f90:764) `    call fson_get(json_data, "sediment_diagenesis_nutrient_linkage", IRPEMSD)` | 일치. 코드는 수주 영양염 결합과 퇴적층 영양염 결합을 별도 선택지로 읽는다. 코드의 주 입력 파일은 wq_rpem.jnp이다. |
| RPEM 계수 입력·구현식 | EQ. (5)   PRPS - Nutrient, light and temperature limited growth rate (/day)`<br>[Eutrophication/mod_rpem.f90:342](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/Eutrophication/mod_rpem.f90:342) `        RJRPRS(L) = RKRPORS*(ROSR*WQRPR(L)-WQRPS(L))                                                                         `<br>[Eutrophication/mod_rpem.f90:349](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/Eutrophication/mod_rpem.f90:349) `        RJRPRS(L) = RKRPRS*RISS(L)/(RISS(L)+RISSS+1E-18)                                                                           `<br>[Eutrophication/mod_rpem.f90:413](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/Eutrophication/mod_rpem.f90:413) `        WQRPSL(L) = (1.-FRPSD)*RLRPS*WQRPS(L)                           ! *** WQRPSL - Shoot carbon biomass loss due to non-respiration processes`<br>[Eutrophication/mod_rpem.f90:1379](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/Eutrophication/mod_rpem.f90:1379) `  WQRPDLN  = 0` | 불일치. 입력 폼의 반응·영양염·온도·광·분배 항목은 코드 입력군에 대응한다. RPEM의 이동식과 온도식은 Theory와 다르다. 쇄설물 질소의 비호흡 반환은 0으로 초기화한 변수를 사용한다. 식 8.155, 8.157, 8.153, 8.158, 8.185, 8.187, 8.189, 8.191의 판정을 적용한다. |
| 퇴적층 플럭스 선택지 | [Sediment_Fluxes_EEMS12.md:10](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/manuals/confluence/spaces/EK/pages/EFDC_Explorer_12_Knowledge_Base/EFDC_Explorer_12_User_Guide/Model_Control_Form/Modules/Water_Quality_EEMS12/Sediment_Fluxes_EEMS12.md:10) `Benthic flux settings are made in the *Sediment Fluxes* form. After a LMC on the *Sediment Fluxes**sub-menu items in* the *Water Quality* module, the user can access the*Sediment Diagenesis Options and Parameters* as shown in [Figure 1](#SedimentFluxes(EEMS12)-Figure1)*.* The top frame, *Benthic Nutrient Flux Method* is the option that controls EFDC's benthic flux approach. If the nutrient mass fluxes are to be specified either as constants or variables in time and space, then the diagenesis tabs are not used and are consequently disabled. If the *Full Diagenesis Model (DiToro & Fitzpatrick, 1993)* option is selected, then the three diagenesis tabs are visible to allow the user to specify the diagenesis parameters.`<br>[Sediment_Fluxes_EEMS12.md:15](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/manuals/confluence/spaces/EK/pages/EFDC_Explorer_12_Knowledge_Base/EFDC_Explorer_12_User_Guide/Model_Control_Form/Modules/Water_Quality_EEMS12/Sediment_Fluxes_EEMS12.md:15) `[Figure 2](#SedimentFluxes(EEMS12)-Figure2) shows one of the diagenesis tabs for defining the diagenesis parameters. The best way to initialize all of the diagenesis parameters is to use the *Load Settings* button. This will read an existing WQSD3D.INP file from another project and initialize the settings for the current project with those from the loaded project. After the user initializes the parameters, they can modify the settings during the model development stages. The diagenesis values from the Chesapeake Bay ICM application (Cerco & Cole, 1995) provide a good starting point for diagenesis model parameterization.`<br>[Sediment_Fluxes_EEMS12.md:35](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/manuals/confluence/spaces/EK/pages/EFDC_Explorer_12_Knowledge_Base/EFDC_Explorer_12_User_Guide/Model_Control_Form/Modules/Water_Quality_EEMS12/Sediment_Fluxes_EEMS12.md:35) `The diagenesis kinetic parameters can be divided into zones using the *Diagenesis-Zones* tab. [Figure 6](#SedimentFluxes(EEMS12)-Figure6) shows how the users access the options to set the zones and assign the zone-specific parameters. The maximum number of zones needed is set by entering the appropriate number in the *# of Zones* field. To edit a specific zone, the user should scroll to the desired zone using the up/down arrow control.` | [Eutrophication/mod_wq.f90:352](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/Eutrophication/mod_wq.f90:352) `    if( IWQBEN == 1 .and. ITNWQ > 0 )then`<br>[Eutrophication/mod_wq.f90:354](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/Eutrophication/mod_wq.f90:354) `      call SMMBE  ` | 일치. Confluence는 상수 플럭스·시계열 플럭스·전체 diagenesis를 구분한다. 코드의 diagenesis 호출도 선택지 조건을 사용한다. 문서는 Chesapeake Bay 값을 보정 시작점으로 제시한다. 이 값은 모든 대상 수역의 코드 기본값이 아니다. |
| 식생 운동량 처리 | [Vegetation.md:19](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/manuals/confluence/spaces/EK/pages/EFDC_Explorer_12_Knowledge_Base/EFDC_Explorer_12_User_Guide/Model_Control_Form/Modules/Hydrodynamics_Module/Vegetation.md:19) `- Use vegetation with implicit momentum adjustment: implement vegetation resistance`<br>[Vegetation.md:20](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/manuals/confluence/spaces/EK/pages/EFDC_Explorer_12_Knowledge_Base/EFDC_Explorer_12_User_Guide/Model_Control_Form/Modules/Hydrodynamics_Module/Vegetation.md:20) `- Use vegetation with explicit momentum sink: implement with diagnostics to file CBOT.LOG` | [calexp2t.f90:761](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/calexp2t.f90:761) `      if(ISVEG == 1 )then`<br>[calexp2t.f90:767](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/calexp2t.f90:767) `            FX(L,K) = FX(L,K) + (FXVEG(L,K)-FXVEGE(L))*U(L,K)*DXYU(L) ! *** (m^4/s^2)`<br>[calexp2t.f90:777](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/calexp2t.f90:777) `            FX(L,K) = FX(L,K) + FXVEG(L,K)*U(L,K) ! *** (m^4/s^2)` | 일치. Confluence는 음해 운동량 보정과 명시 운동량 소멸항을 구분한다. 코드는 ISVEG==2에서 명시 운동량 항을 더한다. 이 구분을 단순 진단 출력의 차이로 축소하지 못한다. |
| MHK 출력·속도 선택 | [Marine_Hydrokinetic_Devices.md:22](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/manuals/confluence/spaces/EK/pages/EFDC_Explorer_12_Knowledge_Base/EFDC_Explorer_12_User_Guide/Model_Control_Form/Modules/Marine_Hydrokinetic_Devices.md:22) `In the *Marine Hydrokinetics Device* form, the *General* tab is shown in [Figure 2](#Figure2), where the user can specify the power extracted from the flow. There are different methods for computing power extracted from flow, though currently only one method is enabled. The method is to use the specified thrust coefficient that is multiplied by velocity cubed. The user may specify the *Velocity Source for Power Calculations required for computation of power*. The available options are local cell velocity and upstream velocity.`<br>[Marine_Hydrokinetic_Devices.md:24](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/manuals/confluence/spaces/EK/pages/EFDC_Explorer_12_Knowledge_Base/EFDC_Explorer_12_User_Guide/Model_Control_Form/Modules/Marine_Hydrokinetic_Devices.md:24) `In the *MHK Interaction with Flow Field* frame, the user must specify coefficients. Guidance on how to determine these definitions can be found in the SNL user guide ([James, 2010](/wiki/spaces/EEREF/pages/2387016/References)).` | [mhkpwr.f90:175](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/mhkpwr.f90:175) `      if( UPSTREAM == 1 )then         ! use the upstream flowspeed to assess power extraction (not typical)`<br>[mhkpwr.f90:200](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/mhkpwr.f90:200) `        if( VELUP<VMINCUT(M) )then    ! no power generation`<br>[mhkpwr.f90:203](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/mhkpwr.f90:203) `          THRSTCOEF = 4.0*(1.0-SQRT(1.0-CTMHK(M)))/(1.0+SQRT(1.0-CTMHK(M))) !From Roc paper`<br>[mhkpwr.f90:212](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/mhkpwr.f90:212) `        FMHK = (B(L,K)+1.0)/DXMHK*0.5*THRSTCOEF*VELUP*VELUP*HP(L)*DZC(L,K)*WIDTHMHK(M)*LAYFRACM(K)*DENMHK(M)  ! area is ASSUMED square`<br>[mhkpwr.f90:214](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/mhkpwr.f90:214) `        PMHK(L,K) = FMHK*FLOWSPEED(K) !ThrustCoef*\|u\|u^2*area [m^5/s^3] (will yield different power outputs depending on UPSTREAM)`<br>[mhkpwr.f90:223](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/mhkpwr.f90:223) `        PMHK(L,K) = PMHK(L,K)*(B(L,K)+1.0)*1000.0` | 불일치. Confluence는 지정 추력계수와 속도 세제곱의 곱으로 출력을 설명한다. 코드는 추력에 상류 속도의 제곱을 적용한다. 코드는 출력에 국소 속도를 곱한다. 상류 속도 선택지에서는 상류 속도 세제곱과 다르다. 코드의 밀도 보정도 추가된다. |
| 독성물질 계산 시간·온도 | [Chemical_Fate_and_Transport.md:30](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/manuals/confluence/spaces/EK/pages/EFDC_Explorer_12_Knowledge_Base/EFDC_Explorer_12_User_Guide/Model_Control_Form/Modules/Chemical_Fate_and_Transport.md:30) `Here the user should assign the *Global Volatilization Settings* if simulating a river. In cases where the temperature is not being simulated in the model, use the *Temperature if NOT Simulated*frame options to set a fixed temperature. The *Computational Time Step (Second)* may also be set in the respective frame.` | [ChemFate/caltox_kinetics.f90:63](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/ChemFate/caltox_kinetics.f90:63) `  TOXTIME = TOXTIME + DTSED`<br>[ChemFate/caltox_kinetics.f90:64](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/ChemFate/caltox_kinetics.f90:64) `  if( TOXTIME < TOXSTEPW ) return`<br>[ChemFate/caltox_kinetics.f90:224](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/ChemFate/caltox_kinetics.f90:224) `          PFTWC = TOXPFTW(L,KC,NT)              ! *** Solids partitioning fraction` | 일치. 코드는 초 단위 계산 시간을 누적한다. 코드의 휘발 계산은 온도 선택과 연결된다. 분해율 주석의 일 단위 충돌은 매개변수 표에서 별도로 기록한다. |
| 독성물질 분배 선택지 | [Chemical_Fate_and_Transport.md:46](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/manuals/confluence/spaces/EK/pages/EFDC_Explorer_12_Knowledge_Base/EFDC_Explorer_12_User_Guide/Model_Control_Form/Modules/Chemical_Fate_and_Transport.md:46) `The series of tabs allow the user to define the toxic contaminant parameters in detail. In the *Partition Coefficients* tab as shown in [Figure 4](https://eemodelingsystem.atlassian.net/wiki/pages/resumedraft.action?draftId=240222357#ChemicalFateandTransport-Figure4), the user should select the S*orption Model* to be implemented from the radial buttons available, which include*: Kd (Non-OC based)*; *DOC & POC (Class Independent)*; *DOC and POC. POC =(foc\*SED) by class*; and *POC only. POC =(foc\*SED) by class*. The contents of the grid (i.e., field) will change based on the partitioning model selected. For partitioning model 3 *(DOC and POC. POC =(foc\*SED) by class)* and model 4 *(POC only. POC =(foc\*SED) by class),* there are two options for the user to set the POC and DOC value. If the user selects *Constant* radial button, then EE will save and read the value for DOC and POC directly from the *WC Partition Coeff (l/mg)* and *Bed Partition Coeff (l/mg)* for each class in parameter table. Otherwise, if the user selects the *Spatially Varying* radial button, then the *Assign* button will enable to allow the user to set the varying values of POC and DOC for each cell and each sediment class. The spatially varying coefficient will be saved to  the PARTITIONW.INP file for WC partition coefficient and PARTITIONB.INP file for bed partition coefficient. Note that if the value of **WC partition coefficient or bed partition coefficient in any class is negative, EE will change the option to** ***Spatially Varying***and read the value from PARTITIONW.INP or PARTITIONB.INP respectively.` | [ChemFate/caltox.f90:306](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/ChemFate/caltox.f90:306) `            TOXPFW(L,K,NS,NT) = TOXPFW(L,K,NS,NT)/(1. + TOXPFTW(L,K,NT))`<br>[ChemFate/caltox.f90:318](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/ChemFate/caltox.f90:318) `            TOXFDFW(L,K,NT) = 1./(1. + TOXPFTW(L,K,NT))`<br>[ChemFate/caltoxb.f90:472](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/ChemFate/caltoxb.f90:472) `            TOXFDFB(L,K,NT) = PORBED(L,K)*HBED(L,K) /(PORBED(L,K)*HBED(L,K) + TOXPFTB(L,K,NT))` | 일치. Confluence는 Kd·DOC/POC·fOC 기반 선택지를 설명한다. 코드는 담체별 평형 분율을 계산한다. 이 선택지 목록은 Theory의 Langmuir 구현을 입증하지 않는다. |
| 독성물질 농도·퇴적층 질량 | [Chemical_Fate_and_Transport.md:48](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/manuals/confluence/spaces/EK/pages/EFDC_Explorer_12_Knowledge_Base/EFDC_Explorer_12_User_Guide/Model_Control_Form/Modules/Chemical_Fate_and_Transport.md:48) `The partitioning factors and the concentration factors units must be consistent. Typical units for the toxic concentrations are mg/kg or μg/kg.`<br>[Chemical_Fate_and_Transport.md:54](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/manuals/confluence/spaces/EK/pages/EFDC_Explorer_12_Knowledge_Base/EFDC_Explorer_12_User_Guide/Model_Control_Form/Modules/Chemical_Fate_and_Transport.md:54) `The *Initial Conditions & Atmospheric Deposition* tab is shown in [Figure 5](#Figure5). The initial conditions option for all contaminants is set here for both *Water Column Concentration*and *Sediment Bed Mass,*which is  based on *IC Options* from *Global Option* tab.`<br>[Chemical_Fate_and_Transport.md:98](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/manuals/confluence/spaces/EK/pages/EFDC_Explorer_12_Knowledge_Base/EFDC_Explorer_12_User_Guide/Model_Control_Form/Modules/Chemical_Fate_and_Transport.md:98) `The initial conditions for chemical fate and transport include water column centration and sediment bed mass. Those parameters can be set at a constant value or spatial varying by clicking *Assign* button.` | [mod_var_global.f90:753](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/mod_var_global.f90:753) `  real,target,allocatable,dimension(:,:,:) :: TOX    !< Toxic contaminant concentration     (mg/m3) or (Mass/L**3)` | 불일치. 문서의 mg/kg 또는 µg/kg는 모든 수주 농도의 단위가 아니다. 문서도 수주 농도와 퇴적층 질량을 구분한다. 코드 TOX는 수주 mg/m³를 사용한다. 농도와 흡착 단위는 함께 지정해야 한다. |
| 독성물질 건식 침적 | [Chemical_Fate_and_Transport.md:68](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/manuals/confluence/spaces/EK/pages/EFDC_Explorer_12_Knowledge_Base/EFDC_Explorer_12_User_Guide/Model_Control_Form/Modules/Chemical_Fate_and_Transport.md:68) `EEMS also allows for dry and wet atmospheric deposition.  Two approaches are available for both dry and wet deposition.  First, the user can specify a temporally constant dry deposition flux in mg/m2/day and/or they can specify a time series of dry deposition.  Although these fluxes are applied over the entire domain, the applied mass flux may vary spatially because the input fluxes are specified based on square meters, so if the cell sizes vary, the applied mass fluxes will also vary.` | [mod_var_global.f90:811](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/mod_var_global.f90:811) `    real    :: TXDRY                !< Constant dry deposition flux (mg/m2/day)` | 일치. Confluence의 건식 침적 단위는 mg/m²/day이다. 코드 선언의 단위도 mg/m²/day이다. |
| LPT 수심 변화 보정 | [LPT_Main_Options_Tab.md:16](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/manuals/confluence/spaces/EK/pages/EFDC_Explorer_12_Knowledge_Base/EFDC_Explorer_12_User_Guide/Model_Control_Form/Modules/Lagrangian_Particle_Tracking/LPT_Main_Options_Tab.md:16) `The *Vertical Movement Option* frame provides the user the option of fixing the temporal position of the particles at their initial seeding depths. If this is unchecked, the particles will drift vertically as *Fully 3D Lagrangian Neutrally Buoyant Particles*.  A sub-option is *Adjust Particle Depth.* This is the default option and adjusts particle depth with a change in water depth. In some cases, the user may wish to disallow this so that particles are not adjusted by the change in water depth, and so can turn off this adjustment by unchecking this box. If the *Adjust Particle Depth* is selected, the amount of adjustment in the vertical direction depends on the ratio of particle depth and water depth. The formula of the vertical adjustment is:`<br>[LPT_Main_Options_Tab.md:18](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/manuals/confluence/spaces/EK/pages/EFDC_Explorer_12_Knowledge_Base/EFDC_Explorer_12_User_Guide/Model_Control_Form/Modules/Lagrangian_Particle_Tracking/LPT_Main_Options_Tab.md:18) `dzp=  pdep/wdep\*dH` | [Drifter/mod_drifter.f90:385](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/Drifter/mod_drifter.f90:385) `      ZLA1 = ZLA1 + (HP(LLA(NP))-H1P(LLA(NP)))*(ZLA1-BELVLA(NP))/HPLA(NP)` | 일치. 코드는 이전 바닥 기준 높이의 수심 비율에 수심 변화를 곱한다. Confluence의 pdep를 바닥 기준 높이로 해석하면 두 식이 일치한다. 문서 줄만으로 pdep의 기준면을 확정하지 못하므로 이 해석 조건을 남긴다. |
| LPT 무작위 이동·슬립 | [LPT_Main_Options_Tab.md:32](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/manuals/confluence/spaces/EK/pages/EFDC_Explorer_12_Knowledge_Base/EFDC_Explorer_12_User_Guide/Model_Control_Form/Modules/Lagrangian_Particle_Tracking/LPT_Main_Options_Tab.md:32) `The wind drift coefficient, B, can range from 0.01 to 0.06; in many applications of oil movement forecast, the coefficient is fixed at 0.03.`<br>[LPT_Main_Options_Tab.md:38](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/manuals/confluence/spaces/EK/pages/EFDC_Explorer_12_Knowledge_Base/EFDC_Explorer_12_User_Guide/Model_Control_Form/Modules/Lagrangian_Particle_Tracking/LPT_Main_Options_Tab.md:38) `If the *Random Walk* checkbox is checked, the user has a number of options for the *Random Walk Dimensional Options*. The user may select the random walk-in only the vertical direction, in only the horizontal direction, or both vertical and horizontal directions at the same time. In addition, the user may specify the *Random Walk Size Options* by selecting to allow EFDC Diffusion calculations to be used for the AH and AV. Alternatively, there is the option for *Specified Constant Diffusivities* that should be inputted by the user.`<br>[LPT_Main_Options_Tab.md:40](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/manuals/confluence/spaces/EK/pages/EFDC_Explorer_12_Knowledge_Base/EFDC_Explorer_12_User_Guide/Model_Control_Form/Modules/Lagrangian_Particle_Tracking/LPT_Main_Options_Tab.md:40) `*Wall Slippage* frame allows the user to set the wall slip factor. For most real-world cases, the wall slip should be 0.0. However, for flume and other test cases, it may be better to use 1.0 or full slip.` | [Drifter/mod_drifter.f90:816](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/Drifter/mod_drifter.f90:816) `    read(ULOC,*) LA_ZCAL, LA_PRAN, LA_DIFOP, LA_HORDIF, LA_VERDIF, DEPOP, ADJVERP, SLIPFACTOR, IOSWD, OSWDA, OSWDB`<br>[Drifter/mod_drifter.f90:2041](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/Drifter/mod_drifter.f90:2041) `      COEF = SQRT(2.*AH(L,K)*DELTD)*LA_HORDIF`<br>[Drifter/mod_drifter.f90:2046](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/Drifter/mod_drifter.f90:2046) `    XLA(NP) = XLA(NP) + (2.*RAND(0)-1.)*COEF` | 일치. 코드는 수평·수직 무작위 이동과 모델·상수 확산을 선택한다. 코드는 슬립 인자를 입력받는다. 문서의 풍압 0.03과 슬립 0 또는 1은 적용 예와 권장값이다. 해당 값은 이 입력문에 하드코딩한 기본값이 아니다. |
| 유출유 소멸·생분해 | [Oil_Spill_Modeling_with_LPT.md:23](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/manuals/confluence/spaces/EK/pages/EFDC_Explorer_12_Knowledge_Base/EFDC_Explorer_12_User_Guide/Model_Control_Form/Modules/Lagrangian_Particle_Tracking/Oil_Spill_Modeling_with_LPT.md:23) `It should be noted that EFDC will cause the drifter to disappear when the oil per drifter is less than 1.0 mm3. The user may manually choose to hide concentrations of oil higher than this by using the crop below in *2DH View*. For post-processing of the oil simulation, *2DH View* provides several options for displaying the oil. Oil thickness, mass, and volume may also be displayed.`<br>[Oil_Spill_Modeling_with_LPT.md:29](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/manuals/confluence/spaces/EK/pages/EFDC_Explorer_12_Knowledge_Base/EFDC_Explorer_12_User_Guide/Model_Control_Form/Modules/Lagrangian_Particle_Tracking/Oil_Spill_Modeling_with_LPT.md:29) `EFDC+ allows for effects of biodegradation of the oil based on a user-defined biodegradation rate with a simple first-order decay approach based on Stewart (1993). A rate of 0.011 per day leads to an approximately two-month half-life for oil. If the temperature of an oil drifter is provided by the user, then this is used as the reference rate for optimal biodegradation.`<br>[Oil_Spill_Modeling_with_LPT.md:31](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/manuals/confluence/spaces/EK/pages/EFDC_Explorer_12_Knowledge_Base/EFDC_Explorer_12_User_Guide/Model_Control_Form/Modules/Lagrangian_Particle_Tracking/Oil_Spill_Modeling_with_LPT.md:31) `To simulate a conservative oil, the user would set the degradation rate and the vapor pressure as zero.` | [Drifter/mod_drifter.f90:324](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/Drifter/mod_drifter.f90:324) `      if( DVOL(NP) <= 1D-9 )then`<br>[Drifter/mod_drifter.f90:929](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/Drifter/mod_drifter.f90:929) `        DRAT(K) = DRAT(K)/86400.`<br>[Drifter/mod_drifter.f90:2299](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/Drifter/mod_drifter.f90:2299) `    BIODEG = EXP(-TDEP*DRAT(NG)*DELTD)                     ! *** FRACTION REMAINING AT END OF TIME STEP` | 일치. Confluence의 소멸 기준은 부피 1 mm³이다. 코드 기준도 10⁻⁹ m³이다. Theory의 질량 10⁻⁹ kg 설명과는 다르다. 코드는 입력 1/day 생분해율을 1/s로 바꾼다. |

## 3 코드에만 있는 처리

이 목록은 지정 문서와 해당 Confluence 원문이 설명하지 않는 코드의 구체 처리이다. 문서에 선택 기능이 있는 경우는 그 기능 전체를 코드 전용으로 분류하지 않는다. 같은 식의 불일치와 관련된 코드 조건도 구체 처리가 문서에 없으면 이 목록에 적는다.

| 번호·처리 | 코드 줄 원문 | 확인한 처리 | 관련 문서·해석 |
|---|---|---|---|
| 1. 태양광의 네 번째 가중치 | [Eutrophication/mod_wq.f90:880](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/Eutrophication/mod_wq.f90:880) `    call fson_get(json_data, "solar_radiation.daily_weighting_factors",             WQCI)`<br>[Eutrophication/mod_wq.f90:884](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/Eutrophication/mod_wq.f90:884) `    WQCIM = WQCI(4)` | 코드는 WQCI(4)를 WQCIM에 저장한다. | Theory 식 8.13은 세 가중치의 과거 광량 합을 제시한다. 네 번째 입력의 저장은 해당 식에 없다. |
| 2. 동물플랑크톤 실리카 키 중복 | [Eutrophication/mod_zoopl.f90:112](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/Eutrophication/mod_zoopl.f90:112) `        call fson_get(item, "predation.fraction_of_silica.SU", ZOOPL(NZO).FSPPZ)`<br>[Eutrophication/mod_zoopl.f90:113](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/Eutrophication/mod_zoopl.f90:113) `        call fson_get(item, "predation.fraction_of_silica.SU", ZOOPL(NZO).FSAPZ)` | 코드는 FSPPZ와 FSAPZ에 predation.fraction_of_silica.SU를 읽는다. | Theory 표 8.17은 두 분배율을 별도 항목으로 제시한다. Confluence의 동물플랑크톤 입력 설명은 이 키 중복을 설명하지 않는다. |
| 3. RPEM 지상부 종자 하한 | [Eutrophication/mod_rpem.f90:363](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/Eutrophication/mod_rpem.f90:363) `      WQRPS(L) = max(WQRPS(L),0.2)   ! KEEP THE "SEED", SINCE IF RPS! = 0, IT WILL NEVER GROW AGAIN, ACCORDING TO EQ.` | 코드는 지상부 탄소 생체량을 최소 0.2로 제한한다. | Theory 식 8.124의 생체량 미분식에는 이 하한이 없다. 코드 생체량의 단위는 g C/m²이다. |

## 4 노트 대조와 수정안

현재 노트 인용은 기존 문장의 원문이다. 수정안은 그 문장을 대체하거나 보완할 문안이다. 이 절은 기존 노트의 수정안을 제시한다. 코드와 모델의 수치 결과를 고친 결과가 아니다.

| 대상 | 현재 노트 파일:줄·원문 | 대조 결과 | 노트 수정안 | 근거 코드 줄 원문 |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| 수질 반응 선택지 | [source-analysis/efdc_water_quality.md:49](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/source-analysis/efdc_water_quality.md:49) `` `WQ3D` dispatches kinetic scheme via `WQSKE0/1/2/3/4`, then optional zooplankton, sediment diagenesis, RPEM (`mod_wq.f90:333-358`). `` | 현재 문장은 모든 선택지를 구현된 반응식으로 읽을 여지를 남긴다. | EFDC+는 ISWQLVL=1에서 WQSKE1을 호출한다. WQSKE0은 STOPP로 중단한다. 이 문서의 수질 식 대조는 WQSKE1을 대상으로 한다. | [Eutrophication/mod_wq.f90:334](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/Eutrophication/mod_wq.f90:334) `    if( ISWQLVL == 0 ) CALL WQSKE0  `<br>[Eutrophication/mod_wq.f90:335](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/Eutrophication/mod_wq.f90:335) `    if( ISWQLVL == 1 ) CALL WQSKE1 ! *** Extension of CEQUAL-ICM for unlimited algae + zooplankton`<br>[Eutrophication/mod_wq.f90:2622](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/Eutrophication/mod_wq.f90:2622) `  call STOPP('BAD KINETICS OPTION: ISWQLVL = 0 Does not work! '매뉴얼은 흔히 입력'이라는 대비는 이번 Theory의 설명과 다르다. | 전체 diagenesis의 SOD는 Brent 근 찾기의 해이다. 코드 전달계수는 RSMSS=SMSOD1/(XSMO20+1E-18)이다. XSMO20에는 저층 DO의 3.0 하한을 적용한다. Theory 식 8.233–8.235와 코드의 플럭스 분해는 이 설명과 다르다. | sech 항은 수체 방향 용존 메탄 플럭스에 들어간다. 메탄 산화에 의한 탄소성 SOD는 CSODMSM에서 용존 메탄 플럭스를 뺀 값이다. 기체 메탄 플럭스는 공급 플럭스에서 CSODMSM을 뺀 값이다. 염분 선택지는 황화물 경로와 메탄 경로를 구분한다. | [Eutrophication/mod_diagen.f90:1221](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/Eutrophication/mod_diagen.f90:1221) `      SMSECH = 2.0 / (SMTT3 + 1.0/SMTT3)`<br>[Eutrophication/mod_diagen.f90:1225](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/Eutrophication/mod_diagen.f90:1225) `    AQJCH4SM = CSODMSM*SMSECH`<br>[Eutrophication/mod_diagen.f90:1226](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/Eutrophication/mod_diagen.f90:1226) `    CSODSM = CSODMSM - AQJCH4SM`<br>[Eutrophication/mod_diagen.f90:1227](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/raw/source_code/EFDCPlus_Stable/EFDC/Eutrophication/mod_diagen.f90:1227) `    GJCH4SM = SMJ2H2S - CSODMSM` |

| 독성물질 풍속 항력계수 | [source-analysis/efdc_toxics.md:116](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/EFDC/source-analysis/efdc_toxics.md:116) `  - **Lake/quiescent** (air turbulence 지배): Schmidt 수($SC_w=\nu_w/D_w$, $SC_a$), wind drag $C_D=(6.1+0.63W)\times10^{-4}$, $U_*=W\sqrt{C_D}$. **KL_OPT=2 Mackay-Yeun(1983)** $K_L=0.0144U_*^{2.2}/\sqrt{SC_w}$($U_*$<0.3) / **KL_OPT=1 O'Connor** $K_L=0.0462U_*(\rho_a/\rho_w)^{0.5}/SC_w^{0.667}$; $K_G=0.0462U_*/SC_a^{0.667}$` | 현재 노트는 항력계수의 하한과 Mackay–Yeun의 높은 전단속도 분기를 생략한다. | 코드 풍속 항력계수는 (6.1+0.63W)·1E-4이다. 코드는 이 값에 최소 0.0011을 적용한다. Mackay–Yeun의 u*<0.3 분기 계수는 0.0144이다. 코드는 u*≥0.3에서 다른 분기식을 사용한다.
